## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/align.py`

```python
import numpy as np, wave, re, subprocess, sys
w = wave.open('vo48.wav'); sr = w.getframerate(); x = np.frombuffer(w.readframes(w.getnframes()), np.int16).astype(float) / 32768
out = subprocess.run(['ffmpeg','-i','vo.wav','-af','silencedetect=noise=-35dB:d=0.18','-f','null','-'],capture_output=True,text=True).stderr
st=[float(v) for v in re.findall(r'silence_start: ([\d.]+)',out)]; en=[float(v) for v in re.findall(r'silence_end: ([\d.]+)',out)]
dur=len(x)/sr
segs=[]; prev=0.0
for a,b in zip(st,en):
    if a-prev>0.05: segs.append((round(prev,2),round(a,2)))
    prev=b
if dur-prev>0.05: segs.append((round(prev,2),round(dur,2)))
hop=int(sr*.01); env=np.array([np.sqrt(np.mean(x[i:i+hop*2]**2)) for i in range(0,len(x)-hop*2,hop)]); env=np.convolve(env,np.hanning(7)/np.hanning(7).sum(),'same')
def peaks(a,b):
    e=env[int(a*100):int(b*100)]
    if len(e)<3: return 0
    thr=max(e.max()*.25,np.median(e)*.6); n=0; last=-10
    for i in range(1,len(e)-1):
        if e[i]>e[i-1] and e[i]>=e[i+1] and e[i]>thr and i-last>=9: n+=1; last=i
    return n
P=[peaks(a,b) for a,b in segs]
lines=[]  # the spoken lines of the script, without [tags]; run: python3 align.py <number of lines> x
def syl(s): return sum(max(1,round(len(re.sub('[اويى]','',w))*0.75)) for w in s.split())
def run(L):
    S=[syl(l) for l in L]; r=sum(P)/sum(S); N,M=len(segs),len(L)
    INF=1e18; D=[[INF]*(N+1) for _ in range(M+1)]; B=[[0]*(N+1) for _ in range(M+1)]; D[0][0]=0
    for i in range(1,M+1):
        for j in range(1,N+1):
            for k in range(i-1,j):
                if D[i-1][k]>=INF: continue
                p=sum(P[k:j]); e=S[i-1]*r; c=D[i-1][k]+(p-e)**2/max(e,1)
                if c<D[i][j]: D[i][j]=c; B[i][j]=k
    j=N; res=[]
    for i in range(M,0,-1): k=B[i][j]; res.append((i,k,j)); j=k
    return D[M][N], r, list(reversed(res)), S
print('segs',len(segs),'peaks',sum(P))
for n in (len(lines),):
    c,r,res,S=run(lines[:n]); print(f'--- {n} lines cost {c:.1f} rate {r:.2f}')
    if n==int(sys.argv[1]):
        for i,k,j in res: print(f'L{i}: segs {k+1}-{j} t={segs[k][0]:.2f}-{segs[j-1][1]:.2f} ' + ' | '.join(f'{segs[q][0]:.2f}' for q in range(k,j)) + f'  pk={sum(P[k:j])} exp={S[i-1]*r:.1f}')
if len(sys.argv)>2:
    for i,(a,b) in enumerate(segs): print(i+1,a,b,round(b-a,2),P[i])
```

```javascript
let LOGO3D, WM;
function drawImg(img, x, y, w, o = {}) {
  const h = w * img.height / img.width;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= (o.alpha ?? 1);
  if (o.glow) { ctx.shadowColor = o.glowColor || C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = o.glow; }
  ctx.drawImage(img, x - w / 2, y - h / 2, w, h);
  ctx.restore();
}
const GOOD = '#5fd38a', BLUE = '#5aa9ff';

// ---------- brightness patterns ----------
function flick(t, seed = 0, rate = 13) { // erratic flicker / cut-out
  const k = Math.floor(t * rate), r = rand(k * 3.17 + seed * 11.3);
  return r < .28 ? .04 : r < .45 ? .45 : 1;
}
const ripple = (t, f = 6) => .58 + .42 * Math.sin(t * Math.PI * 2 * f);
function cycle(t, t0, on = .55, off = .35) { const u = (t - t0) % (on + off); return u < on ? 1 : .03; }

// ---------- drawing helpers ----------
// LED A-bulb, L = brightness 0..1
function bulb(x, y, r, L, o = {}) {
  const a = o.a ?? 1; if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  if (L > .02) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; glow(0, -r * .25, r * 3.2, o.col || '#FFD6A0', .55 * L); glow(0, -r * .25, r * 1.4, '#FFFFFF', .35 * L); ctx.restore(); }
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -r * .25, r, Math.PI * .78, Math.PI * .22); ctx.lineTo(r * .42, r * .95); ctx.lineTo(-r * .42, r * .95); ctx.closePath();
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, -r * .3, r * .1, 0, -r * .2, r * 1.1);
  g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,250,238,${.18 + .82 * L})`); g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(255,214,160,${.08 + .62 * L})`);
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineWidth = r * .04; ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${.35 + .3 * (1 - L)})`; ctx.stroke();
  if (o.filament) { ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,190,90,${.5 + .5 * L})`; ctx.lineWidth = r * .05; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-r * .2, r * .7); ctx.lineTo(-r * .2, -r * .1); for (let i = 0; i <= 8; i++) ctx.lineTo(-r * .2 + i * r * .05, -r * .1 + (i % 2 ? -r * .12 : 0)); ctx.lineTo(r * .2, r * .7); ctx.stroke(); }
  // base
  const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(-r * .42, 0, r * .42, 0); bg.addColorStop(0, '#8d949c'); bg.addColorStop(.5, '#e2e6ea'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#8d949c');
  ctx.fillStyle = bg; rrect(-r * .42, r * .95, r * .84, r * .42, r * .06); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = r * .03;
  for (let i = 1; i < 4; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-r * .42, r * .95 + i * r * .105); ctx.lineTo(r * .42, r * .95 + i * r * .105 - r * .04); ctx.stroke(); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#3a3a3e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-r * .3, r * 1.37); ctx.lineTo(r * .3, r * 1.37); ctx.lineTo(r * .12, r * 1.55); ctx.lineTo(-r * .12, r * 1.55); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}
// driver box
function driverBox(x, y, w, h, o = {}) {
  const a = o.a ?? 1; if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  const col = o.bad ? C.red : (o.good ? GOOD : C.o);
  ctx.shadowColor = rgba(col, .6); ctx.shadowBlur = 40;
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -h / 2, 0, h / 2); g.addColorStop(0, '#2b2730'); g.addColorStop(1, '#17151b');
  rrect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 22); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.stroke();
  // terminals
  ctx.fillStyle = '#9aa2ab';
  [-1, 1].forEach(s => { [-h * .18, h * .18].forEach(dy => { ctx.fillRect(s > 0 ? w / 2 : -w / 2 - 26, dy - 6, 26, 12); }); });
  txt('DRIVER', 0, -h * .22, { size: h * .17, weight: 900, color: C.w, dir: 'ltr', spacing: 6 });
  // heartbeat line
  const beat = o.beat ?? 0;
  ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.beginPath();
  const bw = w * .7, y0 = h * .18;
  for (let i = 0; i <= 60; i++) {
    const u = i / 60, xx = -bw / 2 + u * bw;
    const ph = ((u - beat) % 1 + 1) % 1;
    let v = 0; if (ph > .4 && ph < .46) v = -1; else if (ph >= .46 && ph < .52) v = .9; else if (ph >= .52 && ph < .56) v = -.4;
    i ? ctx.lineTo(xx, y0 + v * h * .22) : ctx.moveTo(xx, y0 + v * h * .22);
  }
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}
// waveform plot fn(u) in [-1,1]
function wave(x0, y, w, h, fn, o = {}) {
  const a = o.a ?? 1, p = o.p ?? 1; if (a <= 0 || p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y); ctx.lineTo(x0 + w, y); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = o.col || C.o2; ctx.lineWidth = o.lw || 7; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.shadowColor = o.col || C.o2; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
  ctx.beginPath();
  const n = 160;
  for (let i = 0; i <= n * p; i++) { const u = i / n; const yy = y - fn(u) * h / 2; i ? ctx.lineTo(x0 + u * w, yy) : ctx.moveTo(x0 + u * w, yy); }
  ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
}
// dimmer wall plate
function dimmer(x, y, s, o = {}) {
  const a = o.a ?? 1; if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30;
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(-s / 2, -s / 2, s / 2, s / 2); g.addColorStop(0, o.old ? '#d8cfbf' : '#f4f1ec'); g.addColorStop(1, o.old ? '#a99f8d' : '#cfcac2');
  rrect(-s / 2, -s / 2, s, s, s * .1); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  const kg = ctx.createRadialGradient(-s * .06, -s * .06, s * .02, 0, 0, s * .28); kg.addColorStop(0, '#5a5560'); kg.addColorStop(1, '#232027');
  ctx.fillStyle = kg; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, s * .26, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  ctx.rotate(o.rot || -.6); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o2; ctx.lineWidth = s * .04; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -s * .06); ctx.lineTo(0, -s * .2); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
  if (o.label) txt(o.label, x, y + s / 2 + 44, { size: 38, weight: 800, color: o.labelCol || C.warm, alpha: a });
}
// rocker switch with optional indicator light
function rocker(x, y, s, on, o = {}) {
  const a = o.a ?? 1; if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30;
  rrect(-s / 2, -s / 2, s, s, s * .1); ctx.fillStyle = '#f2efea'; ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  rrect(-s * .26, -s * .36, s * .52, s * .72, s * .06); ctx.fillStyle = '#dcd7d0'; ctx.fill();
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -s * .36, 0, s * .36);
  if (on) { g.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff'); g.addColorStop(1, '#c9c3ba'); } else { g.addColorStop(0, '#c9c3ba'); g.addColorStop(1, '#ffffff'); }
  rrect(-s * .24, -s * .34, s * .48, s * .68, s * .05); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-s * .24, 0); ctx.lineTo(s * .24, 0); ctx.stroke();
  if (o.ind) { const li = o.ind; glow(0, on ? s * .2 : -s * .2, s * .2, C.o2, .8 * li); ctx.fillStyle = mixHex('#7a4a35', '#ff8a4a', li); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, on ? s * .2 : -s * .2, s * .035, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function thermo(x, y, h, v, a = 1) { // v 0..1
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  rrect(-22, -h / 2, 44, h, 22); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.stroke();
  const col = mixHex('#ffb347', '#ff3b3b', v);
  const fh = (h - 40) * v;
  ctx.fillStyle = col; rrect(-11, h / 2 - 20 - fh, 22, fh + 10, 11); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, h / 2, 36, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); glow(0, h / 2, 90, col, .4 * v);
  ctx.restore();
}
function heatWaves(x, y, t, a) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(C.red, .8); ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  for (let k = -1; k <= 1; k++) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let i = 0; i <= 20; i++) { const u = i / 20, yy = y - u * 110, xx = x + k * 50 + Math.sin(u * 9 - t * 6 + k) * 10; i ? ctx.lineTo(xx, yy) : ctx.moveTo(xx, yy); }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
function sparks(x, y, t, a, seed = 0) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  const k = Math.floor(t * 12);
  if (rand(k + seed * 7) > .45) {
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      const ang = rand(k * 9 + i + seed) * 6.28, L = 20 + rand(k * 5 + i) * 40;
      ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(i % 2 ? C.amber : '#ffffff', a); ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + Math.cos(ang) * L, y + Math.sin(ang) * L); ctx.stroke();
    }
    glow(x, y, 60, C.amber, .7 * a);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
function badge(n, x, y, s, col = C.o) {
  ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, s, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  txt(String(n), x, y + 3, { size: s * 1.1, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' });
}
function label(s, x, y, t, t0, t1, o = {}) { chip(s, x, y, t, t0, t1, { size: 40, ...o }); }
function sparkles(t, t0, n, x0, y0, w, h) {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const sp = prog(t, t0 + i * .06, t0 + .6 + i * .06); if (sp <= 0 || sp >= 1) continue;
    const x = x0 + rand(i + 90) * w, y = y0 + rand(i + 91) * h, s = Math.sin(sp * Math.PI) * 26;
    ctx.fillStyle = C.amber; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y - s); ctx.lineTo(x + s * .3, y); ctx.lineTo(x, y + s); ctx.lineTo(x - s * .3, y); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - s, y); ctx.lineTo(x, y + s * .3); ctx.lineTo(x + s, y); ctx.lineTo(x, y - s * .3); ctx.fill();
  }
}
function header(t, n, title, tN, tT, tEnd) {
  const out = tEnd ? clamp((tEnd - t) / .25) : 1;
  const p = E.back(prog(t, tN, tN + .45));
  if (p > 0) {
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= out; ctx.translate(540, 250); ctx.scale(p, p);
    ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 52, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    txt(String(n), 0, 4, { size: 58, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' });
    ctx.restore();
    txt('السبب', 540, 168, { size: 32, weight: 700, color: C.g, alpha: out * Math.min(1, p) });
  }
  pop(title, 540, 360, t, tT, tEnd, { size: 82, weight: 900, color: C.w, glow: 25 });
}
function arrowR(x0, x1, y, a = 1, col = C.o2) { // arrow pointing from x0 to x1
  if (a <= 0) return;
  const s = Math.sign(x1 - x0);
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y); ctx.lineTo(x1 - s * 14, y); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x1, y); ctx.lineTo(x1 - s * 24, y - 15); ctx.lineTo(x1 - s * 24, y + 15); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}
function arrowD(x, y0, y1, a = 1, col = C.o2) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y0); ctx.lineTo(x, y1 - 14); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y1); ctx.lineTo(x - 15, y1 - 24); ctx.lineTo(x + 15, y1 - 24); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}
// row card: number/icon on right, text, drawing slot on left
function rowCard(t, t0, y, title, o = {}) {
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return 0;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= Math.min(1, p * 2) * (o.a ?? 1); ctx.translate(540, y); ctx.scale(p, p);
  card(-440, -(o.h || 90), 880, (o.h || 90) * 2, 34, 1, o.stroke || C.o);
  if (o.n !== undefined) badge(o.n, 380, 0, 34, o.badgeCol || C.o);
  txt(title, o.n !== undefined ? 320 : 400, o.sub ? -22 : 0, { size: o.size || 48, weight: 900, color: C.w, align: 'right' });
  if (o.sub) txt(o.sub, o.n !== undefined ? 320 : 400, 34, { size: 32, weight: 700, color: C.warm, align: 'right', dir: o.subDir || 'rtl' });
  ctx.restore();
  return p;
}
const ICD = {
  driver: ['M12 30 L88 30 L88 70 L12 70 Z', 'M2 42 L12 42', 'M2 58 L12 58', 'M88 42 L98 42', 'M88 58 L98 58', 'M24 52 L36 52 L41 40 L47 62 L53 46 L57 52 L76 52'],

  warn: ['M50 8 L94 88 L6 88 Z', 'M50 36 L50 62', 'M50 74 L50.5 74'],
  search: ['M42 42 m-28 0 a28 28 0 1 0 56 0 a28 28 0 1 0 -56 0', 'M62 62 L90 90'],

};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/build.sh`

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Assemble scenes.js from the shared base + helpers and this reel's part.js, and set the duration.  Usage: bash build.sh <duration>
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
(echo "// ===== reel ====="; cat base.js maghelp.js kit_help.js vhelp.js helpers.js part.js) > scenes.js
printf "// No captions in this video\nconst PHRASES = [];\nconst CARDS = [];\nconst DURATION = %s;\nconst FPS = 30;\nif (typeof module !== 'undefined') module.exports = { PHRASES, CARDS, DURATION, FPS };\n" "$1" > timeline.js
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/cut.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build the footage edit for AI-video / UGC / store-footage reels.

Usage: python3 cut.py edit.json
edit.json: {"clips": [["clip1.mp4", in_sec, out_sec], ...], "keep_audio": false}
Writes base.mp4 (1080x1920, 30 fps, no audio; each clip scaled+cropped to fill 9:16) and, when keep_audio is true
(UGC: the person on camera is the voice), base_audio.wav at 48 kHz mono to use as vo48.wav.
Prints the cut points so scene timings in part.js can follow the edit.
"""
import json, subprocess, sys

cfg = json.load(open(sys.argv[1], encoding='utf-8'))
parts, t = [], 0.0
for i, (src, a, b) in enumerate(cfg['clips']):
    out = f'_cut_{i}.mp4'
    vf = 'scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=1080:1920,fps=30,setsar=1'
    cmd = ['ffmpeg', '-nostdin', '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-ss', str(a), '-to', str(b), '-i', src, '-vf', vf,
           '-c:v', 'libx264', '-crf', '16', '-preset', 'fast', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p']
    cmd += ['-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '1', out.replace('.mp4', '.mkv')] if cfg.get('keep_audio') else ['-an', out]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
    parts.append(out.replace('.mp4', '.mkv') if cfg.get('keep_audio') else out)
    print(f'clip {i}: {src} [{a}-{b}] -> {t:.2f}-{t + b - a:.2f}')
    t += b - a
open('_cuts.txt', 'w').write(''.join(f"file '{p}'\n" for p in parts))
subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-nostdin', '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'concat', '-safe', '0', '-i', '_cuts.txt', '-an', '-c:v', 'copy', 'base.mp4'], check=True)
if cfg.get('keep_audio'):
    subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-nostdin', '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'concat', '-safe', '0', '-i', '_cuts.txt', '-vn', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '1', 'base_audio.wav'], check=True)
print(f'base.mp4 {t:.2f}s')
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/engine.js`

```javascript
// ===== Core helpers =====
const W = 1080, H = 1920;
const C = {
  o: '#EF5924', o2: '#FF7A35', amber: '#FFB347', warm: '#FFD9A8', bg: '#0B0A0D',
  w: '#FFF6EE', g: '#8C8792', g2: '#4A4650', dull: '#B9A957', red: '#FF3B3B', glass: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)',
};

let grain = null;
function makeGrain() {

// ===== Captions =====
let WORDS = []; // per card: [{w, a, b}]
function buildCaptions() {

    const arr = [];
    for (const i of ids) {
      const [a, b, s] = PHRASES[i];
      const ws = s.split(' ');
      const lens = ws.map(w => w.replace(/[…،!.:]/g, '').length + 1.5);
      const tot = lens.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0);
      let acc = 0;
      ws.forEach((w, k) => { const wa = a + (b - a) * acc / tot; acc += lens[k]; arr.push({ w, a: wa, b: a + (b - a) * acc / tot }); });

function captions(t) {
  for (let ci = 0; ci < CARDS.length; ci++) {
    const ws = WORDS[ci];
    const start = ws[0].a - .15;
    const lastEnd = ws[ws.length - 1].b;
    const next = ci + 1 < CARDS.length ? WORDS[ci + 1][0].a - .17 : 99;
    const end = Math.min(next, lastEnd + .7);
    if (t < start || t > end) continue;
    const a = Math.min(clamp((t - start) / .15), clamp((end - t) / .15));
    const size = 62, maxW = 900, lineH = 92;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.font = `900 ${size}px Cairo`; ctx.direction = 'rtl';
    const sp = ctx.measureText(' ').width + 16;
    // wrap
    const lines = [[]]; let lw = 0;
    for (const o of ws) {
      o.m = ctx.measureText(o.w).width;
      if (lw + o.m > maxW && lines[lines.length - 1].length) { lines.push([]); lw = 0; }
      lines[lines.length - 1].push(o); lw += o.m + sp;

    ctx.globalAlpha = a;

    ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,89,36,0.35)'; ctx.stroke();
    lines.forEach((ln, li) => {
      const tw = ln.reduce((s, o) => s + o.m, 0) + sp * (ln.length - 1);
      let x = 540 + tw / 2; // RTL: start from right
      const y = cy + li * lineH;
      for (const o of ln) {
        const cx = x - o.m / 2;
        const active = t >= o.a && t < o.b + .05;
        const past = t >= o.b + .05;
        const pa = prog(t, o.a, o.a + .18);
        const sc = active ? 1 + .07 * Math.sin(Math.PI * clamp((t - o.a) / Math.max(.2, o.b - o.a))) : 1;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(cx, y - (active ? 6 * E.out(pa) : 0)); ctx.scale(sc, sc);
        ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        ctx.lineWidth = 10; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)'; ctx.strokeText(o.w, 0, 0);
        if (active) { ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 28; ctx.fillStyle = C.o2; }
        else ctx.fillStyle = past ? '#FFFFFF' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.38)';
        ctx.fillText(o.w, 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
        x -= o.m + sp;
      }
    });
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
```

```javascript
}

// Start / end of every frame. In overlay mode (graphics over AI or phone footage) the canvas stays transparent.

  if (o.rightTag) label(o.rightTag, 810, 1250, 1, 0, 0, { size: 52, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  if (o.sub) label(o.sub, 540, 1450, 1, 0, 0, { size: 50, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)' });
  drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1580, 130, { glow: 40 });
}
```

```javascript
function spotTop(x, y, on, s = 1, a = 1) {
  if (a <= 0 || s <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  if (on > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; glow(x, y, 120 * s, '#FFD6A0', .45 * on); ctx.restore(); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1b1a1d'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 20 * s, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = on > 0 ? `rgba(255,240,215,${.35 + .65 * on})` : '#444'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 12 * s, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}
function dimH(x0, x1, y, txtS, a, col = C.amber) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 4;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y); ctx.lineTo(x1, y); ctx.stroke();
  [[x0, 1], [x1, -1]].forEach(([x, s]) => { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x + s * 18, y - 11); ctx.lineTo(x + s * 18, y + 11); ctx.fill(); });
  ctx.restore();
  txt(txtS, (x0 + x1) / 2, y - 40, { size: 46, weight: 900, color: col, dir: 'ltr', alpha: a });
}
function dimV(x, y0, y1, txtS, a, col = C.amber) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 4;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y0); ctx.lineTo(x, y1); ctx.stroke();
  [[y0, 1], [y1, -1]].forEach(([y, s]) => { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x - 11, y + s * 18); ctx.lineTo(x + 11, y + s * 18); ctx.fill(); });
  ctx.restore();
  txt(txtS, x + 50, (y0 + y1) / 2, { size: 46, weight: 900, color: col, dir: 'ltr', rot: -Math.PI / 2, alpha: a });
}
const SP13 = [[.5, .8], [1.5, .8], [2.5, .8], [3.5, .8], [4.5, .8], [1, 2], [2.5, 2], [4, 2], [.5, 3.2], [1.5, 3.2], [2.5, 3.2], [3.5, 3.2], [4.5, 3.2]];
const SP8 = [[.85, 1], [1.95, 1], [3.05, 1], [4.15, 1], [.85, 3], [1.95, 3], [3.05, 3], [4.15, 3]];
function counter(t, t0, n, y, text, col = C.o2) {
  const p = prog(t, t0, t0 + .8); if (p <= 0) return;
  const v = Math.round(lerp(0, n, E.out(p)));
  pop(`≈ ${v}`, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size: 120, weight: 900, color: col, glow: 30, dir: 'ltr' });
  txt(text, 540, y + 100, { size: 48, weight: 800, color: C.w, alpha: prog(t, t0 + .2, t0 + .6) });
}
function bubble(x, y, w, h, a, col = '#2b2730') { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.fillStyle = col; rrect(x, y, w, h, 34); ctx.fill(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + w - 60, y + h); ctx.lineTo(x + w - 20, y + h + 34); ctx.lineTo(x + w - 100, y + h); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
function plane(x, y, s, a) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(s, s); ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; ctx.lineWidth = 9; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-60, -10); ctx.lineTo(60, -50); ctx.lineTo(20, 60); ctx.lineTo(0, 10); ctx.closePath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 10); ctx.lineTo(60, -50); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); }

// ---------- scenes ----------
```

```javascript
// ---------- magnetic-track helpers ----------
const TRK = '#141215';
function track(x0, x1, y, o = {}) { // slim magnetic rail (front view)
  const a = o.a ?? 1, w = o.w ?? 18; if (a <= 0 || x1 <= x0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  ctx.fillStyle = TRK; rrect(x0, y - w / 2, x1 - x0, w, 4); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0 + 4, y - w / 2 + 2); ctx.lineTo(x1 - 4, y - w / 2 + 2); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0 + 4, y + 2); ctx.lineTo(x1 - 4, y + 2); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}
function ceiling(y, a = 1) { // ceiling slab
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y - 120, 0, y); g.addColorStop(0, '#2a2629'); g.addColorStop(1, '#3a353a');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, y - 120, W, 120);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.12)'; ctx.fillRect(0, y - 2, W, 2);
  ctx.restore();
}
function cone(x, y, len, hw, a, col = [255, 222, 180], ang = 0) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(ang);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, len); g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${col},${.55 * a})`); g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${col},0)`);
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-8, 0); ctx.lineTo(8, 0); ctx.lineTo(hw, len); ctx.lineTo(-hw, len); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}
function spotMod(x, y, on, o = {}) { // cylinder spot hanging from track
  const a = o.a ?? 1, ang = o.ang ?? 0, s = o.s ?? 1; if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(s, s);
  if (on > 0) cone(0, 70, o.len ?? 420, o.hw ?? 120, on, undefined, ang);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1d1a1e'; ctx.fillRect(-14, 0, 28, 14);
  ctx.save(); ctx.rotate(ang);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(-26, 0, 26, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#0e0d0f'); g.addColorStop(.5, '#3a363b'); g.addColorStop(1, '#0e0d0f');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; rrect(-24, 12, 48, 60, 6); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#c9a063'; ctx.fillRect(-24, 60, 48, 4);
  if (on > 0) { glow(0, 72, 46, '#FFF1DC', on); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff6e6'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 72, 16, 5, 0, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.restore(); ctx.restore();
}
function linearMod(x0, x1, y, on, a = 1) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  if (on > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, y + 16, 0, y + 380); g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,226,190,${.5 * on})`); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,226,190,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y + 16); ctx.lineTo(x1, y + 16); ctx.lineTo(x1 + 110, y + 380); ctx.lineTo(x0 - 110, y + 380); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1d1a1e'; rrect(x0, y - 2, x1 - x0, 22, 4); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = on > 0 ? '#fff4e2' : '#5a555b'; ctx.fillRect(x0 + 6, y + 12, x1 - x0 - 12, 8);
  if (on > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = '#FFD6A0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30; ctx.fillRect(x0 + 6, y + 12, x1 - x0 - 12, 8); ctx.restore(); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function grilleMod(x, y, on, n = 5, a = 1) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  const cw = 40, w = n * cw;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (on > 0) cone(x - w / 2 + cw / 2 + i * cw, y + 34, 360, 40, on * .5);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1d1a1e'; rrect(x - w / 2, y - 2, w, 36, 4); ctx.fill();
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const cx = x - w / 2 + cw / 2 + i * cw; ctx.fillStyle = '#0b0a0c'; ctx.fillRect(cx - 14, y + 6, 28, 24); if (on > 0) { glow(cx, y + 28, 18, '#FFF1DC', on); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff6e6'; ctx.fillRect(cx - 8, y + 24, 16, 5); } }
  ctx.restore();
}
function pendantMod(x, y, len, on, a = 1) {
  if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2a262b'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y + 10); ctx.lineTo(x, y + len); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1d1a1e'; ctx.fillRect(x - 12, y, 24, 12);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - 50, 0, x + 50, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#8d6a35'); g.addColorStop(.5, '#e6c487'); g.addColorStop(1, '#8d6a35');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - 12, y + len); ctx.lineTo(x + 12, y + len); ctx.lineTo(x + 52, y + len + 60); ctx.lineTo(x - 52, y + len + 60); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  if (on > 0) { cone(x, y + len + 60, 300, 140, on * .8); glow(x, y + len + 62, 60, '#FFF1DC', on); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function magnetFx(x, y, t, t0) { // field arcs at snap moment
  const p = prog(t, t0, t0 + .6); if (p <= 0 || p >= 1) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= (1 - p); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o2; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const r = 20 + k * 16 + p * 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, Math.PI * 1.05, Math.PI * 1.95); ctx.stroke(); }
  ctx.restore();
  glow(x, y, 70, C.amber, .7 * (1 - p));
}
function snapY(t, t0, yTarget) { const p = prog(t, t0, t0 + .35); return yTarget - (1 - E.back(p)) * 140 * (p < 1 ? 1 : 0) - (p <= 0 ? 999 : 0); }
const MI = {
  sofa: IC.sofa, house: IC.house,
  kitchen: ['M10 40 L90 40 L90 90 L10 90 Z', 'M10 55 L90 55', 'M30 40 L30 20 M70 40 L70 20', 'M20 70 L40 70 M60 70 L80 70'],
  corridor: ['M30 10 L70 10 L90 90 L10 90 Z', 'M50 10 L50 90', 'M40 30 L60 30 M36 55 L64 55'],
  dining: ['M15 45 L85 45', 'M25 45 L25 80 M75 45 L75 80', 'M50 10 L50 30', 'M35 30 L65 30 L60 40 L40 40 Z'],
  office: ['M10 55 L90 55', 'M20 55 L20 90 M80 55 L80 90', 'M35 20 L65 20 L65 45 L35 45 Z', 'M50 45 L50 55'],
  shop: ['M10 35 L90 35 L82 15 L18 15 Z', 'M15 35 L15 90 L85 90 L85 35', 'M40 90 L40 60 L60 60 L60 90'],
  phone: IC.phone, bolt: ['M55 5 L25 55 L48 55 L40 95 L75 40 L52 40 Z'],
  box: ['M15 30 L85 30 L85 75 L15 75 Z', 'M25 45 L45 45 M25 58 L60 58', 'M70 40 L78 40'],
  tag: ['M10 50 L50 10 L90 10 L90 50 L50 90 Z', 'M72 28 m-6 0 a6 6 0 1 0 12 0 a6 6 0 1 0 -12 0'],
  ceilingIc: ['M5 30 L95 30', 'M5 30 L5 15 M95 30 L95 15', 'M40 30 L40 40 L60 40 L60 30'],
  wrench: ['M70 15 C55 10 45 25 50 38 L15 73 L27 85 L62 50 C75 55 90 45 85 30 L72 40 L62 30 Z'],
  dimmer: ['M14 14 L86 14 L86 86 L14 86 Z', 'M50 50 m-22 0 a22 22 0 1 0 44 0 a22 22 0 1 0 -44 0', 'M50 50 L38 36'],
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/render.js`

```javascript
// Render the reel canvas (index.html + scenes.js) with headless Chromium.
//   node render.js stills "t1,t2,..."                         -> stills/s_<t>.jpg
//   node render.js video <out.mp4> <start> <end>               -> frames of the motion-graphics reel
//   node render.js overlay <base.mp4> <out.mp4> <start> <end>  -> graphics drawn over a footage edit (window.OVERLAY = true,
//                                                               the canvas stays transparent where nothing is drawn)
const { chromium } = (() => { try { return require('playwright'); } catch { return require('/opt/node22/lib/node_modules/playwright'); } })();
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const [,, mode, a, b, c, d] = process.argv;
(async () => {
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ args: ['--allow-file-access-from-files'] });
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1080, height: 1920 } });
  page.on('console', m => console.log('PAGE', m.text()));
  page.on('pageerror', e => console.log('ERR', e.message));
  if (mode === 'overlay') await page.addInitScript('window.OVERLAY = true;');
  await page.goto('file://' + path.resolve(__dirname, 'index.html'));
  await page.waitForFunction('window.READY === true');
  const fmt = mode === 'overlay' ? 'image/png' : 'image/jpeg';
  const grab = async t => Buffer.from((await page.evaluate(([t, f]) => { draw(t); return document.getElementById('c').toDataURL(f, 0.93); }, [t, fmt])).split(',')[1], 'base64');
  if (mode === 'stills') {
    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(__dirname, 'stills'), { recursive: true });
    for (const t of a.split(',').map(Number)) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname, 'stills', `s_${t.toFixed(2)}.jpg`), await grab(t));
  } else {
    const fps = 30;
    const [out, s, e] = mode === 'overlay' ? [b, Number(c || 0), Number(d)] : [a, Number(b || 0), Number(c)];
    const args = mode === 'overlay'
      ? ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-ss', String(s), '-t', String(e - s), '-i', a, '-f', 'image2pipe', '-framerate', String(fps), '-c:v', 'png', '-i', '-',
        '-filter_complex', '[0:v]scale=1080:1920:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase,crop=1080:1920,fps=30,setpts=PTS-STARTPTS[b];[b][1:v]overlay=format=auto:shortest=1',
        '-an', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-crf', '17', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', out]
      : ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'image2pipe', '-framerate', String(fps), '-c:v', 'mjpeg', '-i', '-',
        '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-crf', '17', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', out];
    const ff = spawn('ffmpeg', args, { stdio: ['pipe', 'inherit', 'inherit'] });
    const n0 = Math.round(s * fps), n1 = Math.round(e * fps);
    const t0 = Date.now();
    for (let n = n0; n < n1; n++) {
      const buf = await grab(n / fps);
      if (!ff.stdin.write(buf)) await new Promise(r => ff.stdin.once('drain', r));
      if (n % 150 === 0) console.log('frame', n, ((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1) + 's');
    }
    ff.stdin.end(); await new Promise(r => ff.on('close', r));
  }
  await browser.close();
})();
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/runvid.sh`

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Render the reel in 4 parallel chunks, then mux with mix.wav.
#   bash runvid.sh <duration> <out.mp4>              motion-graphics reel (3D renders + graphics)
#   BASE=base.mp4 bash runvid.sh <duration> <out.mp4>  graphics over a footage edit made with cut.py (AI clips / UGC / store footage)
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
DUR=$1; OUT=$2; mkdir -p "$(dirname "$OUT")"
P=$(python3 -c "d=$DUR;print(' '.join(str(round(d*i/4,2)) for i in range(5)))"); read -r a b c d e <<< "$P"
if [ -n "$BASE" ]; then R="node render.js overlay $BASE"; else R="node render.js video"; fi
$R c_1.mp4 $a $b > log_1.txt 2>&1 &
$R c_2.mp4 $b $c > log_2.txt 2>&1 &
$R c_3.mp4 $c $d > log_3.txt 2>&1 &
$R c_4.mp4 $d $e > log_4.txt 2>&1 &
wait
printf "file 'c_1.mp4'\nfile 'c_2.mp4'\nfile 'c_3.mp4'\nfile 'c_4.mp4'\n" > list.txt
ffmpeg -nostdin -y -loglevel error -f concat -safe 0 -i list.txt -c copy vid.mp4
ffmpeg -nostdin -y -loglevel error -i vid.mp4 -i mix.wav -map 0:v -map 1:a -c:v libx264 -preset medium -crf 19 -maxrate 3800k -bufsize 7600k -pix_fmt yuv420p -af "loudnorm=I=-14:TP=-1.5:LRA=11" -ar 48000 -c:a aac -b:a 192k -shortest -movflags +faststart "$OUT"
# small preview for chat / Telegram (bots can only fetch files up to 20 MB by URL)
ffmpeg -nostdin -y -loglevel error -i "$OUT" -vf scale=540:-2 -c:v libx264 -crf 30 -preset veryfast -c:a aac -b:a 96k -movflags +faststart "${OUT%.mp4}_preview.mp4"
ffprobe -v error -show_entries format=duration,size -of default=nw=1 "$OUT"
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/synth.py`

```python
import numpy as np, wave, math, json

# music.json: {"dur": total seconds, "drop": beat starts, "hats": hats+arp start, "lift": louder pads+claps, "end": final chord, "transitions": [...], "events": [...]}
CFG = json.load(open("music.json"))

SR = 48000
DUR = CFG["dur"]
N = int(DUR * SR)
rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
mus = np.zeros((2, N))
sfx = np.zeros((2, N))


def add(buf, sig, t0, gain=1.0, pan=0.0):
    i = int(t0 * SR)
    if i >= N: return
    sig = sig[: N - i]
    l, r = math.cos((pan + 1) * math.pi / 4), math.sin((pan + 1) * math.pi / 4)
    buf[0, i:i + len(sig)] += sig * gain * l * 1.414
    buf[1, i:i + len(sig)] += sig * gain * r * 1.414


def tt(d): return np.arange(int(d * SR)) / SR


def band(x, lo, hi):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x); f = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(x), 1 / SR)
    m = ((f >= lo) & (f <= hi)).astype(float)
    return np.fft.irfft(X * m, len(x))


def noise(d): return rng.standard_normal(int(d * SR))


def mtof(m): return 440 * 2 ** ((m - 69) / 12)


def adsr(n, a, r):
    e = np.ones(n); na, nr = int(a * SR), int(r * SR)
    if na: e[:na] = np.linspace(0, 1, na)
    if nr: e[-nr:] *= np.linspace(1, 0, nr)
    return e

# ---------- SFX ----------
def whoosh(d=.7, lo=250, hi=4000):
    t = tt(d); x = band(noise(d), lo, hi)
    e = np.sin(np.pi * np.clip(t / d, 0, 1)) ** 2
    return x * e / (np.abs(x).max() + 1e-9)

def pop(f=900):
    t = tt(.12); return np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t * (1 + 1.5 * np.exp(-t * 40))) * np.exp(-t * 35)

def click():
    t = tt(.05); x = band(noise(.05), 2000, 9000) * np.exp(-t * 200)
    return x / np.abs(x).max() + .5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 1800 * t) * np.exp(-t * 120)

def bell(f, d=1.6):
    t = tt(d); s = sum(a * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * k * t) * np.exp(-t * (2 + k)) for k, a in [(1, 1), (2.76, .4), (5.4, .2), (8.9, .1)])
    return s * (1 - np.exp(-t * 400))

def chime(base=72, n=4, gap=.07):
    out = np.zeros(int((n * gap + 1.2) * SR))
    for k in range(n):
        b = bell(mtof(base + [0, 4, 7, 12, 16][k % 5]), 1.0); i = int(k * gap * SR)
        out[i:i + len(b)] += b * .6
    return out

def impact(d=2.0):
    t = tt(d)
    sub = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (40 + 80 * np.exp(-t * 10)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 2.5)
    n = band(noise(d), 60, 6000) * np.exp(-t * 6); n /= np.abs(n).max()
    return sub + .5 * n

def riser(d=1.2):
    t = tt(d); x = band(noise(d), 800, 9000); x /= np.abs(x).max()
    tone = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (200 * t + 600 * t * t / d))
    return (x * .7 + tone * .3) * (t / d) ** 2

def thud():
    t = tt(.4); return np.sin(2 * np.pi * (55 + 120 * np.exp(-t * 30)) * t) * np.exp(-t * 12)

def coin():
    t = tt(.5); return sum(np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) * np.exp(-t * dcy) for f, dcy in [(2200, 9), (3350, 12), (5100, 16)]) / 3

def crack():
    t = tt(.6); x = band(noise(.6), 150, 7000) * np.exp(-t * 9); x /= np.abs(x).max()
    th = thud(); x[:len(th)] += th
    return x

def buzz(d):
    t = tt(d); s = sum(np.sin(2 * np.pi * 100 * k * t) / k for k in range(1, 12))
    return s / 3

def rand(i):
    x = math.sin(i * 127.1 + 311.7) * 43758.5453; return x - math.floor(x)

def flick(t):
    k = math.floor(t * 14); r = rand(k * 3.1)
    return .12 if r < .22 else .55 + .45 * rand(k * 7.7)


def rand_j(i): return ((i*7919)%13)/100
# ---- SFX from music.json: events are [name, time, gain, *args] ----
FX = {'whoosh': whoosh, 'pop': pop, 'click': click, 'chime': chime, 'impact': impact, 'riser': riser, 'thud': thud, 'coin': coin, 'crack': crack, 'buzz': lambda d=.3: buzz(d) * .3}
for tb in CFG.get('transitions', []): add(sfx, whoosh(.65), tb - .32, .22)
for ev in CFG.get('events', []):
    name, t0, gain, *args = ev
    add(sfx, FX[name](*args), t0, gain)

# ---------- MUSIC ----------
BEAT = .6
T0 = CFG["drop"]  # music drop
prog_ch = [[57, 60, 64], [53, 57, 60], [48, 52, 55], [55, 59, 62]]  # Am F C G
def chord_at(tm):
    k = int(math.floor((tm - T0) / (BEAT * 8))) % 4
    return prog_ch[k]

# Intro drone + clock ticks (tension)
t = tt(CFG["drop"]); drone = (np.sin(2 * np.pi * mtof(33) * t) + .5 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * mtof(40) * t)) * (.6 + .4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * .5 * t))
add(mus, drone * adsr(len(t), 1.0, 1.0), 0, .10)

# Pads
start = T0
while start < CFG["end"]:
    notes = chord_at(start + .01); d = BEAT * 8
    tl = tt(d + .8); seg = np.zeros(len(tl))
    for m in notes + [notes[0] + 12]:
        for det in (-.003, .003):
            f = mtof(m) * (1 + det)
            seg += np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * tl) + .25 * np.sin(4 * np.pi * f * tl) + .1 * np.sin(6 * np.pi * f * tl)
    seg *= adsr(len(tl), .5, .9) / 10
    add(mus, seg, start, .16 if start < CFG["lift"] else .2, pan=0)
    start += d
# Bass, kick, hats, arp, claps
bt = T0
k = 0
while bt < CFG["end"]:
    notes = chord_at(bt + .01)
    t = tt(.5)
    add(mus, np.sin(2 * np.pi * mtof(notes[0] - 24) * t) * np.exp(-t * 4) * (1 - np.exp(-t * 300)), bt, .32)
    kt = tt(.35); kick = np.sin(2 * np.pi * (45 * kt + 105 * (1 - np.exp(-kt * 25)) / 25)) * np.exp(-kt * 9)
    add(mus, kick, bt, .45)
    if bt >= CFG["hats"]:
        h = band(noise(.06), 7000, 16000) * np.exp(-tt(.06) * 70)
        add(mus, h / (np.abs(h).max() + 1e-9), bt + BEAT / 2, .06, pan=.25)
        arp = [notes[0] + 12, notes[1] + 12, notes[2] + 12, notes[1] + 24]
        for j in range(2):
            m = arp[(k * 2 + j) % 4]; at = tt(.35)
            add(mus, (np.sin(2 * np.pi * mtof(m) * at) + .3 * np.sin(4 * np.pi * mtof(m) * at)) * np.exp(-at * 9), bt + j * BEAT / 2, .07, pan=(-.4 if j else .4))
    if bt >= CFG["lift"] and k % 2 == 1:
        cl = band(noise(.18), 900, 4000) * np.exp(-tt(.18) * 22)
        add(mus, cl / np.abs(cl).max(), bt, .12)
    bt += BEAT; k += 1
# final ring-out chord
t = tt(3.0); fin = sum(np.sin(2 * np.pi * mtof(m) * t) for m in [45, 57, 60, 64, 69]) * np.exp(-t * 1.2) / 5
add(mus, fin, CFG["end"], .35)

# ---------- VO + ducking ----------
w = wave.open('vo48.wav'); vo = np.frombuffer(w.readframes(w.getnframes()), np.int16).astype(float) / 32768
vo = vo / max(np.abs(vo).max(), 1e-9) * .89  # a silent track (no-voice reels) stays silent
v = np.zeros(N); v[:len(vo)] = vo[:N]
fr = 480; rms = np.sqrt(np.convolve(v ** 2, np.ones(fr) / fr, 'same'))
act = np.clip(rms / .04, 0, 1)
# attack fast / release slow
g = np.zeros(N); cur = 0.0
step = 240
for i in range(0, N, step):
    tgt = act[i]
    cur = cur + (tgt - cur) * (.5 if tgt > cur else .02)
    g[i:i + step] = cur
duck = (1 - .6 * g) * .42
mix = np.zeros((2, N))
mix += mus * duck
mix += sfx * (1 - .3 * g) * .8
mix += v[None, :]
mix *= .8
peak = np.abs(mix).max(); mix /= max(1, peak / .98)
out = (np.clip(mix.T, -1, 1) * 32767).astype(np.int16)
wo = wave.open('mix.wav', 'wb'); wo.setnchannels(2); wo.setsampwidth(2); wo.setframerate(SR); wo.writeframes(out.tobytes()); wo.close()
print('peak', peak)
def db(x): return 20*np.log10(np.sqrt(np.mean(x**2))+1e-9)
for a,b in [(0,5),(6,DUR-6),(DUR-6,DUR)]:
    s,e=int(a*SR),int(b*SR); sp=act[s:e]>.5
    print(a,b,'vo',round(db(v[s:e][sp]),1),'music(ducked, during speech)',round(db((mus[0]*duck)[s:e][sp]),1),'music gaps',round(db((mus[0]*duck)[s:e][~sp]),1),'sfx',round(db(sfx[0][s:e]),1))
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/template/vhelp.js`

```javascript
// ---------- villa design helpers ----------
const IM = {};
function imgCard(img, x, y, w, h, o = {}) {
  if (!img || (o.a ?? 1) <= 0) return;
  const sx = o.sx ?? 0, sy = o.sy ?? 0, sw = o.sw ?? img.width, sh = o.sh ?? img.height;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= o.a ?? 1;
  if (o.shadow !== false) { ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,.6)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.fillStyle = '#000'; rrect(x, y, w, h, o.r ?? 26); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; }
  ctx.beginPath(); rrect(x, y, w, h, o.r ?? 26); ctx.clip();
  ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh, x, y, w, h);
  ctx.restore();
  if (o.stroke) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= o.a ?? 1; ctx.strokeStyle = o.stroke; ctx.lineWidth = 5; rrect(x, y, w, h, o.r ?? 26); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); }
}
// zoom window into plan image (crop coords in image px)
function planView(img, cx, cy, zw, x, y, w, h, a = 1, stroke) { const zh = zw * h / w; imgCard(img, x, y, w, h, { sx: cx - zw / 2, sy: cy - zh / 2, sw: zw, sh: zh, a, stroke }); }
function stepHead(t, n, title, t0) {
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .45)); if (p <= 0) return;
  txt('الخطوة', 540, 120, { size: 40, weight: 800, color: C.warm, alpha: Math.min(1, p) });
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 220); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 58, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt(String(n), 0, 5, { size: 72, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore();
  pop(title, 540, 360, t, t0 + .15, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.w, glow: 25 });
  // progress dots
  for (let i = 1; i <= 5; i++) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= Math.min(1, p); ctx.fillStyle = i <= n ? C.o : 'rgba(255,255,255,.15)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(540 + (3 - i) * 44, 1840, 12, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
}
function pulseBox(x, y, w, h, t, t0, col = C.o) { const p = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= p * (.7 + .3 * Math.sin(t * 6)); ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; rrect(x, y, w, h, 14); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); }
// plan crop is 2350 x 1330; shown full at x 40..1040 (w 1000, h 566)
const PW = 1000, PH = 566, PXO = 40, PYO = 640, PS = PW / 2350;
const P2S = (u, v) => [PXO + u * PS, PYO + v * PS];
```

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Start a reel work folder from the template.  Usage: bash studio/reel/new.sh <workdir>
set -e
D=$1; mkdir -p "$D/r" "$D/stills"
cp -r "$(dirname "$0")/template/." "$D/"
echo "reel folder ready: $D"
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/thumbs.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Cover (thumbnail) from the video itself: the owner picks one of a few real frames, and it becomes the first frame.

  python3 thumbs.py sheet <video.mp4> <t1> <t2> <t3> [...] <sheet.jpg>
      grabs the frames at those times and lays them side by side with big numbers 1, 2, 3 (sent to the bot as a card)
  python3 thumbs.py apply <video.mp4> <t> <out.mp4> [<cover.jpg>]
      the frame at <t> is held over the first 0.6 s of the video (so it is the cover on Instagram, TikTok and Facebook),
      audio untouched; also writes it as <cover.jpg> when given
"""
import subprocess, sys, tempfile, os
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont

HOLD = 0.6
FONT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'template', 'fonts', 'cairo4.ttf')


def grab(video, t, out):
    subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-loglevel', 'error', '-y', '-ss', str(t), '-i', video, '-frames:v', '1', '-q:v', '2', out], check=True)
    return out


def sheet(video, times, out):
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    w, h = 432, 768
    sh = Image.new('RGB', (w * len(times) + 24 * (len(times) + 1), h + 48), '#0b0a0d')
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(sh)
    font = ImageFont.truetype(FONT, 110) if os.path.exists(FONT) else ImageFont.load_default()
    for i, t in enumerate(times):
        im = Image.open(grab(video, t, f'{tmp}/f{i}.jpg')).resize((w, h))
        x = 24 + i * (w + 24); sh.paste(im, (x, 24))
        d.ellipse([x + w - 150, h - 170, x + w - 20, h - 40], fill='#EF5924')  # bottom-right: clear of the titles
        d.text((x + w - 85, h - 105), str(i + 1), font=font, fill='white', anchor='mm')
    sh.save(out, quality=90)


def apply(video, t, out, cover=None):
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    frame = grab(video, t, f'{tmp}/cover.jpg')
    if cover:
        Image.open(frame).save(cover, quality=92)
    subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-loglevel', 'error', '-y', '-i', video, '-loop', '1', '-t', str(HOLD), '-i', frame,
                    '-filter_complex', f"[1:v]scale=iw:ih,setsar=1[c];[0:v][c]overlay=0:0:enable='lt(t,{HOLD})'[v]",
                    '-map', '[v]', '-map', '0:a?', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-crf', '18', '-preset', 'medium', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p',
                    '-c:a', 'copy', '-movflags', '+faststart', out], check=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    cmd, args = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2:]
    if cmd == 'sheet':
        sheet(args[0], [float(a) for a in args[1:-1]], args[-1])
    elif cmd == 'apply':
        apply(args[0], float(args[1]), args[2], args[3] if len(args) > 3 else None)
    else:
        sys.exit(__doc__)
```

```html
<script type="module">
import * as THREE from 'three';

import { OutputPass } from 'three/addons/postprocessing/OutputPass.js';

const W = window.RW || 2400, H = window.RH || 1500;
const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, preserveDrawingBuffer: true });
renderer.setSize(W, H); renderer.setPixelRatio(1);
renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping; renderer.toneMappingExposure = .82;
renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true; renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

scene.environment = pmrem.fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture;
scene.environmentIntensity = 0.18;

// ---------- procedural textures ----------
const R = (s => () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647)(12345);
function canvasTex(w, h, draw, rep = [1, 1], srgb = true) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; const g = c.getContext('2d'); draw(g, w, h);
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.repeat.set(...rep);
  if (srgb) t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = 8; return t;
}
const marbleTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => {
  g.fillStyle = '#e9e1d4'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < 2500; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${200 + R() * 40},${190 + R() * 40},${175 + R() * 40},0.08)`; g.beginPath(); g.arc(R() * w, R() * h, 4 + R() * 40, 0, 7); g.fill(); }
  for (let v = 0; v < 9; v++) {
    let x = R() * w, y = R() * h; g.strokeStyle = `rgba(150,130,110,${0.12 + R() * 0.25})`; g.lineWidth = .6 + R() * 2.2; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y);
    for (let k = 0; k < 60; k++) { x += 8 + R() * 18; y += (R() - .5) * 26; g.lineTo(x, y); } g.stroke();
  }
  g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,105,90,0.55)'; g.lineWidth = 3; g.strokeRect(0, 0, w, h);
}, [14 / 1.2, 6 / 0.6]);
const plasterTex = canvasTex(512, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#efe9e0'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 6000; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${R() > .5 ? 255 : 200},${R() > .5 ? 250 : 190},${R() > .5 ? 240 : 180},0.05)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2, 2); } }, [4, 2]);
function woodDraw(base, dark) { return (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = base; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) { const x = R() * w; g.strokeStyle = `rgba(${dark},${0.05 + R() * 0.12})`; g.lineWidth = .5 + R() * 2.5; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 20) g.lineTo(x + Math.sin(y * .01 + i) * 4, y); g.stroke(); } }; }
const oakTex = canvasTex(512, 1024, woodDraw('#8a6440', '60,38,20'), [1, 1]);
const flutedTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#7d5a38', '50,30,15')(g, w, h); for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 32) { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + 32, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.35)'); gr.addColorStop(.2, 'rgba(255,230,200,0.10)'); gr.addColorStop(.6, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.30)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(x, 0, 32, h); } }, [6, 1]);
const flutedBump = canvasTex(256, 64, (g, w, h) => { for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 8) { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + 8, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, '#000'); gr.addColorStop(.5, '#fff'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#000'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(x, 0, 8, h); } }, [6, 1], false);
const fabricTex = canvasTex(256, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#d9cdb8'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 2) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${0.03 + R() * 0.04})`; g.fillRect(0, y, w, 1); } for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 2) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${0.02 + R() * 0.04})`; g.fillRect(x, 0, 1, h); } }, [6, 2]);
const darkFabric = canvasTex(256, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#5b4636'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 2) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${0.05 + R() * 0.05})`; g.fillRect(0, y, w, 1); } }, [4, 4]);
const rugTex = canvasTex(2048, 1024, (g, w, h) => {
  g.fillStyle = '#cbb79b'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < 20000; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${R() > .5 ? 120 : 230},${R() > .5 ? 100 : 210},${R() > .5 ? 80 : 180},0.05)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 3, 3); }
  const bd = 90; g.strokeStyle = '#7a5b3a'; g.lineWidth = 26; g.strokeRect(bd, bd, w - 2 * bd, h - 2 * bd);
  g.strokeStyle = '#b89558'; g.lineWidth = 6; g.strokeRect(bd + 40, bd + 40, w - 2 * bd - 80, h - 2 * bd - 80); g.strokeRect(bd - 40, bd - 40, w - 2 * bd + 80, h - 2 * bd + 80);
  // border motif
  g.fillStyle = '#8c6a44';
  for (let x = bd + 60; x < w - bd - 40; x += 70) { [bd, h - bd].forEach(y => { g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.rotate(Math.PI / 4); g.fillRect(-12, -12, 24, 24); g.restore(); }); }
  for (let y = bd + 60; y < h - bd - 40; y += 70) { [bd, w - bd].forEach(x => { g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.rotate(Math.PI / 4); g.fillRect(-12, -12, 24, 24); g.restore(); }); }
  // central medallions (8-point star)
  const star = (cx, cy, r, col) => { g.fillStyle = col; for (let k = 0; k < 2; k++) { g.save(); g.translate(cx, cy); g.rotate(k * Math.PI / 4); g.fillRect(-r, -r, 2 * r, 2 * r); g.restore(); } };
  [[w * .3, h / 2], [w * .7, h / 2]].forEach(([cx, cy]) => { star(cx, cy, 170, '#8c6a44'); star(cx, cy, 140, '#d8c6a8'); star(cx, cy, 90, '#a7824f'); star(cx, cy, 45, '#e6d7bd'); });
  star(w / 2, h / 2, 60, '#a7824f');
  g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(140,106,68,0.5)'; g.lineWidth = 3;
  for (let x = 300; x < w - 300; x += 120) for (let y = 260; y < h - 260; y += 120) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(x, y, 6, 0, 7); g.stroke(); }
});
const stoneTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { // TV wall dark marble
  g.fillStyle = '#3b3632'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let v = 0; v < 26; v++) { let x = R() * w, y = 0; g.strokeStyle = `rgba(220,200,170,${0.08 + R() * 0.3})`; g.lineWidth = .5 + R() * 2.5; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y); for (let k = 0; k < 50; k++) { x += (R() - .5) * 40; y += 22; g.lineTo(x, y); } g.stroke(); }
}, [1, 1]);

// ---------- materials ----------
const M = {
  floor: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, roughness: .18, metalness: 0 }),
  wall: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, roughness: .9 }),
  ceil: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f0ea', roughness: .95 }),
  fluted: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, bumpMap: flutedBump, bumpScale: 3, roughness: .6 }),
  oak: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, roughness: .5 }),
  darkWood: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a2a1e', roughness: .45 }),
  fabric: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, roughness: .95 }),
  cushion: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: darkFabric, roughness: .9 }),
  goldCush: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b08a4e', roughness: .6, metalness: .2 }),
  brass: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', roughness: .28, metalness: 1 }),
  black: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#141212', roughness: .4, metalness: .3 }),
  rug: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: rugTex, roughness: 1 }),
  stone: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: stoneTex, roughness: .15 }),
  tableTop: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), roughness: .12 }),
  screen: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#050608', roughness: .1, metalness: .5 }),
  glass: new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#cfe3ea', roughness: .05, transmission: .9, transparent: true, opacity: .25, thickness: .02 }),
  leaf: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#41603a', roughness: .8 }),
  pot: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d8d0c4', roughness: .7 }),
  courtWall: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cbb89b', roughness: .95 }),
};
M.tableTop.map.repeat.set(1, 1);
const emis = (col, k) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#000', emissive: col, emissiveIntensity: k });
const LED = emis('#ffd9a8', 6), LEDsoft = emis('#ffcf96', 2.5);

// ---------- helpers ----------
const G = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(G);
function box(w, h, d, mat, x, y, z, o = {}) { // x,y,z = min corner
  const geo = o.r ? new RoundedBoxGeometry(w, h, d, 3, o.r) : new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d);
  if (o.uv) { const uv = geo.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) * o.uv[0], uv.getY(i) * o.uv[1]); }
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat); m.position.set(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, z + d / 2);
  m.castShadow = o.cast ?? true; m.receiveShadow = true; (o.parent || G).add(m); return m;
}
function plane(w, h, mat, pos, rot, parent = G) { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), mat); m.position.set(...pos); m.rotation.set(...rot); m.receiveShadow = true; parent.add(m); return m; }
const LX = 8.6, LZ = 6.35, HC = 4.3, HB = 3.45;


function plant(x, z, h = 1.4, potH = .5) {
  box(.42, potH, .42, M.pot, x - .21, 0, z - .21);
  const lm = [M.leaf, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#557a45', roughness: .8 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f4a2a', roughness: .8 })];
  for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
    const a = R() * 6.28, r = R() * .32, y = potH + .3 + R() * (h - .3);
    const lf = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(.1 + R() * .12, 0), lm[i % 3]);
    lf.position.set(x + Math.cos(a) * r * (1 - (y - potH) / h * .4), y, z + Math.sin(a) * r); lf.rotation.set(R() * 3, R() * 3, R() * 3); lf.castShadow = true; scene.add(lf);
  }
  box(.03, h * .7, .03, M.darkWood, x - .015, potH, z - .015);
}
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/tail.html`

```html
// ---------- cameras & render ----------
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, W / H, .05, 100);
const composer = new EffectComposer(renderer);
composer.addPass(new RenderPass(scene, camera));
const gtao = new GTAOPass(scene, camera, W, H); gtao.output = GTAOPass.OUTPUT.Default; gtao.blendIntensity = .9;
gtao.updateGtaoMaterial({ radius: .35, distanceExponent: 1.2, thickness: 1, scale: 1 });
composer.addPass(gtao);
composer.addPass(new UnrealBloomPass(new THREE.Vector2(W, H), .2, .4, .96));
composer.addPass(new OutputPass());

window.render = (name) => {
  const v = VIEWS[name];
  camera.position.set(...v.pos); camera.fov = v.fov; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); camera.lookAt(...v.tgt);
  // a stray NaN pixel can black out the whole frame through the post passes: nudge the camera and retry
  const probe = document.createElement('canvas'); probe.width = probe.height = 8; const pg = probe.getContext('2d');
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
    composer.render(); pg.drawImage(renderer.domElement, 0, 0, 8, 8);
    if (pg.getImageData(0, 0, 8, 8).data.some((v, i) => i % 4 !== 3 && v > 3)) break;
    camera.position.x += .013; camera.position.y += .007; camera.lookAt(...v.tgt);
  }
  return renderer.domElement.toDataURL('image/jpeg', .93);
};
window.READY = true;
</script></body></html>
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/shoot.js`

```javascript
// Render views of a 3D room in headless Chromium (three.js on SwiftShader).
// Usage: node studio/3d/shoot.js <body> "<view>@<state>;<view>@<state>" <width> <height> <outdir>
//   <body>  file name in studio/3d/bodies without .html (each body defines VIEWS and window.setState)
// Writes <outdir>/<view>_<state>.jpg (characters : | + become -). Run `npm install` in studio/3d once.
const { chromium } = (() => { try { return require('playwright'); } catch { return require('/opt/node22/lib/node_modules/playwright'); } })();
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path'), http = require('http');
const [,, body, jobs, w, h, out] = process.argv;
const dir = __dirname;
const page = `_page_${body}.html`;
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, page), ['head.html', `bodies/${body}.html`, 'tail.html'].map(f => fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, f), 'utf8')).join('\n'));
fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
const TYPES = { '.html': 'text/html', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.json': 'application/json' };
const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const f = path.join(dir, decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url, 'http://x').pathname));
  if (!f.startsWith(dir) || !fs.existsSync(f) || fs.statSync(f).isDirectory()) { res.writeHead(404); return res.end(); }
  res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': TYPES[path.extname(f)] || 'application/octet-stream' }); fs.createReadStream(f).pipe(res);
}).listen(0, '127.0.0.1', async () => {
  const b = await chromium.launch({ args: ['--use-gl=angle', '--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'] });
  const p = await b.newPage({ viewport: { width: +w, height: +h } });
  p.on('pageerror', e => { console.log('PAGEERR', e.message); process.exit(1); });
  p.on('console', m => { if (m.type() === 'error') console.log('ERR', m.text()); });
  await p.addInitScript(`window.RW=${w};window.RH=${h};`);
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/${page}`);
  await p.waitForFunction('window.READY === true', null, { timeout: 180000 });
  for (const j of jobs.split(';')) {
    const [v, st] = j.split('@'); await p.evaluate(s => window.setState(s), st);
    const d = await p.evaluate(n => window.render(n), v);
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, `${v}_${st.replace(/[:|+]/g, '-')}.jpg`), Buffer.from(d.split(',')[1], 'base64')); console.log(v, st);
  }
  await b.close(); server.close(); fs.unlinkSync(path.join(dir, page));
});
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/fly.js`

```javascript
// Animated 3D: render a body that defines window.frameShot(t) (camera + state for global time t, e.g. bodies/buyways.html).
//   node studio/3d/fly.js <body> stills "t1,t2,..." <W> <H> <outdir>        -> <outdir>/f_<t>.jpg
//   node studio/3d/fly.js <body> video <t0> <t1> <W> <H> <out.mp4> [fps=30]  -> H.264 clip of [t0, t1)
// Run several `video` chunks in parallel and concat them (ffmpeg concat demuxer) for a long fly-through.
const { chromium } = (() => { try { return require('playwright'); } catch { return require('/opt/node22/lib/node_modules/playwright'); } })();
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path'), http = require('http'), { spawn } = require('child_process');
const [,, body, mode, ...a] = process.argv;
const dir = __dirname;
const page = `_page_${body}_${process.pid}.html`;
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, page), ['head.html', `bodies/${body}.html`, 'tail.html'].map(f => fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, f), 'utf8')).join('\n'));
const [W, H] = mode === 'stills' ? [+a[1], +a[2]] : [+a[2], +a[3]];
const TYPES = { '.html': 'text/html', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.json': 'application/json', '.png': 'image/png', '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.ttf': 'font/ttf' };
const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  const f = path.join(dir, decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url, 'http://x').pathname));
  if (!f.startsWith(dir) || !fs.existsSync(f) || fs.statSync(f).isDirectory()) { res.writeHead(404); return res.end(); }
  res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': TYPES[path.extname(f)] || 'application/octet-stream' }); fs.createReadStream(f).pipe(res);
}).listen(0, '127.0.0.1', async () => {
  const b = await chromium.launch({ args: ['--use-gl=angle', '--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'] });
  const p = await b.newPage({ viewport: { width: W, height: H } });
  p.on('pageerror', e => { console.log('PAGEERR', e.message); process.exit(1); });
  p.on('console', m => { if (m.type() === 'error') console.log('ERR', m.text()); });
  await p.addInitScript(`window.RW=${W};window.RH=${H};`);
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/${page}`);
  await p.waitForFunction('window.READY === true', null, { timeout: 180000 });
  const grab = async t => Buffer.from((await p.evaluate(t => window.frameShot(t), t)).split(',')[1], 'base64');
  if (mode === 'stills') {
    fs.mkdirSync(a[3], { recursive: true });
    for (const t of a[0].split(',').map(Number)) { fs.writeFileSync(path.join(a[3], `f_${t.toFixed(2)}.jpg`), await grab(t)); console.log('still', t); }
  } else {
    const [t0, t1] = [+a[0], +a[1]], out = a[4], fps = +(a[5] || 30);
    const ff = spawn('ffmpeg', ['-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 'image2pipe', '-framerate', String(fps), '-c:v', 'mjpeg', '-i', '-', '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', 'medium', '-crf', '16', '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p', out], { stdio: ['pipe', 'inherit', 'inherit'] });
    const n0 = Math.round(t0 * fps), n1 = Math.round(t1 * fps), st = Date.now();
    for (let n = n0; n < n1; n++) {
      const buf = await grab(n / fps);
      if (!ff.stdin.write(buf)) await new Promise(r => ff.stdin.once('drain', r));
      if ((n - n0) % 30 === 0) console.log('frame', n, '/', n1, ((Date.now() - st) / 1000).toFixed(0) + 's');
    }
    ff.stdin.end(); await new Promise(r => ff.on('close', r));
  }
  await b.close(); server.close(); fs.unlinkSync(path.join(dir, page));
});
```
