```html
// ===== Accent corner: framed painting + console + one focused spot =====
// back wall at z=0 (x -2.2..3), left wall at x=-2.2, ceiling 3.0
marbleTex.repeat.set(3, 3);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#9a8c7d', roughness: .92 });
const woodFloorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#6e4f33', '40,24,12')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(25,14,6,.5)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [3, 3]);
plane(10, 10, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .45 }), [.4, 0, 3], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(6, 3.0, .1, wallM, -2.3, 0, -.1);
box(.1, 3.0, 7, wallM, -2.3, 0, -.1);
box(.1, 3.0, 7, wallM, 3.0, 0, -.1);
box(6, .1, 7, M.ceil, -2.3, 3.0, -.1, { cast: false });
box(5.3, .1, .02, M.darkWood, -2.2, 0, 0, { cast: false }); // skirting

// ---- painting (abstract, brand-warm) ----
const PW = .86, PH = 1.1, PX = .2, PY = 1.8;
function paintDraw(g, w, h) {
  const bg = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h); bg.addColorStop(0, '#13233f'); bg.addColorStop(.55, '#1d3a4f'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#0d1626'); g.fillStyle = bg; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  const blob = (x, y, r, c, a) => { const gr = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, r); gr.addColorStop(0, c); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); g.globalAlpha = a; g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(x - r, y - r, 2 * r, 2 * r); g.globalAlpha = 1; };
  blob(w * .65, h * .35, w * .55, '#ef5924', .9); blob(w * .3, h * .7, w * .45, '#1f9e8f', .85); blob(w * .75, h * .8, w * .3, '#e8b04a', .8);
  g.lineCap = 'round';
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { g.strokeStyle = ['#ef5924', '#f6c35a', '#ffe9c9', '#1fb3a0', '#c43d1a'][i % 5]; g.lineWidth = 14 + R() * 40; g.globalAlpha = .75; g.beginPath(); const y0 = h * (.15 + R() * .7); g.moveTo(-20, y0); g.bezierCurveTo(w * .3, y0 - 200 + R() * 400, w * .7, y0 - 200 + R() * 400, w + 20, y0 + (R() - .5) * 300); g.stroke(); }
  g.globalAlpha = 1;
  g.fillStyle = '#f6c35a'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w * .68, h * .3, w * .13, 0, 7); g.fill();
  g.strokeStyle = '#ffe9c9'; g.lineWidth = 6; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w * .68, h * .3, w * .18, 0, 7); g.stroke();
  for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${R() > .5 ? 255 : 0},${R() > .5 ? 240 : 0},${R() > .5 ? 220 : 0},0.05)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 3, 3); }
}
const paintHi = canvasTex(1000, 1300, paintDraw);
const paintLo = canvasTex(1000, 1300, (g, w, h) => { paintDraw(g, w, h); const d = g.getImageData(0, 0, w, h); const a = d.data; for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i += 4) { const L = .3 * a[i] + .59 * a[i + 1] + .11 * a[i + 2]; a[i] = (L * .62 + a[i] * .38) * .92; a[i + 1] = (L * .62 + a[i + 1] * .38) * .95; a[i + 2] = (L * .62 + a[i + 2] * .38) * .85; } g.putImageData(d, 0, 0); });
const canvasM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: paintHi, roughness: .75 });
const frameM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', roughness: .3, metalness: 1 });
box(PW + .1, PH + .1, .05, M.black, PX - PW / 2 - .05, PY - PH / 2 - .05, .0);
for (const [x, y, w, h] of [[-PW / 2 - .05, PH / 2 + .02, PW + .1, .03], [-PW / 2 - .05, -PH / 2 - .05, PW + .1, .03], [-PW / 2 - .05, -PH / 2 - .05, .03, PH + .1], [PW / 2 + .02, -PH / 2 - .05, .03, PH + .1]]) box(w, h, .06, frameM, PX + x, PY + y, .0);
plane(PW, PH, canvasM, [PX, PY, .052], [0, 0, 0]);
const glassP = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(PW, PH), new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#ffffff', roughness: .03, metalness: 0, transparent: true, opacity: .06, clearcoat: 1 })); glassP.position.set(PX, PY, .062); scene.add(glassP);
// fake specular hotspot on the glass (only when the spot is badly placed)
const hotTex = canvasTex(256, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createRadialGradient(w / 2, h / 2, 0, w / 2, h / 2, w / 2); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(.18, 'rgba(255,250,235,.85)'); gr.addColorStop(.5, 'rgba(255,240,215,.25)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,240,215,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); });
const hotM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: hotTex, transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, opacity: 0, toneMapped: false });
const hot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.5, .32), hotM); hot.position.set(PX + .05, PY - .12, .065); scene.add(hot);

// ---- console + decor ----
const conM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#5a3d26', roughness: .45 });
box(1.6, .05, .4, conM, PX - .8, .8, .03); box(1.6, .03, .38, conM, PX - .8, .25, .04);
for (const x of [-.78, .75]) for (const z of [.05, .38]) box(.03, .8, .03, frameM, PX + x, 0, z);
const ceramic = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9e0d2', roughness: .55 });
const vasePts = [[0, 0], [.07, 0], [.11, .08], [.13, .2], [.11, .32], [.06, .42], [.045, .5], [.06, .55]].map(([r, y]) => new THREE.Vector2(r, y));
const vase = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(vasePts, 48), ceramic); vase.position.set(PX - .5, .85, .2); vase.castShadow = true; scene.add(vase);
const twigM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#4a3424', roughness: .8 }), budM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9b98a', roughness: .7 });
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const a = -.5 + i * .2, len = .55 + R() * .3; const t = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.004, .006, len, 6), twigM); t.position.set(PX - .5 + Math.sin(a) * len / 2, 1.38 + Math.cos(a) * len / 2 - .02, .2 + (R() - .5) * .06); t.rotation.z = -a; t.castShadow = true; scene.add(t);
  for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) { const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.012 + R() * .01, 8, 6), budM); const s = .3 + k * .12; b.position.set(PX - .5 + Math.sin(a) * len * s, 1.38 + Math.cos(a) * len * s - .02, t.position.z); b.castShadow = true; scene.add(b); } }
const bowl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.11, 32, 16, 0, 6.29, 0, 1.5), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1a1a', roughness: .3, metalness: .4 })); bowl.position.set(PX + .2, .97, .2); bowl.rotation.x = Math.PI; scene.add(bowl);
const sph = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.07, 32, 16), frameM); sph.position.set(PX + .2, .93, .2); sph.castShadow = true; scene.add(sph);
for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) box(.3 - i * .02, .035, .22, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: ['#2b2622', '#c9b79c', '#7a1f2b'][i], roughness: .7 }), PX + .4, .85 + i * .035, .1, { r: .005 });
// armchair (left) + plant (right) + rug
const chairM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#b9a383', roughness: .95 });
box(.8, .42, .8, chairM, -1.95, .12, .9, { r: .06 }); box(.8, .5, .16, chairM, -1.95, .5, .9, { r: .06 }); box(.14, .3, .8, chairM, -1.95, .45, .9, { r: .05 }); box(.14, .3, .8, chairM, -1.29, .45, .9, { r: .05 });
plant(1.75, .5, 1.5, .55);
plane(2.6, 1.8, M.rug, [.1, .005, 1.3], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);

// ---- lights ----
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe7cc', '#3a2c20', .1); scene.add(hemi);
const lampL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb36b', 0, 4, 1.8); lampL.position.set(-1.9, 1.2, 2.2); scene.add(lampL);
const general = [];
for (const [x, z] of [[-1.2, 1.4], [1.6, 1.4], [-1.2, 3.2], [1.6, 3.2]]) {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff4e2', 0, 9, 1.0, .9, 1.6); s.position.set(x, 2.98, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target); general.push(s);
  box(.16, .01, .16, emis('#fff4e2', 0), x - .08, 2.985, z - .08, { cast: false }); general.push(G.children[G.children.length - 1]);
}
// the accent spot (good: z=.75 → ~31° off vertical; bad: z=2.6)
const spot = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 12, .2, .35, 1.4); spot.castShadow = true; spot.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); spot.shadow.bias = -.0004; spot.shadow.camera.near = .2;
spot.target.position.set(PX, PY - .05, .05); scene.add(spot, spot.target);
const fixture = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(fixture);
const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.065, .065, .02, 32), M.black); ring.position.y = -.01; fixture.add(ring);
const lensM = emis('#000', 0); const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.032, 24), lensM); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.y = -.025; fixture.add(lens);
// faint visible beam cone
const coneM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ffd6a0', transparent: true, opacity: 0, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
let cone = null;
function setCone(deg, pos) {
  if (cone) scene.remove(cone);
  const from = new THREE.Vector3(...pos), to = new THREE.Vector3(PX, PY - .05, .06), L = from.distanceTo(to), r = Math.tan(deg / 2 * Math.PI / 180) * L;
  const geo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(r, L, 48, 1, true); geo.translate(0, -L / 2, 0);
  cone = new THREE.Mesh(geo, coneM); cone.position.copy(from); cone.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0), to.clone().sub(from).normalize()); scene.add(cone);
}
const CCT = { 2700: '#ffc98a', 3000: '#ffd6a0', 4000: '#fff0dc', 6500: '#dfe9ff' };

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [.15, 1.45, 3.5], tgt: [.12, 1.45, 0], fov: 60 },
  c: { pos: [.2, 1.72, 2.7], tgt: [.2, 1.72, 0], fov: 44 },
  v: { pos: [-.05, 1.3, 1.45], tgt: [-.3, 1.08, .2], fov: 50 },
  u: { pos: [1.15, 1.25, 2.9], tgt: [.25, 2.1, .5], fov: 62 },
};
// state "mode:dark|flat|spot  beam:<deg>  pos:good|bad  cct:<K>  cri:hi|lo  dim:<0..1>  cone:<0..1>"
window.setState = (st) => {
  const o = Object.fromEntries(st.split('|').map(p => p.split(':')));
  const mode = o.mode ?? 'spot', beam = +(o.beam ?? 24), bad = o.pos === 'bad', cct = o.cct ?? '3000', lo = o.cri === 'lo', dim = +(o.dim ?? 1), cv = +(o.cone ?? 1);
  const on = mode === 'spot';
  general.forEach(g => { if (g.isLight) g.intensity = mode === 'flat' ? 9 : 0; else g.material = mode === 'flat' ? emis('#fff4e2', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  hemi.intensity = mode === 'flat' ? .5 : mode === 'dark' ? .07 : .025;
  lampL.intensity = mode === 'flat' ? 0 : mode === 'dark' ? 1.2 : .5;
  scene.environmentIntensity = mode === 'flat' ? .35 : mode === 'dark' ? .05 : .02;
  const pos = bad ? [PX, 2.97, 2.6] : [PX, 2.97, .75];
  spot.position.set(...pos); fixture.position.set(PX, 3.0, pos[2]);
  spot.angle = beam / 2 * Math.PI / 180; spot.penumbra = beam > 40 ? .6 : .3;
  spot.intensity = on ? (bad ? 30 : 14) * dim * (beam > 40 ? 1.5 : 1) : 0;
  const col = lo ? '#f3f6e2' : CCT[cct] ?? CCT[3000]; spot.color.set(col); coneM.color.set(col);
  lensM.emissive.set(col); lensM.emissiveIntensity = on ? (bad ? 14 : 5) * dim : 0;
  canvasM.map = lo ? paintLo : paintHi; canvasM.needsUpdate = true;
  hotM.opacity = on && bad ? .8 : 0;
  setCone(beam, pos); cone.visible = false; coneM.opacity = on ? .006 * cv * dim : 0;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = mode === 'flat' ? .75 : 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/apartment.html`

```html
// ===== Rented apartment living room: one cold ceiling lamp vs no-drill lighting (plug-in wall sconce with its cord to the socket, floor lamp by the sofa, smart table lamp; smart bulbs change colour) =====
// back wall z=0 (x -2.4..2.4) with the sofa, right wall x=2.4 carries the plug-in sconce over a side table, floor lamp at the left end of the sofa, window with curtains on the left wall
const CH = 2.7, RW = 2.4, RD = 5.0;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#a88462', '70,45,22')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(60,35,15,.3)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .5 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#ece5da', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD + .1, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD + .1, wallM, RW, 0, -.1);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD + .1, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
box(RW * 2, .08, .015, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f6f2ec' }), -RW, 0, 0); // skirting
// window with curtains on the left wall (night outside)
box(.02, 1.4, 1.2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0b0f18', emissive: '#1a2440', emissiveIntensity: .4, roughness: .1 }), -RW + .005, .8, 2.2, { cast: false });
const curM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#cfc3ae', roughness: .95 });
for (const z of [1.85, 3.45]) box(.08, 2.3, .4, curM, -RW + .02, .25, z, { r: .03 });
// sofa, cushions, rug, coffee table, art, plant
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8d939a', roughness: .95 });
box(2.2, .42, .9, sofaM, -1.3, .1, .1, { r: .06 }); box(2.2, .45, .22, sofaM, -1.3, .48, .1, { r: .06 });
for (const x of [-1.42, .82]) box(.2, .6, .9, sofaM, x, .1, .1, { r: .06 });
[['#d9a65b', -.9], ['#ece6dc', -.25], ['#5c7f86', .35]].forEach(([c, x]) => { const m = box(.42, .38, .12, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x - .21, .5, .3, { r: .06 }); m.rotation.x = -.2; });
plane(2.6, 1.8, M.rug, [-.2, .006, 1.9], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(1.0, .06, .55, M.oak, -.7, .38, 1.65, { r: .02 }); for (const [x, z] of [[-.65, 1.7], [.22, 1.7], [-.65, 2.12], [.22, 2.12]]) box(.04, .38, .04, M.black, x, 0, z);
box(1.1, .7, .03, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2b2b', roughness: .5 }), -.75, 1.35, .01);
box(1.0, .6, .01, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c97a54', roughness: .9 }), -.7, 1.4, .04, { cast: false });
plant(2.0, 3.6, 1.5, .45);
// side table on the right with the smart table lamp
box(.45, .55, .45, M.oak, 1.7, 0, .25, { r: .02 });
const shadeM = (c) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: .8, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: .96 });
const tShadeM = shadeM('#efe7da'); const tShade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.12, .16, .2, 32, 1, true), tShadeM); tShade.position.set(1.92, .93, .47); scene.add(tShade);
box(.04, .25, .04, M.brass, 1.9, .55, .45); box(.14, .02, .14, M.brass, 1.85, .55, .4);
const tableL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf96', 0, 4, 2); tableL.position.set(1.92, .92, .47); scene.add(tableL);
// plug-in wall sconce on the right wall: arm + shade, the cord runs down the wall to the socket (no drilling into wiring)
const scX = RW - .02, scY = 1.75, scZ = .62;
box(.04, .14, .1, M.black, scX - .04, scY - .07, scZ - .05); box(.26, .025, .025, M.brass, scX - .28, scY + .02, scZ - .012);
const sShadeM = shadeM('#f3ece0'); const sShade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.12, .16, 32, 1, true), sShadeM); sShade.position.set(scX - .3, scY - .02, scZ); sShade.rotation.x = Math.PI; scene.add(sShade);
const scLight = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf96', 0, 4, 55 * Math.PI / 180, .7, 1.4); scLight.position.set(scX - .3, scY - .06, scZ); scLight.target.position.set(scX - .5, 0, scZ + .2); scLight.castShadow = true; scene.add(scLight, scLight.target);
const scGlow = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf96', 0, 2, 2); scGlow.position.set(scX - .2, scY + .1, scZ); scene.add(scGlow);
const cordM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9e0', roughness: .6 });
box(.012, scY - .45, .012, cordM, scX - .014, .4, scZ - .006, { cast: false }); box(.012, .012, .32, cordM, scX - .014, .4, scZ - .006, { cast: false });
box(.02, .12, .08, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f7f5f1', roughness: .4 }), scX - .02, .32, scZ + .27); // socket plate + plug
box(.03, .05, .04, cordM, scX - .045, .35, scZ + .29);
// floor lamp at the left end of the sofa (smart bulb)
box(.3, .03, .3, M.black, -2.05, 0, .3); box(.025, 1.45, .025, M.black, -1.913, .03, .44);
const fShadeM = shadeM('#efe7da'); const fShade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.2, .24, .3, 32, 1, true), fShadeM); fShade.position.set(-1.9, 1.55, .45); scene.add(fShade);
const floorL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf96', 0, 5, 2); floorL.position.set(-1.9, 1.5, .45); floorL.castShadow = true; scene.add(floorL);
// the one cold ceiling lamp (flush, middle of the room)
const ceilLamp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.22, .22, .05, 40), emis('#000', 0)); ceilLamp.position.set(0, CH - .03, 2.0); scene.add(ceilLamp);
const ceilL = new THREE.PointLight('#eef4ff', 0, 10, 1.6); ceilL.position.set(0, CH - .2, 2.0); ceilL.castShadow = true; scene.add(ceilL);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#f4ecdf', '#2a2018', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [-.35, 1.5, 4.7], tgt: [.55, 1.05, .5], fov: 68 },    // the living room
  s: { pos: [1.25, 1.35, 2.3], tgt: [2.25, 1.05, .8], fov: 56 },   // the plug-in sconce and its cord to the socket
  f: { pos: [-.4, 1.2, 2.5], tgt: [-1.75, 1.0, .5], fov: 58 },     // the floor lamp by the sofa
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · ceil (one cold ceiling lamp) · sconce · floor · table · cozy (= sconce+floor+table) · tint:<0..1> (smart bulbs in floor+table lamps change hue)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('cozy', 'sconce+floor+table').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const ce = T.ceil ?? 0, sc = T.sconce ?? 0, fl = T.floor ?? 0, tb = T.table ?? 0;
  const smart = T.tint !== undefined ? new THREE.Color().setHSL(T.tint, .85, .6) : new THREE.Color('#ffcf96');
  ceilL.intensity = 12 * ce; ceilLamp.material = ce ? emis('#f2f7ff', 3) : emis('#000', 0);
  scLight.intensity = 15 * sc; scGlow.intensity = .5 * sc; sShadeM.emissive = new THREE.Color('#ffcf96'); sShadeM.emissiveIntensity = .8 * sc;
  floorL.intensity = 9 * fl; floorL.color.copy(smart); fShadeM.emissive = smart.clone(); fShadeM.emissiveIntensity = .9 * fl;
  tableL.intensity = 4.5 * tb; tableL.color.copy(smart); tShadeM.emissive = smart.clone(); tShadeM.emissiveIntensity = .9 * tb;
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .1 * ce + .04 * Math.max(sc, fl, tb); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .08 * ce;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/bagshoe.html`

```html
// ===== Bags & shoes boutique: flat white ceiling panels (leather looks like plastic, every colour the same) vs a narrow CRI 95 spot on every piece + a hidden strip under every shelf =====
// back wall z=0 (x -3.2..3.2): left = shoe wall with 5 floating oak shelves; right = bag wall, 3 x 3 dark cubbies; a display table in the middle with bags on plinths; mirror + pouf on the right wall; ceiling 3.0
const CH = 3.0, RW = 3.2, RD = 6.0;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#d8d2c8'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 7000; i++) { const c = 190 + R() * 40; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c * .97},${c * .92},.35)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 3, 3); } g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,110,100,.25)'; g.lineWidth = 2; for (let x = 0; x <= w; x += 256) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, h); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, x); g.lineTo(w, x); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .3 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e4ddd3', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// leather materials keep two looks: dull (flat cold panels, low CRI: greyish, matte) and vivid (CRI 95 spot: true colour, a soft sheen)
const leathers = [];
const lea = (vivid, dull) => { const m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: dull, roughness: .85 }); leathers.push({ m, vivid, dull }); return m; };
const L = {
  cognac: lea('#8e4a1f', '#7a6658'), red: lea('#b11e2a', '#7e5f5f'), emerald: lea('#17694a', '#55665e'), navy: lea('#1c2d5e', '#4c5363'),
  mustard: lea('#cf9525', '#9c8f74'), black: lea('#17130f', '#3a3734'), cream: lea('#eadcc2', '#bdb6aa'), blush: lea('#d9879a', '#a39294'), tan: lea('#a9733f', '#8a7b6c'),
};
const soleW = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2efe8', roughness: .6 }), soleB = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c1a18', roughness: .6 });
const goldM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', roughness: .25, metalness: 1 });
// a bag: rounded body, a flap, a half-ring handle, a gold clasp
const bag = (x, y, z, w, h, d, mat, ry = 0) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(x, y, z); g.rotation.y = ry; scene.add(g);
  box(w, h, d, mat, -w / 2, 0, -d / 2, { r: .025, parent: g });
  box(w + .004, h * .38, d * .55, mat, -w / 2 - .002, h * .62, d * .02, { r: .015, parent: g });
  box(.03, .02, .01, goldM, -.015, h * .6, d / 2 + .004, { parent: g, cast: false });
  const hd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(w * .28, .011, 8, 28, Math.PI), mat); hd.position.y = h; hd.castShadow = true; g.add(hd);
  return g;
};
// a shoe: sole + upper + rounded toe (a heel for the pumps)
const shoe = (x, y, z, mat, kind = 0, ry = 0) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(x, y, z); g.rotation.y = ry; scene.add(g);
  if (kind === 2) { // pump: thin sole, heel, low upper
    box(.075, .012, .22, soleB, -.0375, .05, -.11, { parent: g }); box(.02, .06, .02, soleB, -.01, 0, -.11, { parent: g });
    box(.072, .045, .17, mat, -.036, .06, -.08, { r: .02, parent: g });
  } else {
    box(.095, .025, .27, kind ? soleB : soleW, -.0475, 0, -.135, { r: .01, parent: g });
    box(.088, kind ? .1 : .07, .2, mat, -.044, .022, -.125, { r: .03, parent: g });
    const toe = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.045, 16, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), mat); toe.position.set(0, .022, .085); toe.scale.set(1, .9, 1.1); toe.castShadow = true; g.add(toe);
  }
  return g;
};
// LEFT: shoe wall, oak backing panel + 5 floating shelves, 5 pairs each; a hidden strip under each shelf's front edge lights the row below
box(2.9, 2.5, .03, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#b08a62', roughness: .6 }), -3.05, .2, -.08);
const shelfM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#d7b48a', roughness: .5 });
const shelfY = [.45, .88, 1.31, 1.74, 2.17], shoeMats = [L.cream, L.black, L.tan, L.red, L.navy, L.blush, L.cognac, L.emerald, L.mustard];
const shelfStrips = [], shelfL = [];
shelfY.forEach((y, r) => {
  box(2.7, .035, .3, shelfM, -2.95, y, -.05);
  for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const x = -2.7 + k * .55, m = shoeMats[(r * 2 + k) % shoeMats.length], kind = (r + k) % 3; shoe(x - .055, y + .035, .1, m, kind, .5); shoe(x + .055, y + .035, .1, m, kind, .5); }
  // the strip for the row ON this shelf hangs under the shelf above (top row: under a capping shelf)
  const sy = (shelfY[r + 1] ?? 2.6) - .003;
  if (r === 4) box(2.7, .035, .3, shelfM, -2.95, 2.6, -.05);
  shelfStrips.push(box(2.6, .006, .012, emis('#000', 0), -2.9, sy - .004, .2, { cast: false }));
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe2c0', 0, 2.6, .03); l.position.set(-1.6, sy - .01, .2); l.lookAt(-1.6, 0, .05); scene.add(l); shelfL.push(l);
});
// RIGHT: bag wall, 3 x 3 dark-wood cubbies, one bag in each; a strip at the top of each cubby row
const cubM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b211b', roughness: .55 });
const cubIn = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a2e26', roughness: .8 });
const CX0 = .3, CW = .85, CHh = .72, CY0 = .35;
box(CW * 3 + .06, CHh * 3 + .06, .03, cubIn, CX0 - .03, CY0 - .03, -.08);   // back of the cubbies
const bagMats = [L.cognac, L.red, L.black, L.navy, L.emerald, L.cream, L.blush, L.mustard, L.tan];
const cubStrips = [], cubL = [];
for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) {
  for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
    const x = CX0 + c * CW, y = CY0 + r * CHh;
    bag(x + CW / 2, y + .02, .18, .36 - (r === 2 ? .06 : 0), .27, .13, bagMats[r * 3 + c]);
  }
  cubStrips.push(box(CW * 3 - .1, .006, .012, emis('#000', 0), CX0 + .05, CY0 + (r + 1) * CHh - .05, .3, { cast: false }));
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe2c0', 0, CW * 3 - .1, .03); l.position.set(CX0 + CW * 1.5, CY0 + (r + 1) * CHh - .06, .3); l.lookAt(CX0 + CW * 1.5, 0, .3); scene.add(l); cubL.push(l);
}
// the cubby frame: full-depth dark-wood boards
for (let c = 0; c <= 3; c++) box(.04, CHh * 3 + .04, .42, cubM, CX0 + c * CW - .02, CY0 - .02, -.06);
for (let r = 0; r <= 3; r++) box(CW * 3 + .04, .04, .42, cubM, CX0 - .02, CY0 + r * CHh - .02, -.06);
// CENTRE: a low display table with two plinths: bags and a pair of shoes on top
const tabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9df', roughness: .4 });
box(1.6, .7, .7, tabM, -.8, 0, 2.3, { r: .02 });
box(.4, .18, .4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#e6e0d6', roughness: .15 }), -.6, .7, 2.45);
box(.34, .12, .34, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#e6e0d6', roughness: .15 }), .25, .7, 2.48);
bag(-.4, .88, 2.65, .4, .3, .15, L.red, .35);
bag(.42, .82, 2.65, .3, .22, .12, L.emerald, -.4);
shoe(-.05, .7, 2.6, L.cognac, 1, .9); shoe(.06, .7, 2.66, L.cognac, 1, .7);
// right wall: tall mirror and a velvet pouf
box(.03, 1.8, .7, new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#dfe6ea', metalness: 1, roughness: .05 }), RW - .04, .25, 3.6, { cast: false });
box(.05, 1.86, .76, M.brass, RW - .05, .22, 3.57);
const pouf = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.28, .28, .42, 36), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#7b5a4a', roughness: .95 })); pouf.position.set(2.3, .21, 3.95); pouf.castShadow = true; scene.add(pouf);
plant(-2.75, 5.3, 1.6, .45);
// BEFORE: flat cold panels in the ceiling
const panels = [], panelOff = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d2d3d5', roughness: .6 });
for (const x of [-1.8, 0, 1.8]) for (const z of [1.3, 3.6]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.6, .6), panelOff); m.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; m.position.set(x, CH - .005, z); scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef4ff', 0, .6, .6); l.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l); panels.push({ m, l });
}
// AFTER: narrow CRI 95 spots, 3000 K: one per bag column, two on the table pieces, three across the shoe wall
const mkSpot = (x, z, tx, ty, tz, ang, shadow = false) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .04, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffdcb0', 0, 7, ang * Math.PI / 180, .4, 1.2); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(tx, ty, tz); s.castShadow = shadow; scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s };
};
const spots = [
  mkSpot(-.4, 2.65, -.4, .95, 2.65, 12, true), mkSpot(.42, 2.65, .42, .9, 2.65, 12, true),
  mkSpot(CX0 + CW * .5, 1.1, CX0 + CW * .5, 1.3, .15, 16), mkSpot(CX0 + CW * 1.5, 1.1, CX0 + CW * 1.5, 1.3, .15, 16), mkSpot(CX0 + CW * 2.5, 1.1, CX0 + CW * 2.5, 1.3, .15, 16),
  mkSpot(-2.4, 1.1, -2.4, 1.3, .1, 20), mkSpot(-1.6, 1.1, -1.6, 1.3, .1, 20), mkSpot(-.8, 1.1, -.8, 1.3, .1, 20),
];
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ece6dc', '#2a2420', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.3, 1.7, 5.7], tgt: [-.3, 1.25, .6], fov: 64 },       // the boutique from the door
  b: { pos: [1.6, 1.4, 2.3], tgt: [1.58, 1.28, 0], fov: 58 },       // the bag wall, close
  s: { pos: [-1.6, 1.3, 1.75], tgt: [-1.65, 1.25, 0], fov: 58 },     // the shoe wall
  t: { pos: [.1, 1.42, 4.25], tgt: [-.3, 1.0, 2.65], fov: 46 },    // the red bag on its plinth, macro
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  panels (flat cold panels: dull, matte leather) · spots:<0..1> (narrow spots, one by one) · cri (true leather colour + sheen) · shelf:<0..1> (hidden strips under the shoe shelves, row by row, then the bag cubbies) · all (= spots+cri+shelf)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'spots+cri+shelf').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const pn = T.panels ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0, cr = T.cri ?? 0, sh = T.shelf ?? 0;
  panels.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 10 * pn; m.material = pn ? emis('#f4f8ff', 2.5) : panelOff; });
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sp * 1.1 - i / spots.length) / .2)); s.intensity = 16 * k; ring.material = k ? emis('#ffd8a8', 3 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const rows = [...shelfL.map((l, i) => [l, shelfStrips[i]]), ...cubL.map((l, i) => [l, cubStrips[i]])];
  rows.forEach(([l, m], i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sh * 1.1 - i / rows.length) / .15)); l.intensity = 12 * k; m.material = k ? emis('#ffe2c0', 5 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  leathers.forEach(({ m, vivid, dull }) => { m.color.set(cr ? vivid : dull); m.roughness = cr ? .38 : .85; });
  hemi.intensity = .05 + .08 * pn + .07 * Math.min(1, sp + sh); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .05 * pn + .07 * Math.min(1, sp + sh);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/barber.html`

```html
// ===== Barber shop: one spot over the client's head (hard shadow down the face) vs 4000 K CRI 90 light bars each side of the mirror + a ceiling linear =====
// back wall z=0 with two stations (mirrors centred at x = -1.2 and 1.2), chairs in front at z ~1.05, checker floor, ceiling 3.0
// no faces: the client is a plain faceless figure under a cape; the mirror reflects via a cube camera
const CH = 3.0, RW = 2.8, RD = 5.0;
const tileTex = canvasTex(512, 512, (g, w, h) => { for (let y = 0; y < 8; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 8; x++) { g.fillStyle = (x + y) % 2 ? '#1d1d1f' : '#e9e6df'; g.fillRect(x * 64, y * 64, 64, 64); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: tileTex, roughness: .35 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#3c4a46', roughness: .85 });
const sideM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d9d4cb', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD + .1, sideM, -RW - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD + .1, sideM, RW, 0, -.1);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD + .1, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
box(RW * 2, .9, .03, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#7a5a3c', roughness: .6 }), -RW, 0, 0); // wood wainscot
// mirrors with a reflection (cube camera), counters, tools

const mirrorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e3e8ec', metalness: 1, roughness: .02, envMap: cubeRT.texture });
const mirrors = [-1.2, 1.2].map((x) => { box(1.0, 1.3, .03, M.black, x - .5, .98, .02, { cast: false }); return box(.92, 1.22, .02, mirrorM, x - .46, 1.02, .05, { cast: false }); });
const ctrM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1efe9', roughness: .3 });

for (const x of [-1.2, 1.2]) {
  box(1.2, .05, .4, ctrM, x - .6, .9, .02); box(1.2, .85, .02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1f2523', roughness: .6 }), x - .6, .05, .4);
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.035, .04, .16 + R() * .08, 16), bottleMs[i % 4]); b.position.set(x - .45 + i * .11, 1.03, .18); b.castShadow = true; scene.add(b); }
  box(.18, .05, .08, M.black, x + .25, .95, .15); box(.12, .04, .05, chromeM(), x + .3, .99, .25);
}
function chromeM() { return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cfd3d6', metalness: 1, roughness: .2 }); }
// barber chairs + the faceless client under a cape on the left chair
const leather = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1a1c', roughness: .4 });
for (const x of [-1.2, 1.2]) {
  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.28, .32, .06, 32), chromeM()); base.position.set(x, .03, 1.15); scene.add(base);
  const post = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .38, 16), chromeM()); post.position.set(x, .25, 1.15); scene.add(post);
  box(.56, .14, .52, leather, x - .28, .44, .9, { r: .05 }); box(.56, .62, .1, leather, x - .28, .56, 1.38, { r: .04 }); box(.24, .14, .08, leather, x - .12, 1.2, 1.4, { r: .03 });
  for (const s of [-1, 1]) box(.07, .06, .48, chromeM(), x + s * .3 - .035, .74, .92);
  box(.4, .04, .2, chromeM(), x - .2, .2, .62);
}
const capeM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2d33', roughness: .7 });
const cape = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.42, .9, 32, 1, true), capeM); cape.position.set(-1.2, .95, 1.12); cape.castShadow = true; scene.add(cape);
const skinM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b98d6b', roughness: .55 });
const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.11, 32, 24), skinM); head.scale.set(1, 1.18, 1.05); head.position.set(-1.2, 1.55, 1.12); head.castShadow = true; scene.add(head);
const hair = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.116, 32, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI * .45), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1410', roughness: .8 })); hair.scale.set(1, 1.18, 1.05); hair.position.set(-1.2, 1.555, 1.125); scene.add(hair);
const neck = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .06, .1, 16), skinM); neck.position.set(-1.2, 1.4, 1.12); scene.add(neck);
// waiting bench and a plant by the right wall
box(.45, .45, 1.6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#6b4a32', roughness: .8 }), RW - .5, 0, 2.4, { r: .04 });
plant(RW - .35, 4.4, 1.5, .45);
// BEFORE: one warm spot right above the client's head
const spot = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 5, 22 * Math.PI / 180, .4, 1.3); spot.position.set(-1.2, CH - .05, 1.15); spot.target.position.set(-1.2, 0, 1.15); spot.castShadow = true; spot.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(spot, spot.target);
const spotLens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.04, 20), emis('#000', 0)); spotLens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; spotLens.position.set(-1.2, CH - .002, 1.15); scene.add(spotLens);
// AFTER: vertical 4000 K light bars each side of each mirror + a ceiling linear along the stations
const bars = [];
for (const mx of [-1.2, 1.2]) for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
  const x = mx + s * .58;
  const m = box(.05, 1.22, .04, emis('#000', 0), x - .025, 1.02, .04, { cast: false });
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff6ec', 0, .05, 1.22); l.position.set(x, 1.63, .07); l.lookAt(x, 1.63, 3); scene.add(l);
  bars.push({ m, l });
}
const linLens = box(4.2, .01, .05, emis('#000', 0), -2.1, CH - .02, 1.7, { cast: false }); box(4.3, .05, .1, M.black, -2.15, CH - .05, 1.67, { cast: false });
const linL = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff6ec', 0, 4.2, .06); linL.position.set(0, CH - .03, 1.72); linL.lookAt(0, 0, 1.72); scene.add(linL);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#e6e2da', '#2a2622', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [2.1, 1.65, 4.7], tgt: [-.6, 1.25, .6], fov: 60 },     // the shop
  c: { pos: [-.95, 1.6, 2.7], tgt: [-1.2, 1.4, 0], fov: 50 },       // behind the client: the mirror and the bars
  s: { pos: [.0, 1.5, 1.75], tgt: [-1.2, 1.45, 1.1], fov: 40 },     // close on the head from the side: shadows or not
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · spot (one spot over the head) · mirror (side bars) · linear (ceiling linear) · all (= mirror+linear)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'mirror+linear').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const sp = T.spot ?? 0, mr = T.mirror ?? 0, ln = T.linear ?? 0;
  spot.intensity = 26 * sp; spotLens.material = sp ? emis('#ffe2b8', 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  bars.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 30 * mr; m.material = mr ? emis('#fff4e8', 5) : emis('#000', 0); });
  linL.intensity = 45 * ln; linLens.material = ln ? emis('#fff4e8', 4) : emis('#000', 0);
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .06 * Math.max(mr, ln); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .05 * Math.max(mr, ln);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
  mirrors.forEach((m) => m.visible = false); cubeCam.update(renderer, scene); mirrors.forEach((m) => m.visible = true);
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/buyways.html`

```html
// ===== How to buy from QUALITY LIGHT: an animated 3D fly-through (render with studio/3d/fly.js, global time t in seconds) =====
// stations (each its own group, only the active one is visible):
//   P x=0    product stage: spot, ring chandelier, LED strip reel, garden spike light popping onto glowing pedestals + QL sign
//   S x=40   showroom at night: facade with the wordmark sign, glass front, interior walls of products switching on
//   W x=80   phone with a chat (photo of a ceiling sent, reply with product cards), 3D card flying in, products flying out
//   E x=120  laptop with the shop website: product grid, cursor, a spot jumps out into a 3D cart, checkout with cash on delivery
//   I x=160  phone with a social DM chat, hearts floating
//   V x=200  villa at night: a QL box slides to the door, then the facade lights up layer by layer
// window.frameShot(t) picks the station + camera for global time t (voice timing of the reel) and renders.
const ease = x => x <= 0 ? 0 : x >= 1 ? 1 : x * x * (3 - 2 * x);
const cl = (x, a = 0, b = 1) => Math.min(b, Math.max(a, x));
const pr = (t, a, b) => cl((t - a) / (b - a));
const back = x => { x = cl(x); const c1 = 1.9, c3 = c1 + 1; return 1 + c3 * Math.pow(x - 1, 3) + c1 * Math.pow(x - 1, 2); };
const V3 = (a) => new THREE.Vector3(...a);
async function font(n, w) { const b = await (await fetch('assets/' + n)).arrayBuffer(); const f = new FontFace('Cairo', b, { weight: w }); await f.load(); document.fonts.add(f); }
await font('cairo2.ttf', '600'); await font('cairo4.ttf', '900');
const loadImg = s => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = s; });
const WMI = await loadImg('assets/wordmark.png'), QLI = await loadImg('assets/logo3d.png');
const imgTex = (img) => { const t = new THREE.Texture(img); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.needsUpdate = true; t.anisotropy = 8; return t; };
const WMT = imgTex(WMI), QLT = imgTex(QLI);
// a canvas texture we redraw every frame
function liveTex(w, h) { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = 8; return { c, g: c.getContext('2d'), t }; }
function rr(g, x, y, w, h, r) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x + r, y); g.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r); g.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r); g.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r); g.closePath(); }
function T(g, s, x, y, size, col, w = 600, align = 'right') { g.font = `${w} ${size}px Cairo`; g.fillStyle = col; g.textAlign = align; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.direction = align === 'left' ? 'ltr' : 'rtl'; g.fillText(s, x, y); }
// little product icons drawn in 2D (for screens)
function icon(g, k, x, y, s) {
  g.save(); g.translate(x, y); g.scale(s / 100, s / 100);
  if (k === 0) { g.fillStyle = '#1b1b1b'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(0, 0, 38, 0, 7); g.fill(); g.fillStyle = '#ffd59a'; g.shadowColor = '#ffb15a'; g.shadowBlur = 25; g.beginPath(); g.arc(0, 0, 14, 0, 7); g.fill(); }
  if (k === 1) { g.strokeStyle = '#c9a063'; g.lineWidth = 9; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(0, 8, 42, 14, 0, 0, 7); g.stroke(); g.strokeStyle = '#ffd59a'; g.shadowColor = '#ffb15a'; g.shadowBlur = 20; g.lineWidth = 4; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(0, 8, 42, 14, 0, 0, 7); g.stroke(); g.shadowBlur = 0; g.strokeStyle = '#888'; g.lineWidth = 2; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, -45); g.lineTo(0, -6); g.stroke(); }
  if (k === 2) { g.strokeStyle = '#ffe2b8'; g.shadowColor = '#ffb15a'; g.shadowBlur = 18; g.lineWidth = 7; g.beginPath(); for (let a = 0; a < 18; a += .2) { const r = 8 + a * 1.8; g.lineTo(Math.cos(a) * r, Math.sin(a) * r); } g.stroke(); }
  if (k === 3) { g.fillStyle = '#3a3a3a'; g.fillRect(-6, -5, 12, 50); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(-6, 45); g.lineTo(6, 45); g.lineTo(0, 60); g.fill(); g.fillStyle = '#2a2a2a'; rr(g, -18, -40, 36, 40, 6); g.fill(); g.fillStyle = '#ffe0a8'; g.shadowColor = '#ffb15a'; g.shadowBlur = 22; g.fillRect(-13, -44, 26, 6); }
  g.restore();
}
// fake volumetric beam (excluded from the AO pass)
const BEAMS = [];
const beamTex = canvasTex(64, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)'); gr.addColorStop(.35, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.35)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); const gx = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, 0); gx.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); gx.addColorStop(.5, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); gx.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out'; g.fillStyle = gx; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1]);

function mkSpot() { // deep anti-glare downlight: trim face at y=0, lens looking down (-y), heat-sink fins above
  const g = new THREE.Group();
  add(g, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.16, .16, .025, 48), std('#f2f0ec', .35)), 0, .012, 0);
  add(g, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.11, .085, .16, 48, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0b0b0b', roughness: .3, metalness: .5, side: THREE.DoubleSide })), 0, .08, 0);
  add(g, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.09, .09, .02, 32), darkAlu), 0, .16, 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { const f = add(g, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.01, .13, .19), darkAlu), 0, .235, 0); f.rotation.y = i * Math.PI / 10; }
  const lensM = emis('#ffd9a8', 0); const lens = add(g, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.05, 40), lensM), 0, .15, 0); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  const bm = beam(.05, .75, 1.8, '#ffcf96', .2, g); bm.position.y = .14;

function mkChand() { // modern brass ring chandelier with a glowing inner line
  const g = new THREE.Group(); const ring = new THREE.Group(); g.add(ring);
  add(ring, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.42, .03, 20, 96), brassM)).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  const ledM = emis('#ffd29a', 0); add(ring, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.42, .012, 12, 96), ledM), 0, -.028, 0).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  const r2 = new THREE.Group(); g.add(r2); r2.position.y = .16; r2.rotation.z = .35;

  PROD.forEach((p, i) => {

    p.g.scale.setScalar(s); p.glow(ease(pr(t, T0[i] + .1, T0[i] + .5)));
    p.g.rotation.y = (i === 2 ? 0 : 1) * (t * .7 + i) + (1 - k) * 2.5;
    if (i === 0) { p.g.rotation.set(-.55, .4 * Math.sin(t * .8), 0); } if (i === 3) p.g.rotation.x = 0;
    if (i === 2) { p.g.rotation.y = -.1 + Math.sin(t * .8) * .12; p.g.rotation.z = t * .5; }
    p.g.position.y = 1.45 + Math.sin(t * 1.6 + i) * .04 + (i === 1 ? .1 : 0);
    PED[i].rm.emissiveIntensity = 4 * ease(k); PED[i].pl.intensity = 1.4 * ease(k);
  });
  const sg = ease(pr(t, 4.6, 5.6)); signP.material.color.setScalar(sg); qlP.material.color.setScalar(ease(pr(t, 5.0, 6.0)));
}

  add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6.8, .1, .12), std('#111', .4, .6)), 0, 3.3, .02);
  for (const [x, w] of [[-2.25, 2.2], [2.25, 2.2]]) { const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, 3.1), new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#9fb6c0', roughness: .04, metalness: 0, transparent: true, opacity: .12, depthWrite: false })); gl.position.set(x, 1.75, 0); S.add(gl); }
  // interior shell z -9..0

  add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(16, 9), inF), 0, .01, -4.5).rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
  add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(16, 9), std('#1d1a17', .9)), 0, 4.0, -4.5).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(14, 4), std('#2b2622', .85)), 0, 2, -9);
  add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(9, 4), inW), -6.8, 2, -4.5).rotation.y = Math.PI / 2;
  add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(9, 4), inW), 6.8, 2, -4.5).rotation.y = -Math.PI / 2;
  // section 0: back wall pendants (3 rows of globes)
  const sec = [];
  { const ms = [], pls = []; for (let r = 0; r < 2; r++) for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const x = -4.2 + i * 1.4, y = 2.9 - r * 1.1, z = -8.2 + r * .7; add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.006, .006, 4 - y, 6), std('#222')), x, y + (4 - y) / 2, z); const m = emis('#ffcf8f', 0); ms.push(m); add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.17 + (i % 3) * .04, 32, 16), m), x, y, z); }
    const pl = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc98a', 0, 9, 1.3); pl.position.set(0, 2.6, -7.2); S.add(pl); sec.push({ on: k => { ms.forEach(m => m.emissiveIntensity = 3 * k); pl.intensity = 6 * k; } }); }
  // section 1 / 2: left & right walls: display panels with grids of spots
  for (const side of [-1, 1]) { const ms = []; const px = side * 6.75;
    for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const z = -7.6 + i * 1.15, y = .9 + r * .75; const m = emis('#fff0d8', 0); ms.push(m); const d = add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.11, .11, .04, 24), std('#ddd', .4)), px - side * .02, y, z); d.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; const l = add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.06, 24), m), px - side * .045, y, z); l.rotation.y = -side * Math.PI / 2; }
    const pl = new THREE.PointLight('#fff0dc', 0, 7, 1.4); pl.position.set(side * 5.6, 2, -4.3); S.add(pl); sec.push({ on: k => { ms.forEach(m => m.emissiveIntensity = 4 * k); pl.intensity = 3.5 * k; } }); }
  // section 3: centre table + two ring chandeliers

    const ch = [mkChand(), mkChand()]; ch.forEach((c, i) => { S.add(c.g); c.g.position.set(-.8 + i * 1.6, 2.7, -4.6); c.g.scale.setScalar(1.1); });
    sec.push({ on: k => ch.forEach(c => c.glow(k)) }); }
  // section 4: cove strip line along the ceiling edge
  { const m = emis('#ffd6a0', 0); add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(13.4, .04, .04), m), 0, 3.95, -8.9); add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.04, .04, 8.8), m), -6.75, 3.95, -4.5); add(S, new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.04, .04, 8.8), m), 6.75, 3.95, -4.5);
    const pl = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 12, 1.2); pl.position.set(0, 3.7, -4); S.add(pl); sec.push({ on: k => { m.emissiveIntensity = 5 * k; pl.intensity = 3 * k; } }); }
  SHOW.sections = sec;
  const moon = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#4a5c7a', '#0c0b0a', .35); S.add(moon);
  const street = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf9a', 40, 18, .8, .7, 1.5); street.position.set(-7, 6, 6); street.target.position.set(-2, 0, 1); S.add(street, street.target);
  const base = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd2a0', .8, 12, 1.4); base.position.set(0, 2.5, -2); S.add(base);
}
```

```html
// ===== Café at night: flat cold panels (6500K) vs warm layers (low pendants over the tables, bar spots, shelf LEDs) =====
// floor y=0, ceiling 3.2; x -3..3; back wall (bar) at z=-5, the camera end at z=3.5; window wall on the left
const CW = 3, CZ0 = -5, CZ1 = 3.6, CH = 3.2;
const woodFloorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#5e4330', '35,20,10')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(20,10,4,.55)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 96) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 6]);
plane(6, 8.6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .5 }), [0, 0, -.7], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#cdbfae', roughness: .92 });
const brickTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => {
  g.fillStyle = '#5a3a2c'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let y = 0, r = 0; y < h; y += 42, r++) for (let x = (r % 2) * -60; x < w; x += 120) {
    const c = 120 + R() * 50; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c},${c * .58},${c * .42})`; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 3, 114, 36);
    g.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.12)'; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 30, 114, 9);
  }
}, [3, 1.6]);
const brickM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: brickTex, roughness: .9 });
box(.1, CH, CZ1 - CZ0, brickM, CW, 0, CZ0);                                   // right wall: brick
box(6.2, CH, .1, wallM, -3.1, 0, CZ0 - .1);                                    // back wall
box(6.2, CH, .1, wallM, -3.1, 0, CZ1);                                         // camera wall
box(6.2, .1, CZ1 - CZ0, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2420', roughness: .9 }), -3.1, CH, CZ0, { cast: false }); // dark ceiling
// left wall: low wall + big window to the night street
box(.1, .6, CZ1 - CZ0, wallM, -CW - .1, 0, CZ0); box(.1, .5, CZ1 - CZ0, wallM, -CW - .1, CH - .5, CZ0);
const nightTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => {
  const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#060912'); gr.addColorStop(.7, '#101a2c'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#1b2232'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const x = R() * w, y = h * (.45 + R() * .5), r = 6 + R() * 26; const b = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, r); const c = ['255,190,120', '255,230,190', '140,180,255', '255,140,90'][i % 4]; b.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${c},.55)`); b.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${c},0)`); g.fillStyle = b; g.fillRect(x - r, y - r, 2 * r, 2 * r); }
});
plane(CZ1 - CZ0, CH - 1.1, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: nightTex, toneMapped: true }), [-CW - .05, .6 + (CH - 1.1) / 2, (CZ0 + CZ1) / 2], [0, Math.PI / 2, 0]);
for (let z = CZ0; z <= CZ1; z += 1.45) box(.06, CH - 1.1, .06, M.black, -CW - .02, .6, z);
box(.08, .05, CZ1 - CZ0, M.black, -CW - .02, .6, CZ0); box(.08, .05, CZ1 - CZ0, M.black, -CW - .02, CH - .55, CZ0);

// ---- bar along the back wall ----
const barWood = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, bumpMap: flutedBump, bumpScale: 3, color: '#a57b52', roughness: .55 });
box(3.8, 1.02, .6, barWood, -1.9, 0, -3.9);
box(3.9, .05, .68, M.tableTop, -1.95, 1.02, -3.94);
box(3.8, .9, .5, M.darkWood, -1.9, 0, -4.95);                                  // back counter
box(3.8, .04, .52, M.tableTop, -1.9, .9, -4.96);
// espresso machine + grinder + cups on the back counter
const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c8c8c8', roughness: .22, metalness: 1 });
box(.8, .42, .42, steel, -.4, .94, -4.9, { r: .04 }); box(.8, .06, .3, M.black, -.4, 1.36, -4.85);
for (const x of [-.25, .15]) box(.08, .12, .08, M.black, x, .98, -4.55);
box(.18, .4, .18, M.black, .7, .94, -4.85, { r: .03 });
const cupM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2ece2', roughness: .4 });
function cup(x, y, z, s = 1) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045 * s, .035 * s, .08 * s, 24), cupM); c.position.set(x, y + .04 * s, z); c.castShadow = true; scene.add(c); const p = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.075 * s, .075 * s, .01, 24), cupM); p.position.set(x, y + .005, z); scene.add(p); }
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) cup(-1.6 + i * .16, 1.07, -3.65);
// shelves on the back wall with jars and bottles, an LED strip under each
const shelves = [];
const jarCols = ['#6b3a1e', '#a46a2c', '#2f2a26', '#c99b5a', '#3f5a3a', '#7a2f22'];
for (const y of [1.55, 1.95, 2.35]) {
  box(3.4, .04, .26, M.oak, -1.7, y, -4.98);
  for (let x = -1.6; x < 1.6; x += .17 + R() * .1) {
    const h = .12 + R() * .14, r = .04 + R() * .02;
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r, r, h, 20), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: jarCols[Math.floor(R() * 6)], roughness: .35, metalness: .05 }));
    m.position.set(x, y + .04 + h / 2, -4.86); m.castShadow = true; scene.add(m);
  }
  const strip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.3, .01, .02), emis('#000', 0)); strip.position.set(0, y - .006, -4.8); scene.add(strip);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffc98a', 0, 3.3, .06); l.position.set(0, y - .012, -4.82); l.lookAt(0, y - 1, -4.82); scene.add(l);
  shelves.push({ strip, l });
}
// bar stools
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
  const x = -1.4 + i * .95;
  const seat = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.19, .19, .06, 32), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6a4228', roughness: .5 })); seat.position.set(x, .76, -3.45); seat.castShadow = true; scene.add(seat);
  const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.025, .025, .74, 12), M.brass); leg.position.set(x, .37, -3.45); scene.add(leg);
  const foot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.16, .012, 8, 32), M.brass); foot.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; foot.position.set(x, .28, -3.45); scene.add(foot);
}

// ---- tables, chairs, banquette ----
const TABLES = [[-1.1, -1.7], [1.1, -2.0], [-1.1, .9], [1.1, .6]];
const chairM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#9a6a48', roughness: .9 });
function chair(x, z, face) {
  box(.42, .06, .42, chairM, x - .21, .45, z - .21, { r: .02 });
  box(.42, .42, .05, chairM, x - .21, .5, face > 0 ? z - .23 : z + .18, { r: .02 });
  for (const dx of [-.18, .16]) for (const dz of [-.18, .16]) box(.025, .45, .025, M.darkWood, x + dx, 0, z + dz);
}
for (const [x, z] of TABLES) {
  const top = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.42, .42, .04, 48), M.tableTop); top.position.set(x, .75, z); top.castShadow = top.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(top);
  const stem = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .04, .72, 16), M.brass); stem.position.set(x, .37, z); scene.add(stem);
  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.22, .24, .03, 32), M.brass); base.position.set(x, .015, z); scene.add(base);
  chair(x, z - .7, 1); chair(x, z + .7, -1);
  cup(x - .14, .77, z - .08, 1.1); cup(x + .16, .77, z + .1, 1.1);
  const plate = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.11, .11, .012, 32), cupM); plate.position.set(x + .05, .776, z - .2); scene.add(plate);
  const cake = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.09, .06, .06), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6b3b22', roughness: .6 })); cake.position.set(x + .05, .812, z - .2); cake.rotation.y = .5; cake.castShadow = true; scene.add(cake);
}
plant(2.6, 2.6, 1.6, .6); plant(-2.6, -4.3, 1.3, .5);

// ---- lights ----
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe9d2', '#2a2018', .05); scene.add(hemi);
// layer 1: flat ceiling panels (the "before")
const panels = [];
for (const x of [-1.5, 1.5]) for (const z of [-3, -.6, 1.8]) {
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef3ff', 0, .6, .6); l.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l);
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.6, .015, .6), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(x, CH - .008, z); scene.add(m);
  panels.push({ l, m });
}
// layer 2: low pendants over every table (dome shades: the light pools on the table)
const shadeM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1d1a17', roughness: .35, metalness: .6, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const pendants = [];
for (const [x, z] of TABLES) {
  const y = 1.62;
  const shade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.2, 40, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), shadeM); shade.position.set(x, y, z); shade.castShadow = true; scene.add(shade);
  const rim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.2, .006, 8, 48), M.brass); rim.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; rim.position.set(x, y, z); scene.add(rim);
  const cord = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.004, .004, CH - y - .2, 6), M.black); cord.position.set(x, (CH + y + .2) / 2, z); scene.add(cord);
  const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.05, 20, 12), emis('#000', 0)); bulb.position.set(x, y + .04, z); scene.add(bulb);
  const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc27a', 0, 6, 1.6); l.position.set(x, y - .02, z); l.castShadow = true; l.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); l.shadow.bias = -.002; l.shadow.camera.near = .05; scene.add(l);
  pendants.push({ l, bulb });
}
// layer 3: narrow spots on the bar top
const barSpots = [];
for (const x of [-1.25, 0, 1.25]) {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd09a', 0, 8, 12 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.5); s.position.set(x, CH - .02, -3.1); s.target.position.set(x, 1.05, -3.65); s.castShadow = true; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(s, s.target);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 24), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .01, -3.1); scene.add(ring);
  barSpots.push({ s, ring });
}
// layer 4: warm wall grazing on the brick
const wash = [];
for (const z of [-3.2, -1, 1.2]) { const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffb870', 0, 6, .55, .9, 1.4); s.position.set(CW - .25, CH - .05, z); s.target.position.set(CW, .3, z); scene.add(s, s.target); wash.push(s); }

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.55, 3.3], tgt: [0, 1.15, -4.5], fov: 74 },      // the whole café from the door
  p: { pos: [-1.1, 1.2, 1.75], tgt: [.1, .95, -3.6], fov: 70 },  // POV: sitting at a table, looking at the bar
  t: { pos: [-.45, 1.35, -.7], tgt: [-1.05, 1.05, -1.7], fov: 62 }, // close-up of a table under its pendant
  b: { pos: [.6, 1.45, -1.6], tgt: [0, 1.15, -4.4], fov: 58 },     // the bar
};
const CCT = { 2700: '#ffc27a', 3000: '#ffd09a', 4000: '#fff0dc', 6500: '#e6eeff' };
// state "panels:<0..1>|pend:<0..1>|bar:<0..1>|shelf:<0..1>|wash:<0..1>"  presets: flat | warm | dark
window.setState = (st) => {
  const preset = { flat: 'panels:1', warm: 'panels:0|pend:1|bar:1|shelf:1|wash:1', dark: 'panels:0' }[st] ?? st;
  const o = Object.fromEntries(preset.split('|').map(p => p.split(':')).map(([k, v]) => [k, +v]));
  const P = o.panels ?? 0, pe = o.pend ?? 0, ba = o.bar ?? 0, sh = o.shelf ?? 0, wa = o.wash ?? 0;
  for (const p of panels) { p.l.intensity = 9 * P; p.m.material = P ? emis(CCT[6500], 2.2 * P) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const p of pendants) { p.l.intensity = 6 * pe; p.bulb.material = pe ? emis(CCT[2700], 8 * pe) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const b of barSpots) { b.s.intensity = 24 * ba; b.ring.material = ba ? emis(CCT[3000], 6 * ba) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const s of shelves) { s.l.intensity = 9 * sh; s.strip.material = sh ? emis(CCT[2700], 5 * sh) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const s of wash) s.intensity = 18 * wa;
  hemi.color.set(P ? '#eef3ff' : '#ffe2c0'); hemi.intensity = P ? .9 : .05;
  scene.environmentIntensity = P ? .55 : .05;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = P ? .72 : 1.15;
};
```

```html
const sandTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#c9a777'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 20000; i++) { const c = 170 + R() * 50; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c * .82},${c * .58},.35)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2, 2); } }, [30, 30]);
plane(150, 150, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: sandTex, roughness: 1 }), [0, 0, 0], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);

box(1.2, 1.5, .02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a1f15', roughness: 1 }), -.6, 0, -.49, { cast: false });

'#e0b56a' : '#1f3a5c'; g.beginPath(); const x = 180 + i * 130, y = h / 2; g.moveTo(x, y - 60); g.lineTo(x + 50, y); g.lineTo(x, y + 60); g.lineTo(x - 50, y); g.fill(); } }, [1, 1]);

for (const x of [-2.1, 1.5]) { box(.6, .15, 2.6, cushM, x, .01, .3, { r: .05 }); box(.2, .4, 2.6, backM, x + (x < 0 ? 0 : .4), .16, .3, { r: .06 }); }
box(1.0, .25, .6, M.darkWood, -.5, 0, 1.0, { r: .02 });
const brassM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', metalness: 1, roughness: .25 });
const dallah = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .1, .22, 20), brassM); dallah.position.set(-.2, .36, 1.25); dallah.castShadow = true; scene.add(dallah);
for (const x of [.05, .15, .25]) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.025, .02, .04, 12), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2efe8', roughness: .4 })); c.position.set(x - .4, .27, 1.05); scene.add(c); }
const pit = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.35, .3, .2, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2622', metalness: .4, roughness: .7 })); pit.position.set(0, .1, 2.4); scene.add(pit);
const embers = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.28, 24), emis('#ff6a1f', 1.5)); embers.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; embers.position.set(0, .21, 2.4); scene.add(embers);
const fireL = new THREE.PointLight('#ff7a2a', 1.2, 4, 2); fireL.position.set(0, .5, 2.4); scene.add(fireL);
// cars on the right (simple shapes)

for (const [x, z] of [[4.6, 2.2], [5.2, 4.6]]) { box(1.8, .7, 4.2, carM, x, .3, z, { r: .12 }); box(1.6, .5, 2.2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c2229', roughness: .2, metalness: .3 }), x + .1, .95, z + .9, { r: .1 }); for (const dz of [.6, 3.4]) for (const dx of [0, 1.7]) { const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.33, .33, .2, 20), M.black); w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; w.position.set(x + dx + .05, .33, z + dz); scene.add(w); } }
// night sky dome-ish backdrop
const skyTex = canvasTex(2048, 1024, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#02040a'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#1a2036'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 1600; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${.3 + R() * .7})`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h * .8, 1.5, 1.5); } }, [1, 1]);
const skyDome = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(70, 48, 24, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: skyTex, toneMapped: false, side: THREE.BackSide })); skyTex.wrapS = THREE.RepeatWrapping; skyTex.repeat.set(3, 1); scene.add(skyDome);
// BEFORE: one harsh white floodlight on a pole by the tent

const flood = new THREE.SpotLight('#f4f7ff', 0, 30, 60 * Math.PI / 180, .3, 1.0); flood.position.set(2.45, 3.95, 0); flood.target.position.set(-.5, 0, 1.5); flood.castShadow = true; scene.add(flood, flood.target);
// AFTER: warm string lights over the seating (between the tent and two poles), lanterns, a small aimed floodlight for the path

const bulbs = [], bulbLights = [];
const strand = (ax, ay, az, bx, by, bz, n) => { for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const t = i / n, sag = Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * .35; const p = new THREE.Vector3(ax + (bx - ax) * t, ay + (by - ay) * t - sag, az + (bz - az) * t); const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.04, 10, 8), emis('#000', 0)); b.position.copy(p); scene.add(b); bulbs.push(b); if (i % 3 === 1) { const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc070', 0, 3.5, 2); l.position.copy(p); scene.add(l); bulbLights.push(l); } } };
strand(-1.9, 1.8, -.5, -2.3, 2.5, 3.0, 12); strand(1.9, 1.8, -.5, 2.3, 2.5, 3.0, 12); strand(-2.3, 2.5, 3.0, 2.3, 2.5, 3.0, 12); strand(-1.9, 1.8, -.5, 1.9, 1.8, -.5, 10);
const lanterns = [[-1.8, 1.9], [1.3, .1], [-.9, 2.0]].map(([x, z]) => { const g = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.08, .1, .25, 6), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#fff', emissive: '#ffb45a', emissiveIntensity: 0, transparent: true, opacity: .9 })); g.position.set(x, .3, z); scene.add(g); const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb45a', 0, 2.5, 2); l.position.set(x, .4, z); scene.add(l); return { g, l }; });
box(.2, .15, .15, M.black, 2.9, .05, 3.2);
const pathL = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe6c4', 0, 12, 22 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.2); pathL.position.set(3.0, .5, 3.25); pathL.target.position.set(3.9, 0, 6.8); scene.add(pathL, pathL.target);
const pathLens = box(.01, .1, .1, emis('#000', 0), 3.1, .07, 3.22, { cast: false });
// dust in the air for the IP65 close-up
const dustM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#f3e2c4', transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false });
const dust = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.01, 6, 4), dustM, 900); const dd = new THREE.Object3D();
for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) { dd.position.set(2.6 + (R() - .5) * 3.4, .03 + R() * 3.8, 1.6 + (R() - .5) * 4.4); dd.updateMatrix(); dust.setMatrixAt(i, dd.matrix); } scene.add(dust);
const moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#8fa3d0', .25); moon.position.set(-6, 10, 6); scene.add(moon);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#6f84b8', '#2a2018', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.6, 2.0, 8.0], tgt: [.2, 1.0, .5], fov: 58 },       // the camp
  s: { pos: [1.5, 1.35, 4.6], tgt: [-.4, .5, .6], fov: 58 },         // the seating close
  p: { pos: [-2.6, 2.6, 11.5], tgt: [4.4, .3, 4.4], fov: 50 },       // the path to the cars
  d: { pos: [1.4, 3.1, 2.4], tgt: [2.35, 3.9, -.15], fov: 48 },    // the fixture up close, dust in the air
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · flood (harsh white floodlight) · warm:<0..1> (string lights + lanterns) · path (aimed floodlight to the cars) · dust · ip (the pole head re-lit as a warm IP65 floodlight) · all (= warm+path)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'warm+path').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const ip = T.ip ?? 0, fl = T.flood ?? 0, wa = T.warm ?? 0, pa = T.path ?? 0, du = T.dust ?? 0;
  flood.color.set(ip ? '#ffd2a0' : '#f4f7ff'); flood.intensity = ip ? 28 * ip : 75 * fl; floodLens.material = ip ? emis('#ffd2a0', 6) : fl ? emis('#f4f8ff', 8) : emis('#000', 0);
  bulbs.forEach((b, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (wa * 1.1 - i / bulbs.length) / .2)); b.material = k ? emis('#ffc070', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  bulbLights.forEach((l, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (wa * 1.1 - i / bulbLights.length) / .2)); l.intensity = 3.0 * k; });
  lanterns.forEach(({ g, l }) => { g.material.emissiveIntensity = 2.5 * Math.min(1, wa); l.intensity = 1.5 * Math.min(1, wa); });
  pathL.intensity = 40 * pa; pathLens.material = pa ? emis('#ffe6c4', 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  dustM.opacity = .45 * du;
  hemi.intensity = .06 + .04 * fl; scene.environmentIntensity = .03;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
// ===== Home coffee corner: a far ceiling light leaves the machine and cups in shadow vs a hidden LED strip under the shelf + a small warm pendant =====
// back wall z=0 (x -1.8..1.8) with tiled backsplash, counter top y .92 (z 0..0.6), open wooden shelf at y 1.55 (z 0..0.28), ceiling 2.8

box(RW * 2 + 1, CH, .1, wallM, -RW - .5, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .6, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW + .5, 0, -.1);
box(RW * 2 + 1.2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .6, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// backsplash: small glossy tiles (zellige look)
const tileTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#d8d2c6'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 32) for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 64) { const c = 200 + R() * 30; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c * .82},${c * .9},${c * .88})`; g.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, 60, 28); } }, [2, 1]);
const tileM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: tileTex, roughness: .15, metalness: .05 });
box(RW * 2, .63, .02, tileM, -RW, .92, 0, { cast: false });
// cabinet + counter
const cabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f3a34', roughness: .5 });
box(RW * 2, .86, .58, cabM, -RW, 0, .02, { r: .01 });
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) box(.02, .3, .02, M.brass, -RW + .45 + i * .9, .55, .61);
box(RW * 2 + .04, .06, .64, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#f2efe9', roughness: .2 }), -RW - .02, .86, 0);
// open shelf with the hidden strip under its front lip
const shelfM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#b08a5e', roughness: .5 });
box(RW * 2 - .2, .05, .28, shelfM, -RW + .1, 1.55, 0); box(RW * 2 - .2, .04, .02, shelfM, -RW + .1, 1.51, .26, { cast: false });

// on the shelf: cups, jars, a small plant
const cupM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ea', roughness: .3 });
const jarM = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#d9e2e6', roughness: .05, transmission: .7, transparent: true, opacity: .6 });
const beansM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3b2416', roughness: .8 });
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .035, .08, 20), cupM); c.position.set(-1.3 + i * .12, 1.64, .12); c.castShadow = true; scene.add(c); }
for (const x of [.1, .3]) { const j = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .18, 24), jarM); j.position.set(x, 1.69, .12); scene.add(j); const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.055, .055, .12, 24), beansM); b.position.set(x, 1.66, .12); scene.add(b); }
plant(1.2, .14, .45, .14);
// on the counter: espresso machine, grinder, cups on a tray, a kettle
const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d7dade', metalness: .55, roughness: .3 });
const blackM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c1c1e', roughness: .35 });
box(.42, .38, .36, steel, -.55, .92, .08, { r: .03 }); box(.42, .05, .36, blackM, -.55, 1.3, .08); box(.1, .06, .1, blackM, -.45, 1.08, .42);
box(.04, .1, .04, steel, -.3, 1.18, .42); box(.3, .02, .12, blackM, -.5, .93, .38);
const gh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .045, .06, 24), steel); gh.position.set(-.4, 1.12, .47); scene.add(gh);
box(.16, .025, .03, blackM, -.4, 1.08, .48); // portafilter handle
const gauge = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.035, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ea', roughness: .3 })); gauge.position.set(-.55 + .3, 1.2, .442); scene.add(gauge);
const espCup = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.03, .025, .05, 16), cupM); espCup.position.set(-.4, .975, .47); scene.add(espCup);

box(.34, .02, .2, shelfM, .35, .92, .25); for (const x of [.42, .58]) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .04, .09, 20), cupM); c.position.set(x, .99, .35); c.castShadow = true; scene.add(c); }
const kettle = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07, .09, .18, 24), blackM); kettle.position.set(1.0, 1.01, .2); kettle.castShadow = true; scene.add(kettle);
// a stool in front

const ceilL = new THREE.PointLight('#f2f4ff', 0, 8, 1.6); ceilL.position.set(0, CH - .2, 2.6); ceilL.castShadow = true; scene.add(ceilL);
// AFTER: a small warm pendant over the machine
const pendShade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.13, .16, 32, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c08a4a', metalness: .8, roughness: .3, side: THREE.DoubleSide }));
pendShade.position.set(-.3, 2.05, .45); scene.add(pendShade);
box(.006, CH - 2.13, .006, M.black, -.303, 2.13, .447, { cast: false });
const pendBulb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.035, 16, 12), emis('#000', 0)); pendBulb.position.set(-.3, 1.98, .45); scene.add(pendBulb);
const pendL = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffc98a', 0, 4, 50 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.4); pendL.position.set(-.3, 1.97, .45); pendL.target.position.set(-.3, .9, .3); pendL.castShadow = true; scene.add(pendL, pendL.target);
const pendGlow = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc98a', 0, 2.5, 2); pendGlow.position.set(-.3, 2.0, .5); scene.add(pendGlow);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#f4ecdf', '#2a2018', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.1, 1.5, 3.1], tgt: [-.2, 1.2, .2], fov: 60 },     // the coffee corner
  c: { pos: [.55, 1.4, 1.75], tgt: [-.25, 1.15, .2], fov: 55 },   // close on the machine and the cups
  s: { pos: [-.9, 1.3, 1.25], tgt: [-.6, 1.55, .1], fov: 55 },    // the shelf and the strip under it
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · ceil (far ceiling light) · strip:<0..1> · pend · all (= strip+pend)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'strip+pend').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const ce = T.ceil ?? 0, st = T.strip ?? 0, pe = T.pend ?? 0;
  ceilL.intensity = 6 * ce; ceilLamp.material = ce ? emis('#f2f7ff', 3) : emis('#000', 0);
  stripL.intensity = 24 * st; strip.material = st ? emis('#ffe2b8', 6 * st) : emis('#000', 0);
  pendL.intensity = 11 * pe; pendGlow.intensity = .6 * pe; pendBulb.material = pe ? emis('#ffd9a0', 8) : emis('#000', 0);
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .05 * ce + .03 * Math.max(st, pe); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .04 * ce;

};
```
