## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/cove_hall.html`

```html
// ===== Long hall with a gypsum cove: one LED strip along both sides, showing voltage drop (12V) vs even light (24V) =====
// x: -2..2, floor y=0, raised ceiling 3.2, perimeter drop 2.85; the hall runs from z=2 (camera end) to z=-14
const HW = 2.0, Z0 = 1.6, Z1 = -13.6, CEIL_H = 3.2, DROP_H = 2.85;
marbleTex.repeat.set(4, 16);
const floor = plane(4, 16, M.floor, [0, 0, -6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e8dfd2', roughness: .9 });
box(.1, CEIL_H, 16.4, wallM, -HW - .1, 0, -14.2); box(.1, CEIL_H, 16.4, wallM, HW, 0, -14.2);
box(4.2, CEIL_H, .1, wallM, -2.1, 0, -14.2); box(4.2, CEIL_H, .1, wallM, -2.1, 0, 2.2);
const gyp = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f0ea', roughness: .95 });
box(4, .06, 16.4, gyp, -2, CEIL_H, -14.2, { cast: false });                       // raised ceiling
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {                                                   // perimeter drop with a cove lip
  box(.55, .1, 16.4, gyp, sx < 0 ? -HW : HW - .55, DROP_H - .1, -14.2);
  box(.04, .12, 16.4, gyp, sx < 0 ? -HW + .55 : HW - .59, DROP_H - .02, -14.2);  // lip hiding the strip
}
box(4, .1, .5, gyp, -2, DROP_H - .1, -14.2); box(4, .1, .4, gyp, -2, DROP_H - .1, 1.8);
// far wall feature: fluted wood panel + niche
box(2.2, 2.4, .06, M.fluted, -1.1, .2, -14.1);
// majlis seating along both walls
const seat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#d9c7a6', roughness: .95 });
const back = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8a6a4a', roughness: .95 });
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  box(.7, .38, 11, seat, sx < 0 ? -HW + .02 : HW - .72, 0, -12.5, { r: .05 });
  box(.18, .5, 11, back, sx < 0 ? -HW + .02 : HW - .2, .38, -12.5, { r: .05 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) box(.12, .32, .4, M.goldCush, sx < 0 ? -HW + .2 : HW - .32, .42, -12 + i * 1.2, { r: .05 });
}
plane(2.2, 10, M.rug, [0, .006, -7], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
for (const z of [-4, -8.5]) { const t = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.4, .4, .35, 48), M.darkWood); t.position.set(0, .175, z); t.castShadow = true; scene.add(t); }

// the strip: segments along each cove. The wash on the raised ceiling is an additive gradient band per segment (cheap and
// continuous); a few long rect lights add the bounce into the room. Each segment's brightness follows the voltage profile.
const SEG = 28, SL = (Z0 - Z1) / SEG;
const washTex = canvasTex(64, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(.25, 'rgba(255,255,255,.55)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1], false);
const strip = []; // { m, w, t, sx }
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (let i = 0; i < SEG; i++) {
  const z = Z0 - (i + .5) * SL, x = sx * (HW - .62);
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.03, .012, SL * .995), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(x, DROP_H + .02, z); scene.add(m);
  const wm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: washTex, color: '#000', transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
  const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(SL, 1.25), wm);
  w.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; w.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2 * (sx < 0 ? 1 : -1); w.position.set(x - sx * .55, CEIL_H - .006, z); scene.add(w);
  // the band also climbs the cove's inner face a little
  const v = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(SL, .35), wm); v.position.set(sx * (HW - .55 + .001) - sx * .0, DROP_H + .17, z); v.rotation.y = sx < 0 ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(v);
  strip.push({ m, wm, t: i / (SEG - 1), sx });
}
const bounce = [];
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
  const len = (Z0 - Z1) / 6, z = Z0 - (i + .5) * len, x = sx * (HW - .9);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd6a0', 0, .5, len); l.position.set(x, CEIL_H - .05, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l);
  bounce.push({ l, t: i / 5 });
}
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe9d2', '#3a2c20', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const fillL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf9a', 0, 10, 2); fillL.position.set(0, 1.4, -13); scene.add(fillL);

const VIEWS = {
  h: { pos: [0, 1.45, 1.9], tgt: [0, 2.15, -12], fov: 72 },
  f: { pos: [0, 1.6, -5.2], tgt: [0, 2.55, -13.4], fov: 62 },
  u: { pos: [.5, 1.55, -1.2], tgt: [-1.3, 2.9, -4.2], fov: 62 },
};
const C12 = new THREE.Color('#ffd6a0'), CLOW = new THREE.Color('#ff9a4a');
const CNEW = new THREE.Color('#fff1dc'), CAGED = new THREE.Color('#ffb648');
const DEAD = new Set([5, 6, 13, 19, 20, 21, 34, 41, 42, 50]); // strip segments that died on the aged (cheap) strip
// state: off | 12v | 24v | new (a fresh white strip) | aged (a cheap strip a year later: yellow, dim, dead patches)
window.setState = (st) => {
  const prof = (t) => st === '12v' ? 1 - .93 * Math.pow(t, .95) : st === '24v' || st === 'new' ? 1 - .05 * t : st === 'aged' ? .5 : 0;
  strip.forEach((s, i) => {
    let k = prof(s.t); const col = st === 'new' ? CNEW.clone() : st === 'aged' ? CAGED.clone() : C12.clone();
    if (st === 'aged') { k *= DEAD.has(i) ? 0 : .7 + .3 * Math.sin(i * 2.7) ** 2; col.lerp(new THREE.Color('#ff9a40'), (i * 37 % 10) / 25); }
    if (st === '12v') col.lerp(CLOW, Math.pow(s.t, 1.1));
    s.wm.color.copy(col).multiplyScalar(.85 * k);
    s.m.material = k > 0 ? emis('#' + col.getHexString(), 3 * k + .3) : emis('#000', 0);
  });
  for (const b of bounce) { b.l.intensity = 2.2 * prof(b.t); b.l.color.copy(st === '12v' ? C12.clone().lerp(CLOW, b.t * .7) : st === 'new' ? CNEW : st === 'aged' ? CAGED : C12); }
  fillL.intensity = st === 'off' ? .8 : 1.2;
  hemi.intensity = st === 'off' ? .15 : .12;
  scene.environmentIntensity = .04;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.05;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/curtain.html`

```html
// ===== Living room curtain wall with a gypsum pelmet: curtains in the dark, then a hidden LED strip washing them from the top =====
// curtain wall at z=0 (x -2.7..2.7), floor y=0, ceiling 2.9; pelmet box z 0..0.28 at the top hides the strip; room runs to z=5.5
// detail station at x = PX: the pelmet cut open (gypsum box, aluminium profile + strip, the folds below)
const CH = 2.9, PW = 2.7, PD = .28, PB = 2.62; // pelmet depth and bottom
const woodFloorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#8a6a4c', '50,30,15')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(25,14,6,.35)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(7, 7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, 2.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e4dbcf', roughness: .92 });
box(6, CH, .1, wallM, -3, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, 6, wallM, -PW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, 6, wallM, PW, 0, -.12);
box(6, .1, 6, M.ceil, -3, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
const gyp = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2eee8', roughness: .95 });
box(2 * PW, CH - PB, .05, gyp, -PW, PB, PD - .05, { cast: false }); // pelmet face
box(2 * PW, .04, PD, gyp, -PW, CH - .04, 0, { cast: false });
// curtains: heavy folds the full width, a sheer panel behind
function folds(w, h, amp, n, mat, z, x0) {
  const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h, 160, 1); const p = geo.attributes.position;
  for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { const x = p.getX(i); p.setZ(i, Math.sin((x / w) * n * Math.PI * 2) * amp); }
  geo.computeVertexNormals(); const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat); m.position.set(x0, h / 2 + .02, z); m.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(m); return m;
}
const curtM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#d9cbb6', roughness: .95, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const sheerM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9df', roughness: .9, transparent: true, opacity: .55, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
folds(2 * PW - .1, PB - .04, .03, 26, sheerM, .05, 0);
folds(1.5, PB - .04, .055, 9, curtM, .16, -PW + .8); folds(1.5, PB - .04, .055, 9, curtM, .16, PW - .8);
// the wash: a soft gradient band over each curtain, strongest at the top (additive), plus area lights pointing down
const washTex = canvasTex(64, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(.35, 'rgba(255,255,255,.5)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,.04)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1], false);
const SEG = 18, washes = [];
for (let i = 0; i < SEG; i++) {
  const sw = 2 * PW / SEG, x = -PW + (i + .5) * sw;
  const wm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: washTex, color: '#000', transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(sw * 1.02, PB - .04), wm); m.position.set(x, (PB - .04) / 2 + .02, .24); scene.add(m);
  const strip = box(sw * .98, .008, .02, emis('#000', 0), x - sw * .49, PB + .02, .13, { cast: false });
  washes.push({ wm, strip, u: (i + .5) / SEG });
}
const areas = [-1.8, -.6, .6, 1.8].map((x) => { const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffdcb0', 0, 1.2, .1); l.position.set(x, PB - .02, .2); l.lookAt(x, 0, .5); scene.add(l); return l; });
// room: sofa facing the window, armchair, coffee table, rug, floor lamp, plant; downlights in the ceiling
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8f8577', roughness: .95 });
box(2.4, .4, .9, sofaM, -1.2, .1, 3.6, { r: .06 }); box(2.4, .5, .22, sofaM, -1.2, .48, 4.3, { r: .06 });
[['#c9a46a', -.8], ['#e8e1d4', .1], ['#c9a46a', .7]].forEach(([c, x]) => { const m = box(.44, .38, .13, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x - .22, .48, 4.12, { r: .07 }); m.rotation.x = .25; });
box(.85, .4, .85, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#b7a58b', roughness: .95 }), 1.5, .1, 2.2, { r: .08 });
plane(3.2, 2.2, M.rug, [0, .006, 2.7], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const ct = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.5, .5, .05, 48), M.tableTop); ct.position.set(-.1, .4, 2.7); ct.castShadow = ct.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(ct);
const ctl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .1, .38, 24), M.brass); ctl.position.set(-.1, .19, 2.7); scene.add(ctl);
plant(-2.2, .7, 1.7, .5);
const downs = [[-1.2, 1.6], [1.2, 1.6], [-1.2, 3.6], [1.2, 3.6]].map(([x, z]) => {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe6c4', 0, 7, 32 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.5); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.035, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, CH - .002, z); scene.add(lens);
  return { s, lens };
});
const fillL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 6, 2); fillL.position.set(.5, 1.4, 1.2); scene.add(fillL);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe9d2', '#3a2c20', .04); scene.add(hemi);

// ---- detail station: the pelmet cut open ----
const PX = 30, dk = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0e0c0b', roughness: 1 });
plane(6, 5, dk, [PX, 1.5, -.6], [0, 0, 0]);
box(1.4, .5, .04, gyp, PX - .7, 1.6, .26, { cast: false });  // pelmet face (cut)
box(1.4, .04, .3, gyp, PX - .7, 2.06, -.04, { cast: false }); // pelmet top
const ALU = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c3c8ce', roughness: .28, metalness: 1 });
box(1.3, .012, .05, ALU, PX - .65, 2.04, .08, { parent: G }); box(1.3, .03, .006, ALU, PX - .65, 2.01, .08); box(1.3, .03, .006, ALU, PX - .65, 2.01, .124);
const dStrip = box(1.24, .01, .03, emis('#000', 0), PX - .62, 2.02, .09, { cast: false });
const dDiff = box(1.3, .008, .05, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#fff', emissive: '#fff1dc', emissiveIntensity: 0, transparent: true, opacity: .92 }), PX - .65, 2.0, .08, { cast: false });
folds(1.3, 2.0, .05, 6, curtM, .02, PX);
const dArea = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffdcb0', 0, 1.2, .05); dArea.position.set(PX, 1.99, .1); dArea.lookAt(PX, 0, .2); scene.add(dArea);
const dKey = new THREE.PointLight('#bcc8e0', 0, 5, 2); dKey.position.set(PX + 1.2, 1.6, 1.8); scene.add(dKey);

const VIEWS = {
  q: { pos: [-1.2, 1.45, 3.9], tgt: [.4, 1.5, 0], fov: 64 },        // the curtain wall from the right side of the room (3-questions reel)
  w: { pos: [1.0, 1.55, 3.3], tgt: [-.2, 1.35, 0], fov: 70 },           // the whole curtain wall from the back of the room
  c: { pos: [1.1, 1.2, 1.8], tgt: [-.6, 1.9, 0], fov: 58 },          // closer, looking up the folds to the pelmet
  s: { pos: [-2.0, 1.3, 4.8], tgt: [.6, 1.0, 1.0], fov: 62 },        // the room with the sofa
  d: { pos: [PX + 1.35, 2.05, .85], tgt: [PX + .55, 1.8, .1], fov: 46 }, // detail: pelmet cut open
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off | room (ceiling downlights) · wash:<0..1> (strip lighting up across the width) · dim:<0..1> · det (detail station)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const room = T.room ?? 0, wash = T.wash ?? 0, dim = T.dim ?? 1, det = !!T.det;
  washes.forEach(({ wm, strip, u }) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (wash * 1.15 - u) / .15)) * dim; wm.color.set(new THREE.Color('#ffd9a8').multiplyScalar(.75 * k)); strip.material = k ? emis('#ffe2b8', 4 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  areas.forEach((l) => l.intensity = 5 * Math.min(1, wash) * dim);
  downs.forEach(({ s, lens }) => { s.intensity = 9 * room; lens.material = room ? emis('#ffe6c4', 6) : emis('#000', 0); });
  fillL.intensity = .25 + .9 * room + .5 * Math.min(1, wash) * dim;
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .06 * room;
  dStrip.material = det ? emis('#ffe2b8', 5) : emis('#000', 0); dDiff.material.emissiveIntensity = det ? 1.4 : 0;
  dArea.intensity = det ? 6 : 0; dKey.intensity = det ? .8 : 0;
  scene.environmentIntensity = det ? .06 : .03 + .05 * room;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/diwan.html`

```html
// ===== Kuwaiti diwaniya, layer by layer (satisfying switch-on) =====
// A long room (x -3..3, z -10..0) seen from the back toward the carved entrance door at z = -10.
// Low Arabic seating (Sadu cushions) along both side walls, carpet + brass tray with a dallah in the middle,
// raised gypsum ceiling with a hidden cove, wall-grazing spots over the seating, a tiered crystal chandelier,
// up/down sconces flanking the door.
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · cove:<0..1> (the hidden strip, side by side) · spots:<0..1> (wall grazers, front to back)
//   · chand:<0..1> (chandelier tier by tier) · ent (door sconces) · all = cove+spots+chand+ent · dim:<x>
const DX = 2.4, DZ = 10, DH = 3.4;
scene.background = new THREE.Color('#050404');
marbleTex.repeat.set(3, 5);
plane(DX * 2, DZ, M.floor, [0, 0, -DZ / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e9dfcf', roughness: .9 });
box(DX * 2, DH, .12, wallM, -DX, 0, -DZ - .12);                    // entrance wall
box(.12, DH, DZ, wallM, -DX - .12, 0, -DZ); box(.12, DH, DZ, wallM, DX, 0, -DZ);
box(DX * 2, DH, .12, wallM, -DX, 0, .3);                            // wall behind the camera
// oak dado on the side walls
const dado = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#7a5636', roughness: .5 });
box(.04, .9, DZ, dado, -DX, 0, -DZ, { cast: false }); box(.04, .9, DZ, dado, DX - .04, 0, -DZ, { cast: false });
// ceiling: a drop band all round (h .35) and a raised centre panel; the strip sits on the band's inner lip
const gyp = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3eee6', roughness: .95 });
const IX = 1.55, IZ0 = -9.1, IZ1 = -.9, BAND = DH - .35;
box(DX * 2, .06, DZ, gyp, -DX, DH, -DZ, { cast: false });                                 // raised centre (the true ceiling)
box(DX - IX, .06, DZ, gyp, -DX, BAND, -DZ, { cast: false }); box(DX - IX, .06, DZ, gyp, IX, BAND, -DZ, { cast: false });
box(IX * 2, .06, IZ0 + DZ, gyp, -IX, BAND, -DZ, { cast: false }); box(IX * 2, .06, -IZ1, gyp, -IX, BAND, IZ1, { cast: false });
for (const x of [-IX, IX - .06]) box(.06, .14, IZ1 - IZ0, gyp, x, BAND, IZ0, { cast: false });  // lips
for (const z of [IZ0, IZ1 - .06]) box(IX * 2, .14, .06, gyp, -IX, BAND, z, { cast: false });
const WARM = '#ffd7a6';
const COVE = [];
for (const [x, z, w, d, lx, lz] of [[-IX + .1, IZ0, .03, IZ1 - IZ0, -1, 0], [IX - .13, IZ0, .03, IZ1 - IZ0, 1, 0], [-IX, IZ0 + .1, IX * 2, .03, 0, -1], [-IX, IZ1 - .13, IX * 2, .03, 0, 1]]) {
  const s = box(w, .02, d, emis('#000', 0), x, BAND + .07, z, { cast: false });
  const len = Math.max(w, d), l = new THREE.RectAreaLight(WARM, 0, len, .25);
  l.position.set(x + w / 2, BAND + .1, z + d / 2); l.lookAt(x + w / 2 + lx * 1.5, DH + 1.2, z + d / 2 + lz * 1.5); scene.add(l);
  COVE.push({ s, l });
}
// Sadu pattern for the cushions
const sadu = canvasTex(512, 256, (g, w, h) => {
  g.fillStyle = '#6e1f1b'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  const cols = ['#1a1414', '#e8d9b8', '#b8862f', '#2b3f5a'];
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 32) { g.fillStyle = cols[(y / 32) % 4]; g.fillRect(0, y + 10, w, 8); for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 32) { g.save(); g.translate(x + 16, y + 14); g.rotate(Math.PI / 4); g.fillRect(-5, -5, 10, 10); g.restore(); } }
}, [3, 1]);
const saduM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: sadu, roughness: .9 });
const seatM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8a2a24', roughness: .9 });
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  const x0 = sx < 0 ? -DX + .02 : DX - .82;
  box(.8, .22, DZ - 1.6, seatM, x0, 0, -DZ + .2, { r: .04 });                                   // mattress
  for (let z = -DZ + .4; z < -1.6; z += .75) box(.18, .5, .66, saduM, sx < 0 ? -DX + .04 : DX - .22, .22, z, { r: .05 }); // back cushions
  for (let z = -DZ + 1.2; z < -1.6; z += 2.3) { const p = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.12, .12, .5, 18), saduM); p.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; p.position.set(sx < 0 ? -DX + .62 : DX - .62, .34, z); p.castShadow = true; scene.add(p); }
}
box(DX * 2 - 1.8, .012, DZ - 3.0, M.rug, -DX + .9, .002, -DZ + 1.6, { cast: false });
// brass tray, dallah, cups
const tray = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.55, .55, .03, 40), M.brass); tray.position.set(0, .3, -6.4); scene.add(tray);
const stand = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.3, .4, .28, 6), dado); stand.position.set(0, .14, -6.4); scene.add(stand);
const dallah = new THREE.Group(); dallah.position.set(-.15, .315, -6.45); scene.add(dallah);
{ const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .11, .22, 24), M.brass); b.position.y = .11; dallah.add(b); const n = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.035, .06, .1, 20), M.brass); n.position.y = .27; dallah.add(n); const lid = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.05, .08, 20), M.brass); lid.position.y = .36; dallah.add(lid); const sp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.01, .018, .2, 8), M.brass); sp.position.set(.11, .22, 0); sp.rotation.z = -.9; dallah.add(sp); }
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.03, .022, .045, 16), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4efe6', roughness: .3 })); c.position.set(.15 + (i % 3) * .09, .34, -6.25 - Math.floor(i / 3) * .1); scene.add(c); }
// carved double door on the entrance wall + stone frame
const doorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: canvasTex(256, 512, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#5b3a22', '35,20,10')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(20,10,4,.6)'; g.lineWidth = 5; for (let y = 40; y < h - 20; y += 110) { g.strokeRect(24, y, w - 48, 90); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(w / 2, y + 10); g.lineTo(w - 40, y + 45); g.lineTo(w / 2, y + 80); g.lineTo(40, y + 45); g.closePath(); g.stroke(); } }, [1, 1]), roughness: .5 });
const frameM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cbb894', roughness: .8 });
box(2.1, 2.75, .1, frameM, -1.05, 0, -DZ - .02);
box(.86, 2.45, .06, doorM, -.88, 0, -DZ + .07); box(.86, 2.45, .06, doorM, .02, 0, -DZ + .07);
for (const x of [-.12, .12]) { const h = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.05, .01, 8, 20), M.brass); h.position.set(x, 1.2, -DZ + .14); scene.add(h); }
// small high windows on the side walls (night)
const nightTex = canvasTex(128, 128, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#0d1630'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.6)'; for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) g.fillRect(Math.random() * w, Math.random() * h, 2, 2); }, [1, 1]);
const winM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: nightTex, emissive: '#ffffff', emissiveMap: nightTex, emissiveIntensity: .3 });
for (const z of [-3.2, -6.8]) { plane(.9, .6, winM, [-DX + .005, 2.4, z], [0, Math.PI / 2, 0]); plane(.9, .6, winM, [DX - .005, 2.4, z], [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0]); }
// wall grazers: recessed in the drop band near the side walls, aimed down the wall over the seating
const SP = [];
for (let z = -1.6; z > -9.2; z -= 1.5) for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  const x = sx * (DX - .35);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .045, .02, 18), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, BAND - .005, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 6, 24 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.4); s.position.set(x, BAND - .04, z); s.target.position.set(sx * DX, .6, z); scene.add(s, s.target);
  SP.push({ ring, s, z });
}
// chandelier: three tiers of crystal drops around a brass hoop, hanging from the centre of the raised panel
const CZ = -5.2, CY0 = DH - 1.15;
const rod = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.012, .012, 1.0, 8), M.brass); rod.position.set(0, DH - .5, CZ); scene.add(rod);
const TIERS = [];
[[.62, CY0 + .3], [.45, CY0], [.26, CY0 - .28]].forEach(([r, y], ti) => {
  const hoop = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(r, .015, 8, 48), M.brass); hoop.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; hoop.position.set(0, y, CZ); scene.add(hoop);
  const drops = [];
  const n = Math.round(r * 40);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = i / n * Math.PI * 2; const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(.028, 0), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9f0f5', roughness: .05, metalness: .2, emissive: '#000' })); d.scale.y = 1.8; d.position.set(Math.cos(a) * r, y - .08, CZ + Math.sin(a) * r); scene.add(d); drops.push(d); }
  const bulbs = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const a = i / 6 * Math.PI * 2; const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.03, 10, 8), emis('#000', 0)); b.position.set(Math.cos(a) * r, y + .04, CZ + Math.sin(a) * r); scene.add(b); bulbs.push(b); }
  const pt = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd09a', 0, 7, 1.6); pt.position.set(0, y, CZ); scene.add(pt);
  TIERS.push({ drops, bulbs, pt });
});
const chDown = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd09a', 0, 6, 55 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.2); chDown.position.set(0, CY0 - .4, CZ); chDown.target.position.set(0, 0, CZ); scene.add(chDown, chDown.target);
// door sconces: brass up/down cylinders either side of the door
const SC = [-1.45, 1.45].map((x) => {
  const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .32, 20), M.brass); b.position.set(x, 1.9, -DZ + .1); scene.add(b);
  const capU = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.05, 16), emis('#000', 0)); capU.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; capU.position.set(x, 2.065, -DZ + .1); scene.add(capU);
  const capD = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.05, 16), emis('#000', 0)); capD.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; capD.position.set(x, 1.735, -DZ + .1); scene.add(capD);
  const up = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 3, 16 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.5); up.position.set(x, 2.08, -DZ + .14); up.target.position.set(x, DH, -DZ + .14);
  const dn = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 3, 16 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.5); dn.position.set(x, 1.72, -DZ + .14); dn.target.position.set(x, 0, -DZ + .14);
  scene.add(up, up.target, dn, dn.target); return { capU, capD, up, dn };
});
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#8ea2cc', '#1a120c', .1); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 2.15, -.3], tgt: [0, 1.05, -10], fov: 70 },          // the whole diwaniya toward the door
  c: { pos: [.9, 1.35, -2.6], tgt: [0, 2.35, -5.2], fov: 52 },        // the chandelier
  s: { pos: [-.6, 1.2, -4.2], tgt: [2.4, .65, -5.6], fov: 56 },      // the seating along the wall and the door corner
};
const cl = (v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'cove+spots+chand+ent').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const cv = T.cove ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0, ch = T.chand ?? 0, en = T.ent ?? 0, dm = T.dim ?? 1;
  COVE.forEach(({ s, l }, i) => { const k = cl((cv * 1.15 - [0, 0, .5, .5][i]) / .5); l.intensity = 14 * k * dm; s.material = k > .02 ? emis(WARM, 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  SP.forEach(({ ring, s, z }) => { const k = cl((sp * 1.2 - (-z - 1.6) / 9) / .25); s.intensity = 28 * k * dm; ring.material = k > .02 ? emis('#fff1dc', 3 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  TIERS.forEach(({ drops, bulbs, pt }, i) => { const k = cl((ch * 1.2 - i / 3) / .4); pt.intensity = 2.2 * k * dm; bulbs.forEach((b) => { b.material = k > .02 ? emis('#ffe2b8', 9 * k) : emis('#000', 0); }); drops.forEach((d) => { d.material.emissive.set(k > .02 ? '#ffcf90' : '#000'); d.material.emissiveIntensity = .9 * k; }); });
  chDown.intensity = 10 * cl(ch * 1.2 - .6) * dm;
  SC.forEach(({ capU, capD, up, dn }) => { up.intensity = dn.intensity = 10 * en * dm; capU.material = capD.material = en ? emis(WARM, 8) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const lit = Math.min(1, cv * .3 + sp * .35 + ch * .25 + en * .1);
  hemi.intensity = .1 + .1 * lit; scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .1 * lit;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.05;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/entrance.html`

```html
// ===== Villa entrance at night: a dark entrance vs up/down wall lights each side of the door, brick lights along the steps, a halo-lit house number; IP65 under rain =====
// facade face z=0 (x -4.5..4.5, height 4.2), stone cladding frame around the door (x -1.7..1.7), landing y .51 (z 0..1.6), two steps down to the paving (z 1.6..2.3)
// cheek walls each side of the steps (x ±1.6..1.9) carry the brick lights; house number on the plaster right of the cladding
const paveTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#8e8579'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 128) for (let x = (y / 128 % 2) * 64; x < w; x += 128) { const c = 125 + R() * 30; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c},${c * .95},${c * .88})`; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 3, 122, 122); } }, [6, 6]);
plane(14, 12, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: paveTex, roughness: .75 }), [0, 0, 4], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
for (const x of [-4.2, 4.2]) plane(3.6, 6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f4a2a', roughness: 1 }), [x, .004, 4.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const facadeM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e8dccb', roughness: .9 });
box(9, 4.2, .3, facadeM, -4.5, 0, -.3);
box(9.2, .25, .5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d8ccb9', roughness: .8 }), -4.6, 4.2, -.35); // coping
const cladTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#b8a487'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 48) for (let x = -(y / 48 % 2) * 60; x < w; x += 120 + R() * 50) { const c = 150 + R() * 55; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c},${c * .9},${c * .75})`; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 3, 115 + R() * 40, 43); for (let k = 0; k < 30; k++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${R() * .14})`; g.fillRect(x + R() * 120, y + R() * 44, 3 + R() * 10, 2 + R() * 5); } } }, [1.5, 2]);
const cladM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cladTex, roughness: .95, bumpMap: cladTex, bumpScale: 3 });
box(3.4, 3.69, .07, cladM, -1.7, .51, 0);
// the door: tall double timber door with a slim handle
const doorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, color: '#7a5434', roughness: .55 });
box(.89, 2.7, .08, doorM, -.9, .51, .07); box(.89, 2.7, .08, doorM, .01, .51, .07);
for (const x of [-.06, .06]) box(.02, .7, .03, M.brass, x - .01, 1.5, .15);
// landing, steps, cheek walls
const stepM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#d9d2c6', roughness: .4 });
box(3.2, .51, 1.6, stepM, -1.6, 0, 0); box(3.2, .34, .35, stepM, -1.6, 0, 1.6); box(3.2, .17, .35, stepM, -1.6, 0, 1.95);
for (const x of [-1.9, 1.6]) box(.3, 1.0, 2.3, facadeM, x, 0, 0);
// windows each side (dark glass, a faint warm room behind), planters
const glassM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0b0d10', emissive: '#ffcf9a', emissiveIntensity: .05, roughness: .1, metalness: .3 });
for (const x of [-3.9, 2.7]) { box(1.2, 1.6, .04, glassM, x, 1.2, .0, { cast: false }); box(1.3, .06, .12, M.black, x - .05, 1.17, .0); }
for (const x of [-2.6, 2.6]) { box(.6, .6, .6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a3532', roughness: .8 }), x - .3, 0, 1.2); plant(x, 1.5, 1.7, .6); }
// up/down wall lights each side of the door (3000 K), each = a body + an up spot + a down spot
const sconceM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2724', metalness: .6, roughness: .4 });
const sconces = [-1.35, 1.35].map((x) => {
  box(.12, .32, .12, sconceM, x - .06, 2.26, .07);
  const lensU = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.035, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lensU.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; lensU.position.set(x, 2.585, .13); scene.add(lensU);
  const lensD = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.035, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lensD.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lensD.position.set(x, 2.255, .13); scene.add(lensD);
  const up = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 4, 16 * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.2); up.position.set(x, 2.6, .16); up.target.position.set(x, 4.2, .05); scene.add(up, up.target);
  const dn = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 4, 18 * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.2); dn.position.set(x, 2.24, .16); dn.target.position.set(x, .51, .1); dn.castShadow = true; scene.add(dn, dn.target);
  return { up, dn, lensU, lensD };
});
const porch = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 5, 2); porch.position.set(0, 2.2, 1.0); scene.add(porch);
// brick lights in the cheek walls, one per tread each side (bottom step first)
const bricks = [];
[[.17, 2.125], [.34, 1.775], [.51, 1.3]].forEach(([top, z], i) => {
  for (const [x, ry, dx] of [[-1.6, Math.PI / 2, 1], [1.6, -Math.PI / 2, -1]]) {
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.14, .05), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(x + .002 * dx, top + .2, z); m.rotation.y = ry; scene.add(m);
    const l = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 3, 1.05, .8, 1.3); l.position.set(x + .04 * dx, top + .2, z); l.target.position.set(x + 1.2 * dx, top, z); scene.add(l, l.target);
    bricks.push({ m, l, u: i / 3 });
  }
});
// house number, halo-lit: bronze digits on a stand-off over a glow
const digits = (fill, blur) => canvasTex(512, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); g.filter = blur ? 'blur(14px)' : 'none'; g.fillStyle = fill; g.font = '900 200px Arial'; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.textBaseline = 'middle'; g.fillText('17', w / 2, h / 2 + 8); }, [1, 1], true);
const numM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: digits('#5a4632', false), transparent: true, metalness: .6, roughness: .35 });
const num = plane(.56, .28, numM, [2.02, 2.35, .05], [0, 0, 0]);
const haloM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: digits('#ffffff', true), color: '#000', transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
plane(.7, .35, haloM, [2.02, 2.35, .012], [0, 0, 0]);
const numL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 1.2, 2); numL.position.set(2.02, 2.35, .06); scene.add(numL);
// rain for the IP65 close-up
const rainM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#dfe8ff', transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false });
const rain = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.003, .18, .003), rainM, 500); const dm = new THREE.Object3D();
for (let i = 0; i < 500; i++) { dm.position.set(.4 + R() * 2.4, .6 + R() * 3.4, .15 + R() * 1.6); dm.rotation.z = .08; dm.updateMatrix(); rain.setMatrixAt(i, dm.matrix); } scene.add(rain);
// night sky behind and above
const skyTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#05070f'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#1f2640'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 140; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${R() * .6})`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h * .6, 1.5, 1.5); } }, [1, 1]);
plane(40, 20, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: skyTex, toneMapped: false }), [0, 9, -8], [0, 0, 0]);
const moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#9fb4e0', 0); moon.position.set(-4, 8, 6); scene.add(moon);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#7f93c0', '#2a2018', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [.1, 1.6, 8.6], tgt: [.1, 1.65, 0], fov: 58 },        // the whole entrance from the driveway
  s: { pos: [1.3, .75, 4.3], tgt: [-.3, .45, 1.3], fov: 60 },    // low on the steps and the brick lights
  n: { pos: [1.4, 2.0, 2.9], tgt: [1.65, 2.35, 0], fov: 52 },    // the house number and the right wall light
  e: { pos: [2.2, 2.2, 1.9], tgt: [1.35, 2.45, 0], fov: 44 },    // the wall light close, in the rain
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · sconce:<0..1> · steps:<0..1> (bottom step first) · num · rain · all (= sconce+steps+num)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'sconce+steps+num').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const sc = T.sconce ?? 0, st = T.steps ?? 0, nm = T.num ?? 0, rn = T.rain ?? 0;
  sconces.forEach(({ up, dn, lensU, lensD }) => { up.intensity = 22 * sc; dn.intensity = 26 * sc; lensU.material = lensD.material = sc ? emis('#ffe2b8', 6 * sc) : emis('#000', 0); });
  porch.intensity = 1.2 * sc;
  bricks.forEach(({ m, l, u }) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (st * 1.1 - u) / .3)); l.intensity = 6 * k; m.material = k ? emis('#ffe2b8', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  haloM.color.set(new THREE.Color('#ffcf8f').multiplyScalar(1.4 * nm)); numL.intensity = .6 * nm;
  rainM.opacity = .2 * rn; moon.intensity = .5;
  hemi.intensity = .1; scene.environmentIntensity = .04;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/facade.html`

```html
// ===== Villa facade at night, seen from the street =====
// Two-storey villa (front at z = 0, x -4.5..4.5): stone-clad centre bay with the entrance and a canopy, plaster wings,
// dark windows; front yard (path + lawn + two palms) behind a boundary wall with a gate at z = 8; dark neighbour houses.
// state tokens joined by '+':
//   bad            one cold white floodlight on the gate pillar (glare), nothing else
//   wash:<0..1>    in-ground up-lights grazing the facade, from the centre outward
//   palm:<0..1>    up-lights at the palms   path:<0..1> bollards along the path, gate to door
//   rain           rain streaks + drops (for the IP65 detail view d)   win  warm interior glow in two windows
//   all = wash+palm+path+win     dim:<x>
const WARM = '#ffd2a0';
scene.background = new THREE.Color('#060a18');
const ground = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2f26', roughness: 1 });
plane(80, 60, ground, [0, -.01, 10], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const street = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c1d20', roughness: .85 });
plane(80, 8, street, [0, 0, 13], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const plasterV = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e4dccd', roughness: .92 });
const stoneV = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: canvasTex(512, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#b9a88c'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 48) for (let x = (y / 48) % 2 ? -60 : 0; x < w; x += 120) { const c = 150 + Math.random() * 40; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c},${c - 14},${c - 34})`; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 3, 114, 42); } }, [1, 2]), roughness: .85, bumpScale: 2 });
// villa body
box(9, 7, 6, plasterV, -4.5, 0, -6);
box(2.6, 7.4, .3, stoneV, -1.3, 0, 0);                                  // stone centre bay (proud of the wall)
box(9.6, .35, .5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a3632', roughness: .7 }), -4.8, 7, -.2);   // roof cornice
const canopy = box(3.4, .25, 1.8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9e2d6', roughness: .8 }), -1.7, 3.1, .3);
const doorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#4a3220', roughness: .5 });
box(1.6, 2.8, .1, doorM, -.8, 0, .3);
const glassD = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0d1220', roughness: .08, metalness: .6 });
const WIN = [];
for (const [x, y, w, h] of [[-3.9, 1.0, 2.0, 1.8], [1.9, 1.0, 2.0, 1.8], [-3.9, 4.3, 2.0, 1.6], [1.9, 4.3, 2.0, 1.6], [-.7, 4.1, 1.4, 2.2]]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), glassD.clone()); m.position.set(x + w / 2, y + h / 2, x > -1 && x < 1 ? .31 : .01); scene.add(m); WIN.push(m);
  box(w + .12, .08, .12, M.black, x - .06, y - .08, 0); box(w + .12, .08, .12, M.black, x - .06, y + h, 0);
}
// boundary wall + gate + pillars
const bwM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d6cbb6', roughness: .95 });
box(5.0, 1.8, .3, bwM, -7.0, 0, 8); box(5.0, 1.8, .3, bwM, 2.0, 0, 8);
box(.3, 1.8, 8, bwM, -7.0, 0, 0); box(.3, 1.8, 8, bwM, 6.7, 0, 0);
for (const x of [-2.0, 1.4]) box(.6, 2.3, .6, stoneV, x, 0, 7.85);
const gateM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1d1c1b', roughness: .4, metalness: .7 });
for (let x = -1.35; x < 1.4; x += .14) box(.04, 1.7, .04, gateM, x, .05, 8.1);
box(2.8, .08, .06, gateM, -1.4, 1.72, 8.09); box(2.8, .08, .06, gateM, -1.4, .3, 8.09);
// yard: path pavers, two palms, shrubs
const paveM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b5aa96', roughness: .85 });
for (let z = .8; z < 7.8; z += .7) box(1.6, .03, .55, paveM, -.8, 0, z, { cast: false });
const trunkM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6b5236', roughness: .95 }), frondM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a4020', roughness: .8, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
function palm(x, z, h) {
  const t = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.14, .22, h, 12), trunkM); t.position.set(x, h / 2, z); t.castShadow = true; scene.add(t);
  for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { const a = i / 14 * Math.PI * 2; const f = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.3, 2.2), frondM); f.position.set(x + Math.cos(a) * .95, h - .35, z + Math.sin(a) * .95); f.lookAt(x + Math.cos(a) * 2.4, h - 1.3, z + Math.sin(a) * 2.4); f.castShadow = true; scene.add(f); }
}
palm(-3.4, 4.2, 5.6); palm(3.3, 4.6, 5.1);
for (const [x, z] of [[-5.6, 2.2], [5.4, 2.0], [-2.1, 2.9], [2.0, 2.9]]) { const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.5, 14, 10), M.leaf); b.position.set(x, .3, z); b.scale.y = .65; scene.add(b); }
// neighbours (dark), street poles far
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { box(8, 6.5, 6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#4a4842', roughness: .95 }), sx * 9.5 - 4, 0, -6); box(8, 1.8, .3, bwM, sx * 9.5 - 4, 0, 8); }
// lights
const FX = () => emis('#000', 0);
const WASH = [-4.0, -2.6, -1.0, 1.0, 2.6, 4.0].map((x) => { const f = box(.16, .03, .16, FX(), x - .08, .0, .06, { cast: false }); const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 9, 13 * Math.PI / 180, .35, 1.2); s.position.set(x, .05, .17); s.target.position.set(x, 7, -.02); scene.add(s, s.target); return { f, s, x }; });
const PALM = [];
for (const [px, pz, h] of [[-3.4, 4.2, 5.6], [3.3, 4.6, 5.1]]) for (const dx of [-.55, .55]) { const f = box(.14, .03, .14, FX(), px + dx - .07, 0, pz + .5 - .07, { cast: false }); const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 9, 18 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.2); s.position.set(px + dx, .05, pz + .5); s.target.position.set(px, h, pz); scene.add(s, s.target); PALM.push({ f, s }); }
const PATH = [];
for (const z of [7.0, 5.2, 3.4, 1.6]) for (const x of [-1.15, 1.15]) { box(.1, .5, .1, M.black, x - .05, 0, z - .05); const head = box(.12, .06, .12, FX(), x - .06, .5, z - .06, { cast: false }); const l = new THREE.PointLight(WARM, 0, 2.4, 2); l.position.set(x, .42, z); scene.add(l); PATH.push({ head, l, z }); }
const canSpot = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 6, 40 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.3); canSpot.position.set(0, 3.08, 1.0); canSpot.target.position.set(0, 0, 1.0); scene.add(canSpot, canSpot.target);
const canRing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .02, 20), FX()); canRing.position.set(0, 3.09, 1.0); scene.add(canRing);
// the bad one: a cold floodlight on the left gate pillar, aimed out and down
const flood = box(.32, .22, .12, M.black, -1.86, 2.3, 8.05); const floodFace = box(.28, .18, .01, FX(), -1.84, 2.32, 8.17, { cast: false });
const floodS = new THREE.SpotLight('#e6f0ff', 0, 30, 50 * Math.PI / 180, .2, 1.0); floodS.position.set(-1.7, 2.4, 8.25); floodS.target.position.set(.5, 0, 14); scene.add(floodS, floodS.target);
const floodBack = new THREE.SpotLight('#e6f0ff', 0, 12, 60 * Math.PI / 180, .3, 1.2); floodBack.position.set(-1.7, 2.6, 8.0); floodBack.target.position.set(-1, 0, 4); scene.add(floodBack, floodBack.target);
const glare = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.35, 16, 12), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#eaf2ff', transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false })); glare.position.set(-1.72, 2.41, 8.3); scene.add(glare);
// rain around the detail fixture (x -2.6) and drops on its lens
const RAIN = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(RAIN);
const rainM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#bcd0ff', transparent: true, opacity: .32, depthWrite: false });
for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) { const r = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.003, .1 + Math.random() * .08, .003), rainM); r.position.set(-2.6 + (Math.random() - .5) * 1.8, Math.random() * 2.2, .1 + Math.random() * .8); r.rotation.z = .12; RAIN.add(r); }
for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.008 + Math.random() * .008, 8, 6), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#dfe8ff', roughness: .02, metalness: .1, transparent: true, opacity: .8 })); d.position.set(-2.6 + (Math.random() - .5) * .12, .035, .14 + (Math.random() - .5) * .12); RAIN.add(d); }
const moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#7f93c8', .12); moon.position.set(-10, 18, 20); scene.add(moon);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#36446e', '#0d0b08', .1); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 3.4, 17.5], tgt: [0, 2.9, 0], fov: 56 },          // from the street outside the gate
  g: { pos: [1.6, 1.5, 10.8], tgt: [0, 1.6, 3.5], fov: 62 },        // over the gate: path, palms, door
  d: { pos: [-2.2, .45, 1.15], tgt: [-2.6, .8, 0], fov: 66 },     // an in-ground up-light in the rain, wash rising above
  s: { pos: [-11, 2.6, 34], tgt: [0, 3.0, 0], fov: 42 },            // far down the street: our villa among dark neighbours
};
const cl = (v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'wash+palm+path+win').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const bad = T.bad ?? 0, wa = T.wash ?? 0, pa = T.palm ?? 0, pt = T.path ?? 0, dm = T.dim ?? 1;
  WASH.forEach(({ f, s, x }) => { const k = cl((wa * 1.2 - Math.abs(x) / 5) / .25); s.intensity = 120 * k * dm; f.material = k > .02 ? emis(WARM, 5 * k) : FX(); });
  PALM.forEach(({ f, s }, i) => { const k = cl((pa * 1.2 - i / 5) / .3); s.intensity = 32 * k * dm; f.material = k > .02 ? emis(WARM, 5 * k) : FX(); });
  PATH.forEach(({ head, l, z }) => { const k = cl((pt * 1.2 - (7 - z) / 7) / .25); l.intensity = 1.6 * k * dm; head.material = k > .02 ? emis(WARM, 6 * k) : FX(); });
  const anyOn = Math.max(wa, pa, pt);
  canSpot.intensity = 14 * cl(pt * 1.5 - .5) * dm; canRing.material = pt > .6 ? emis(WARM, 4) : FX();
  floodS.intensity = 160 * bad; floodBack.intensity = 30 * bad; floodFace.material = bad ? emis('#f2f7ff', 14) : FX(); glare.material.opacity = .55 * bad;
  WIN.forEach((m, i) => { const on = T.win && (i === 1 || i === 4); m.material.emissive = new THREE.Color(on ? '#ffbe7a' : '#000'); m.material.emissiveIntensity = on ? .55 : 0; });
  RAIN.visible = !!T.rain;
  hemi.intensity = .1 + .06 * anyOn; scene.environmentIntensity = .03;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/floatbed.html`

```html
// ===== Floating bed: a plain bedroom under one ceiling lamp vs a hidden warm strip under a raised bed (the bed "floats") and a motion-sensor night glow toward the door =====
// back wall z=0 (x -2.6..2.6) with a padded headboard; bed against it (x -1..1, z 0.05..2.15) on a recessed plinth; door on the right wall near the front; ceiling 2.8
const CH = 2.8, RW = 2.6, RD = 5.0;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#a58460', '70,45,22')(g, w, h); }, [3, 3]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e6ddd0', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.1);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// headboard wall panel (fabric) + art
const hbM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8f7d6a', roughness: .95 });
box(2.6, 1.3, .08, hbM, -1.3, .45, .0, { r: .03 });
box(1.2, .7, .03, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c8b79c', roughness: .9 }), -.6, 1.95, .02);
// the bed: a recessed dark plinth (set back 25 cm) carrying the mattress frame, so the frame overhangs and "floats"
box(1.5, .14, 1.6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#141210', roughness: .8 }), -.75, 0, .3);
const bedM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#6d5240', roughness: .5 });
box(2.0, .16, 2.1, bedM, -1.0, .14, .08, { r: .02 });
box(1.9, .22, 2.0, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3efe8', roughness: .9 }), -.95, .3, .12, { r: .06 });
box(1.96, .08, 1.3, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#d8c7ae', roughness: .95 }), -.98, .5, .85, { r: .04 });
for (const x of [-.55, .15]) box(.55, .14, .32, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#f6f2ea', roughness: .9 }), x, .52, .2, { r: .06 });
// nightstands with lamps (off), rug, wardrobe, door
for (const x of [-1.6, 1.15]) box(.45, .5, .4, bedM, x, 0, .1, { r: .02 });
plane(2.8, 1.6, M.rug, [0, .006, 2.9], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(.6, 2.3, 1.6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9df', roughness: .7 }), -RW, 0, 2.6, { r: .02 });
box(.05, 2.1, .9, M.darkWood, RW - .06, 0, 3.6);
// BEFORE: one ceiling lamp
const ceilLamp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.25, .25, .05, 40), emis('#000', 0)); ceilLamp.position.set(0, CH - .03, 2.4); scene.add(ceilLamp);
const ceilL = new THREE.PointLight('#f2f4ff', 0, 10, 1.6); ceilL.position.set(0, CH - .2, 2.4); ceilL.castShadow = true; scene.add(ceilL);
// AFTER: hidden warm strip under the overhang (front + both sides) washing the floor
const stripM = [];
for (const [w, d, x, z] of [[1.9, .01, -.95, 2.12], [.01, 1.95, -.97, .12], [.01, 1.95, .97, .12]]) { const m = box(w, .008, d, emis('#000', 0), x, .13, z, { cast: false }); stripM.push(m); }
const glows = [[0, 2.25, 1.9, .3], [-1.15, 1.1, .3, 1.9], [1.15, 1.1, .3, 1.9]].map(([x, z, w, d]) => { const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffbe7a', 0, w, d); l.position.set(x, .12, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l); return l; });
// motion-sensor night path: two small floor-level lights from the bed toward the door
const sensor = box(.05, .03, .03, M.black, .9, .12, 2.13, { cast: false });
const path = [[1.6, 2.6], [2.3, 3.3]].map(([x, z]) => { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.12, .05), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(RW - .002, .25, z); m.rotation.y = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(m); const l = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffb15c', 0, 2.5, 1.1, .9, 1.3); l.position.set(RW - .05, .26, z); l.target.position.set(RW - .9, 0, z); scene.add(l, l.target); return { m, l }; });
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#d8dcf0', '#2a2018', .03); scene.add(hemi);
// soft warm room fill for the 'after' states (the owner found the night scenes too dark)
const fillL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd2a0', 0, 9, 1.6); fillL.position.set(.3, 2.2, 2.4); scene.add(fillL);
const lampL = [-1.38, 1.38].map((x) => { const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc98a', 0, 3, 2); l.position.set(x, .85, .3); scene.add(l); return l; });
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.7, 1.55, 4.6], tgt: [-.3, .6, .9], fov: 62 },      // the bedroom
  f: { pos: [1.6, .35, 3.2], tgt: [-.2, .2, 1.4], fov: 55 },       // low: the gap under the bed and the glow
  n: { pos: [-1.6, 1.4, 4.4], tgt: [1.2, .4, 2.4], fov: 64 },      // night: from the bed side toward the door
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · ceil (one ceiling lamp) · glow:<0..1> (under-bed strip) · path (motion-sensor lights to the door)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const ce = T.ceil ?? 0, gl = T.glow ?? 0, pa = T.path ?? 0;
  ceilL.intensity = 12 * ce; ceilLamp.material = ce ? emis('#f2f7ff', 3) : emis('#000', 0);
  glows.forEach((l) => l.intensity = 26 * gl); fillL.intensity = 2.2 * Math.min(1, gl); lampL.forEach((l) => l.intensity = 1.2 * Math.min(1, gl)); stripM.forEach((m) => m.material = gl ? emis('#ffd09a', 6 * gl) : emis('#000', 0));
  path.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 6 * pa; m.material = pa ? emis('#ffb15c', 6) : emis('#000', 0); });
  hemi.intensity = .03 + .1 * ce + .1 * gl; scene.environmentIntensity = .02 + .08 * ce + .06 * gl;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
    const geo = new THREE.ShapeGeometry(shape, 12); const p = geo.attributes.position;

    geo.computeVertexNormals();

  }
  return g;
}

});

}

for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const z = -1.5 - i * 2.1; const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd6a2', 0, .05, .9); l.position.set(-4.55, .08, z); l.lookAt(-4.9, 3, z); scene.add(l); L.wall.push([l, 55]); const m = box(.06, .04, .5, emis('#000', 0), -4.6, 0, z - .25, { cast: false }); Lm.wall.push(m); }

}
for (const [x, z] of [[-1.3, -11.8], [1.3, -11.8], [-1.3, -13.0], [1.3, -13.0], [0, -12.35]]) { const p = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc98a', 0, 6, 1.6); p.position.set(x, 2.45, z); scene.add(p); L.seat.push([p, 2.6]); }
// table lantern
const lantern = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb867', 0, 3, 2); lantern.position.set(0, .7, -12.25); scene.add(lantern); L.seat.push([lantern, 1.2]);
const doorL = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffcf96', 0, 1.6, 2.4); doorL.position.set(4.3, 1.2, -2.8); doorL.lookAt(0, 1.2, -2.8); scene.add(doorL);

const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#fff3dd', 0); sun.position.set(6, 9, 4); sun.target.position.set(0, 0, -7); sun.castShadow = true; sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); Object.assign(sun.shadow.camera, { left: -10, right: 10, top: 10, bottom: -10 }); scene.add(sun, sun.target);
const moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#8ea2d6', 0); moon.position.set(-5, 8, 3); moon.target.position.set(0, 0, -7); scene.add(moon, moon.target);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#cfe0ff', '#4a5a3a', .2); scene.add(hemi);

const VIEWS = {
  g: { pos: [1.2, 1.75, 1.6], tgt: [-.3, .9, -9.5], fov: 68 },
  p: { pos: [1.1, .9, -1.2], tgt: [-.2, .2, -5.5], fov: 60 },
  t: { pos: [-.6, 1.2, -2.6], tgt: [-2.9, 3.0, -6.4], fov: 64 },
  w: { pos: [.6, 1.5, -8.6], tgt: [-2.6, 1.0, -14.2], fov: 64 },
  s: { pos: [.4, 1.35, -8.9], tgt: [-.2, 1.1, -12.9], fov: 64 },
};
// state: day | dusk | night[:layers]  layers = path+tree+wall+seat
window.setState = (st) => {
  const [mode, ls] = st.split(':'); const on = (ls ?? '').split('+').filter(Boolean);
  skyM.map = SKY[mode === 'day' ? 'day' : mode === 'dusk' ? 'dusk' : 'night']; skyM.needsUpdate = true;
  const day = mode === 'day', dusk = mode === 'dusk';
  sun.intensity = day ? 2.6 : dusk ? .7 : 0; sun.color.set(dusk ? '#ffb070' : '#fff3dd'); sun.position.set(dusk ? -8 : 6, dusk ? 2 : 9, dusk ? -10 : 4);
  moon.intensity = day || dusk ? 0 : .22; hemi.intensity = day ? .9 : dusk ? .35 : .05; hemi.color.set(day ? '#cfe0ff' : dusk ? '#ffc79a' : '#5a6ea8');
  scene.environmentIntensity = day ? .5 : dusk ? .15 : .02;
  for (const k of ['path', 'tree', 'wall', 'seat']) { const o = on.includes(k); L[k].forEach(([l, i]) => l.intensity = o ? i : 0); Lm[k].forEach(m => { m.material = o ? emis(k === 'seat' ? '#ffd08a' : '#fff1dc', k === 'seat' ? 4 : 3) : emis('#000', 0); }); }
  doorL.intensity = mode === 'night' ? 3 : 0; doorGlow.emissiveIntensity = mode === 'night' ? 1.2 : 0;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = day ? .8 : dusk ? .9 : 1.0;
};
```

```html
// ===== Home gym: one yellow bulb in the middle (sleepy, shadows on the mirror, glare when lying on the bench) vs recessed 4000 K anti-glare linear rows + side lights at the mirror =====
// back wall z=0 (x -2.6..2.6), mirror on the left wall x=-2.6, floor y=0 (rubber tiles), ceiling 2.9; bench in the middle along z, dumbbell rack on the back wall, treadmill on the right
// no faces: the person at the mirror is a plain faceless figure
const CH = 2.9, RW = 2.6, RD = 5.4;
const rubberTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#1c1d1f'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 14000; i++) { const c = 30 + R() * 30; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c},${c + 4},.5)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2, 2); } g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.7)'; g.lineWidth = 4; for (let p = 0; p <= w; p += 256) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(p, 0); g.lineTo(p, h); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, p); g.lineTo(w, p); g.stroke(); } }, [3, 3]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: rubberTex, roughness: .9 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const sideM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d9dbdd', roughness: .9 });
const backM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#55595e', roughness: .85 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, backM, -RW, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD + .1, sideM, -RW - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD + .1, sideM, RW, 0, -.1);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD + .1, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// mirror wall on the left (x = -RW), framed in black
const cubeRT = new THREE.WebGLCubeRenderTarget(512); const cubeCam = new THREE.CubeCamera(.05, 20, cubeRT); cubeCam.position.set(-RW + 1.6, 1.4, 2.5); scene.add(cubeCam);
const mirrorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#dfe4e8', metalness: 1, roughness: .02, envMap: cubeRT.texture, envMapIntensity: 1 });
const mirror = box(.02, 2.0, 3.0, mirrorM, -RW + .005, .35, 1.0, { cast: false });
box(.03, 2.06, .03, M.black, -RW, .32, .97); box(.03, 2.06, .03, M.black, -RW, .32, 4.0);
// dumbbell rack on the back wall
const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2b2e', metalness: .7, roughness: .35 });
const chrome = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9ccd0', metalness: 1, roughness: .2 });
box(2.2, .06, .45, steel, -1.1, .55, .1); box(2.2, .06, .45, steel, -1.1, .2, .1);
for (const x of [-1.1, 1.04]) box(.06, .62, .45, steel, x, 0, .1);
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (const [y, s] of [[.62, .8], [.27, 1.1]]) {
  const x = -.9 + i * .36, r = .055 * s * (1 + i * .08);
  for (const dz of [.16, .42]) { const pl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r, r, .05, 24), steel); pl.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; pl.position.set(x, y + r, dz); pl.castShadow = true; scene.add(pl); }
  const bar = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.015, .015, .22, 12), chrome); bar.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; bar.position.set(x, y + r, .29); scene.add(bar);
}
// adjustable bench in the middle (along z), kettlebells, a treadmill on the right, a yoga mat
const padM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#141416', roughness: .6 });
box(.32, .1, 1.25, padM, -.06, .42, 1.8, { r: .03 }); box(.06, .42, .06, steel, .07, 0, 1.9); box(.06, .42, .06, steel, .07, 0, 2.9); box(.3, .05, 1.2, steel, -.05, .05, 1.82);
for (const [x, z, s] of [[.8, 1.2, 1], [1.0, 1.25, .8], [-.9, 3.3, 1.1]]) { const kb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.11 * s, 24, 16), steel); kb.position.set(x, .11 * s, z); kb.castShadow = true; scene.add(kb); const h = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.07 * s, .015, 10, 24, Math.PI), steel); h.position.set(x, .2 * s, z); scene.add(h); }
box(.8, .18, 1.9, steel, 1.55, 0, 1.4, { r: .03 }); box(.7, .02, 1.5, padM, 1.6, .18, 1.6);
box(.06, 1.1, .06, steel, 1.6, .18, 1.42); box(.06, 1.1, .06, steel, 2.24, .18, 1.42); box(.7, .25, .12, steel, 1.58, 1.25, 1.36, { r: .02 });
plane(.6, 1.7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f6f73', roughness: .9 }), [-1.8, .005, 2.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
// the faceless figure at the mirror (plain shapes)
const figM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8a8f96', roughness: .7 });
const fig = (x, z) => {
  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(.17, .55, 8, 16), figM); body.position.set(x, 1.12, z); body.castShadow = true; scene.add(body);
  const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.12, 24, 16), figM); head.position.set(x, 1.67, z); head.castShadow = true; scene.add(head);
  for (const dz of [-.09, .09]) { const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(.07, .6, 6, 12), figM); leg.position.set(x, .38, z + dz); leg.castShadow = true; scene.add(leg); }
  for (const dz of [-.23, .23]) { const arm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(.05, .45, 6, 12), figM); arm.position.set(x + .05, 1.15, z + dz); arm.rotation.z = .15; arm.castShadow = true; scene.add(arm); }
};
fig(-1.75, 2.4);
// BEFORE: one bare yellow bulb on a cord in the middle
const bulbM = emis('#000', 0); const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.06, 24, 16), bulbM); bulb.position.set(0, 2.55, 2.2); scene.add(bulb);
box(.006, CH - 2.6, .006, M.black, -.003, 2.6, 2.197, { cast: false });
const bulbL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb35c', 0, 9, 1.6); bulbL.position.set(0, 2.5, 2.2); bulbL.castShadow = true; bulbL.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(bulbL);
// AFTER: three recessed 4000 K linear rows with deep anti-glare channels (lens set back, black baffles)
const rows = [-1.3, 0, 1.3].map((x, i) => {
  box(.1, .06, 3.6, M.black, x - .05, CH - .06, .9, { cast: false });
  const lens = box(.05, .005, 3.5, emis('#000', 0), x - .025, CH - .045, .95, { cast: false });
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff6ec', 0, .06, 3.5); l.position.set(x, CH - .05, 2.7); l.lookAt(x, 0, 2.7); scene.add(l);
  return { lens, l, u: i / 3 };
});
// mirror side lights: two vertical bars beside the mirror, lighting the figure from the front-side
const mBars = [.88, 4.12].map((z) => {
  const m = box(.02, 1.7, .05, emis('#000', 0), -RW + .01, .5, z - .025, { cast: false });
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff6ec', 0, .05, 1.7); l.position.set(-RW + .04, 1.35, z); l.lookAt(0, 1.35, z); scene.add(l);
  return { m, l };
});
const fill = new THREE.PointLight('#fff6ec', 0, 8, 2); fill.position.set(0, 2.0, 3.5); scene.add(fill);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#dfe6ee', '#202022', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.9, 1.65, 5.2], tgt: [-.6, 1.15, 1.4], fov: 64 },   // the gym from the door corner
  b: { pos: [.11, .8, 3.1], tgt: [.25, 2.9, 1.0], fov: 78 },     // lying on the bench, looking up
  m: { pos: [-.1, 1.55, 3.6], tgt: [-2.2, 1.25, 1.9], fov: 56 },   // the figure at the mirror
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · bulb (one yellow bulb) · linear:<0..1> (rows on one by one) · mirror · all (= linear+mirror)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'linear+mirror').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const bu = T.bulb ?? 0, ln = T.linear ?? 0, mr = T.mirror ?? 0;
  bulbL.intensity = 5 * bu; bulb.material = bu ? emis('#ffc070', 12) : emis('#000', 0);
  rows.forEach(({ lens, l, u }) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (ln * 1.1 - u) / .3)); l.intensity = 70 * k; lens.material = k ? emis('#fff4e8', 4 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  mBars.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 22 * mr; m.material = mr ? emis('#fff4e8', 5) : emis('#000', 0); });
  fill.intensity = 1.5 * Math.min(1, ln);
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .05 * Math.min(1, ln); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .06 * Math.min(1, ln);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
  mirror.visible = false; cubeCam.update(renderer, scene); mirror.visible = true;
};
```

```html
  box(.03, .14, iz1 - iz0, boardRaw, ix0 - .03, DY + .08, iz0, { cast: false }), box(.03, .14, iz1 - iz0, boardRaw, ix1, DY + .08, iz0, { cast: false })];
const lipGlowM = emis('#ff8a2a', 0);
const lipGlow = [box(ix1 - ix0, .012, .035, lipGlowM, ix0, DY + .075, iz0 - .035, { cast: false }), box(ix1 - ix0, .012, .035, lipGlowM, ix0, DY + .075, iz1, { cast: false }),
  box(.035, .012, iz1 - iz0, lipGlowM, ix0 - .035, DY + .075, iz0, { cast: false }), box(.035, .012, iz1 - iz0, lipGlowM, ix1, DY + .075, iz0, { cast: false })];

for (let x = ix0; x <= ix1 + .01; x += 1.14) box(.04, .04, iz1 - iz0, steel, x - .02, GC + .04, iz0, { parent: grid });
for (let x = ix0 + .3; x < ix1; x += 1.4) for (let z = iz0 + .4; z < iz1; z += 1.4) box(.008, SLAB - GC - .06, .008, steel, x, GC + .06, z, { parent: grid });
const tiles = [], OPEN = new Set(['1,1', '2,1', '2,2', '3,4', '0,5', '1,5', '4,3', '3,6']);

function cable(pts, parent = cables, m = cableM, r = .013) { const c = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts.map(p => new THREE.Vector3(...p))); const t = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(c, 60, r, 8), m); t.castShadow = true; parent.add(t); return t; }
cable([[-1.5, 3.1, 1.2], [-.5, 3.18, 2.0], [.6, 3.12, 2.6], [1.8, 3.1, 1.6]]);
cable([[1.2, 3.12, 4.2], [0, 3.2, 3.6], [-1.6, 3.1, 4.4]]);

const markM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: markTex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false });

  [-RW + .33, DY, 2.2, -RW, 1.6, 2.2, 22], [-RW + .33, DY, 3.2, -RW, 1.6, 3.2, 22], [0, GC, 2.3, 0, .45, 2.3, 30]];

  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .012, 28), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ec', roughness: .4 })); ring.position.set(x, y - .006, z); scene.add(ring);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.03, 24), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, y - .013, z); scene.add(lens);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 8, a * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.4); s.position.set(x, y - .03, z); s.target.position.set(tx, ty, tz); s.castShadow = true; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(s, s.target);
  const from = new THREE.Vector3(x, y - .02, z), to = new THREE.Vector3(tx, ty, tz), len = from.distanceTo(to);
  const cone = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(Math.tan(a * Math.PI / 360) * len, len, 32, 1, true), beamM); cone.position.copy(from).lerp(to, .5); cone.lookAt(to); cone.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); scene.add(cone);
  return { mark, ring, lens, s, cone };
});
// ---- decision 2: the cove channel (aluminium profile on the lip + strip), highlighted in orange while on site ----
const chanM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d0d4d8', roughness: .3, metalness: .9, emissive: '#000' });
const chan = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(chan);
const strips = [], coveL = [];
const run = (x, z, w, d, lx, lz) => {

  const m = box(w > d ? w : .012, .006, w > d ? .012 : d, emis('#000', 0), x + (w > d ? 0 : d > w ? .01 : 0), DY + .1, z + (w > d ? .01 : 0), { cast: false, parent: chan }); strips.push(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd3a0', 0, Math.max(w, d), .08); l.position.set(x + w / 2, DY + .12, z + d / 2); l.lookAt(lx, SLAB, lz); scene.add(l); coveL.push(l);
};
run(ix0, iz0 - .1, ix1 - ix0, .04, 0, iz0 + .9); run(ix0, iz1 + .06, ix1 - ix0, .04, 0, iz1 - .9);

  const e = new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.EdgesGeometry(g.geometry), edgeM); e.position.copy(g.position); ghost.add(e);

}

piece(2.4, .38, .45, -1.2, .15, .03, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#5b3e2a', roughness: .45 }));   // floating unit

piece(3.2, .42, .95, -1.6, 0, 3.7, sofaM, { r: .05 }); piece(3.2, .5, .22, -1.6, .4, 4.43, sofaM, { r: .06 });
piece(1.3, .4, .7, -.65, 0, 2.0, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#d8d0c4', roughness: .15 }), { r: .02 });   // coffee table

box(.04, .96, 1.46, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1714', roughness: .5 }), -RW, 1.22, 1.97, { parent: real });
plane(1.38, .88, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: artTex, roughness: .8 }), [-RW + .045, 1.7, 2.7], [0, Math.PI / 2, 0], real);

for (let k = 1; k < 6; k++) box(.48, .03, .05, ladM, 1.66, k * .34, 1.66 + k * .05, { parent: props });
for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) box(.5, .14, .35, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cfc3a8', roughness: 1 }), -2.6 + k * .05, k * .14, 4.8, { parent: props, r: .04 });
box(.25, .2, .12, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2c230', roughness: .5 }), -2.72, 1.55, 4.6, { parent: props }); box(.03, 1.55, .03, M.black, -2.615, 0, 4.65, { parent: props });
const work = new THREE.SpotLight('#f4f6ff', 0, 14, 70 * Math.PI / 180, .9, 1); work.position.set(-2.6, 1.7, 4.6); work.target.position.set(1, 2.6, 1.5);
const fill = new THREE.PointLight('#f2f4ff', 0, 9, 1.4); fill.position.set(1.4, 2.2, 3.6); scene.add(fill); scene.add(work, work.target);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#eef2ff', '#4a4038', .4); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.6, 6.0], tgt: [0, 1.85, .5], fov: 70 },          // the whole hall from the entrance, ceiling in view
  u: { pos: [.6, 1.45, 4.6], tgt: [-.2, 2.9, 1.2], fov: 68 },       // looking up at the marked spots and their aim beams
  c: { pos: [-4.7, 3.15, 4.0], tgt: [-2.9, 2.8, 2.4], fov: 50 },   // section: left wall hidden (token cut), the band, channel and lip from outside     // the cove channel along the left band
  d: { pos: [1.1, 1.55, 3.4], tgt: [3.5, 1.8, 5.0], fov: 60 },      // the dimmer box by the door and its conduit
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  site (raw, ceiling open) · ghost:<0..1> planned furniture · marks:<0..1> spot X marks + aim beams · channel:<0..1> cove profile built + highlighted
//   · dimmer:<0..1> back box + conduit highlighted · done (finished room, ceiling closed) · lights:<0..1> (cove + spots) · dim:<0..1> dimmer level (default 1)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const done = !!T.done, gh = T.ghost ?? 0, mk = T.marks ?? 0, ch = T.channel ?? (done ? 1 : 0), dm = T.dimmer ?? (done ? 1 : 0), li = (T.lights ?? 0) * (T.dim ?? 1);
  for (const { t, open } of tiles) { t.visible = done || !open; t.material = done ? boardDone : boardRaw; }
  band.forEach((b) => b.material = done ? boardDone : boardRaw); lip.forEach((b) => { b.visible = ch > 0; b.material = done ? boardDone : boardRaw; });
  walls.forEach((w) => w.material = done ? wallDone : wallRaw); walls[1].visible = !T.cut; floorDone.visible = done; floorRaw.visible = !done;
  grid.visible = cables.visible = !done; props.visible = !done; real.visible = done;
  ghostM.opacity = .16 * gh; edgeM.opacity = .9 * gh; ghost.visible = gh > 0 && !done;
  spots.forEach(({ mark, ring, lens, s, cone }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, mk * 1.3 - i * .05)); mark.visible = !done && k > 0; mark.scale.setScalar(.3 + .7 * k); ring.visible = done; cone.visible = !done && k > 0; beamM.opacity = .07 * mk; s.intensity = 40 * li; lens.material = li ? emis('#ffd9a8', 5 * li) : emis('#000', 0); });
  chan.visible = ch > 0; chan.children.forEach((c, i) => c.scale.set(1, 1, 1)); chanM.emissive.set(!done && ch ? '#ff8a2a' : '#000'); chanM.emissiveIntensity = !done ? 1.6 * ch : 0;
  strips.forEach((m) => m.material = li ? emis('#ffd3a0', 6 * li) : (!done && ch ? emis('#ff9a3a', 1.2 * ch) : emis('#000', 0)));
  coveL.forEach((l) => l.intensity = 40 * li); lipGlowM.emissiveIntensity = !done ? 2 * ch : 0; lipGlow.forEach((g) => g.visible = !done && ch > 0);
  dimG.visible = dm > 0 && !done; boxM.emissive.set(!done && dm ? '#2f7dff' : '#000'); boxM.emissiveIntensity = 1.2 * dm; conduitM.emissive.set(!done && dm ? '#ff8a2a' : '#000'); conduitM.emissiveIntensity = 1.4 * dm;
  plate.visible = knob.visible = done;
  work.intensity = done ? 0 : 22; fill.intensity = done ? 0 : 3;
  hemi.intensity = done ? .05 + .55 * li : .4; scene.environmentIntensity = done ? .03 + .25 * li : .16;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = done ? 1.0 : .9;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/gypsum.html`

```html
// ===== Gypsum ceiling before closing: open grid with drivers, cables, the strip channel and marked spot positions → closed, tested and finished =====
// room x -3..3, z -4..2, floor y=0, concrete slab 3.3, gypsum ceiling 2.9 with a perimeter cove on the left and right walls
const RX = 3, Z0 = -4, Z1 = 2, SLAB = 3.3, GC = 2.9, COVE = .5;
const concreteTex = canvasTex(512, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#8d8a85'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { const c = 110 + R() * 60; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c - 3},${c - 8},.25)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2, 2); } }, [4, 4]);
const concrete = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: concreteTex, roughness: .95 });
const floorRaw = plane(6, 6, concrete, [0, 0, -1], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
marbleTex.repeat.set(3, 3);
const floorDone = plane(6, 6, M.floor, [0, .001, -1], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallRaw = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#b9b2a8', roughness: .95 });
const wallDone = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#efe7da', roughness: .9 });
const walls = [box(.1, SLAB, 6.2, wallRaw, -RX - .1, 0, Z0 - .1), box(.1, SLAB, 6.2, wallRaw, RX, 0, Z0 - .1), box(6.2, SLAB, .1, wallRaw, -RX - .1, 0, Z0 - .1)];
box(6.2, .15, 6.2, concrete, -RX - .1, SLAB, Z0 - .1, { cast: false });

// ---- metal grid + hangers (only visible while the ceiling is open) ----
const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b8bcc0', roughness: .35, metalness: .9 });
const grid = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(grid);
for (let z = Z0 + .3; z < Z1; z += .6) box(2 * (RX - COVE), .03, .05, steel, -RX + COVE, GC + .015, z - .025, { parent: grid });
for (let x = -RX + COVE + .2; x < RX - COVE; x += 1.2) box(.05, .04, Z1 - Z0, steel, x - .025, GC + .045, Z0, { parent: grid });
for (let x = -2; x <= 2; x += 1.2) for (let z = Z0 + .6; z < Z1; z += 1.2) box(.008, SLAB - GC - .08, .008, steel, x, GC + .08, z, { parent: grid });

// ---- gypsum tiles (1.2 × .6); some are left out while the ceiling is open ----
const boardTex = canvasTex(512, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#e9e6e0'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(160,150,140,.35)'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, 6); g.fillRect(0, 0, 6, h); for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { g.fillStyle = 'rgba(200,190,175,.25)'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(R() * w, R() * h, 4 + R() * 6, 0, 7); g.fill(); } });
const boardRaw = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: boardTex, roughness: .95, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const boardDone = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f6f2ec', roughness: .95, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const tiles = [];
const OPEN = new Set(['1,2', '2,2', '1,3', '2,3', '3,4', '3,5', '2,5', '0,7', '1,7', '2,8', '3,8', '1,9']);
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) for (let j = 0; j < 10; j++) {
  const x = -RX + COVE + i * 1.25, z = Z0 + j * .6;
  const t = plane(1.25, .6, boardRaw, [x + .625, GC, z + .3], [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
  tiles.push({ t, open: OPEN.has(`${i},${j}`) });
}
// perimeter drop + cove lip on both side walls, with the aluminium channel for the strip
const covePieces = [];
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  covePieces.push(box(COVE, .1, Z1 - Z0, boardRaw, sx < 0 ? -RX : RX - COVE, GC - .25, Z0));
  covePieces.push(box(.03, .12, Z1 - Z0, boardRaw, sx < 0 ? -RX + COVE - .03 : RX - COVE, GC - .15, Z0));
}
const channelM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d0d4d8', roughness: .3, metalness: .9 });
const strips = [];
const washTex = canvasTex(256, 64, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(.3, 'rgba(255,255,255,.5)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1], false);
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  box(.03, .015, Z1 - Z0, channelM, sx * (RX - COVE + .09) - .015, GC - .15, Z0);
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.012, .006, Z1 - Z0 - .02), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(sx * (RX - COVE + .09), GC - .135, (Z0 + Z1) / 2); scene.add(m);
  // the wash on the ceiling: an additive gradient band from the cove edge toward the middle
  const wm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: washTex, color: '#000', transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
  const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.3, Z1 - Z0), wm); w.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; if (sx > 0) w.rotation.z = Math.PI;
  w.position.set(sx * (RX - COVE - .65), GC - .006, (Z0 + Z1) / 2); scene.add(w);
  const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 4, 2); l.position.set(sx * (RX - COVE - .4), GC - .3, (Z0 + Z1) / 2); scene.add(l);
  strips.push({ m, wm, l });
}
// raised middle above the cove: the wash falls on the tiles near the walls
// ---- drivers on top of the grid, a maintenance hatch under each one ----
const driverM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2e33', roughness: .4, metalness: .5 });
const DRIVERS = [[1.15, -1.0], [-1.25, .3]];
const hatches = [];
for (const [x, z] of DRIVERS) {
  box(.24, .045, .07, driverM, x - .12, GC + .1, z - .035);
  box(.06, .03, .04, emis('#5fd38a', 1.2), x + .05, GC + .145, z - .02, { cast: false });
  const frame = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1ede6', roughness: .6 });
  for (const [w, d, ox, oz] of [[.6, .02, 0, -.3], [.6, .02, 0, .3], [.02, .6, -.3, 0], [.02, .6, .3, 0]]) box(w, .02, d, frame, x + ox - w / 2, GC - .02, z + oz - d / 2, { cast: false });
  const door = new THREE.Group(); door.position.set(x, GC - .005, z - .3); scene.add(door);
  box(.58, .012, .58, frame, -.29, -.006, .01, { parent: door });
  hatches.push(door);
}
const underHatch = (t) => DRIVERS.some(([x, z]) => Math.abs(t.position.x - x) < .63 && Math.abs(t.position.z - z) < .31);
// ---- cables: conduits from the drivers to the cove and the spots ----
const cableM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e8742a', roughness: .6 });
const cables = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(cables);
function cable(pts) { const c = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts.map(p => new THREE.Vector3(...p))); const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(c, 60, .012, 8), cableM); m.castShadow = true; cables.add(m); }
cable([[1.15, 3.05, -1.0], [1.8, 3.1, -1.4], [2.5, 3.05, -1.6], [2.42, 2.9, -2.4]]);
cable([[-1.25, 3.05, .3], [-1.9, 3.1, .1], [-2.42, 2.9, -.5]]);
cable([[1.15, 3.05, -1.0], [.6, 3.12, -1.8], [0, 3.1, -2.6], [-.6, 3.05, -3.2]]);
cable([[-1.25, 3.05, .3], [-.6, 3.12, .9], [.3, 3.08, 1.2]]);
// ---- spots: positions marked with an X while open, fixtures when finished ----
const SPOTS = []; for (const x of [-1.2, 0, 1.2]) for (const z of [-2.8, -1.6, -.4, .8]) SPOTS.push([x, z]);
const markTex = canvasTex(128, 128, (g, w, h) => { g.strokeStyle = '#d0281e'; g.lineWidth = 12; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(24, 24); g.lineTo(104, 104); g.moveTo(104, 24); g.lineTo(24, 104); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.arc(64, 64, 46, 0, 7); g.lineWidth = 6; g.stroke(); }, [1, 1]);
const markM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: markTex, transparent: true, depthWrite: false });
const spots = [];
for (const [x, z] of SPOTS) {
  const mark = plane(.32, .32, markM, [x, GC - .003, z], [Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .012, 28), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ec', roughness: .4 })); ring.position.set(x, GC - .006, z); scene.add(ring);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.028, 24), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, GC - .013, z); scene.add(lens);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 8, 18 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.6); s.position.set(x, GC - .03, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target);
  spots.push({ mark, ring, lens, s });
}
// ---- construction props: ladder + work lamp ----
const props = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(props);
const ladM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9ccd0', roughness: .4, metalness: .8 });
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const r = box(.04, 2.0, .04, ladM, -1.8 + sx * .22, 0, -3.1, { parent: props }); r.rotation.x = .18; const r2 = box(.04, 2.0, .04, ladM, -1.8 + sx * .22, 0, -2.5, { parent: props }); r2.rotation.x = -.18; }
for (let k = 1; k < 6; k++) box(.48, .03, .05, ladM, -2.04, k * .32, -3.05 + k * .05, { parent: props });
const work = new THREE.PointLight('#f2f4ff', 0, 12, 1.2); work.position.set(-1.5, 1.6, 1.2); scene.add(work);
box(.25, .2, .12, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2c230', roughness: .5 }), -1.62, 1.5, 1.14, { parent: props });
box(.03, 1.5, .03, M.black, -1.5, 0, 1.2, { parent: props });
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#eef2ff', '#4a4038', .3); scene.add(hemi);

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.35, 1.2], tgt: [0, 3.4, -1.6], fov: 76 },     // the whole ceiling from below
  h: { pos: [1.55, 1.75, .1], tgt: [1.15, 3.0, -1.0], fov: 55 },   // the driver above its hatch
  c: { pos: [-1.3, 1.65, 1.6], tgt: [-2.6, 2.85, -1.6], fov: 60 }, // the cove channel on the left wall
  k: { pos: [.3, 1.6, -.5], tgt: [0, 3.2, -2.3], fov: 60 },       // cables through the open tiles
  m: { pos: [-.3, 1.6, .3], tgt: [0, 2.9, -1.6], fov: 55 },       // a spot position marked on the board
};
// state "build:open|closed|done|marks:0|1|hatch:0|1|lights:0|1"   presets: open | closed | test | done
window.setState = (st) => {
  const P = { open: 'build:open|marks:1|hatch:0|lights:0', closed: 'build:closed|marks:0|hatch:0|lights:0', test: 'build:closed|marks:0|hatch:0|lights:1', done: 'build:done|marks:0|hatch:0|lights:1', hatch: 'build:closed|marks:0|hatch:1|lights:0' };
  const o = Object.fromEntries((P[st] ?? st).split('|').map(p => p.split(':')));
  const open = o.build === 'open', done = o.build === 'done', on = o.lights === '1';
  for (const { t, open: gap } of tiles) { t.visible = !(open && gap) && !(o.hatch === '1' && underHatch(t)); t.material = done ? boardDone : boardRaw; }
  covePieces.forEach(p => p.material = done ? boardDone : boardRaw);
  walls.forEach(w => w.material = done ? wallDone : wallRaw);
  floorDone.visible = done; props.visible = !done && o.props !== '0';
  hatches.forEach(d => d.rotation.x = o.hatch === '1' ? 1.3 : 0);
  for (const s of spots) { s.mark.visible = o.marks === '1'; s.ring.visible = !open; s.s.intensity = on ? 7 : 0; s.lens.material = on ? emis('#ffd9a8', 5) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const s of strips) { s.l.intensity = on ? 1.5 : 0; s.wm.color.set(on ? '#c9a070' : '#000'); s.m.material = on ? emis('#ffd6a0', 6) : emis('#000', 0); }
  work.intensity = on ? 0 : 6; hemi.intensity = on ? .1 : .35;
  scene.environmentIntensity = on ? .08 : .25;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = on ? 1.0 : .85;
};
```
