## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/kidsroom.html`

```html
// ===== Kids' bedroom at night: harsh cold ceiling light vs a warm low night light near the floor + a dimmable reading light for the story =====
// back wall z=0 (x -2.4..2.4), floor y=0, ceiling 2.8; bed against the back wall on the left, door to the bathroom on the right wall
// no faces: the child is a shape under the duvet; the teddy and toys are simple forms
const CH = 2.8, RW = 2.4;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#b08a62', '70,45,22')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(60,35,15,.3)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(6, 6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .5 }), [0, 0, 2.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#cfe0e6'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.55)'; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const x = R() * w, y = R() * h * .6, s = 6 + R() * 10; g.beginPath(); for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) { const a = k * Math.PI / 5, r = k % 2 ? s * .45 : s; g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(a) * r, y + Math.sin(a) * r); } g.fill(); } }, [2, 1]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: wallTex, roughness: .9 });
box(4.8, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 5.6, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 5.6, wallM, RW, 0, -.1);
box(4.8, .1, 5.6, M.ceil, -RW, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// window with a curtain on the left wall (moonlight)
const winM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#000', emissive: '#7f9bd6', emissiveIntensity: 0 });
box(.02, 1.2, 1.0, winM, -RW + .01, 1.0, 2.0, { cast: false });
const moon = new THREE.SpotLight('#9fb8ff', 0, 8, .6, .7, 1.2); moon.position.set(-RW - .4, 1.8, 2.5); moon.target.position.set(.5, 0, 2.2); scene.add(moon, moon.target);
// bed: wooden frame, mattress, duvet with the sleeping child (a soft shape), pillow, teddy
const woodM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#e8d8bf', roughness: .5 });
box(1.1, .3, 2.0, woodM, -2.25, 0, .05, { r: .03 }); box(1.1, .9, .06, woodM, -2.25, .3, .02, { r: .03 });
box(1.0, .18, 1.9, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ea', roughness: .9 }), -2.2, .3, .1, { r: .05 });
const duvetM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#f2c38b', roughness: .95 });
box(1.04, .1, 1.35, duvetM, -2.22, .46, .65, { r: .05 });
const lump = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(.16, .55, 8, 16), duvetM); lump.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lump.scale.set(1.1, 1, .6); lump.position.set(-1.72, .58, .9); lump.castShadow = true; scene.add(lump);
box(.5, .12, .3, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#ffffff', roughness: .9 }), -1.95, .48, .15, { r: .05 });
const bearM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#a8754a', roughness: 1 });
const bear = (x, y, z, s) => { [[0, 0, 0, .14], [0, .17, 0, .1], [-.07, .26, 0, .04], [.07, .26, 0, .04], [-.12, .03, .03, .05], [.12, .03, .03, .05]].forEach(([dx, dy, dz, r]) => { const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(r * s, 20, 14), bearM); m.position.set(x + dx * s, y + dy * s, z + dz * s); m.castShadow = true; scene.add(m); }); };
bear(-1.35, .62, .35, 1);
// nightstand with a small wall reading light above, a rug, toy blocks, a toy box, the door to the bathroom
box(.45, .5, .4, woodM, -1.05, 0, .05, { r: .02 });
const wlArm = box(.04, .04, .22, M.brass, -.85, 1.25, .0); const wlHead = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.07, .12, 24, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4efe6', roughness: .6, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); wlHead.position.set(-.83, 1.22, .24); wlHead.rotation.x = Math.PI; scene.add(wlHead);
const readL = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf8a', 0, 3.5, 34 * Math.PI / 180, .7, 1.4); readL.position.set(-.83, 1.18, .24); readL.target.position.set(-1.55, .45, .55); readL.castShadow = true; scene.add(readL, readL.target);
const readLens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.05, 20), emis('#000', 0)); readLens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; readLens.position.set(-.83, 1.165, .24); scene.add(readLens);
plane(2.2, 1.6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#9cc7c9', roughness: 1 }), [-.3, .006, 2.0], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
[['#e85d4a', 0], ['#f2b134', .13], ['#4aa3c9', .26], ['#7cb342', .065]].forEach(([c, dx], i) => box(.1, .1, .1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: .6 }), .1 + dx, i === 3 ? .1 : 0, 1.9 + (i % 2) * .05));
box(.7, .45, .45, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2efe8', roughness: .7 }), 1.4, 0, .05, { r: .03 });
box(.05, 2.1, .9, M.darkWood, RW - .06, 0, 2.6); // the door, right wall
// lights: the harsh cold ceiling light, and the warm low night lights (step lights in the wall near the floor + under-bed glow)
const ceilLamp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.28, .28, .05, 40), emis('#000', 0)); ceilLamp.position.set(0, CH - .03, 2.2); scene.add(ceilLamp);
const ceilL = new THREE.PointLight('#e6f0ff', 0, 10, 1.6); ceilL.position.set(0, CH - .2, 2.2); ceilL.castShadow = true; scene.add(ceilL);
const steps = [];
for (const [x, z, ry] of [[RW - .02, 1.4, -Math.PI / 2], [RW - .02, 3.8, -Math.PI / 2], [-.4, -.04, 0], [.9, -.04, 0]]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.14, .06), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(x, .25, z + (ry ? 0 : .06)); m.rotation.y = ry; scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffb15c', 0, 3.2, 1.1, .9, 1.2); l.position.set(x - (ry ? .05 : 0), .26, z + (ry ? 0 : .1)); l.target.position.set(x - (ry ? .8 : 0), 0, z + (ry ? 0 : .8)); scene.add(l, l.target);
  steps.push({ m, l });
}
const underBed = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffb15c', 0, 1.0, 1.8); underBed.position.set(-1.7, .04, 1.05); underBed.lookAt(-1.7, -1, 1.05); scene.add(underBed);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#c8d6ff', '#2a2018', .03); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.7, 1.55, 4.6], tgt: [-.8, .8, .9], fov: 64 },    // the room from the doorway corner
  b: { pos: [-.2, 1.1, 2.2], tgt: [-1.6, .6, .5], fov: 56 },     // the bed with the child and the teddy
  f: { pos: [.6, .45, 4.2], tgt: [1.4, .1, 2.0], fov: 60 },      // low: the night lights along the floor to the door
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · cold (the harsh white ceiling light) · night:<0..1> (warm low night lights one by one) · read:<0..1> (the reading light, dimmable)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const cold = T.cold ?? 0, night = T.night ?? 0, read = T.read ?? 0;
  ceilL.intensity = 14 * cold; ceilLamp.material = cold ? emis('#f2f7ff', 3) : emis('#000', 0);
  steps.forEach(({ m, l }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (night * 1.2 - i / 4) / .25)); l.intensity = 7 * k; m.material = k ? emis('#ffb15c', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  underBed.intensity = 5 * Math.min(1, night);
  readL.intensity = 11 * read; readLens.material = read ? emis('#ffcf8a', 5 * read) : emis('#000', 0);
  moon.intensity = cold ? 0 : 3; winM.emissiveIntensity = cold ? .1 : .35; hemi.intensity = .03 + .12 * cold;
  scene.environmentIntensity = .02 + .1 * cold;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
const spots = [[-2.4, .95], [-1.2, .95], [0, .95], [1.2, .95], [2.5, 1.2], [-2.6, 4.3], [2.6, 4.3], [0, 5.2]].map(([x, z]) => {

plane(1.4, 1.5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: nightTex, emissive: '#ffffff', emissiveMap: nightTex, emissiveIntensity: .35, roughness: .3 }), [-RW + .01, 1.65, 3.2], [0, Math.PI / 2, 0]);
for (const [h, y] of [[.06, .88], [.06, 2.36]]) box(.05, h, 1.5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2b2b', roughness: .5 }), -RW, y, 2.45);

  w: { pos: [0, 2.2, 6.3], tgt: [0, 1.1, .8], fov: 68 },        // the whole kitchen from the dining side
  c: { pos: [.35, 1.35, 4.9], tgt: [0, 1.45, 2.7], fov: 50 },        // the island and its pendants
  s: { pos: [-.6, 1.4, 2.0], tgt: [-1.4, 1.1, .2], fov: 58 },        // the counter under the wall cabinets
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · under:<0..1> (strip under the wall cabinets, piece by piece) · spots · pend:<0..1> (one pendant after another) · shelf · all (= under+spots+pend+shelf)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'under+spots+pend+shelf').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const un = T.under ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0, pe = T.pend ?? 0, sh = T.shelf ?? 0;
  under.forEach(({ s, l }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (un * 1.1 - i / 4) / .3)); l.intensity = 22 * k; s.material = k ? emis('#fff0dc', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.intensity = 42 * sp; ring.material = sp ? emis('#fff3e2', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  pends.forEach(({ inner, bulb, s, pt }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (pe * 1.2 - i / 3) / .4)); s.intensity = 16 * k; pt.intensity = .8 * k; bulb.material = k ? emis('#ffe0b0', 7 * k) : new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe6d6', roughness: .3 }); inner.material.emissive.set(k ? '#ffb86a' : '#000'); inner.material.emissiveIntensity = .5 * k; });
  shelves.forEach(({ s, l }) => { l.intensity = 34 * sh; s.material = sh ? emis('#ffe3bd', 9) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const lit = Math.min(1, un) * .25 + sp * .35 + Math.min(1, pe) * .25 + sh * .15;
  hemi.intensity = .03 + .3 * lit; scene.environmentIntensity = .02 + .13 * lit; moon.intensity = .6 * (1 - lit * .7);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/ledlab.html`

```html
// ===== LED myths lab: three stations in a dark studio =====
// H (x=0): two downlights on a stand, LED face down: a cheap flat one with no heat sink vs a good one with a deep finned
//   aluminium heat sink.  tokens: on · heat:<0..1> (the cheap one's back glows hot) · age:<0..1> (the cheap one turns yellow, dim, dead dots)
// L (x=12): a ceiling with two "12 W" spots washing a white wall: lm = the left one weak (≈ 700 lm) and the right one strong.
//   tokens: lm · lmon
// D (x=24): a living wall with a dimmer plate and three wall-washing spots.  tokens: dl:<0..1> dim level (smooth, matching)
//   · flick (cheap driver: uneven levels, one spot almost out, a little colour shift)
const WARM = '#ffdcb0';
scene.background = new THREE.Color('#09080a');
const floorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1a1c', roughness: .6, metalness: .1 });
plane(60, 20, floorM, [12, 0, -2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const back = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#232124', roughness: .9 });
plane(60, 8, back, [12, 4, -3], [0, 0, 0]);
// ---------- H station ----------

const plasticW = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e8e6e0', roughness: .5 });
box(1.1, .04, .06, M.black, -.55, 1.3, -.03);                                 // the stand's bar
for (const x of [-.55, .5]) box(.05, 1.3, .05, M.black, x, 0, -.025);
for (const x of [-.22, .22]) box(.012, .14, .012, M.black, x - .006, 1.16, -.006);
box(1.2, .04, .7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9d4cc', roughness: .6 }), -.6, .44, -.35); for (const [x, z] of [[-.56, -.3], [.52, -.3], [-.56, .29], [.52, .29]]) box(.04, .44, .04, M.black, x, 0, z);
// cheap: thin flat white body, a small driver box glued on top
const cheap = new THREE.Group(); cheap.position.set(-.22, .92, 0); scene.add(cheap);
{ const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.13, .13, .045, 40), plasticW); cheap.add(body); const drv = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.11, .04, .07), plasticW); drv.position.y = .045; cheap.add(drv); }
const cheapHot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.128, .128, .005, 40), emis('#000', 0)); cheapHot.position.set(-.22, .945, 0); scene.add(cheapHot);
const cheapHalo = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.15, 24, 16), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ff5a1a', transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false })); cheapHalo.position.set(-.22, 1.0, 0); cheapHalo.scale.y = .5; scene.add(cheapHalo);
const cheapFace = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.11, 40), emis('#000', 0)); cheapFace.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; cheapFace.position.set(-.22, .896, 0); scene.add(cheapFace);
const deadDots = []; for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const a = i * 2.3, r = .03 + (i % 3) * .025; const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.012, 10), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#2a2016' })); d.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; d.position.set(-.22 + Math.cos(a) * r, .8955, Math.sin(a) * r); d.visible = false; scene.add(d); deadDots.push(d); }
// good: deep spot with a finned heat sink
const good = new THREE.Group(); good.position.set(.22, .9, 0); scene.add(good);
{ const can = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.1, .09, .1, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2f0ec', roughness: .4 })); can.position.y = .02; good.add(can);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.13, .13, .015, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2f0ec', roughness: .4 })); ring.position.y = -.03; good.add(ring);
  const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .2, 24), alu); core.position.y = .17; good.add(core);
  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) { const a = i / 16 * Math.PI; const fin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.2, .18, .008), alu); fin.position.y = .17; fin.rotation.y = a; good.add(fin); } }
const goodWarm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.105, .105, .17, 24, 1, true), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#ff8a3a', transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); goodWarm.position.set(.22, 1.07, 0); scene.add(goodWarm);
const goodFace = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.07, 40), emis('#000', 0)); goodFace.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; goodFace.position.set(.22, .854, 0); scene.add(goodFace);
const hL = [-.22, .22].map((x) => { const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 2, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.4); s.position.set(x, .84, 0); s.target.position.set(x, .46, 0); scene.add(s, s.target); return s; });
// little product tags under each (blank cards; the text is drawn in 2D)
const rim = new THREE.SpotLight('#cfd8ff', 6, 6, 40 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.5); rim.position.set(1.8, 2.4, -1.6); rim.target.position.set(0, 1.0, 0); scene.add(rim, rim.target);
const key = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff1e0', 4, 6, 35 * Math.PI / 180, .7, 1.5); key.position.set(-1.2, 2.2, 1.8); key.target.position.set(0, 1.0, 0); scene.add(key, key.target);
// ---------- L station (x = 12) ----------
const wallW = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#efe9df', roughness: .9 });
box(4.4, 3.0, .1, wallW, 9.8, 0, -.6);
box(4.4, .1, 2.2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3efe8', roughness: .95 }), 9.8, 3.0, -.6, { cast: false });
plane(4.4, 2.2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cfc7bb', roughness: .7 }), [12, .005, .5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const LS = [[11.2, .22], [12.8, 1]].map(([x, k]) => { const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.055, .055, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, 2.995, -.15); scene.add(ring); const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 6, 26 * Math.PI / 180, .4, 1.3); s.position.set(x, 2.96, -.15); s.target.position.set(x, .3, -.55); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s, k }; });
const lFill = new THREE.PointLight('#fff', 0, 6, 2); lFill.position.set(12, 2, 3); scene.add(lFill);
// ---------- D station (x = 24) ----------
box(4.4, 3.0, .1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, color: '#cdb59a', roughness: .6, bumpMap: flutedBump, bumpScale: 2 }), 21.8, 0, -.6);
box(4.4, .1, 2.2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3efe8', roughness: .95 }), 21.8, 3.0, -.6, { cast: false });
plane(4.4, 2.2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, roughness: .5 }), [24, .005, .5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(2.2, .42, .85, M.fabric, 22.9, 0, -.35, { r: .06 }); box(2.2, .45, .2, M.fabric, 22.9, .42, -.45, { r: .06 });
const plate = box(.09, .14, .015, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efece6', roughness: .4 }), 25.75, 1.1, -.5);
const knob = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.025, .025, .02, 24), M.brass); knob.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; knob.position.set(25.795, 1.17, -.48); scene.add(knob);
const DS = [23.0, 24.0, 25.0].map((x) => { const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, 2.995, -.2); scene.add(ring); const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 6, 28 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.3); s.position.set(x, 2.96, -.2); s.target.position.set(x, .4, -.55); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s }; });
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#8a95b8', '#120f0c', .12); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  h: { pos: [.5, 1.08, .48], tgt: [.22, 1.0, 0], fov: 46 },        // hero: the good spot's heat sink close up
  b: { pos: [0, 1.2, 1.3], tgt: [0, .82, 0], fov: 50 },         // both spots on the stand
  c: { pos: [-.48, 1.05, .45], tgt: [-.22, .95, 0], fov: 46 },      // the cheap one close up
  f: { pos: [0, .62, .75], tgt: [0, .9, 0], fov: 54 },            // from below: the two LED faces
  l: { pos: [12, 1.35, 4.0], tgt: [12, 1.35, 0], fov: 54 },       // the lumen wall
  d: { pos: [24.3, 1.35, 3.9], tgt: [24.3, 1.3, 0], fov: 54 },    // the dimmer wall
};
const cl = (v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const on = T.on ?? 0, heat = T.heat ?? 0, age = T.age ?? 0;
  const cheapCol = new THREE.Color(WARM).lerp(new THREE.Color('#ffb84a'), age);
  hL[0].color.copy(cheapCol); hL[0].intensity = on * 7 * (1 - .55 * age); hL[1].intensity = on * 8;
  cheapFace.material = on ? emis(cheapCol, 5 * (1 - .5 * age)) : emis('#000', 0);
  goodFace.material = on ? emis(WARM, 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  deadDots.forEach((d, i) => { d.visible = on && age > i / 8; });
  cheapHot.material = heat > .02 ? emis('#ff4a10', 3.5 * heat) : emis('#000', 0);
  cheapHalo.material.opacity = .28 * heat;
  goodWarm.material.opacity = .08 * heat;
  // lumen wall
  const lm = T.lm ?? 0, lmon = T.lmon ?? 0;
  LS.forEach(({ ring, s, k }) => { const kk = lm ? k : 1; s.intensity = lmon * 32 * kk; ring.material = lmon ? emis('#fff1dc', 3 * kk) : emis('#000', 0); });
  lFill.intensity = lmon * .4;
  // dimmer wall
  const dl = T.dl ?? 0, fl = T.flick ?? 0;
  DS.forEach(({ ring, s }, i) => { const k = fl ? [1.0, .06, .45][i] * dl : dl; s.color.set(fl && i === 2 ? '#dfe8ff' : WARM); s.intensity = 30 * k; ring.material = k > .02 ? emis('#fff1dc', 3 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  knob.rotation.z = dl * 2.4;
  hemi.intensity = .12; renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
const downs = [];

const fill = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff', 0, 3, 2); fill.position.set(.5, 2.2, 3.6); fill.lookAt(-.3, .7, 0); scene.add(fill);
// mixed-light state: left half cold, right half warm

const mixR = new THREE.SpotLight('#ff9f3d', 0, 8, .55, .6, 1.4); mixR.position.set(.6, 2.97, .7); mixR.target.position.set(.6, .4, -.1); scene.add(mixR, mixR.target);

const VIEWS = {
  q: { pos: [-1.6, 1.7, 4.2], tgt: [.2, .75, .3], fov: 58 },       // the corner from the left (3-questions reel)
  m: { pos: [2.0, 1.55, 4.4], tgt: [-.15, .72, .3], fov: 56 },
  c: { pos: [1.05, 1.1, 2.55], tgt: [-.3, .62, .2], fov: 48 },
};
const COL = { show: '#fff6ea', cold: '#a9c2ff', warm: '#ffcf94', yellow: '#ff9f3d', k4: '#fff1de' };
// state: show | cold | warm | yellow | k4 | mixed
window.setState = (st) => {
  const mixed = st === 'mixed', col = COL[st] ?? '#fff';
  downs.forEach(s => { s.color.set(col); s.intensity = mixed ? 0 : st === 'show' ? 10 : 8; });
  fill.color.set(col); fill.intensity = mixed ? .2 : st === 'show' ? 1.2 : .6;
  hemi.color.set(mixed ? '#fff' : col); hemi.intensity = st === 'show' ? .35 : .15;
  mixL.intensity = mixR.intensity = mixed ? 30 : 0;
  scene.environmentIntensity = st === 'show' ? .3 : .08;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = st === 'show' ? .95 : .9;
};
```

```html
// ===== Apartment-building lobby: cold flat ceiling panels (feels like a car park) vs a designed scheme: hidden 3000 K strip in a ceiling cove, up/down wall sconces, glare-free downlights over the mailboxes and the concierge desk =====
// back wall z=0 with the lift (x -0.9..0.9) on a wood-clad feature wall; mailboxes on the left wall; concierge desk on the right; glass entrance on the front wall (z=6.9); ceiling 3.0 with a gypsum drop band around it (the cove)
const CH = 3.0, RW = 3.2, RD = 6.9;
marbleTex.repeat.set(4, 4);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#e6e1d8', roughness: .18 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e8e3dc', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD + .12, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD + .12, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD + .12, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// front wall with a glass double door in the middle; dark street outside
box(RW - 1.0, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, RD); box(RW - 1.0, CH, .1, wallM, 1.0, 0, RD); box(2.0, CH - 2.4, .1, wallM, -1.0, 2.4, RD);
const steelM0 = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9ccd0', metalness: .9, roughness: .3 });
const glassM = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#9fb3bf', roughness: .05, transmission: .6, transparent: true, opacity: .35 });
box(.98, 2.4, .02, glassM, -1.0, 0, RD + .04, { cast: false }); box(.98, 2.4, .02, glassM, .02, 0, RD + .04, { cast: false });
for (const x of [-1.0, -.02, .98]) box(.04, 2.4, .06, M.black, x, 0, RD + .02);
plane(14, 8, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#16202f' }), [0, 3, RD + 5], [0, Math.PI, 0]);
plane(14, 8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2b2d', roughness: .9 }), [0, -.01, RD + 4], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);   // the pavement outside
for (const [x, y] of [[-2.2, 2.2], [1.6, 2.4], [3.4, 1.9], [-4.2, 2.0]]) box(.08, .08, .02, emis('#ffc77a', 4), x, y, RD + 4.9, { cast: false });   // street lights far away
for (const x of [-.12, .1]) box(.03, .9, .04, steelM0, x, .8, RD + .07, { cast: false });   // door pull handles
// feature wall behind the lift: vertical wood cladding, the lift doors in brushed steel, a small floor indicator
const slatM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#a87a50', roughness: .55 });
box(3.6, CH - .02, .04, slatM, -1.8, .01, -.02);
const steelM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9ccd0', metalness: .9, roughness: .3 });
box(1.8, 2.3, .06, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2b2e', metalness: .5, roughness: .4 }), -.9, 0, .02);
box(.78, 2.15, .04, steelM, -.8, 0, .08); box(.78, 2.15, .04, steelM, .02, 0, .08);
box(.3, .1, .03, emis('#ff9a3a', 1.2), -.15, 2.4, .05, { cast: false });
box(.08, .14, .03, M.black, 1.0, 1.1, .05);
// mailboxes on the left wall: a 6 x 5 grid of small brushed doors
const mbM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c3c6cb', metalness: .55, roughness: .4 });
box(.12, 1.3, 2.1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3b3d41', roughness: .6 }), -RW, .55, 1.6);
for (let r = 0; r < 5; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 6; c++) box(.03, .22, .31, mbM, -RW + .12, .6 + r * .25, 1.65 + c * .34);
// concierge desk on the right
const deskM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efebe5', roughness: .45 });
box(.7, 1.05, 1.8, deskM, RW - 1.3, 0, 2.2, { r: .02 });
box(.85, .05, 1.95, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#4b4541', roughness: .2 }), RW - 1.4, 1.05, 2.12);
box(.6, .75, 1.2, M.darkWood, RW - .55, 0, 2.5);
// bench and plants near the entrance
box(1.6, .42, .5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#5a4b3e', roughness: .9 }), -RW + .2, 0, 5.0, { r: .05 });
plant(-2.6, 6.3, 1.5, .45); plant(2.6, 6.3, 1.5, .45);
// the ceiling: a gypsum drop band 0.55 m wide around the room, its inner edge hides an LED strip that washes the raised centre
const gypM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2f0ec', roughness: .9 });
const B = .55, DY = 2.72, DH = .12;
box(RW * 2, DH, B, gypM, -RW, DY, -.12, { cast: false }); box(RW * 2, DH, B, gypM, -RW, DY, RD - B, { cast: false });
box(B, DH, RD - 2 * B + .12, gypM, -RW, DY, -.12 + B, { cast: false }); box(B, DH, RD - 2 * B + .12, gypM, RW - B, DY, -.12 + B, { cast: false });
// upstand lip on the inner edge so the strip stays hidden from below
const ix0 = -RW + B, ix1 = RW - B, iz0 = -.12 + B, iz1 = RD - B;
box(ix1 - ix0, .08, .03, gypM, ix0, DY + DH, iz0, { cast: false }); box(ix1 - ix0, .08, .03, gypM, ix0, DY + DH, iz1 - .03, { cast: false });
box(.03, .08, iz1 - iz0, gypM, ix0, DY + DH, iz0, { cast: false }); box(.03, .08, iz1 - iz0, gypM, ix1 - .03, DY + DH, iz0, { cast: false });
const coveStrips = [], coveLights = [];
const cove = (x, z, w, d, lx, lz) => { const s = box(w, .006, d, emis('#000', 0), x, DY + DH + .002, z, { cast: false }); coveStrips.push(s); const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd6a8', 0, Math.max(w, d), .08); l.position.set(x + w / 2, DY + DH + .02, z + d / 2); l.lookAt(lx, CH + 1, lz); scene.add(l); coveLights.push(l); };
cove(ix0 - .1, iz0 - .06, ix1 - ix0 + .2, .03, 0, iz0 + .8);       // back edge, aims into the room
cove(ix0 - .1, iz1 + .03, ix1 - ix0 + .2, .03, 0, iz1 - .8);       // front edge
cove(ix0 - .06, iz0, .03, iz1 - iz0, ix0 + .8, (iz0 + iz1) / 2);   // left edge
cove(ix1 + .03, iz0, .03, iz1 - iz0, ix1 - .8, (iz0 + iz1) / 2);   // right edge
// BEFORE: flat cold 60 x 60 panels in the raised centre
const panels = [], panelOff = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cfd0d2', roughness: .6 });
for (const x of [-1.3, 0, 1.3]) for (const z of [1.5, 3.4, 5.3]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.6, .6), panelOff); m.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; m.position.set(x, CH - .005, z); scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef4ff', 0, .6, .6); l.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l);
  panels.push({ m, l });
}
// AFTER: up/down wall sconces flanking the lift and the entrance
const scM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1d1d20', metalness: .6, roughness: .4 });
const sconces = [[-1.3, .06, 1], [1.3, .06, 1], [-1.4, RD - .06, -1], [1.4, RD - .06, -1]].map(([x, z, dir]) => {
  box(.1, .3, .1, scM, x - .05, 1.75, z - .05);
  const lens = [box(.08, .01, .08, emis('#000', 0), x - .04, 1.745, z - .04, { cast: false }), box(.08, .01, .08, emis('#000', 0), x - .04, 2.05, z - .04, { cast: false })];
  const ls = [[1.7, 0], [2.1, CH]].map(([y, ty]) => { const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf95', 0, 4, 22 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.4); s.position.set(x, y, z + dir * .07); s.target.position.set(x, ty, z + dir * .2); scene.add(s, s.target); return s; });
  return { lens, ls };
});
// AFTER: glare-free downlights (deep recessed, 3000 K) over the mailboxes, the desk and the lift
const downs = [[-2.6, 2.3], [-2.6, 3.3], [2.4, 2.6], [2.4, 3.5], [0, 1.0]].map(([x, z]) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .02, 24), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, DY - .005, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffdcb0', 0, 6, 26 * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.4); s.position.set(x, DY - .02, z); s.target.position.set(x + (x < -2 ? -.4 : x > 2 ? .2 : 0), 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s };
});
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#f4ecdf', '#3a3028', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.0, 1.6, 6.4], tgt: [-.2, 1.4, .5], fov: 64 },       // the lobby from the entrance, toward the lift
  m: { pos: [-.6, 1.5, 4.6], tgt: [-3.0, 1.15, 2.4], fov: 58 },    // the mailboxes, close
  e: { pos: [.8, 1.55, 1.3], tgt: [-.1, 1.5, 6.9], fov: 64 },      // the entrance seen from inside (sconces either side of the door)
  u: { pos: [.4, 1.35, 5.2], tgt: [0, 2.95, 1.8], fov: 64 },       // looking up at the ceiling cove
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · panels (flat cold panels) · cove:<0..1> (hidden strip, edge by edge) · sconce · downs · all (= cove+sconce+downs)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'cove+sconce+downs').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const pn = T.panels ?? 0, cv = T.cove ?? 0, sc = T.sconce ?? 0, dn = T.downs ?? 0;
  panels.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 9 * pn; m.material = pn ? emis('#f4f8ff', 2.5) : panelOff; });
  coveLights.forEach((l, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (cv * 1.1 - i / 4) / .3)); l.intensity = 30 * k; coveStrips[i].material = k ? emis('#ffd6a8', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  sconces.forEach(({ lens, ls }) => { ls.forEach((s) => s.intensity = 9 * sc); lens.forEach((m) => m.material = sc ? emis('#ffcf95', 5) : emis('#000', 0)); });
  downs.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.intensity = 16 * dn; ring.material = dn ? emis('#ffe2bf', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  hemi.intensity = .05 + .08 * pn + .08 * Math.min(1, cv); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .06 * pn + .05 * Math.min(1, cv);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/lounge.html`

```html
// ===== Living room in three moods: guests (everything on), TV (cove + TV backlight only), evening (warm, low, dimmed) =====
// TV wall at z=0 (x -3..3), floor y=0, ceiling 2.9 with a perimeter cove; sofa facing the TV at z≈3.2
const CH = 2.9, RX = 3.2;
const woodFloorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#8a6644', '50,30,15')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(25,14,6,.4)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(7, 7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, 2.5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e6ddd0', roughness: .92 });
box(6.6, CH, .1, wallM, -3.3, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 7, wallM, -RX - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 7, wallM, RX, 0, -.1);
box(6.6, .1, 7, M.ceil, -3.3, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// TV wall: fluted wood panel, floating unit, TV
box(3.4, 2.5, .06, M.fluted, -1.7, .25, 0);
box(2.6, .3, .45, M.darkWood, -1.3, .35, .06);
const tvScreen = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#050608', roughness: .1, metalness: .5, emissive: '#000' });
const tv = box(1.7, .98, .04, tvScreen, -.85, 1.2, .1);
const tvBack = []; // backlight strip behind the TV
for (const [w, h, x, y] of [[1.7, .02, -.85, 1.19], [1.7, .02, -.85, 2.18], [.02, 1, -.86, 1.19], [.02, 1, .85, 1.19]]) { const m = box(w, h, .01, emis('#000', 0), x, y, .09, { cast: false }); tvBack.push(m); }
const tvGlow = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 2.0, 1.3); tvGlow.position.set(0, 1.7, .08); tvGlow.lookAt(0, 1.7, -1); scene.add(tvGlow);
// cove: perimeter drop 2.65 with a wash on the raised ceiling (additive bands)
const washTex = canvasTex(256, 64, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(.35, 'rgba(255,255,255,.45)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1], false);
const gyp = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f0ea', roughness: .95 });
const coves = [];
for (const [x, z, w, d, rot, len] of [[-RX, -.1, .5, 7, 0, 7], [RX - .5, -.1, .5, 7, Math.PI, 7], [-RX, -.1, 6.4, .5, -Math.PI / 2, 6.4]]) {
  box(w, .1, d, gyp, x, CH - .3, z, { cast: false });
}
for (const [cx, cz, len, rz, ry] of [[-RX + 1.05, 3.4, 7, 0, 0], [RX - 1.05, 3.4, 7, Math.PI, 0], [0, .95, 6.4, 0, 1]]) {
  const wm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: washTex, color: '#000', transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
  const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.2, len), wm); w.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; w.rotation.z = ry ? -Math.PI / 2 : rz; w.position.set(cx, CH - .005, cz); scene.add(w);
  coves.push(wm);
}
const coveFill = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffcf96', '#000000', 0); scene.add(coveFill);
// sofa, rug, coffee table, floor lamp, plant
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#cbbca5', roughness: .95 });
box(2.6, .42, .95, sofaM, -1.3, .1, 3.0, { r: .06 }); box(2.6, .5, .22, sofaM, -1.3, .5, 3.73, { r: .06 });
[['#9a3b2e', -.9], ['#2f5d6b', .35]].forEach(([c, x]) => { const m = box(.45, .4, .14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x, .5, 3.55, { r: .07 }); m.rotation.x = .25; });
plane(3.2, 2.2, M.rug, [0, .006, 2.0], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const ct = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.55, .55, .06, 48), M.tableTop); ct.position.set(0, .4, 2.0); ct.castShadow = ct.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(ct);
const ctl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.25, .3, .37, 32), M.darkWood); ctl.position.set(0, .185, 2.0); scene.add(ctl);
const lampPole = box(.03, 1.5, .03, M.brass, -1.85, 0, 2.3); const shadeM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1e6d2', roughness: .8, side: THREE.DoubleSide, emissive: '#000' });
const shade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.18, .24, .3, 32, 1, true), shadeM); shade.position.set(-1.835, 1.55, 2.315); scene.add(shade);
const floorLamp = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb870', 0, 5, 2); floorLamp.position.set(-1.835, 1.5, 2.315); floorLamp.castShadow = true; floorLamp.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(floorLamp);
plant(-2.6, .6, 1.6, .55);
// downlights over the seating
const downs = [];
for (const [x, z] of [[-1.6, 1.4], [1.6, 1.4], [-1.6, 3.0], [1.6, 3.0], [0, 2.2]]) {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe0b8', 0, 8, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.5); s.position.set(x, CH - .32, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.03, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, CH - .315, z); scene.add(lens);
  downs.push({ s, lens });
}
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe9d2', '#3a2c20', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [2.5, 1.85, 5.9], tgt: [-.5, .95, 1.2], fov: 66 },  // the room from behind the sofa, high on the right
  t: { pos: [0, 1.3, 3.6], tgt: [0, 1.5, 0], fov: 55 },         // the TV wall from the sofa
};
// presets: off | guests | tv | evening
window.setState = (st) => {
  const P = { off: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0], guests: [1, 1, .8, 0, 1], tv: [0, .7, 0, 1, .15], evening: [0, .3, 1, 0, .05] }[st] ?? [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
  const [dn, cove, lamp, tvb, amb] = P;
  downs.forEach((d) => { d.s.intensity = 9 * dn; d.lens.material = dn ? emis('#ffe0b8', 6 * dn) : emis('#000', 0); });
  coves.forEach((m) => m.color.set(new THREE.Color('#c9a070').multiplyScalar(cove))); coveFill.intensity = .35 * cove;
  floorLamp.intensity = 2.2 * lamp; shadeM.emissive.set(lamp ? '#ffb870' : '#000'); shadeM.emissiveIntensity = .9 * lamp;
  tvScreen.emissive.set(st === 'tv' ? '#24476e' : '#000'); tvScreen.emissiveIntensity = st === 'tv' ? 1.1 : 0;
  tvGlow.intensity = 3 * tvb; tvBack.forEach((m) => m.material = tvb ? emis('#ffd6a0', 5 * tvb) : emis('#000', 0));
  hemi.intensity = .03 + .12 * amb; scene.environmentIntensity = .02 + .08 * amb;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = st === 'evening' ? 1.1 : 1.0;
};
```

```html
// wall z=0 with a picture; the track runs along x at z=1.2 in the ceiling (y=2.8); dining table under it; no people, no hands
const CH = 2.8, RW = 3.0, RD = 5.0, TZ = 1.2;

plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .5 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e2ddd5', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// lounge: fluted oak wall, low sofa under the track, coffee table, a framed picture and a tall plant
box(RW * 2, CH, .05, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, color: '#a07a55', roughness: .55, bumpMap: flutedBump, bumpScale: 2 }), -RW, 0, -.07, { cast: false });
const artTex = canvasTex(768, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#efe4d2'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); const cs = ['#2f4d5c', '#c4693c', '#d9a85b']; for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { g.fillStyle = cs[i]; g.globalAlpha = .9; g.fillRect(90 + i * 210, 80 + (i % 2) * 60, 170, 300); } }, [1, 1]);
box(1.16, .82, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1714', roughness: .5 }), -.83, 1.3, -.02);
plane(1.06, .72, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: artTex, roughness: .8 }), [-.25, 1.71, .03], [0, 0, 0]);
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#d4c6b0', roughness: .95 });
box(2.6, .42, .9, sofaM, -1.3, 0, .1, { r: .06 }); box(2.6, .45, .22, sofaM, -1.3, .42, .1, { r: .06 });
for (const x of [-1.3, 1.1]) box(.2, .55, .9, sofaM, x, 0, .1, { r: .06 });
const tabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#6a4a30', roughness: .45 });
box(1.1, .36, .6, tabM, -.55, 0, TZ + .3, { r: .02 });
const vase = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .09, .26, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f4a5a', roughness: .25 })); vase.position.set(.2, .49, TZ + .6); vase.castShadow = true; scene.add(vase);
box(.32, .04, .22, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', roughness: .3, metalness: .9 }), -.4, .36, TZ + .5);
plant(1.85, .45, 1.7, .5);
// the track: a black channel recessed flush in the ceiling, with a thin inner slot
const blk = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#141416', metalness: .4, roughness: .45 });
box(4.2, .06, .06, blk, -2.1, CH - .055, TZ - .03, { cast: false });
box(4.2, .005, .02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2c2c30', metalness: .8, roughness: .3 }), -2.1, CH - .058, TZ - .01, { cast: false });
// a spot head: magnetic adapter + tilted cylinder + lens; returns a handle we can move and switch
const head = (aimX, aimY, aimZ) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(g);
  const ad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.07, .03, .05), blk); ad.position.y = -.015; g.add(ad);
  const arm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.008, .008, .05, 12), blk); arm.position.y = -.05; g.add(arm);
  const body = new THREE.Group(); body.position.y = -.08; g.add(body);
  const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.034, .034, .13, 28), blk); c.castShadow = true; body.add(c);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.026, 24), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.y = -.066; body.add(lens);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe2bc', 0, 7, 16 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.3); s.castShadow = true; scene.add(s, s.target);
  return { g, body, lens, s, aim: new THREE.Vector3(aimX, aimY, aimZ) };
};
const spots = [head(-.25, 1.7, 0), head(.2, .4, TZ + .6), head(1.85, 1.3, .45)];
const homeX = [-.33, .2, 1.1];
// linear module: a slim bar clipped into the track, opal diffuser
const lin = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(lin);
const linBody = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.6, .04, .03), blk); linBody.position.y = -.02; lin.add(linBody);
const linLens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.58, .02), emis('#000', 0)); linLens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; linLens.position.y = -.041; lin.add(linLens);
const linL = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe6c8', 0, .58, .03); linL.position.y = -.045; linL.lookAt(0, -10, 0); lin.add(linL);
lin.position.set(.72, CH - .055, TZ);
// small pendant hanging from the track over the table end
const pend = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(pend);
const cord = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.003, .003, .9, 6), blk); cord.position.y = -.45; pend.add(cord);
const shade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.12, .14, 32, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1a1c', metalness: .5, roughness: .4, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); shade.position.y = -.95; pend.add(shade);
const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.03, 16, 12), emis('#000', 0)); bulb.position.y = -.99; pend.add(bulb);
const pendL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf95', 0, 3, 2); pendL.position.y = -1.0; pend.add(pendL);
pend.position.set(-.95, CH - .055, TZ + .25);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#efe7dc', '#2a2018', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const amb = new THREE.PointLight('#ffe8d0', 0, 9, 1.6); amb.position.set(.5, 2.3, 3.2); scene.add(amb);
const VIEWS = {
  c: { pos: [.35, 1.75, 3.1], tgt: [.25, 2.45, 1.2], fov: 68 },     // close, looking up at the track and the heads
  w: { pos: [.4, 1.6, 4.8], tgt: [.1, 1.25, .6], fov: 64 },       // the room: track, table, picture
  s: { pos: [.05, 2.4, 1.72], tgt: [-.1, 2.72, 1.2], fov: 40 },    // macro on one head and the track slot
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  n:<0..3> spot heads on the track · on (light them) · slide:<0..1> head 2 slides along the track to its place (default 1 = in place) · lift:<0..1> head 3 still below the track (1) / clicked on (0) · linear · pend · room (soft fill)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const n = T.n ?? 0, on = T.on ?? 0, sl = T.slide ?? 1, lf = T.lift ?? 0, li = T.linear ?? 0, pe = T.pend ?? 0, rm = T.room ?? 0;
  spots.forEach((h, i) => {
    const vis = i < n; h.g.visible = vis;
    let x = homeX[i]; if (i === 1) x = 1.3 + (homeX[1] - 1.3) * sl;
    const y = CH - .055 - (i === 2 ? .28 * lf : 0);
    h.g.position.set(x, y, TZ);
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(h.aim, new THREE.Vector3(x, y - .08, TZ)).normalize();
    h.body.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0), dir);
    const lit = vis && on && !(i === 2 && lf > .05);
    h.s.position.set(x, y - .1, TZ); h.s.target.position.copy(h.aim); h.s.intensity = lit ? 26 : 0;
    h.lens.material = lit ? emis('#fff1dc', 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  });
  lin.visible = !!li; linL.intensity = li && on ? 18 : 0; linLens.material = li && on ? emis('#ffe6c8', 5) : emis('#000', 0);
  pend.visible = !!pe; pendL.intensity = pe && on ? 1.6 : 0; bulb.material = pe && on ? emis('#ffcf95', 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  amb.intensity = 1.8 + 1.2 * rm; hemi.intensity = .05 + .05 * rm; scene.environmentIntensity = .04 + .04 * rm;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
    const lit = vis && on && !(i === 2 && lf > .05);
    h.s.position.set(x, y - .1, TZ); h.s.target.position.copy(h.aim); h.s.intensity = lit ? 26 : 0;
    h.lens.material = lit ? emis('#fff1dc', 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  });
  lin.visible = !!li; linL.intensity = li && on ? 18 : 0; linLens.material = li && on ? emis('#ffe6c8', 5) : emis('#000', 0);
  pend.visible = !!pe; pendL.intensity = pe && on ? 1.6 : 0; bulb.material = pe && on ? emis('#ffcf95', 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  amb.intensity = 1.8 + 1.2 * rm; hemi.intensity = .05 + .05 * rm; scene.environmentIntensity = .04 + .04 * rm;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
// ===== Kuwaiti majlis (reference mood) =====
const TW = .25, H1 = 3.3, H2 = 3.7;
const stucco = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#d8c7ae'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { const r = 2 + R() * 26; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${R() > .5 ? 255 : 150},${R() > .5 ? 240 : 125},${R() > .5 ? 215 : 95},${.03 + R() * .05})`; g.beginPath(); g.arc(R() * w, R() * h, r, 0, 7); g.fill(); } }, [1.5, 1.5]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: stucco, roughness: .92 });
const nicheM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: stucco, color: '#efe0c8', roughness: .95 });
const floorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#d9d1c6', roughness: .22 });
marbleTex.repeat.set(4, 4);
plane(LX, LZ, floorM, [LX / 2, 0, LZ / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const ceilG = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(ceilG);
const archL = [], archM = [], coveL = [], coveMs = [], sofaL = [], sofaMs = [], downs = [], downMs = [], lantL = [], lantMs = [];
function archShape(a, b, y0, ys, yt) { // pointed arch outline: x a..b, from y0 up to spring ys then apex yt
  const s = new THREE.Shape(); const c = (a + b) / 2; s.moveTo(a, y0); s.lineTo(a, ys); s.quadraticCurveTo(a, yt - (yt - ys) * .15, c, yt); s.quadraticCurveTo(b, yt - (yt - ys) * .15, b, ys); s.lineTo(b, y0); s.lineTo(a, y0); return s; }
function archPts(a, b, ys, yt, n = 24) { const c = (a + b) / 2, pts = []; const q = (p0, p1, p2, t) => (1 - t) * (1 - t) * p0 + 2 * (1 - t) * t * p1 + t * t * p2;
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const t = i / n; pts.push([q(a, a, c, t), q(ys, yt - (yt - ys) * .15, yt, t)]); } for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) { const t = i / n; pts.push([q(c, b, b, t), q(yt, yt - (yt - ys) * .15, ys, t)]); } return pts; }
// wall builder: width W along local x, holes: [{a,b,y0,ys,yt,kind}] ; placed via transform
function wallWithArches(Wd, holes, place) {
  const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(0, 0); s.lineTo(Wd, 0); s.lineTo(Wd, H1); s.lineTo(0, H1); s.lineTo(0, 0);
  holes.forEach(h => s.holes.push(archShape(h.a, h.b, h.y0, h.ys, h.yt)));
  const geo = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: TW, bevelEnabled: false, curveSegments: 24 });
  geo.attributes.uv.array.forEach((v, i, arr) => arr[i] = v * .4);
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(geo, wallM); m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true; place(m); scene.add(m);
  holes.forEach(h => {
    if (h.kind === 'open') return;
    const back = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ShapeGeometry(archShape(h.a, h.b, h.y0, h.ys, h.yt), 24), nicheM); back.receiveShadow = true;
    const g = new THREE.Group(); g.add(back); back.position.z = -.001; place(g); scene.add(g);
    if (h.kind === 'lit') { // LED along the inner reveal + grazing rect lights
      const pts = archPts(h.a + .04, h.b - .04, h.ys, h.yt - .04).map(([x, y]) => new THREE.Vector3(x, y, .07));
      pts.unshift(new THREE.Vector3(h.a + .04, h.y0 + .02, .07)); pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(h.b - .04, h.y0 + .02, .07));
      const tube = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts), 120, .012, 6), emis('#ffbe6a', 5)); const tg = new THREE.Group(); tg.add(tube); place(tg); scene.add(tg); archM.push(tube);
      const hh = h.ys - h.y0; const mk = (w, hgt, x, y, tx, ty) => { const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffb766', 0, w, hgt); l.position.set(x, y, .12); l.lookAt(tx, ty, .0); const gg = new THREE.Group(); gg.add(l); place(gg); scene.add(gg); archL.push([l, h.k || 9]); };
      mk(.05, hh, h.a + .05, h.y0 + hh / 2, h.a + .6, h.y0 + hh / 2); mk(.05, hh, h.b - .05, h.y0 + hh / 2, h.b - .6, h.y0 + hh / 2);
      mk(h.b - h.a - .3, .05, (h.a + h.b) / 2, h.yt - .25, (h.a + h.b) / 2, h.y0);
    }
  });
}
// back wall (z=0, faces +z): local x -> world x, extrude toward -z
wallWithArches(LX, [{ a: 2.15, b: 3.85, y0: .0, ys: 2.15, yt: 3.0, kind: 'lit', k: 10 }, { a: .75, b: 1.65, y0: .0, ys: 1.9, yt: 2.6, kind: 'niche' }, { a: 4.35, b: 5.25, y0: .0, ys: 1.9, yt: 2.6, kind: 'niche' }], m => { m.position.set(0, 0, -TW); });
// right wall (x=LX), local x -> world z (0..LZ), faces -x
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/master.html`

```html
// ===== Master bedroom, layer by layer (satisfying switch-on): dark room → hidden strip in a ceiling cove → bedside wall sconces → spots on the wardrobe and the painting =====
// headboard wall z=0 (x -3..3) with a tall upholstered panel; king bed against it; wardrobe on the right wall; painting on the left wall; ceiling 3.0 with a gypsum drop band (the cove)
const CH = 3.0, RW = 3.0, RD = 5.4;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#8f6e4f', '58,36,18')(g, w, h); }, [3, 3]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .5 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d9cfc2', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// headboard wall: a tall channel-tufted panel behind the bed
const hbM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#6f6357', roughness: .95 });
for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) box(.26, 2.1, .08, hbM, -1.2 + i * .27, .3, -.07, { r: .04 });
// the bed: base, mattress, duvet, pillows, throw
const baseM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#4d443c', roughness: .9 });
box(2.1, .32, 2.2, baseM, -1.05, 0, .05, { r: .04 });
box(2.0, .24, 2.05, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ea', roughness: .9 }), -1.0, .32, .1, { r: .07 });
box(2.04, .07, 1.35, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#e6dccb', roughness: .95 }), -1.02, .55, .82, { r: .04 });
box(2.06, .06, .45, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8a6a4c', roughness: .95 }), -1.03, .6, 1.6, { r: .03 });
for (const x of [-.85, -.25, .3]) box(.52, .16, .3, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#f8f5ef', roughness: .9 }), x, .56, .18, { r: .07 });
// nightstands
const nsM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#5e4430', roughness: .5 });
for (const x of [-1.75, 1.2]) box(.55, .5, .42, nsM, x, 0, .05, { r: .02 });
// wardrobe on the right wall (tall, handle-less doors), painting on the left wall, a bench at the foot of the bed, a rug
const wdM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9e0', roughness: .55 });
box(.62, 2.6, 2.4, wdM, RW - .62, 0, 1.6, { r: .01 });
for (const z of [2.2, 3.0, 3.8]) box(.01, 2.5, .004, M.black, RW - .63, .05, z, { cast: false });
const artTex = canvasTex(512, 768, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#d8c3a5'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#8c6a4f'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(40,25,15,.55)'; g.lineWidth = 10; for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(60, 120 + i * 120); g.bezierCurveTo(200, 60 + i * 120, 320, 200 + i * 120, 450, 110 + i * 120); g.stroke(); } }, [1, 1]);
box(.04, 1.14, .82, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1714', roughness: .5 }), -RW, 1.08, 2.2);
plane(.74, 1.06, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: artTex, roughness: .85 }), [-RW + .05, 1.65, 2.61], [0, Math.PI / 2, 0]);
box(1.4, .45, .4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#7a6a5a', roughness: .95 }), -.7, 0, 2.5, { r: .04 });
plane(3.0, 2.2, M.rug, [0, .006, 1.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
// ceiling cove: a gypsum drop band around the room with a hidden strip on its inner lip, washing the raised centre
const gypM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2efe9', roughness: .9 });
const B = .5, DY = 2.74, DH = .1;
box(RW * 2, DH, B, gypM, -RW, DY, -.12, { cast: false }); box(RW * 2, DH, B, gypM, -RW, DY, RD - B - .12, { cast: false });
box(B, DH, RD - 2 * B, gypM, -RW, DY, -.12 + B, { cast: false }); box(B, DH, RD - 2 * B, gypM, RW - B, DY, -.12 + B, { cast: false });
const ix0 = -RW + B, ix1 = RW - B, iz0 = -.12 + B, iz1 = RD - B - .12;
const coveS = [], coveL = [];
const cv = (x, z, w, d, lx, lz) => { coveS.push(box(w, .006, d, emis('#000', 0), x, DY + DH + .002, z, { cast: false })); const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffcf9a', 0, Math.max(w, d), .07); l.position.set(x + w / 2, DY + DH + .02, z + d / 2); l.lookAt(lx, CH + 1, lz); scene.add(l); coveL.push(l); };
cv(ix0, iz0 - .04, ix1 - ix0, .03, 0, iz0 + .8); cv(ix0, iz1 + .01, ix1 - ix0, .03, 0, iz1 - .8);
cv(ix0 - .04, iz0, .03, iz1 - iz0, ix0 + .8, (iz0 + iz1) / 2); cv(ix1 + .01, iz0, .03, iz1 - iz0, ix1 - .8, (iz0 + iz1) / 2);
// bedside wall sconces (warm, a soft glow up and down the headboard)
const scM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b08a5a', metalness: .8, roughness: .3 });
const sconces = [-1.5, 1.45].map((x) => {
  box(.12, .22, .08, scM, x - .06, 1.25, .0);
  const lens = box(.1, .01, .06, emis('#000', 0), x - .05, 1.245, .01, { cast: false });
  const glow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.05, 16, 12), emis('#000', 0)); glow.position.set(x, 1.36, .1); scene.add(glow);
  const up = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffc27a', 0, 3, 40 * Math.PI / 180, .8, 1.4); up.position.set(x, 1.48, .1); up.target.position.set(x, 3, .1); scene.add(up, up.target);
  const dn = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffc27a', 0, 3, 45 * Math.PI / 180, .8, 1.4); dn.position.set(x, 1.24, .12); dn.target.position.set(x, 0, .25); scene.add(dn, dn.target);
  const pt = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb866', 0, 2.2, 2); pt.position.set(x, 1.36, .25); scene.add(pt);
  return { lens, glow, up, dn, pt };
});
// spots: three grazing down the tufted headboard
const spots = [[-.9, .35, -.9, .9, 0], [0, .35, 0, .9, 0], [.9, .35, .9, .9, 0]].map(([x, z, tx, ty, tz]) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .045, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, DY - .005, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffdcb0', 0, 6, 20 * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.3); s.position.set(x, DY - .02, z); s.target.position.set(tx, ty, tz); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s };
});
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#c9cfe0', '#1a1410', .03); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [-.15, 2.0, 5.2], tgt: [-.15, 1.25, .4], fov: 62 },        // the room from the door
  b: { pos: [-.1, 1.4, 3.3], tgt: [-.1, 1.15, 0], fov: 64 },       // the bed and the headboard, close
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · cove:<0..1> (hidden strip, edge by edge) · sconce · spots (grazing the headboard) · all (= cove+sconce+spots)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'cove+sconce+spots').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const co = T.cove ?? 0, sc = T.sconce ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0;
  coveL.forEach((l, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (co * 1.1 - i / 4) / .3)); l.intensity = 26 * k; coveS[i].material = k ? emis('#ffcf9a', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  sconces.forEach(({ lens, glow, up, dn, pt }) => { up.intensity = 7 * sc; dn.intensity = 7 * sc; pt.intensity = .8 * sc; lens.material = sc ? emis('#ffc27a', 5) : emis('#000', 0); glow.material = sc ? emis('#ffd6a0', 4) : emis('#000', 0); });
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.intensity = 22 * sp; ring.material = sp ? emis('#ffe2bf', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const any = Math.min(1, co) || sc || sp;
  hemi.intensity = .03 + .04 * any; scene.environmentIntensity = .02 + .04 * any;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/mirror.html`

```html
// ===== Vanity mirror: a plaster bust lit by one downlight above the head (shadows under the eyes) vs light from the sides of the mirror =====
// mirror wall at z=0, floor y=0, ceiling 2.7; the bust stands at z=.55 facing the mirror (-z)
const BH = 2.7, HY = 1.6, HZ = .55;
const tileTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => {
  g.fillStyle = '#d9d2c8'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 128) for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 256) { const c = 205 + R() * 25; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c},${c - 6},${c - 14})`; g.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, 252, 124); }
}, [3, 3]);
const tileM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: tileTex, roughness: .35 });
plane(6, 6, M.floor, [0, 0, 1.5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(4, BH, .1, tileM, -2, 0, -.1);
box(.1, BH, 4, tileM, -1.6, 0, -.1); box(.1, BH, 4, tileM, 1.5, 0, -.1);
box(4, .1, 4, M.ceil, -2, BH, -.1, { cast: false });
// vanity: oak cabinet + stone top + basin + tap
box(1.2, .8, .5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#9b7350', roughness: .5 }), -.6, 0, 0);
box(1.24, .04, .54, M.tableTop, -.62, .8, -.02);
const basin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.2, .16, .12, 48), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ec', roughness: .2 })); basin.position.set(0, .9, .25); basin.castShadow = true; scene.add(basin);
box(.03, .25, .03, M.brass, -.015, .84, .05); box(.03, .03, .14, M.brass, -.015, 1.06, .05);
// mirror (dark polished glass: the views look from the mirror at the face, so it needn't reflect)
const sheenTex = canvasTex(256, 512, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, h, w, 0); gr.addColorStop(0, '#3a4146'); gr.addColorStop(.45, '#59626a'); gr.addColorStop(.5, '#7d878f'); gr.addColorStop(.56, '#56606a'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#353c42'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); });
box(.7, .95, .02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: sheenTex, roughness: .12, metalness: 0 }), -.35, 1.15, 0);
// two vertical LED bars at the sides of the mirror (the fix)
const bars = [];
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.05, .7, .03), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(sx * .45, 1.62, .03); scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff0dc', 0, .05, .7); l.position.set(sx * .45, 1.62, .05); l.lookAt(sx * .45, 1.62, 2); scene.add(l);
  bars.push({ m, l });
}
// a towel, a plant and a soap dish for scale
box(.04, .5, .3, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#e8e0d4', roughness: 1 }), 1.42, .9, .4);
plant(-1.25, .4, 1.1, .45);

// ---- the bust: a sculpted ellipsoid (brow, eye sockets, nose, cheekbones, lips, chin) on a neck and shoulders ----
const plaster = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e7dccd', roughness: .75 });
const gs = (d, s) => Math.exp(-(d * d) / (2 * s * s));
const smooth = (a, b, x) => { const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (x - a) / (b - a))); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
const headGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 256, 192);
const pos = headGeo.attributes.position, v = new THREE.Vector3();
for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
  v.fromBufferAttribute(pos, i);
  let x = v.x * .078, y = v.y * .108, z = v.z * .098;
  x *= 1 - .3 * smooth(-.01, -.105, y);                                  // narrower jaw
  z *= 1 - .1 * smooth(0, -.105, y);
  const f = smooth(0, .06, z);                                           // only the front of the face gets features
  let d = .004 * gs(y - .03, .009) * gs(x, .035);                        // brow ridge
  for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
    d -= .009 * gs(Math.hypot(x - sx * .031, (y - .012) * 1.3), .013);  // eye sockets
    d += .004 * gs(Math.hypot(x - sx * .031, y - .011), .008);          // eyeballs
    d += .004 * gs(Math.hypot(x - sx * .045, y + .015), .017);          // cheekbones
  }
  d += .022 * gs(x, .008 + .007 * smooth(.015, -.035, y)) * smooth(.02, -.03, y) * smooth(-.047, -.035, y); // nose
  d += .004 * gs(y + .058, .007) * gs(x, .018);                          // lips
  d += .007 * gs(Math.hypot(x * .8, y + .088), .02);                     // chin
  const n = new THREE.Vector3(x / .078 ** 2, y / .108 ** 2, z / .098 ** 2).normalize();
  pos.setXYZ(i, x + n.x * d * f, y + n.y * d * f, z + n.z * d * f);
}
headGeo.computeVertexNormals();
const head = new THREE.Mesh(headGeo, plaster); head.castShadow = head.receiveShadow = true;
const bust = new THREE.Group(); bust.add(head);
const neck = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .058, .16, 40), plaster); neck.position.set(0, -.15, -.01); neck.castShadow = neck.receiveShadow = true; bust.add(neck);
const sh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 60, 30, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), plaster); sh.scale.set(.2, .12, .11); sh.position.set(0, -.3, -.01); sh.castShadow = sh.receiveShadow = true; bust.add(sh);
bust.position.set(0, HY, HZ); bust.rotation.y = Math.PI; scene.add(bust);

// ---- lights ----
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#fff4e6', '#5a4a3c', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const top = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff3e2', 0, 6, 22 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.4); top.position.set(0, BH - .02, HZ + .02); top.target.position.set(0, 0, HZ + .02);
top.castShadow = true; top.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); top.shadow.bias = -.0003; top.shadow.camera.near = .3; scene.add(top, top.target);
const topRing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .015, 24), emis('#000', 0)); topRing.position.set(0, BH - .008, HZ + .02); scene.add(topRing);
const fill = new THREE.PointLight('#ffe8cc', 0, 5, 2); fill.position.set(.6, 1.5, 1.8); scene.add(fill);

const VIEWS = {
  v: { pos: [1.05, 1.55, 1.9], tgt: [-.05, 1.45, .2], fov: 50 },  // the vanity from behind the shoulder
  m: { pos: [0, 1.5, 2.6], tgt: [0, 1.35, 0], fov: 52 },          // the vanity straight on (bust hidden)
  u: { pos: [.35, 1.25, 1.2], tgt: [0, 2.3, .5], fov: 60 },       // looking up at the downlight over the head
};
// the face itself is drawn as a 2D diagram in the reel: a sculpted 3D face reads uncanny at phone size
// state "top:<0..1>|side:<0..1>|bust:0|1"  presets: top | side | dark
window.setState = (st) => {
  const preset = { top: 'top:1', side: 'top:.12|side:1', dark: 'top:0' }[st] ?? st.replace(/^(top|side|dark)(\|)/, (m, k, bar) => ({ top: 'top:1', side: 'top:.12|side:1', dark: 'top:0' })[k] + bar);
  const o = Object.fromEntries(preset.split('|').map(p => p.split(':')).map(([k, v]) => [k, +v]));
  const T = o.top ?? 0, S = o.side ?? 0;
  bust.visible = (o.bust ?? 1) > 0;
  top.intensity = 18 * T; topRing.material = T ? emis('#fff3e2', 6 * T) : emis('#000', 0);
  for (const b of bars) { b.l.intensity = 14 * S; b.m.material = S ? emis('#fff0dc', 5 * S) : emis('#000', 0); }
  fill.intensity = S ? .4 : .05;
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .06 * S;
  scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .05 * S;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/newhome.html`

```html
// ===== New home, 5 lighting mistakes and their fixes in one flat: mixed colour temperatures, a "sieve" ceiling of spots, a downlight over the head at the vanity, a bare LED strip that yellowed, and no dimmer =====
// living room: back wall z=0 (x -3..3) with a TV wall and a gypsum cove above; sofa in front; a vanity niche on the right wall (x=3) with a mirror and basin; window on the left wall; ceiling 2.8
const CH = 2.8, RW = 3.0, RD = 5.2;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#b49a7a', '80,55,30')(g, w, h); }, [3, 3]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#ebe6df', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// window on the left wall: night sky
box(.02, 1.4, 1.6, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#141b2b' }), -RW + .01, .9, 2.4, { cast: false });
for (const z of [2.4, 3.2, 4.0]) box(.04, 1.44, .04, M.black, -RW, .88, z - .02);
// TV wall: dark panel, TV, low console; a gypsum cove pocket above it holding the LED strip
box(3.4, 2.1, .05, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d9d2c7', roughness: .85 }), -1.7, 0, -.06);
box(1.5, .85, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0d0d10', roughness: .2, metalness: .3 }), -.75, 1.0, .0);
box(2.2, .4, .4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9df', roughness: .5 }), -1.1, 0, .0, { r: .02 });
const gyp = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1efea', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, .1, .35, gyp, -RW, 2.55, -.07, { cast: false });                 // drop band
box(RW * 2, .06, .02, gyp, -RW, 2.65, .26, { cast: false });                 // lip that hides the strip
// the strip: segments so a "bare, aged" strip can show patches; a profile with an opal cover for the fixed version
const SEG = 24, segs = [];
for (let i = 0; i < SEG; i++) { const m = box(RW * 2 / SEG - .004, .006, .012, emis('#000', 0), -RW + i * RW * 2 / SEG, 2.655, .2, { cast: false }); segs.push(m); }
const prof = box(RW * 2, .018, .03, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9ccd0', metalness: .8, roughness: .35 }), -RW, 2.648, .185, { cast: false });
const cover = box(RW * 2, .004, .022, emis('#000', 0), -RW, 2.667, .19, { cast: false });
const stripL = [-2, 0, 2].map((x) => { const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd9ad', 0, 2, .05); l.position.set(x, 2.67, .2); l.lookAt(x, CH + 1, -.3); scene.add(l); return l; });
// sofa, rug, coffee table, floor plant
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8c8a84', roughness: .95 });
box(2.4, .42, .9, sofaM, -1.2, 0, 3.3, { r: .06 }); box(2.4, .5, .2, sofaM, -1.2, .42, 4.0, { r: .05 });
plane(2.6, 1.7, M.rug, [0, .006, 2.3], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(1.0, .38, .55, M.darkWood, -.5, 0, 2.1, { r: .02 });
plant(2.3, 3.9, 1.5, .45);
// vanity niche on the right wall: mirror, basin counter, a downlight right above the head position, and side bars by the mirror
box(.03, 1.0, .8, new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#dfe6ea', metalness: 1, roughness: .05 }), RW - .04, 1.15, 1.0, { cast: false });
box(.5, .85, 1.1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#ffffff', roughness: .35 }), RW - .5, 0, .85, { r: .02 });
box(.36, .04, .5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f5f5f3', roughness: .2 }), RW - .43, .85, 1.15);
const bars = [.9, 1.9].map((z) => box(.04, .9, .05, emis('#000', 0), RW - .06, 1.2, z - .025, { cast: false }));
const barL = [.9, 1.9].map((z) => { const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff1e0', 0, .05, .9); l.position.set(RW - .07, 1.65, z); l.lookAt(RW - 1.5, 1.6, z); scene.add(l); return l; });
const vanTop = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff4e8', 0, 4, 28 * Math.PI / 180, .4, 1.4); vanTop.position.set(RW - .55, CH - .03, 1.4); vanTop.target.position.set(RW - .55, 0, 1.4); vanTop.castShadow = true; scene.add(vanTop, vanTop.target);
const vanRing = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); vanRing.position.set(RW - .55, CH - .01, 1.4); scene.add(vanRing);
// a simple head shape (no face) standing at the mirror so the shadow under the "eyes" reads; it is a matte dome, not a person
const headM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a58a', roughness: .8 });
const dome = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.11, 24, 18), headM); dome.position.set(RW - .75, 1.62, 1.4); dome.castShadow = true; scene.add(dome);
const brow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.04, .02, .16), headM); brow.position.set(RW - .65, 1.64, 1.4); brow.castShadow = true; scene.add(brow);
// ceiling spots: a planned set of 6 and a "sieve" of 20; each can be warm or cold
const mkSpot = (x, z) => { const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .045, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); scene.add(ring); const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 6, 34 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.4); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z < 1 ? z - .35 : z); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s }; };
const plan = [[-2.2, .45], [-1.1, .45], [0, .45], [1.1, .45], [-1.4, 2.9], [.6, 2.9]].map(([x, z]) => mkSpot(x, z));
const sieve = []; for (let ix = 0; ix < 5; ix++) for (let iz = 0; iz < 4; iz++) sieve.push(mkSpot(-2.3 + ix * 1.05, 1.0 + iz * .9));
// spots quiz: a crowded 4x4 grid of 16 vs 8 spots spaced evenly (two rows of four, 1.4 m apart)
const crowd = []; for (let ix = 0; ix < 4; ix++) for (let iz = 0; iz < 4; iz++) crowd.push(mkSpot(-1.5 + ix * 1.0, .9 + iz * .75));
const even = []; for (let ix = 0; ix < 4; ix++) for (const z of [1.1, 3.1]) even.push(mkSpot(-2.1 + ix * 1.4, z));
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#e9e4dc', '#2a2018', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  q: { pos: [0, 1.45, 5.05], tgt: [-.1, 1.75, 1.2], fov: 72 },
  k: { pos: [0, 1.1, 4.95], tgt: [0, 2.8, 2.0], fov: 92 },          // looking up: the whole ceiling, every spot in view (spots quiz)     // the room with the ceiling in view (spots quiz)
  w: { pos: [.9, 1.75, 5.1], tgt: [-.5, 1.35, 0], fov: 60 },      // the living room
  c: { pos: [.2, 1.3, 4.6], tgt: [0, 2.8, 1.9], fov: 70 },         // looking up at the ceiling
  b: { pos: [1.2, 1.55, 1.45], tgt: [2.95, 1.55, 1.4], fov: 52 },  // the vanity mirror
  p: { pos: [-.4, 2.25, 1.15], tgt: [-.6, 2.62, .2], fov: 44 },    // the strip pocket over the TV wall
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  mix (plan spots alternate 2700/6500 K) · plan (6 spots, 3000 K) · sieve (20 spots) · vtop (downlight over the head at the vanity) · vside (side bars by the mirror) · bare (strip without profile, yellowed and patchy) · strip (strip in a profile, even) · crowd (16 spots in a tight 4x4 grid, cold) · even (8 spots in two rows of four, 3000 K) · dim:<0..1> (dimmer level for everything, default 1)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const mx = T.mix ?? 0, pl = T.plan ?? 0, sv = T.sieve ?? 0, vt = T.vtop ?? 0, vs = T.vside ?? 0, br = T.bare ?? 0, st = T.strip ?? 0, dm = T.dim ?? 1;
  const warm = '#ffd9a8', cold = '#e6efff';
  plan.forEach(({ ring, s }, i) => { const on = pl || mx; const c = mx ? (i % 2 ? cold : '#ffc27a') : warm; s.color.set(c); s.intensity = on ? 18 * dm : 0; ring.material = on ? emis(c, 3 * dm) : emis('#000', 0); });
  sieve.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.color.set('#fff2e2'); s.intensity = sv ? 7 * dm : 0; ring.material = sv ? emis('#fff2e2', 3 * dm) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const cr = T.crowd ?? 0, ev = T.even ?? 0;
  const quiz = cr || ev; plan.concat(sieve).forEach(({ ring }) => ring.visible = !quiz); crowd.forEach(({ ring }) => ring.visible = !!cr); even.forEach(({ ring }) => ring.visible = !!ev);
  crowd.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.color.set('#f4f6ff'); s.intensity = cr ? 10 * dm : 0; ring.material = cr ? emis('#f4f6ff', 9 * dm) : emis('#000', 0); });
  even.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.color.set(warm); s.intensity = ev ? 26 * dm : 0; ring.material = ev ? emis(warm, 3 * dm) : emis('#000', 0); });
  vanTop.intensity = vt ? 22 * dm : 0; vanRing.material = vt ? emis('#fff4e8', 3) : emis('#000', 0);
  barL.forEach((l) => l.intensity = vs ? 14 * dm : 0); bars.forEach((m) => m.material = vs ? emis('#fff1e0', 4 * dm) : emis('#000', 0));
  prof.visible = !!st; cover.material = st ? emis('#ffd9ad', 5 * dm) : emis('#000', 0);
  segs.forEach((m, i) => { m.visible = !st; if (!br) { m.material = emis('#000', 0); return; } const dead = (i % 7 === 3) || (i % 11 === 5); m.material = dead ? emis('#3a2a10', .2) : emis(i % 3 ? '#ffb347' : '#e8a23a', (2 + (i % 4)) * dm); });
  stripL.forEach((l, i) => { l.color.set(br ? '#ffb04a' : '#ffd9ad'); l.intensity = st ? 16 * dm : br ? (i === 1 ? 4 : 9) * dm : 0; });
  hemi.intensity = .05 + .05 * (pl || mx || sv || cr || ev ? 1 : 0) * dm; scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .04 * (pl || mx || sv || cr || ev ? 1 : 0) * dm;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = .45 + .55 * dm;
};
```

```html
// ===== Perfume shop: one flat ceiling panel (glass looks dead) vs hidden light under every shelf + backlit glass + a narrow 15° spot on the hero bottle =====
// display wall z=0 (x -2..2): 4 glass shelves with backlit panel behind, bottles on each; a hero plinth in front centre with the star bottle; ceiling 3.0
const CH = 3.0, RW = 2.6, RD = 5.0;
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#e7e1d8', roughness: .15 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#2b2622', roughness: .8 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d8cfc2', roughness: .9 }), -RW - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d8cfc2', roughness: .9 }), RW, 0, -.1);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// display unit: dark wood frame, backlit panel, glass shelves with a hidden strip under the front edge of each
const frameM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a2a1e', roughness: .45 });
box(4.2, 2.5, .05, frameM, -2.1, .3, .0); box(.1, 2.5, .4, frameM, -2.1, .3, 0); box(.1, 2.5, .4, frameM, 2.0, .3, 0); box(4.2, .3, .45, frameM, -2.1, 0, 0);
const backM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3ead9', emissive: '#ffe2b8', emissiveIntensity: 0, roughness: .6 });
const back = box(4.0, 2.3, .02, backM, -2.0, .4, .05, { cast: false });
const glassM = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#e8f0f2', roughness: .02, transmission: .9, transparent: true, opacity: .35, thickness: .01 });
const shelfYs = [.75, 1.25, 1.75, 2.25];
const shelfStrips = [], shelfLights = [];
for (const y of shelfYs) {
  box(3.9, .015, .34, glassM, -1.95, y, .06, { cast: false });
  const st = box(3.8, .006, .012, emis('#000', 0), -1.9, y - .01, .36, { cast: false }); shelfStrips.push(st);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe6c4', 0, 3.8, .04); l.position.set(0, y - .02, .3); l.lookAt(0, y - 1, .15); scene.add(l); shelfLights.push(l);
}
// perfume bottles: faceted glass bodies with metal caps, amber / clear / rose
const tints = ['#d9a24a', '#f2efe8', '#d78aa0', '#7fb3a6', '#2a2a2a', '#c9b37a'];
const capM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', metalness: 1, roughness: .2 });
const bottle = (x, y, z, s, tint) => {
  const m = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: tint, roughness: .03, transmission: .75, thickness: .05, ior: 1.5, transparent: true, opacity: .92, clearcoat: 1 });
  const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045 * s, .05 * s, .12 * s, 8), m); b.position.set(x, y + .06 * s, z); b.castShadow = true; scene.add(b);
  const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.022 * s, .022 * s, .045 * s, 16), capM); c.position.set(x, y + .14 * s, z); scene.add(c);
};
shelfYs.forEach((y, r) => { for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) bottle(-1.7 + i * .42 + (r % 2) * .1, y + .015, .2, 1 + R() * .3, tints[(i + r) % tints.length]); });
// hero plinth with the star bottle and a small box
box(.6, 1.0, .6, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2ede4', roughness: .3 }), -.3, 0, 1.4, { r: .02 });
bottle(0, 1.0, 1.7, 2.6, '#d9a24a');
box(.22, .14, .1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1d1a17', roughness: .5 }), .08, 1.0, 1.55, { r: .01 });
// counter on the right, a mirror and a plant
box(1.4, .95, .6, frameM, 1.0, 0, 3.0, { r: .02 }); box(1.45, .04, .65, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#efe9e0', roughness: .2 }), .98, .95, 2.98);
plant(-2.2, 2.6, 1.6, .5);
// BEFORE: one flat cold ceiling panel
const panel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.2, .6), emis('#000', 0)); panel.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; panel.position.set(0, CH - .005, 2.0); scene.add(panel);
const panelL = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef4ff', 0, 1.2, .6); panelL.position.set(0, CH - .01, 2.0); panelL.lookAt(0, 0, 2.0); scene.add(panelL);
// AFTER: a narrow 15° spot on the hero bottle (from a track on the ceiling)
box(3.0, .04, .04, M.black, -1.5, CH - .04, 2.2, { cast: false });
const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .05, .16, 20), M.black); head.position.set(0, CH - .14, 2.2); head.rotation.x = -.5; scene.add(head);
const hero = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff0dc', 0, 5, 12 * Math.PI / 180, .3, 1.0); hero.position.set(0, CH - .2, 2.15); hero.target.position.set(0, 1.3, 1.7); hero.castShadow = true; scene.add(hero, hero.target);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#f4ecdf', '#2a2018', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.2, 1.6, 4.6], tgt: [-.2, 1.3, .6], fov: 60 },     // the shop wall and the plinth
  s: { pos: [.6, 1.45, 1.4], tgt: [-.3, 1.5, .1], fov: 55 },       // close on the shelves
  h: { pos: [.5, 1.35, 2.6], tgt: [0, 1.2, 1.7], fov: 42 },        // the hero bottle on the plinth
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · panel (flat ceiling panel) · shelves:<0..1> (shelf strips bottom to top + backlight) · hero (15° spot) · all (= shelves+hero)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'shelves+hero').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const pa = T.panel ?? 0, sh = T.shelves ?? 0, he = T.hero ?? 0;
  panelL.intensity = 14 * pa; panel.material = pa ? emis('#f4f8ff', 2.5) : emis('#000', 0);
  shelfLights.forEach((l, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sh * 1.1 - i / 4) / .3)); l.intensity = 9 * k; shelfStrips[i].material = k ? emis('#ffe2b8', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  backM.emissiveIntensity = .35 * Math.min(1, sh);
  hero.intensity = 70 * he;
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .08 * pa + .03 * Math.max(sh, he); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .06 * pa + .03 * Math.max(sh, he);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```
