## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/phoneshop.html`

```html
// ===== Phone shop: one cold white panel over everything (screens catch the glare, the phones get lost) vs a hidden strip inside every vitrine (each phone lit from below, a jewel) + 4000 K spots over the demo table (clear screens, no reflection) =====
// back wall z=0 (x -3.2..3.2) with a dark slatted display and 3 glass shelves of phones; two glass vitrines in front (left and right); a demo table in the middle; ceiling 3.0
const CH = 3.0, RW = 3.2, RD = 6.0;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#cfcac3'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 6000; i++) { const c = 180 + R() * 50; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c},${c * .97},.3)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 3, 3); } g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(90,90,90,.25)'; g.lineWidth = 2; for (let x = 0; x <= w; x += 341) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 0); g.lineTo(x, h); g.stroke(); g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, x); g.lineTo(w, x); g.stroke(); } }, [3, 3]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .25 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e6e3de', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// screen wallpapers: a few colourful gradients
const papers = [['#3a1c71', '#d76d77', '#ffaf7b'], ['#0f2027', '#2c5364', '#46c3db'], ['#1d2b64', '#6a82fb', '#fc5c7d'], ['#134e5e', '#71b280', '#d9f0a3'], ['#41295a', '#2f0743', '#f7971e'], ['#000428', '#004e92', '#7fd1ff']];
const screenMats = papers.map(([a, b, c]) => { const t = canvasTex(128, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w * .4, h); gr.addColorStop(0, a); gr.addColorStop(.55, b); gr.addColorStop(1, c); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.85)'; g.font = 'bold 34px sans-serif'; g.textAlign = 'center'; g.fillText('10:09', w / 2, 70); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.25)'; for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(22 + (i % 4) * 28, 170 + Math.floor(i / 4) * 32, 9, 0, 7); g.fill(); } }, [1, 1]); return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: t, emissive: '#ffffff', emissiveMap: t, emissiveIntensity: .9, roughness: .08, metalness: .2 }); });
const glareTex = canvasTex(128, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); gr.addColorStop(.35, 'rgba(255,255,255,.95)'); gr.addColorStop(.55, 'rgba(255,255,255,.35)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1]);
const glares = [];
const bodyCols = ['#c9ccd1', '#2b2d31', '#3b5675', '#d8c3a5', '#6c5a7e', '#1d1f22'];
let pi = 0;
// a phone standing on a small clear stand, leaning back; facing +z unless ry given
const phone = (x, y, z, s = 1, ry = 0, lean = .25) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(x, y, z); g.rotation.y = ry; scene.add(g);
  const st = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.05 * s, .01, .05 * s), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e8eaec', roughness: .2, transparent: true, opacity: .6 })); st.position.y = .005; g.add(st);
  const p = new THREE.Group(); p.position.set(0, .01, 0); p.rotation.x = -lean; g.add(p);
  const b = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(.075 * s, .158 * s, .008 * s, 3, .008 * s), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: bodyCols[pi % bodyCols.length], metalness: .7, roughness: .25 })); b.position.y = .079 * s; b.castShadow = true; p.add(b);
  const sc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.069 * s, .152 * s), screenMats[pi % screenMats.length]); sc.position.set(0, .079 * s, .0042 * s); p.add(sc);
  const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.069 * s, .152 * s), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: glareTex, transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false })); gl.position.set(0, .079 * s, .0046 * s); p.add(gl); glares.push(gl);
  pi++; return g;
};
// BACK WALL: dark slatted panel, 3 glass shelves, 7 phones each; a hidden strip under each shelf
box(5.4, 2.2, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, color: '#3a3836', roughness: .6 }), -2.7, .5, -.02);
const glassShelf = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#dfeef2', roughness: .05, transparent: true, opacity: .35 });
const shelfY = [1.05, 1.5, 1.95], wallStrips = [], wallL = [];
shelfY.forEach((y) => {
  box(5.0, .012, .3, glassShelf, -2.5, y, .02, { cast: false });
  for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) phone(-2.0 + k * .8, y + .012, .17, 1.3, 0, .2);
  wallStrips.push(box(5.0, .006, .012, emis('#000', 0), -2.5, y + .41, .28, { cast: false }));
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#f4f6ff', 0, 5.0, .04); l.position.set(0, y + .4, .26); l.lookAt(0, y - 1, .1); scene.add(l); wallL.push(l);
});
// two glass vitrines: white base, glass top and front, two levels of phones inside, a hidden strip along the inside front edge of each level
const baseM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3f2ef', roughness: .35 });
const glassM = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#e6f1f4', roughness: .03, metalness: .1, transparent: true, opacity: .16, depthWrite: false });
const caseStrips = [], caseL = [];
for (const x0 of [-2.7, .6]) {
  box(2.1, .5, .7, baseM, x0, 0, 1.2, { r: .01 });                               // base
  box(2.1, .5, .02, glassM, x0, .5, 1.88, { cast: false }); box(2.1, .02, .7, glassM, x0, 1.0, 1.2, { cast: false });   // front glass + top glass
  box(.02, .5, .7, glassM, x0, .5, 1.2, { cast: false }); box(.02, .5, .7, glassM, x0 + 2.08, .5, 1.2, { cast: false });
  box(2.1, .5, .02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c1a18', roughness: .7 }), x0, .5, 1.2);                   // dark back inside the case
  box(2.06, .01, .66, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c1a18', roughness: .8 }), x0 + .02, .5, 1.22);           // dark velvet floor
  box(2.06, .14, .22, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1c1a18', roughness: .8 }), x0 + .02, .51, 1.25);
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) phone(x0 + .3 + k * .5, .65, 1.34, 1.45, 0, .35);                                            // upper step, back row
  for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) phone(x0 + .55 + k * .5, .51, 1.64, 1.45, 0, .5);                                            // front row
  for (const zz of [1.86, 1.47]) {
    caseStrips.push(box(2.0, .006, .01, emis('#000', 0), x0 + .05, zz > 1.8 ? .515 : .655, zz, { cast: false }));
    const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#f4f6ff', 0, 2.0, .05); l.position.set(x0 + 1.05, zz > 1.8 ? .52 : .66, zz); l.lookAt(x0 + 1.05, 1.0, zz - .35); scene.add(l); caseL.push(l);
  }
  const top = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#f4f6ff', 0, 2.0, .04); top.position.set(x0 + 1.05, .99, 1.85); top.lookAt(x0 + 1.05, .4, 1.45); scene.add(top); caseL.push(top);
}
// demo table in the middle: light oak, phones and a tablet on stands
const tabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#c9a77f', roughness: .45 });
box(1.9, .06, .9, tabM, -.95, .86, 3.0, { r: .01 }); box(1.7, .86, .7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1efea', roughness: .4 }), -.85, 0, 3.1, { r: .01 });
for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) phone(-.66 + k * .4, .92, 3.5, 1.6, 0, .35);
for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) phone(-.46 + k * .4, .92, 3.15, 1.6, 0, .25);
// a tablet
{ const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(.75, .92, 3.45); g.rotation.y = -.5; scene.add(g); const p = new THREE.Group(); p.rotation.x = -.5; g.add(p);
  const b = new THREE.Mesh(new RoundedBoxGeometry(.25, .18, .008, 3, .01), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8f9399', metalness: .7, roughness: .25 })); b.position.y = .09; p.add(b);
  const sc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.236, .166), screenMats[1]); sc.position.set(0, .09, .0042); p.add(sc);
  const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.236, .166), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: glareTex, transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthWrite: false })); gl.position.set(0, .09, .0046); p.add(gl); glares.push(gl); }
plant(2.75, 5.3, 1.6, .45); plant(-2.75, 5.3, 1.6, .45);
// BEFORE: cold flat panels
const panels = [], panelOff = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d2d3d5', roughness: .6 });
for (const x of [-1.6, 0, 1.6]) for (const z of [1.5, 3.6]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.6, .6), panelOff); m.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; m.position.set(x, CH - .005, z); scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef4ff', 0, .6, .6); l.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l); panels.push({ m, l });
}
// AFTER: 4000 K spots over the demo table (aimed from behind the customer so screens stay clear) and grazing the back wall
const mkSpot = (x, z, tx, ty, tz, ang, int, shadow = false) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .04, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff1de', 0, 7, ang * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.2); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(tx, ty, tz); s.castShadow = shadow; scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s, int };
};
const spots = [mkSpot(-.5, 3.0, -.5, .9, 3.4, 22, 18, true), mkSpot(.5, 3.0, .5, .9, 3.4, 22, 18, true), mkSpot(-1.8, .9, -1.8, 1.5, 0, 28, 14), mkSpot(0, .9, 0, 1.5, 0, 28, 14), mkSpot(1.8, .9, 1.8, 1.5, 0, 28, 14)];
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#e9ecf0', '#24221f', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const amb = new THREE.PointLight('#fff4e6', 0, 9, 1.6); amb.position.set(0, 1.9, 4.8); scene.add(amb);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.2, 1.7, 5.7], tgt: [-.2, 1.0, 1.0], fov: 64 },       // the shop from the door
  v: { pos: [-1.6, 1.22, 2.55], tgt: [-1.6, .66, 1.48], fov: 46 },    // into the left vitrine
  t: { pos: [.1, 1.38, 4.4], tgt: [0, 1.02, 3.3], fov: 44 },         // the demo table
  s: { pos: [-.6, 1.5, 3.6], tgt: [-.6, 1.45, 0], fov: 44 },         // the back wall shelves
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  panels (cold flat panels: glare on every screen, dull) · case:<0..1> (hidden strips inside the vitrines, then the wall shelves) · spots (4000 K over the table + the wall) · all (= case+spots)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'case+spots').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const pn = T.panels ?? 0, cs = T.case ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0;
  panels.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 9 * pn; m.material = pn ? emis('#f4f8ff', 2.5) : panelOff; });
  const rows = [...caseL.map((l, i) => [l, caseStrips[i]]), ...wallL.map((l, i) => [l, wallStrips[i]])];
  rows.forEach(([l, m], i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (cs * 1.1 - i / rows.length) / .2)); l.intensity = 18 * k; if (m) m.material = k ? emis('#f4f6ff', 8 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s, int }) => { s.intensity = int * sp; ring.material = sp ? emis('#fff1de', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  glares.forEach((g) => g.material.opacity = .55 * pn);
  screenMats.forEach((m) => m.emissiveIntensity = pn ? .45 : .9);
  const on = Math.min(1, cs + sp);
  amb.intensity = 1.2 + 1.0 * on; hemi.intensity = .06 + .06 * pn + .06 * on; scene.environmentIntensity = .04 + .05 * pn + .05 * on;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/reception.html`

```html
// ===== Company reception: flat ceiling panels everywhere vs a designed scheme (lit logo wall, hidden strip under the counter, linear pendant over the desk) =====
// feature wall z=0 (x -3..3), floor y=0, ceiling 3.0; reception counter in front of the wall; waiting area on the right
// the logo is a generic mark (not a real company)
const CH = 3.0, RW = 3.2;
marbleTex.repeat.set(4, 4);
plane(8, 8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#e9e5de', roughness: .2 }), [0, 0, 3], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e4e0da', roughness: .9 });
box(6.4, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, 7, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, 7, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(6.4, .1, 7, M.ceil, -RW, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// feature wall: vertical wood slats behind the counter, the logo in brushed metal letters
const slatM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#b8885a', roughness: .55 });
for (let i = 0; i < 44; i++) box(.072, CH - .02, .05, slatM, -2.2 + i * .1, .01, .0);
box(4.4, CH - .02, .02, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#4a3626', roughness: .9 }), -2.2, .01, -.02);
const logoM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9d4cc', metalness: .85, roughness: .25 });
const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.26, .045, 16, 64), logoM); ring.position.set(-.85, 2.3, .1); scene.add(ring);
box(.09, .52, .05, logoM, -.895, 2.04, .07); // a simple monogram mark
[[-.38, .5], [-.1, .5], [.18, .5], [.46, .5], [.74, .5]].forEach(([x], i) => box(.15, .28, .04, logoM, x, 2.16, .07));      // wordmark blocks
const logoSpot = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff4e6', 0, 6, 20 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.3); logoSpot.position.set(.4, CH - .05, 1.3); logoSpot.target.position.set(.4, 2.25, 0); scene.add(logoSpot, logoSpot.target);
const logoSpot2 = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff4e6', 0, 6, 20 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.3); logoSpot2.position.set(-.75, CH - .05, 1.3); logoSpot2.target.position.set(-.75, 2.25, 0); scene.add(logoSpot2, logoSpot2.target);
// reception counter: solid body with an overhanging top; hidden strip under the overhang
const counterM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1eee9', roughness: .5 });
box(2.6, 1.05, .6, counterM, -1.3, 0, 1.25, { r: .02 });
box(2.8, .05, .8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#4a4440', roughness: .2 }), -1.4, 1.05, 1.15);
const ctrStrip = box(2.5, .01, .02, emis('#000', 0), -1.25, 1.03, 1.86, { cast: false });
const ctrWash = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe2c0', 0, 2.5, .06); ctrWash.position.set(0, 1.02, 1.88); ctrWash.lookAt(0, 0, 2.0); scene.add(ctrWash);
// desk behind: monitor, chair (no person)
box(1.6, .75, .6, M.darkWood, -.8, 0, .35); box(.5, .32, .03, M.black, -.25, .9, .5);
// linear pendant over the desk
const pend = box(1.6, .05, .06, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1b1d', metalness: .6, roughness: .4 }), -.8, 1.95, .62);
for (const x of [-.75, .75]) box(.004, CH - 2.0, .004, M.black, x, 2.0, .65, { cast: false });
const pendLens = box(1.56, .01, .04, emis('#000', 0), -.78, 1.94, .63, { cast: false });
const pendL = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff1e0', 0, 1.5, .05); pendL.position.set(0, 1.93, .65); pendL.lookAt(0, 0, .65); scene.add(pendL);
// waiting area: sofa + side table + plant on the right
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#4f5b66', roughness: .9 });
box(.8, .42, 1.8, sofaM, 2.2, 0, 2.2, { r: .06 }); box(.2, .45, 1.8, sofaM, 2.85, .42, 2.2, { r: .05 });
plant(2.6, 1.6, 1.8, .5);
// ceiling: flat 60x60 panels in a grid (the "before") and a few small downlights (the designed scheme)
const panels = [], panelOff = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cfd0d2', roughness: .6 });
for (let ix = 0; ix < 4; ix++) for (let iz = 0; iz < 3; iz++) {
  const x = -2.4 + ix * 1.6, z = .9 + iz * 1.7;
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.6, .6), panelOff); m.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; m.position.set(x, CH - .005, z); scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef4ff', 0, .6, .6); l.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l);
  panels.push({ m, l });
}
const downs = [[2.2, 2.6], [-2.2, 3.4], [.5, 3.4]].map(([x, z]) => { const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe6c8', 0, 7, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.4); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target); return s; });
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#f4ecdf', '#3a3028', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.2, 1.6, 6.2], tgt: [-.4, 1.4, .5], fov: 62 },     // the lobby from the entrance
  c: { pos: [1.5, 1.35, 3.9], tgt: [-.7, 1.15, 1.0], fov: 56 },    // the counter, close
  l: { pos: [-.1, 1.75, 4.6], tgt: [-.1, 1.85, 0], fov: 52 },     // the logo wall
};
// tokens joined by '+':  off · panels (flat panels everywhere, cold) · logo · counter · linear · downs   (design = logo+counter+linear+downs)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('design', 'logo+counter+linear+downs').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const pn = T.panels ?? 0, lg = T.logo ?? 0, ct = T.counter ?? 0, ln = T.linear ?? 0, dn = T.downs ?? 0;
  panels.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 9 * pn; m.material = pn ? emis('#f4f8ff', 2.5) : panelOff; });
  logoSpot.intensity = 30 * lg; logoSpot2.intensity = 30 * lg;
  ctrStrip.material = ct ? emis('#ffe2c0', 5) : emis('#000', 0); ctrWash.intensity = 14 * ct;
  pendLens.material = ln ? emis('#fff1e0', 5) : emis('#000', 0); pendL.intensity = 22 * ln;
  downs.forEach((s) => s.intensity = 14 * dn);
  hemi.intensity = .05 + .1 * pn + .05 * dn; scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .08 * pn + .03 * dn;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/restaurant.html`

```html
// ===== Restaurant dining room: flat white ceiling panels (food looks dull, nobody takes photos) vs a narrow 2700 K spot over every table, high-CRI colour on the plates, and a soft wall wash =====
// back wall z=0 (x -3.2..3.2) with vertical wood slats; 4 tables in two rows; plates of food on each table (tomato red, salad green, golden fries, a steak); ceiling 3.0
const CH = 3.0, RW = 3.2, RD = 6.0;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#3b3632'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { const c = 50 + R() * 30; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c * .95},${c * .9},.4)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2, 2); } }, [6, 6]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .6 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#cfc4b6', roughness: .9 });
box(RW * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2622', roughness: .9 }), -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
box(RW * 2 - .1, CH - .02, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: flutedTex, color: '#b08868', roughness: .6 }), -RW + .05, .01, -.07);
// a long banquette along the back wall
box(RW * 2 - .4, .45, .55, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#5b2f2a', roughness: .9 }), -RW + .2, 0, .0, { r: .04 });
box(RW * 2 - .4, .55, .14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#5b2f2a', roughness: .9 }), -RW + .2, .45, -.02, { r: .04 });
// tables with plates; food materials keep two colour sets: dull (low CRI under cold panels) and vivid (high CRI)
const tableM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#5a3d28', roughness: .45 });
const chairM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1e1b18', roughness: .5 });
const plateM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#fbfaf7', roughness: .25 });
const foods = [];   // { m, vivid, dull }
const food = (vivid, dull, rough = .55) => { const m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: dull, roughness: rough }); foods.push({ m, vivid, dull }); return m; };
const tomatoM = food('#d6261c', '#8d5a52'), saladM = food('#3f9a2c', '#6d7a5e'), friesM = food('#f0b43a', '#b49c72'), steakM = food('#7a3b22', '#5e4a40', .7), sauceM = food('#e8d27a', '#b9ad8c', .3);
const tables = [[-1.6, 1.4], [1.6, 1.4], [-1.6, 3.4], [1.6, 3.4]];
tables.forEach(([x, z], ti) => {
  box(1.1, .04, .8, tableM, x - .55, .74, z - .4, { r: .01 });
  box(.08, .74, .08, chairM, x - .04, 0, z - .04);
  for (const dz of [-.65, .65]) { box(.42, .45, .42, chairM, x - .21, 0, z + dz - .21, { r: .03 }); box(.42, .45, .05, chairM, x - .21, .45, z + dz + (dz < 0 ? -.23 : .18), { r: .02 }); }
  for (const [px, pz, kind] of [[-.25, -.15, 0], [.25, .15, 1]]) {
    const p = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.14, .1, .025, 36), plateM); p.position.set(x + px, .79, z + pz); p.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(p);
    if ((kind + ti) % 2 === 0) { // salad with tomatoes
      const s = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.08, 18, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), saladM); s.position.set(x + px, .8, z + pz); s.scale.y = .6; s.castShadow = true; scene.add(s);
      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const tm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.022, 12, 8), tomatoM); tm.position.set(x + px + Math.cos(k * 1.3) * .06, .835, z + pz + Math.sin(k * 1.3) * .06); scene.add(tm); }
    } else { // steak, fries and sauce
      const st = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07, .075, .03, 20), steakM); st.position.set(x + px - .03, .81, z + pz); st.scale.z = .7; st.castShadow = true; scene.add(st);
      for (let k = 0; k < 9; k++) { const f = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.012, .012, .08), friesM); f.position.set(x + px + .06 + (k % 3) * .015, .815 + Math.floor(k / 3) * .01, z + pz - .02 + (k % 2) * .02); f.rotation.y = k * .7; scene.add(f); }
      const sc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.025, .02, .02, 16), sauceM); sc.position.set(x + px - .02, .81, z + pz + .085); scene.add(sc);
    }
    const gl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.03, .025, .12, 16), new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#e9f1f3', transmission: .8, roughness: .05, transparent: true, opacity: .5 })); gl.position.set(x + px + .18, .82, z + pz - .18); scene.add(gl);
  }
});
plant(-2.8, 5.4, 1.6, .45); plant(2.8, 5.4, 1.6, .45);
// BEFORE: flat cold panels in the ceiling
const panels = [], panelOff = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#4a4744', roughness: .6 });
for (const x of [-1.6, 1.6]) for (const z of [1.4, 3.4, 5.0]) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.6, .6), panelOff); m.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; m.position.set(x, CH - .005, z); scene.add(m);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#eef4ff', 0, .6, .6); l.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l); panels.push({ m, l });
}
// AFTER: a narrow 2700 K spot over each table (black ceiling, the table glows), and a soft wash on the slat wall
const spots = tables.map(([x, z]) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .04, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf98', 0, 6, 15 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.2); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(x, .76, z); s.castShadow = true; scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s };
});
const wash = [-2.4, -.8, .8, 2.4].map((x) => { const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffbf80', 0, 5, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .8, 1.4); s.position.set(x, CH - .05, .45); s.target.position.set(x, .4, -.05); scene.add(s, s.target); return s; });
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#e7e1d8', '#1a1612', .04); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  q: { pos: [-1.2, 1.75, 2.75], tgt: [-1.6, .78, 1.35], fov: 50 },   // the table from the other side, plates in front (3-questions reel)
  w: { pos: [-.4, 2.0, 5.6], tgt: [-1.35, .8, 2.3], fov: 60 },        // the dining room
  t: { pos: [-.6, 1.35, 2.35], tgt: [-1.55, .78, 1.4], fov: 50 }, // one table, close
  d: { pos: [-1.45, 1.05, 1.75], tgt: [-1.75, .8, 1.35], fov: 42 },// the plates, macro
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  panels (flat cold panels, dull food) · spots:<0..1> (table spots, one by one) · cri (vivid high-CRI food colours) · wash (wall wash) · all (= spots+cri+wash)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'spots+cri+wash').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const pn = T.panels ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0, cr = T.cri ?? 0, wa = T.wash ?? 0;
  panels.forEach(({ m, l }) => { l.intensity = 11 * pn; m.material = pn ? emis('#f4f8ff', 2.5) : panelOff; });
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sp * 1.1 - i / 4) / .25)); s.intensity = 13 * k; ring.material = k ? emis('#ffd8a8', 3 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  wash.forEach((s) => s.intensity = 6 * wa);
  foods.forEach(({ m, vivid, dull }) => m.color.set(cr ? vivid : dull));
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .08 * pn + .03 * Math.min(1, sp); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .06 * pn + .02 * Math.min(1, sp);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/roof.html`

```html
// ===== Villa roof terrace on a winter night: pergola with warm string lights, stone feature wall with up-lights, ground spots along the path =====
// deck y=0; stone wall at z=-3.2 (x -3.6..3.6); pergola over the seating (x -2..2, z -2.8..0); path from the camera (z 5) to the seating
// detail station at x = PX: an IP65 ground spot and a wall light under rain, for the close-ups
const deckTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#7d6a58'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 64) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${R() > .5 ? 40 : 120},${R() > .5 ? 30 : 100},20,.18)`; g.fillRect(0, y, w, 60); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,14,8,.6)'; g.fillRect(0, y + 60, w, 4); } }, [5, 5]);
const deckM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: deckTex, roughness: .7 });
plane(14, 14, deckM, [0, 0, 1], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const stoneTex2 = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { // rough split-face stone in courses
  g.fillStyle = '#b9a588'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 64) for (let x = -(y / 64 % 2) * 80; x < w; x += 160 + R() * 60) { const c = 150 + R() * 60; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${c},${c * .88},${c * .72})`; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 3, 150 + R() * 50, 58); for (let k = 0; k < 40; k++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${R() * .12})`; g.fillRect(x + R() * 160, y + R() * 60, 3 + R() * 12, 2 + R() * 6); } }
}, [2, 1.2]);
const stoneM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: stoneTex2, roughness: .95, bumpMap: stoneTex2, bumpScale: 2 });
box(7.4, 2.6, .3, stoneM, -3.7, 0, -3.5);                                         // feature wall (stair room)
const parapetM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e6ddd0', roughness: .9 });
box(.25, 1.1, 8.6, parapetM, -4.0, 0, -3.5); box(.25, 1.1, 8.6, parapetM, 3.75, 0, -3.5);  // side parapets
// planters with plants in front of the stone wall
for (const x of [-3.0, -1.0, 1.0, 3.0]) { box(.7, .45, .5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3b3632', roughness: .8 }), x - .35, 0, -3.15); plant(x, -2.9, 1.5, .45); }
// pergola: dark steel posts and beams
const steel = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2622', roughness: .5, metalness: .6 });
for (const [x, z] of [[-2.1, -2.8], [2.0, -2.8], [-2.1, .2], [2.0, .2]]) box(.1, 2.65, .1, steel, x, 0, z);
for (const z of [-2.8, .2]) box(4.2, .12, .1, steel, -2.1, 2.6, z);
for (const x of [-2.1, 2.0]) box(.1, .12, 3.1, steel, x, 2.6, -2.8);
// outdoor seating: L sofa, cushions, low table, rug, a lantern
const fabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#cdbfa6', roughness: .95 });
box(3.2, .38, .85, fabM, -1.6, 0, -2.6, { r: .05 }); box(3.2, .45, .2, fabM, -1.6, .38, -2.65, { r: .05 });
box(.85, .38, 1.8, fabM, -2.0, 0, -1.75, { r: .05 }); box(.2, .45, 1.8, fabM, -2.05, .38, -1.75, { r: .05 });
[['#b5532f', -1.1], ['#3d5f6b', -.4], ['#b5532f', .5], ['#d9c9a8', 1.1]].forEach(([c, x]) => { const m = box(.42, .38, .13, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x - .21, .38, -2.4, { r: .06 }); m.rotation.x = -.2; });
plane(3.4, 2.4, M.rug, [-.2, .006, -1.3], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(1.1, .32, .7, M.darkWood, -.65, 0, -1.6, { r: .03 });
const mugM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe8dc', roughness: .4 }); for (const x of [-.4, -.15]) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .035, .09, 20), mugM); c.position.set(x, .37, -1.3); scene.add(c); }
// city skyline + night sky behind (emissive, so it reads at night)
const skyTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#05070f'); gr.addColorStop(.7, '#18213a'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#3b3046'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 160; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${R() * .7})`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h * .55, 1.5, 1.5); } }, [1, 1]);
const sky = plane(60, 30, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: skyTex, toneMapped: false }), [0, 10, -22], [0, 0, 0]);
const moon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.9, 40), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#f4f0e2', toneMapped: false })); moon.position.set(8, 13, -21.9); scene.add(moon);
const towerM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0b0d14', roughness: 1 });
for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { const w = 1.5 + R() * 2.5, h = 3 + R() * 9, x = -24 + i * 3.6 + R(); box(w, h, 1, towerM, x, -2, -20);
  for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) if (R() > .45) { const win = plane(.25, .35, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: R() > .3 ? '#ffcf8a' : '#cfe4ff', toneMapped: false }), [x + .3 + R() * (w - .6), -1 + R() * (h - 1), -18.98], [0, 0, 0]); win.material.opacity = .6; } }
const moonL = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#9fb4e0', 0); moonL.position.set(6, 10, -6); scene.add(moonL);

// ---- string lights: three festoon runs across the pergola, sagging between beams ----
const bulbs = []; // { m, u }
const strings = [[-2.05, -2.75, 2.0, .15], [-2.05, .15, 2.0, -2.75], [-2.05, -1.3, 2.0, -1.3]];
const wireM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#111', roughness: .6 });
strings.forEach(([x0, z0, x1, z1], si) => {
  const N = 14, pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 40; i++) { const f = i / 40; pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(x0 + (x1 - x0) * f, 2.58 - Math.sin(f * Math.PI) * .38, z0 + (z1 - z0) * f)); }
  const tube = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TubeGeometry(new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(pts), 60, .006, 6), wireM); scene.add(tube);
  for (let i = 1; i < N; i++) { const f = i / N, x = x0 + (x1 - x0) * f, z = z0 + (z1 - z0) * f, y = 2.58 - Math.sin(f * Math.PI) * .38 - .07;
    const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.045, 16, 12), emis('#000', 0)); b.position.set(x, y, z); scene.add(b);
    bulbs.push({ m: b, u: (si + f) / strings.length }); }
});
const strLights = [[-1.2, 2.2, -1.9], [.9, 2.2, -1.9], [-1.2, 2.2, -.6], [.9, 2.2, -.6], [0, 2.25, -1.3]].map(([x, y, z]) => { const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb866', 0, 5, 2); l.position.set(x, y, z); scene.add(l); return l; });
// ---- stone wall up-lights: grazing light from the ground, between the planters ----
const ups = [-2.0, 0, 2.0, -3.4, 3.4].map((x) => {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf96', 0, 4.5, 22 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.3); s.position.set(x, .05, -3.0); s.target.position.set(x, 2.6, -3.35); scene.add(s, s.target);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .02, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8f9399', metalness: 1, roughness: .3 })); ring.position.set(x, .01, -2.98); scene.add(ring);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.04, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, .021, -2.98); scene.add(lens);
  return { s, lens };
});
// ---- ground spots along the path to the seating ----
const path = [];
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  const x = sx * .9 + .1, z = 3.6 - i * 1.0;
  const l = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 1.6, 2); l.position.set(x, .15, z); scene.add(l);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 20), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8f9399', metalness: 1, roughness: .3 })); ring.position.set(x, .01, z); scene.add(ring);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.032, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, .021, z); scene.add(lens);
  path.push({ l, lens, u: i / 3 });
}
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#7f93c0', '#2a2018', .05); scene.add(hemi);

// ---- detail station: an IP65 ground spot and an up/down wall light, wet ----
const PX = 40, darkM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2622', roughness: .35, metalness: .1 });
plane(6, 6, darkM, [PX, 0, 0], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(4, 3, .2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: stoneTex2, roughness: .95 }), PX - 2, 0, -1.3);
const inox = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9ccd1', metalness: 1, roughness: .18 });
const gs = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.16, .16, .03, 48), inox); gs.position.set(PX - .25, .015, .25); scene.add(gs);
const gsLens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.11, 40), emis('#000', 0)); gsLens.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; gsLens.position.set(PX - .25, .031, .25); scene.add(gsLens);
const gsL = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 3, .5, .6, 1.2); gsL.position.set(PX - .25, .05, .25); gsL.target.position.set(PX - .3, 2, -1.2); scene.add(gsL, gsL.target);
// wall light (cylinder up/down) on the stone
const wl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .28, 32), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2b2e', metalness: .7, roughness: .35 })); wl.position.set(PX + .55, 1.45, -1.02); scene.add(wl);
const wlUp = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 2.4, .32, .5, 1.3); wlUp.position.set(PX + .55, 1.6, -1.0); wlUp.target.position.set(PX + .55, 3, -1.08); scene.add(wlUp, wlUp.target);
const wlDn = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 2.4, .32, .5, 1.3); wlDn.position.set(PX + .55, 1.3, -1.0); wlDn.target.position.set(PX + .55, 0, -1.08); scene.add(wlDn, wlDn.target);
// rain drops on the fixtures and the floor
const dropM = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#ffffff', roughness: .02, transmission: .9, transparent: true, opacity: .55, thickness: .01 });
const drops = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(drops);
for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) { const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.006 + R() * .012, 10, 8), dropM); const a = R() * 6.28, r = R() * 1.6; d.position.set(PX - .25 + Math.cos(a) * r, .03, .25 + Math.sin(a) * r * .8); d.scale.y = .45; drops.add(d); }
for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.007 + R() * .006, 10, 8), dropM); d.position.set(PX + .55 + (R() - .5) * .11, 1.33 + R() * .26, -.96); drops.add(d); }
const streaks = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(streaks); // falling rain lit by the lights
const streakM = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: '#cfe2ff', transparent: true, opacity: .35 });
for (let i = 0; i < 180; i++) { const s = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.004, .18, .004), streakM); s.position.set(PX - 1.2 + R() * 2.4, .2 + R() * 2.6, -.8 + R() * 1.8); s.rotation.z = .12; streaks.add(s); }
const keyD = new THREE.PointLight('#9fb4e0', 0, 6, 2); keyD.position.set(PX + 1.4, 2.2, 1.6); scene.add(keyD);

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.5, 1.65, 4.4], tgt: [-.3, 1.1, -1.9], fov: 62 },     // the whole terrace from the path
  s: { pos: [1.3, 1.25, 1.6], tgt: [-.9, 1.5, -2.3], fov: 62 },       // the seating under the strings
  u: { pos: [.4, 1.0, .8], tgt: [-.3, 2.5, -1.6], fov: 66 },          // looking up at the strings
  g: { pos: [.25, .55, -.2], tgt: [-.3, 1.3, -3.4], fov: 60 },        // the stone wall grazed by the up-lights
  p: { pos: [1.6, 1.0, 5.6], tgt: [.1, .1, 1.8], fov: 50 },           // the path spots
  d: { pos: [PX + .95, .95, 1.35], tgt: [PX + .15, .65, -.5], fov: 50 },      // detail: ground spot + wall light in the rain
  e: { pos: [PX + .25, .45, .85], tgt: [PX - .25, .02, .2], fov: 40 }, // macro: the wet ground spot
};
// state: tokens joined by '+':  dusk | night (base) · str:<0..1> (string bulbs lighting up along the runs) · wall · path:<0..1> · rain (detail)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const str = T.str ?? 0, wall = T.wall ?? 0, pth = T.path ?? 0, dusk = T.dusk ?? 0, det = !!T.ip;
  bulbs.forEach((b) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (str * 1.1 - b.u) / .1)); b.m.material = k > 0 ? emis('#ffb866', 4.5 * k) : emis('#3a2c20', .05); });
  strLights.forEach((l) => l.intensity = 2.6 * Math.min(1, str * 1.2));
  ups.forEach(({ s, lens }) => { s.intensity = 16 * wall; lens.material = wall ? emis('#ffd6a0', 5 * wall) : emis('#000', 0); });
  path.forEach(({ l, lens, u }) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (pth * 1.15 - u) / .15)); l.intensity = 1.1 * k; lens.material = k ? emis('#ffd6a0', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const lit = Math.min(1, str + wall + pth);
  hemi.intensity = det ? .25 : .05 + .3 * dusk + .03 * lit; moonL.intensity = det ? 0 : .12 + .3 * dusk;
  sky.material.color.set(new THREE.Color('#ffffff').multiplyScalar(.5 + .8 * dusk));
  // detail station
  const on = det ? 1 : 0;
  gsL.intensity = 4 * on; gsLens.material = on ? emis('#ffe2b8', 4) : emis('#000', 0);
  wlUp.intensity = 4 * on; wlDn.intensity = 4 * on; keyD.intensity = det ? 1.5 : 0;
  drops.visible = det; streaks.visible = det && (T.rain ?? 0) > 0;
  scene.environmentIntensity = det ? .12 : .03 + .1 * dusk;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
  g.globalAlpha = .7; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { g.fillStyle = ['#1f3b4d', '#b5562f', '#e8c88f', '#2c2420', '#7c8f6a', '#d9b48a'][i]; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(160 + i * 140, 320 + Math.sin(i) * 120, 120 - i * 8, 220 - i * 18, i * .4, 0, 7); g.fill(); }
  g.globalAlpha = 1; g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(20,14,10,.6)'; g.lineWidth = 8; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(60, 520); g.bezierCurveTo(300, 380, 600, 600, 980, 420); g.stroke();
}, [1, 1]);
box(1.7, 1.06, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b08a55', roughness: .35, metalness: .7 }), -.85, 1.45, .02);

box(3.0, .5, .25, sofaM, -1.5, .4, .12, { r: .08 });
for (const x of [-1.5, 1.25]) box(.25, .62, .95, sofaM, x, 0, .12, { r: .08 });
const cuM = [new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#b07a4f', roughness: .9 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#3f5563', roughness: .9 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#d8c3a0', roughness: .9 })];
[-1.1, -.55, .3, .85].forEach((x, i) => box(.42, .4, .14, cuM[i % 3], x, .45, .4, { r: .06 }));
// rug + coffee table (marble top on brass legs) + a vase and books
plane(4.0, 2.8, M.rug, [0, .006, 2.2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(1.6, .06, .8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#cfc7ba', roughness: .15 }), -.8, .36, 1.75, { r: .02 });
for (const [x, z] of [[-.7, 1.82], [.66, 1.82], [-.7, 2.48], [.66, 2.48]]) box(.04, .36, .04, M.brass, x, 0, z);
const vase = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07, .1, .28, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f4a5a', roughness: .2, metalness: .2 })); vase.position.set(.3, .56, 2.1); scene.add(vase);
box(.32, .06, .22, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8c3b2a', roughness: .7 }), -.45, .42, 2.0); box(.28, .05, .2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9dfcc', roughness: .7 }), -.43, .48, 2.01);
// two armchairs facing the sofa (seen from behind in the wide view, a soft silhouette)

// curtains on the left wall (a sheer + heavy drape)
const crM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9cbb6', roughness: 1, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) box(.06, 2.95, .2, crM, -RW + .02 + (i % 2) * .04, 0, 1.0 + i * .2, { r: .02 });
// right wall: a long console, a plant, a mirror
box(.45, .8, 1.8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#6b4a32', roughness: .45 }), RW - .45, 0, 1.6, { r: .02 });
plant(RW - .55, 4.1, 1.5, .55);
const lampV = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.13, 24, 16), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', roughness: .2, metalness: .9 })); lampV.position.set(RW - .22, .93, 2.0); scene.add(lampV);
box(.04, 1.0, 1.1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b08a55', roughness: .3, metalness: .8 }), RW - .04, 1.25, 1.95);
plane(1.0, .9, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#9fa9ad', roughness: .05, metalness: 1 }), [RW - .045, 1.75, 2.5], [0, -Math.PI / 2, 0]);
// ceiling cove: gypsum drop band with a hidden strip on its inner lip washing the raised centre
const gypM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1eb', roughness: .9 });
const B = .65, DY = 2.92, DH = .1;
box(RW * 2, DH, B, gypM, -RW, DY, -.12, { cast: false }); box(RW * 2, DH, B, gypM, -RW, DY, RD - B - .12, { cast: false });
box(B, DH, RD - 2 * B, gypM, -RW, DY, -.12 + B, { cast: false }); box(B, DH, RD - 2 * B, gypM, RW - B, DY, -.12 + B, { cast: false });
const ix0 = -RW + B, ix1 = RW - B, iz0 = -.12 + B, iz1 = RD - B - .12;
const coveS = [], coveL = [];
const cv = (x, z, w, d, lx, lz) => { coveS.push(box(w, .006, d, emis('#000', 0), x, DY + DH + .002, z, { cast: false })); const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd3a0', 0, Math.max(w, d), .08); l.position.set(x + w / 2, DY + DH + .02, z + d / 2); l.lookAt(lx, CH + 1, lz); scene.add(l); coveL.push(l); };
cv(ix0, iz0 - .04, ix1 - ix0, .03, 0, iz0 + .8); cv(ix1 + .01, iz0, .03, iz1 - iz0, ix1 - .8, (iz0 + iz1) / 2);
cv(ix0, iz1 + .01, ix1 - ix0, .03, 0, iz1 - .8); cv(ix0 - .04, iz0, .03, iz1 - iz0, ix0 + .8, (iz0 + iz1) / 2);
// recessed spots in the drop band: painting (x2), coffee table, console
const spots = [[-.55, .3, -.45, 2.0, .05, 22], [.55, .3, .45, 2.0, .05, 22], [0, 2.1, 0, .4, 2.1, 18], [RW - .35, 2.0, RW - .25, .8, 2.0, 18]].map(([x, z, tx, ty, tz, ang]) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .045, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, z < 1 || x > 2 ? DY - .005 : CH - .005, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffdcb0', 0, 7, ang * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.3); s.position.set(x, ring.position.y - .02, z); s.target.position.set(tx, ty, tz); s.castShadow = true; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s };
});
// wall sconces flanking the painting (brass, up + down light on the fluted panel)
const scM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', metalness: .9, roughness: .25 });
const sconces = [-1.25, 1.25].map((x) => {
  box(.1, .3, .06, scM, x - .05, 1.75, .02);
  const shade = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.08, .1, .2, 24, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3e6cf', roughness: .9, side: THREE.DoubleSide, emissive: '#000' })); shade.position.set(x, 2.02, .16); scene.add(shade);
  const up = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffc98a', 0, 3, 38 * Math.PI / 180, .8, 1.4); up.position.set(x, 2.08, .12); up.target.position.set(x, 3.2, .05); scene.add(up, up.target);
  const dn = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffc98a', 0, 3, 40 * Math.PI / 180, .8, 1.4); dn.position.set(x, 1.96, .12); dn.target.position.set(x, .4, .05); scene.add(dn, dn.target);
  const pt = new THREE.PointLight('#ffbf7a', 0, 2.6, 2); pt.position.set(x, 2.0, .35); scene.add(pt);
  return { shade, up, dn, pt };
});
// crystal chandelier from the raised centre: brass rings, candle bulbs, hanging crystal drops
const chand = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(chand); const CY = 2.35, CZ = 2.4;
const rod = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.012, .012, CH - CY - .3, 8), M.brass); rod.position.set(0, (CH + CY + .3) / 2, CZ); chand.add(rod);
const crystalOff = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#e8eef2', roughness: .05, metalness: 0, transmission: .6, transparent: true, opacity: .7 });
const crystals = [], bulbs = [];
const bulbOff = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe6d6', roughness: .3 });
for (const [r, y, n] of [[.55, CY, 12], [.36, CY + .28, 8], [.18, CY + .5, 6]]) {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(r, .014, 8, 48), M.brass); ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; ring.position.set(0, y, CZ); chand.add(ring);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const a = i / n * Math.PI * 2, x = Math.cos(a) * r, z = CZ + Math.sin(a) * r;
    const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.028, 12, 10), bulbOff); b.position.set(x, y + .07, z); chand.add(b); bulbs.push(b);
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(.022 + k * .004, 0), crystalOff); c.scale.y = 1.8; const aa = a + (k - 1) * .14; c.position.set(Math.cos(aa) * r, y - .06 - k * .05, CZ + Math.sin(aa) * r); chand.add(c); crystals.push(c); }
  }
}
const chPt = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 7, 1.6); chPt.position.set(0, CY + .1, CZ); scene.add(chPt);
const chDn = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd7a3', 0, 6, 55 * Math.PI / 180, .9, 1.2); chDn.position.set(0, CY, CZ); chDn.target.position.set(0, 0, CZ); scene.add(chDn, chDn.target);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#d9dde8', '#2a2018', .03); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.75, 6.0], tgt: [0, 1.45, .8], fov: 66 },          // the whole room from the entrance
  c: { pos: [.2, 1.5, 4.4], tgt: [0, 2.35, 2.4], fov: 55 },          // looking up at the chandelier with the room behind
  s: { pos: [.3, 1.3, 2.8], tgt: [0, 1.45, 0], fov: 56 },           // the sofa, painting and sconces
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · cove:<0..1> (hidden strip edge by edge) · spots · sconce · chand:<0..1> (crystals sparkle in) · all (= cove+spots+sconce+chand)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'cove+spots+sconce+chand').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const co = T.cove ?? 0, sp = T.spots ?? 0, sc = T.sconce ?? 0, ch = T.chand ?? 0;
  coveL.forEach((l, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (co * 1.1 - i / 4) / .3)); l.intensity = 34 * k; coveS[i].material = k ? emis('#ffd3a0', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.intensity = 30 * sp; ring.material = sp ? emis('#ffe2bf', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  sconces.forEach(({ shade, up, dn, pt }) => { up.intensity = 9 * sc; dn.intensity = 9 * sc; pt.intensity = 1.2 * sc; shade.material.emissive.set(sc ? '#ffcf96' : '#000'); shade.material.emissiveIntensity = 1.6 * sc; });
  bulbs.forEach((b, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (ch * 1.2 - i / bulbs.length * .5) / .5)); b.material = k ? emis('#ffe0b0', 7 * k) : bulbOff; });
  crystals.forEach((c, i) => { c.material = ch ? emis(i % 3 ? '#ffe9cc' : '#ffd9a6', (.35 + (i * 7 % 5) * .22) * ch) : crystalOff; });
  chPt.intensity = 6 * ch; chDn.intensity = 12 * ch;
  const lit = Math.min(1, co) * .35 + sp * .2 + sc * .15 + ch * .3;
  hemi.intensity = .03 + .3 * lit; scene.environmentIntensity = .02 + .13 * lit;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/salonspots.html`

```html
// ===== Living room: where do the spots go? =====
// Room x -3..3, z 0..5 (back wall z=0 with a TV wall on the left and a framed painting on the right), sofa facing the TV
// along the right... kept simple: the sofa sits against the right half of the back wall, the coffee table in front,
// the TV wall on the left wall. Camera from the open front.
// state tokens joined by '+':
//   mid        the common mistake: one row of spots down the middle of the ceiling (walls dark, harsh pools on the floor)
//   good:<0..1> spots along the seating ~60 cm off the wall, then one aimed at the painting, then one over the table
//   tv         (with mid) a spot right above the TV: a hot reflection on the screen
//   marks      orange rings on the ceiling where the good spots go (for the plan moment)
const CH = 2.9, RX = 3.0, RZ = 5.0;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#a07c58', '62,40,20')(g, w, h); }, [3, 3]);
plane(RX * 2, RZ, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .5 }), [0, 0, RZ / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e6dfd4', roughness: .9 });
box(RX * 2, CH, .1, wallM, -RX, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RZ, wallM, -RX - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, RZ, wallM, RX, 0, -.1);
box(RX * 2 + .2, .08, RZ, M.ceil, -RX - .1, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
// TV wall on the left wall: dark stone panel + screen
box(.05, 2.2, 2.4, M.stone, -RX + .01, .3, 1.2, { cast: false });
const screenM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#06070a', roughness: .08, metalness: .6 });
const screen = box(.03, .85, 1.5, screenM, -RX + .07, 1.0, 1.65);
box(.4, .35, 1.8, M.darkWood, -RX + .06, 0, 1.5);
// painting on the back wall
const artTex = canvasTex(768, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#e7dcc8'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); const cs = ['#b8553a', '#2f4d5c', '#d9a85b', '#5d7a52']; for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { g.fillStyle = cs[i]; g.globalAlpha = .85; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(170 + i * 140, 260 + (i % 2 ? -50 : 50), 120, 160, i * .4, 0, 7); g.fill(); } }, [1, 1]);
box(1.36, .92, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1714', roughness: .5 }), .02, 1.38, -.04);
plane(1.26, .82, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: artTex, roughness: .8 }), [.7, 1.84, .01], [0, 0, 0]);
// sofa against the back wall (right half), coffee table, rug, armchair, plant
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#cfc2ad', roughness: .95 });
box(2.6, .42, .9, sofaM, -.6, 0, .15, { r: .06 }); box(2.6, .48, .22, sofaM, -.6, .42, .1, { r: .06 });
box(1.2, .38, .7, M.tableTop, .1, 0, 1.6);
box(3.2, .012, 2.2, M.rug, -.9, .002, .9, { cast: false });
box(.8, .42, .8, M.cushion, 2.0, 0, 1.6, { r: .06 }); box(.8, .45, .18, M.cushion, 2.0, .42, 2.22, { r: .06 });
plant(2.55, .5, 1.6, .5);
// lights
const WARM = '#ffdcb0';
function spot(x, z, aim, ang = 30) { const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 18), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .005, z); scene.add(ring); const mk = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.11, .015, 8, 28), emis('#000', 0)); mk.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; mk.position.set(x, CH - .012, z); scene.add(mk); const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 8, ang * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.3); s.position.set(x, CH - .04, z); s.target.position.set(...aim); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, mk, s }; }
const MID = [-1.8, -.6, .6, 1.8].map((x) => spot(x, 2.6, [x, 0, 2.6], 38));
const TVS = spot(-RX + .45, 1.65, [-RX + .1, 1.4, 1.65], 30);
const GOOD = [
  ...[-.2, .7, 1.6].map((x) => spot(x, .6, [x, .9, .05], 30)),     // along the seating, ~60 cm off the wall, washing it
  spot(.7, 1.0, [.7, 1.8, 0], 18),                                   // aimed at the painting
  spot(.7, 1.95, [.7, .38, 1.95], 26),                                // over the coffee table
];
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#efe7dc', '#2a2018', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [.7, 1.6, 5.6], tgt: [.2, 1.15, .6], fov: 62 },        // the room from the front
  p: { pos: [.9, 1.5, 2.9], tgt: [.6, 1.6, 0], fov: 50 },           // the sofa + painting
  t: { pos: [.3, 1.3, 3.4], tgt: [-RX, 1.3, 1.6], fov: 52 },         // the TV wall
  c: { pos: [.7, 1.2, 4.2], tgt: [.4, 2.9, 1.2], fov: 72 },          // looking up at the ceiling (plan / marks)
};
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const mid = T.mid ?? 0, g = T.good ?? 0, tv = T.tv ?? 0, mk = T.marks ?? 0;
  MID.forEach(({ ring, s }) => { s.intensity = 40 * mid; ring.material = mid ? emis('#fff1dc', 3) : emis('#000', 0); });
  TVS.s.intensity = 30 * tv; TVS.ring.material = tv ? emis('#fff1dc', 3) : emis('#000', 0);
  GOOD.forEach(({ ring, mk: m, s }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (g * 1.25 - [0, 0, 0, .4, .7][i]) / .3)); s.intensity = (i === 3 ? 30 : 32) * k; ring.material = k > .02 ? emis('#fff1dc', 3 * k) : emis('#000', 0); m.material = mk ? emis('#ff7a1a', 4) : emis('#000', 0); });
  [MID, [TVS]].flat().forEach(({ mk: m }) => { m.material = emis('#000', 0); });
  screenM.emissive = new THREE.Color(tv ? '#3a3226' : '#000');
  hemi.intensity = .06 + .06 * Math.max(mid, g); scene.environmentIntensity = .04; renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/sdcm.html`

```html
// ===== Colour consistency (SDCM): a row of "3000K" downlights washing a white wall, mixed batches vs matched =====
// wall at z=0 (x -3.5..3.5), floor y=0, ceiling 2.8; five spots 0.5 m from the wall
const CH = 2.8;
marbleTex.repeat.set(3, 3);
plane(9, 6, M.floor, [0, 0, 2.5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#f3efe8', roughness: .9 });
box(9, CH, .1, wallM, -4.5, 0, -.1);
box(9, .1, 6, M.ceil, -4.5, CH, -.1, { cast: false });
box(.1, CH, 6, wallM, -3.6, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 6, wallM, 3.5, 0, -.1);
// a low console and two vases so the scene reads as a home
const conM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#6b4a30', roughness: .45 });
box(3.2, .06, .4, conM, -1.6, .78, .05); box(3.2, .04, .38, conM, -1.6, .2, .06);
for (const x of [-1.55, 1.5]) for (const z of [.07, .4]) box(.04, .78, .04, M.brass, x, 0, z);
const ceramic = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9e0d2', roughness: .5 });
for (const [x, s] of [[-.9, 1], [.7, .8]]) { const pts = [[0, 0], [.07, 0], [.11, .1], [.12, .25], [.09, .38], [.05, .45], [.06, .5]].map(([r, h]) => new THREE.Vector2(r * s, h * s)); const v = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 40), ceramic); v.position.set(x, .84, .25); v.castShadow = true; scene.add(v); }
plant(2.8, .6, 1.5, .55);
// five downlights; each gets its own tint in "mixed"
const XS = [-2.4, -1.2, 0, 1.2, 2.4];
const MIXED = ['#ffd38f', '#ffcfc8', '#e7f0c8', '#ffd9a6', '#f8c9d9']; // yellowish, pinkish, greenish, ok, pink
const MATCHED = '#ffd8a8';
const spots = XS.map((x) => {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight(MATCHED, 0, 7, 26 * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.4); s.position.set(x, CH - .02, .5); s.target.position.set(x, .6, 0); s.castShadow = true; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); scene.add(s, s.target);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .012, 28), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ec', roughness: .4 })); ring.position.set(x, CH - .006, .5); scene.add(ring);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.028, 24), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, CH - .013, .5); scene.add(lens);
  return { s, lens };
});
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#fff4e6', '#3a3028', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.4, 4.6], tgt: [0, 1.35, 0], fov: 64 },      // the whole wall
  u: { pos: [.4, 1.3, 2.6], tgt: [0, 2.6, .4], fov: 62 },      // looking up at the spots
  c: { pos: [-.6, 1.5, 2.2], tgt: [-1.2, 1.6, 0], fov: 50 },   // two neighbouring pools close
};
// state "mixed" | "matched" | "off"
window.setState = (st) => {
  spots.forEach((p, i) => {
    const on = st !== 'off', col = st === 'mixed' ? MIXED[i] : MATCHED;
    p.s.color.set(col); p.s.intensity = on ? 9 : 0; p.lens.material = on ? emis(col, 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  });
  hemi.intensity = st === 'off' ? .15 : .05; scene.environmentIntensity = st === 'off' ? .12 : .04;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/shop.html`

```html
// ===== Boutique wall: the same products under flat ceiling light vs track spots (24°, CRI 95) + linear LEDs under the shelves =====
// shelving wall at z=0 (x -1.8..1.8), floor y=0, ceiling 3.0; the camera stands in the shop at z>0
const SX = 1.8, SH = 3.0;
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d8cfc4', roughness: .92 });
marbleTex.repeat.set(3, 3);
plane(8, 8, M.floor, [0, 0, 2.5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(8, SH, .1, wallM, -4, 0, -.45);
box(.1, SH, 7, wallM, -2.8, 0, -.45); box(.1, SH, 7, wallM, 2.7, 0, -.45);
box(8, .1, 7, M.ceil, -4, SH, -.45, { cast: false });
// shelving unit: dark wood frame, 4 shelves, back panel in warm oak
const frameM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b211b', roughness: .5 });
const backM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#b48a62', roughness: .55 });
box(SX * 2, 2.6, .04, backM, -SX, 0, -.36);
for (const x of [-SX - .05, -.62, .58, SX]) box(.05, 2.6, .4, frameM, x, 0, -.36);
const SHELVES = [.35, .95, 1.55, 2.15];
const shelves = [];
for (const y of SHELVES) {
  box(SX * 2, .04, .4, frameM, -SX, y, -.36);
  // linear LED under the shelf above (the bottom shelf is lit from the shelf at .95, the top one from the frame top)
  const yl = y + .6 - .012;
  const strip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(SX * 2 - .1, .01, .02), emis('#000', 0)); strip.position.set(0, yl, -.04); scene.add(strip);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff1dc', 0, SX * 2 - .1, .05); l.position.set(0, yl - .006, -.06); l.lookAt(0, yl - 1, -.1); scene.add(l);
  shelves.push({ strip, l });
}
box(SX * 2 + .05, .06, .4, frameM, -SX - .05, 2.6, -.36);

// ---- products (left column: bags · middle: perfumes / jewellery · right: shoes), one product family per shelf ----
const leather = (c, r = .45) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: r });
const goldM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d4a64a', roughness: .25, metalness: 1 });
function bag(x, y, c) {
  box(.34, .24, .14, leather(c), x - .17, y, -.25, { r: .03 });
  const h = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.09, .012, 10, 32, Math.PI), leather(c)); h.position.set(x, y + .24, -.18); h.castShadow = true; scene.add(h);
  box(.06, .03, .01, goldM, x - .03, y + .16, -.115);
}
function perfume(x, y, c, s = 1) {
  const g = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.08 * s, .13 * s, .05 * s), new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: c, roughness: .05, transmission: .6, thickness: .05, ior: 1.5 }));
  g.position.set(x, y + .065 * s, -.15); g.castShadow = true; scene.add(g);
  const cap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.022 * s, .022 * s, .05 * s, 20), goldM); cap.position.set(x, y + .155 * s, -.15); cap.castShadow = true; scene.add(cap);
}
function clutch(x, y, c) { // standing clutch with a gold clasp
  box(.24, .15, .05, leather(c, .3), x - .12, y, -.2, { r: .02 });
  box(.05, .025, .01, goldM, x - .025, y + .09, -.152);
}
const ceramic = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9e0d2', roughness: .5 });
function vase(x, y) {
  const pts = [[0, 0], [.05, 0], [.08, .06], [.09, .15], [.07, .24], [.035, .3], [.04, .33]].map(([r, h]) => new THREE.Vector2(r, h));
  const v = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 40), ceramic); v.position.set(x, y, -.2); v.castShadow = true; scene.add(v);
}
const BAGS = ['#a0602e', '#7d1d22', '#1b1918', '#c9a77c'];
for (const [i, y] of SHELVES.entries()) { bag(-1.42, y + .04, BAGS[i]); bag(-1.0, y + .04, BAGS[(i + 2) % 4]); }
for (const [i, y] of SHELVES.entries()) {
  for (let j = 0; j < 5; j++) perfume(-.42 + j * .2, y + .04, ['#f2c46a', '#e48aa0', '#9fd0e8', '#f6e7c8', '#c36a3a'][(i + j) % 5], j % 2 ? 1.1 : .9);
  for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) clutch(.85 + j * .32, y + .04, ['#7a1c20', '#c79a68', '#151313', '#2e4a63', '#e7d9c4'][(i + j) % 5]);
  vase(1.5, y + .04);
}
// display table in front with folded fabrics
const tableM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b9b0a3', roughness: .4 });
box(1.2, .78, .6, tableM, -.6, 0, .7);
const FAB = ['#c84b2f', '#e9d6b5', '#2e4a63', '#7c5a3c', '#a33a5a'];
for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
  const fm = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: FAB[i], roughness: .95 });
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) box(.18, .035, .26, fm, -.52 + i * .22, .78 + k * .036, .85, { r: .01 });
}

// ---- lights ----
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#eef3ff', '#3a3028', .05); scene.add(hemi);
// flat: wide cold downlights in the middle of the shop (CRI ~80 look: colours slightly washed)
const flat = [];
for (const [x, z] of [[-1.2, 1.6], [1.2, 1.6], [-1.2, 3.2], [1.2, 3.2]]) {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#f1f5ff', 0, 10, 1.1, .9, 1.4); s.position.set(x, SH - .02, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target);
  if (z < 2) { s.castShadow = true; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); s.shadow.bias = -.0005; }
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.09, .09, .01, 24), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(x, SH - .005, z); scene.add(m);
  flat.push({ s, m });
}
// track: a black rail with narrow spots aimed at the product groups
box(3.8, .04, .05, M.black, -1.9, SH - .05, 1.1, { cast: false });
const track = [];
const AIM = [[-1.22, 2.3], [-1.22, 1.1], [0, 1.65], [0, .45], [1.15, 1.7], [1.15, .55], [0, .9]];
for (const [i, [x, y]] of AIM.entries()) {
  const tx = x + (i % 2 ? .12 : -.12);
  const z = i === 6 ? 1.5 : 1.12;
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe2bd', 0, 9, 12 * Math.PI / 180, .4, 1.5); s.position.set(tx, SH - .2, z);
  s.target.position.set(x, y, i === 6 ? .95 : -.2); s.castShadow = i < 4; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); s.shadow.bias = -.0005; scene.add(s, s.target);
  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04, .045, .16, 20), M.black); body.position.copy(s.position); body.lookAt(s.target.position); body.rotateX(Math.PI / 2); scene.add(body);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.03, 20), emis('#000', 0)); scene.add(lens);
  const dir = s.target.position.clone().sub(s.position).normalize(); lens.position.copy(s.position).addScaledVector(dir, .081); lens.lookAt(s.position.clone().addScaledVector(dir, 2));
  const stem = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.008, .008, .16, 8), M.black); stem.position.set(tx, SH - .11, z); scene.add(stem);
  track.push({ s, lens });
}

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.45, 3.4], tgt: [0, 1.3, -.3], fov: 62 },        // the whole wall
  c: { pos: [-.65, 1.7, 1.75], tgt: [-.7, 1.55, -.3], fov: 60 },  // bags + perfumes close
  s: { pos: [.75, 1.35, 1.5], tgt: [.95, 1.1, -.3], fov: 58 },    // clutches + vases close
  u: { pos: [.6, 1.3, 2.4], tgt: [0, 2.85, 1.0], fov: 62 },       // looking up at the track spots
};
// state "flat:<0..1>|track:<0..1>|shelf:<0..1>"  presets: flat | lit | dark
window.setState = (st) => {
  const preset = { flat: 'flat:1', lit: 'flat:.15|track:1|shelf:1', dark: 'flat:0' }[st] ?? st;
  const o = Object.fromEntries(preset.split('|').map(p => p.split(':')).map(([k, v]) => [k, +v]));
  const F = o.flat ?? 0, T = o.track ?? 0, S = o.shelf ?? 0;
  for (const f of flat) { f.s.intensity = 15 * F; f.s.color.set(F > .5 ? '#f1f5ff' : '#ffe9cf'); f.m.material = F ? emis('#f4f7ff', 3 * F) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const t of track) { t.s.intensity = 14 * T; t.lens.material = T ? emis('#ffe2bd', 8 * T) : emis('#000', 0); }
  for (const s of shelves) { s.l.intensity = 7 * S; s.strip.material = S ? emis('#fff1dc', 5 * S) : emis('#000', 0); }
  hemi.intensity = F > .5 ? .45 : .06; hemi.color.set(F > .5 ? '#eef3ff' : '#ffe9d2');
  scene.environmentIntensity = F > .5 ? .5 : .05;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = F > .5 ? .72 : 1.05;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/spotlin.html`

```html
// ===== Spot or linear? one open-plan flat: narrow spots pick out the painting, the sofa and the plant corner; linear lights draw soft lines over the dining table, down the corridor and above the kitchen counter; then both together =====
// back wall z=0 (x -3.4..3.4): living on the left (painting over the sofa, a plant corner), a corridor opening in the middle (x -.45..+.45, running back to z=-3), kitchen counter on the right; dining table in front of the kitchen; ceiling 2.9
const CH = 2.9, RW = 3.4, RD = 6.5;
const floorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#c4a27c', '90,60,32')(g, w, h); }, [3, 3]);
plane(RW * 2, RD, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, RD / 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#ece6dd', roughness: .9 });
// back wall in two parts around the corridor opening; header above it
box(RW - .45, CH, .1, wallM, -RW, 0, -.12); box(RW - .45, CH, .1, wallM, .45, 0, -.12); box(.9, CH - 2.3, .1, wallM, -.45, 2.3, -.12);
box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, -RW - .1, 0, -.12); box(.1, CH, RD, wallM, RW, 0, -.12);
box(RW * 2 + .2, .1, RD, M.ceil, -RW - .1, CH, -.12, { cast: false });
// the corridor: 0.9 wide, 3 deep, a door at the end, a small console with a vase
plane(.9, 3.0, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: floorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, .001, -1.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(.1, CH, 3.0, wallM, -.55, 0, -3.1); box(.1, CH, 3.0, wallM, .45, 0, -3.1); box(1.1, CH, .1, wallM, -.55, 0, -3.2);
box(1.1, .1, 3.0, M.ceil, -.55, CH, -3.1, { cast: false });
box(.8, 2.1, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#9c7552', roughness: .55 }), -.4, 0, -3.1);
box(.25, .8, .5, M.darkWood, -.44, 0, -2.2); const cvase = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .07, .3, 20), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c96f45', roughness: .4 })); cvase.position.set(-.32, .95, -1.95); scene.add(cvase);
// LIVING: the painting, a long sofa under it, a coffee table, a rug, the plant corner
const artTex = canvasTex(1024, 640, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#1f3b4d'); gr.addColorStop(.55, '#c46a3a'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#efc77a'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,240,220,.85)'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w * .68, h * .38, 90, 0, 7); g.fill(); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(20,25,35,.6)'; g.lineWidth = 14; for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, h * (.7 + i * .09)); g.bezierCurveTo(w * .3, h * (.6 + i * .09), w * .6, h * (.85 + i * .05), w, h * (.68 + i * .09)); g.stroke(); } }, [1, 1]);
box(1.66, 1.06, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1714', roughness: .5 }), -2.83, 1.35, -.02);
plane(1.56, .96, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: artTex, roughness: .8 }), [-2.0, 1.88, .025], [0, 0, 0]);
const sofaM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#b8a58c', roughness: .95 });
box(2.3, .42, .9, sofaM, -3.15, 0, .2, { r: .06 }); box(2.3, .45, .22, sofaM, -3.15, .42, .2, { r: .05 });
for (const [x, c] of [[-2.85, '#c46a3a'], [-1.55, '#1f3b4d']]) box(.42, .36, .14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x, .45, .45, { r: .06 });
plane(2.6, 1.8, M.rug, [-2.0, .006, 1.7], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(1.0, .36, .55, M.darkWood, -2.5, 0, 1.5, { r: .02 });
plant(-3.0, 2.85, 1.7, .5);
// KITCHEN: a counter along the back wall (right of the corridor), upper cabinets, a backsplash
const cabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f1ede6', roughness: .45 });
box(2.6, .88, .62, cabM, .7, 0, -.07, { r: .01 });
box(2.64, .04, .65, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#e8e4dd', roughness: .15 }), .68, .88, -.08);
box(2.6, .6, .04, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#dcd6cc', roughness: .2 }), .7, .92, -.07, { cast: false });
box(2.6, .7, .35, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#d2ae86', roughness: .5 }), .7, 1.6, -.07);
for (const [x, c] of [[1.1, '#e9e2d4'], [1.4, '#3c5f4a'], [2.6, '#c96f45']]) { const j = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .2, 20), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: .4 })); j.position.set(x, 1.02, .2); j.castShadow = true; scene.add(j); }
// DINING: a long table and four chairs in front of the kitchen
const tabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#b48a60', roughness: .45 });
box(1.9, .05, .95, tabM, .95, .74, 2.15, { r: .01 });
for (const [x, z] of [[1.05, 2.25], [2.75, 2.25], [1.05, 2.95], [2.75, 2.95]]) box(.06, .74, .06, tabM, x, 0, z);
const chM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8a7663', roughness: .9 });
for (const x of [1.2, 2.2]) for (const dz of [-1, 1]) { const z = 2.62 + dz * .72; box(.44, .45, .44, chM, x, 0, z - .22, { r: .03 }); box(.44, .48, .05, chM, x, .45, z + (dz < 0 ? -.22 : .17), { r: .02 }); }
const bowl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.16, 24, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9df', roughness: .3, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); bowl.rotation.x = Math.PI; bowl.position.set(1.9, .95, 2.62); scene.add(bowl);
for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) { const f = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.045, 14, 10), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: ['#e0a128', '#c43a24', '#7aa03a'][k % 3], roughness: .5 })); f.position.set(1.9 + Math.cos(k * 1.3) * .07, .86, 2.62 + Math.sin(k * 1.3) * .07); scene.add(f); }
// a soft base light so the room never goes black (evening, a lamp somewhere)
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#efe6da', '#2a2018', .08); scene.add(hemi);
const amb = new THREE.PointLight('#ffe2c0', 0, 9, 1.6); amb.position.set(0, 1.9, 4.2); scene.add(amb);
// SPOTS: narrow, 3000 K: the painting (2, crossing), the sofa (1), the plant corner (1), the corridor console (1)
const mkSpot = (x, z, tx, ty, tz, ang, int, shadow = false) => {
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.045, .045, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CH - .01, z); scene.add(ring);
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 7, ang * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.2); s.position.set(x, CH - .03, z); s.target.position.set(tx, ty, tz); s.castShadow = shadow; scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s, int };
};
const spots = [
  mkSpot(-2.0, .95, -2.0, 1.8, 0, 26, 11),
  mkSpot(-2.0, 1.5, -2.0, .45, .65, 26, 16, true),
  mkSpot(-2.85, 2.6, -3.0, 1.0, 2.85, 18, 20, true),
  mkSpot(0, -1.7, -.32, .9, -1.95, 18, 14),
];
// LINEAR: a suspended linear over the dining table, a recessed line down the corridor ceiling, a line under the upper cabinets over the counter
const blk = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#151517', metalness: .4, roughness: .45 });
const lins = [];
const mkLin = (len, mesh, lensMesh, lights, int) => { lins.push({ mesh, lensMesh, lights, int }); };
{ // dining suspended linear
  const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(1.9, 1.55, 2.62); scene.add(g);
  const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.4, .05, .06), blk); body.castShadow = true; g.add(body);
  for (const x of [-.6, .6]) { const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.003, .003, CH - 1.55, 6), blk); w.position.set(x, (CH - 1.55) / 2, 0); g.add(w); }
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.36, .008, .045), emis('#000', 0)); lens.position.y = -.027; g.add(lens);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe0bc', 0, 1.36, .2); l.position.set(1.9, 1.51, 2.62); l.lookAt(1.9, 0, 2.62); scene.add(l);
  const up = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe0bc', 0, 1.36, .1); up.position.set(1.9, 1.58, 2.62); up.lookAt(1.9, CH + 1, 2.62); scene.add(up);
  mkLin(1.4, g, lens, [[l, 45], [up, 10]]);
}
{ // corridor ceiling recessed line
  const lens = box(.05, .006, 2.9, emis('#000', 0), -.025, CH - .008, -3.0, { cast: false });
  const l1 = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe0bc', 0, .12, 1.4); l1.position.set(0, CH - .02, -2.3); l1.lookAt(0, 0, -2.3);
  const l2 = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe0bc', 0, .12, 1.4); l2.position.set(0, CH - .02, -.8); l2.lookAt(0, 0, -.8); scene.add(l1, l2);
  mkLin(2.9, null, lens, [[l1, 30], [l2, 30]]);
}
{ // under the upper cabinets, over the counter
  const lens = box(2.5, .008, .03, emis('#000', 0), .75, 1.592, .22, { cast: false });
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffe0bc', 0, 2.5, .08); l.position.set(2.0, 1.585, .2); l.lookAt(2.0, 0, .12); scene.add(l);
  mkLin(2.5, null, lens, [[l, 20]]);
}
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [.9, 1.45, 6.2], tgt: [-.3, 1.05, .8], fov: 66 },       // the whole flat: living left, corridor, kitchen and dining right
  p: { pos: [-1.6, 1.5, 3.9], tgt: [-2.1, 1.3, .2], fov: 56 },    // living: the painting, the sofa, the plant corner
  d: { pos: [2.6, 1.55, 5.0], tgt: [1.6, 1.15, 1.6], fov: 58 },     // dining table + kitchen counter
  c: { pos: [.05, 1.5, 1.6], tgt: [0, 1.3, -3.0], fov: 58 },        // down the corridor
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off (base light only) · spots:<0..1> (spots one by one) · linear:<0..1> (linear lights one by one) · all (= spots+linear)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'spots+linear').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const sp = T.spots ?? 0, li = T.linear ?? 0;
  spots.forEach(({ ring, s, int }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (sp * 1.1 - i / spots.length) / .2)); s.intensity = int * k; ring.material = k ? emis('#ffd8a8', 3 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  lins.forEach(({ lensMesh, lights }, i) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (li * 1.1 - i / lins.length) / .3)); lights.forEach(([l, v]) => l.intensity = v * k); lensMesh.material = k ? emis('#ffe0bc', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  const on = Math.min(1, sp + li);
  amb.intensity = 3.0 + 1.5 * on; hemi.intensity = .14 + .08 * on; scene.environmentIntensity = .08 + .06 * on;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

```html
// ===== Two 12 W spots: the same living-room corner under a cheap spot vs a quality one, and the two fixtures side by side =====
// back wall at z=0, floor y=0, ceiling 2.9; a sofa, a coffee table and a painting under one downlight at (0, 2.9, 1.6)

plane(8, 8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#d9cdbd', roughness: .92 });
box(8, CH, .1, wallM, -4, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 8, wallM, -2.6, 0, -.1); box(.1, CH, 8, wallM, 2.6, 0, -.1);

box(2.2, .42, .9, sofaM, -1.1, .1, .1, { r: .06 }); box(2.2, .5, .22, sofaM, -1.1, .5, .1, { r: .06 });
box(.22, .32, .9, sofaM, -1.1, .45, .1, { r: .05 }); box(.22, .32, .9, sofaM, .88, .45, .1, { r: .05 });
[['#b3262e', -.75], ['#e0a32a', -.15], ['#1f6f8b', .45]].forEach(([c, x]) => { const m = box(.42, .38, .14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x, .5, .3, { r: .07 }); m.rotation.x = -.2; });
const table = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.5, .5, .05, 48), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#8a5a36', roughness: .4 })); table.position.set(0, .42, 1.5); table.castShadow = table.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(table);
const leg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .08, .4, 24), M.brass); leg.position.set(0, .2, 1.5); scene.add(leg);
const fruitCols = ['#d9381e', '#f2b134', '#7cb342', '#d9381e', '#f2b134'];

fruitCols.forEach((c, i) => { const f = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.055, 20, 14), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: .45 })); f.position.set(-.05 + Math.cos(i * 1.3) * .07, .5 + (i > 2 ? .06 : 0), 1.45 + Math.sin(i * 1.3) * .07); f.castShadow = true; scene.add(f); });
plane(2.8, 2, M.rug, [0, .006, 1.4], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const artTex = canvasTex(800, 600, (g, w, h) => { const bg = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, h); bg.addColorStop(0, '#e9d8bf'); bg.addColorStop(1, '#c9a882'); g.fillStyle = bg; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { g.fillStyle = ['#b3262e', '#1f6f8b', '#e0a32a', '#2e2a26', '#7cb342'][i % 5]; g.globalAlpha = .85; g.beginPath(); g.arc(120 + i * 95, 300 + Math.sin(i) * 120, 60 + (i % 3) * 25, 0, 7); g.fill(); } g.globalAlpha = 1; });
box(1.3, .95, .04, M.black, -.65, 1.25, .0); plane(1.22, .87, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: artTex, roughness: .7 }), [0, 1.725, .045], [0, 0, 0]);
plant(1.9, .5, 1.5, .55);
// the downlight in the ceiling
const spot = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffe0b8', 0, 9, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.5); spot.position.set(0, CH - .02, 1.3); spot.target.position.set(0, .5, 1.1);
spot.castShadow = true; spot.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); spot.shadow.bias = -.0004; scene.add(spot, spot.target);
const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .012, 32), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f1ec', roughness: .4 })); ring.position.set(0, CH - .006, 1.3); scene.add(ring);
const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.032, 24), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(0, CH - .013, 1.3); scene.add(lens);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe9d2', '#3a2c20', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const fill = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 8, 2); fill.position.set(1.8, 1.4, 3.2); scene.add(fill);

// ---- product display: the two fixtures on a dark plinth (shown in a separate view far from the room) ----
const PX = 20;
const plinth = box(1.6, .9, .7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#16120f', roughness: .6 }), PX - .8, 0, -.35);
const backdrop = plane(6, 4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0e0b09', roughness: 1 }), [PX, 2, -1], [0, 0, 0]);
const alu = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b9bec4', roughness: .3, metalness: 1 });
const plastic = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2f0ea', roughness: .5 });
function fixture(x, quality) { // a downlight lying on its side: trim ring + lens, then the body behind it
  const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(x, 1.05, 0); g.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2 * 0; scene.add(g);
  const trim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.11, .11, .015, 48), quality ? alu : plastic); trim.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; trim.position.z = .1; g.add(trim);
  const ln = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.07, 32), emis('#fff1dc', quality ? 2.2 : 1.2)); ln.position.z = .109; g.add(ln);
  if (quality) { // deep finned aluminium heat sink
    const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.065, .065, .14, 32), alu); core.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; core.position.z = .02; g.add(core);
    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) { const a = i / 18 * Math.PI * 2; const fin = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.006, .045, .13), alu); fin.position.set(Math.cos(a) * .085, Math.sin(a) * .085, .02); fin.rotation.z = a + Math.PI / 2; g.add(fin); }
  } else { // thin plastic body
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07, .06, .05, 32), plastic); body.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; body.position.z = .07; g.add(body);
  }
  g.traverse(m => { if (m.isMesh) m.castShadow = true; });
  g.rotation.y = -.55; return g;
}
fixture(PX - .35, false); fixture(PX + .35, true);
const key = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffffff', 0, 8, .6, .6, 1.2); key.position.set(PX + .8, 2.0, 1.4); key.target.position.set(PX, 1.0, 0); scene.add(key, key.target);
const rim = new THREE.PointLight('#ffb36b', 0, 5, 2); rim.position.set(PX - 1.2, 1.6, -.5); scene.add(rim);

const VIEWS = {
  q: { pos: [-.9, 1.6, 3.7], tgt: [.1, .9, .6], fov: 58 },        // the corner from the left, a bit higher (3-questions reel)
  r: { pos: [0, 1.35, 4.3], tgt: [0, 1.05, .4], fov: 62 },      // the room
  t: { pos: [.35, 1.05, 2.6], tgt: [-.05, .55, 1.4], fov: 48 }, // the table + cushions close
  p: { pos: [PX, 1.3, 1.1], tgt: [PX, 1.05, 0], fov: 40 },      // the two fixtures
};
// state "cheap" | "good" | "off" | "prod", optionally "|dim:<0..1>"
window.setState = (state) => {
  const [st, ...rest] = state.split('|'); const dim = +(Object.fromEntries(rest.map(p => p.split(':'))).dim ?? 1); // "good|dim:.3"
  const good = st === 'good', on = st === 'good' || st === 'cheap', prod = st === 'prod';
  spot.intensity = on ? (good ? 12 : 4.5) * dim : 0; spot.color.set(good ? '#ffdcb0' : '#e4eef8'); spot.angle = (good ? 30 : 38) * Math.PI / 180; spot.penumbra = good ? .5 : .3;
  lens.material = on ? emis(good ? '#ffdcb0' : '#e4eef8', (good ? 6 : 4) * Math.max(dim, .15)) : emis('#000', 0);
  fill.intensity = on ? (good ? .35 : .2) * dim : .05;
  hemi.intensity = on ? .06 : .03; hemi.color.set(good ? '#ffe9d2' : '#eef2f0');
  key.intensity = prod ? 30 : 0; rim.intensity = prod ? 4 : 0; hemi.intensity = prod ? .25 : hemi.intensity;
  scene.environmentIntensity = prod ? .12 : on ? .05 : .02;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```
