## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/stairs.html`

```html
// ===== Staircase with hidden LED under each tread =====
const NS = 14, RISE = .17, GO = .29, SW = 1.2, TT = .05, NOSE = .035; // steps, rise, going, width, tread thickness, nosing
const TOPY = NS * RISE, TOPZ = -NS * GO;
marbleTex.repeat.set(6, 6);
const floorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, color: '#e9e3da', roughness: .16 });
const woodTex = canvasTex(1024, 256, woodDraw('#9a6b3f', '70,40,18'), [1, 1]);
woodTex.rotation = Math.PI / 2;
const treadM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodTex, roughness: .38 });
const riserM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex.clone(), color: '#f2ede6', roughness: .2 });
riserM.map.repeat.set(1.5, .3);
const wallStone = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { // warm travertine wall cladding with joints
  g.fillStyle = '#d9cbb5'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
  for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(${R() > .5 ? 150 : 240},${R() > .5 ? 130 : 225},${R() > .5 ? 100 : 200},0.06)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2 + R() * 30, 1 + R() * 2); }
  g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(90,70,50,.35)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 256) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); }
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 256) for (let x = (y / 256) % 2 ? 256 : 0; x <= w; x += 512) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y); g.lineTo(x, y + 256); g.stroke(); }
}, [3, 3]);
const stoneWallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: wallStone, roughness: .75 });
const plainWallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#ece6dc', roughness: .92 });
const glassM = new THREE.MeshPhysicalMaterial({ color: '#dfeff2', roughness: .03, transmission: .95, transparent: true, opacity: .18, thickness: .012 });

// floors: lower hall and upper landing
plane(8, 8, floorM, [1.5, 0, 1.5], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(4.5, .25, 3.2, floorM, -0.3, TOPY - .25, TOPZ - 3.2);
// left wall (stone cladding) full height, back wall at landing, far wall on the right (lower hall)
box(.12, 6, 12, stoneWallM, -.12, 0, TOPZ - 3.2, { uv: [3, 2] });
box(6, 6, .12, plainWallM, -.3, 0, TOPZ - 3.3);
box(.12, 6, 12, plainWallM, 5.2, 0, TOPZ - 3.2);
box(6, .1, 12, M.ceil, -.3, 5.2, TOPZ - 3.2, { cast: false });
// tall window on the far right wall (moonlight at night)
const winM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#000', emissive: '#9fb3d6', emissiveIntensity: .25 });
box(.02, 3.2, 1.6, winM, 5.17, .6, -2.4, { cast: false });
for (let k = 0; k <= 4; k++) box(.03, 3.2, .04, M.black, 5.15, .6, -2.4 + k * .39);
// opening in the right wall for the window so the moon glow reads
// stair: closed risers recessed behind a wooden tread with a nosing
const strips = [], stripL = [], steps = [];
for (let i = 0; i < NS; i++) {
  const y = (i + 1) * RISE, z0 = -i * GO;
  box(SW, RISE - TT, .03, riserM, 0, i * RISE, z0 - .03);                       // riser
  box(SW, TT, GO + NOSE, treadM, 0, y - TT, z0 - GO, { r: .006 });             // tread with nosing
  // LED channel under the nosing: aluminium profile + diffuser
  box(SW - .04, .012, .02, M.black, .02, y - TT - .012, z0 - .005 + .0, { cast: false });
  const s = box(SW - .06, .004, .012, LEDsoft, .03, y - TT - .014, z0 + .0, { cast: false });
  strips.push(s);
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#ffd6a2', 0, SW - .06, .03);
  l.position.set(SW / 2, y - TT - .02, z0 + .005); l.lookAt(SW / 2, y - TT - 1, z0 - .35); scene.add(l); stripL.push(l);
  steps.push({ y, z0 });
}
// solid stair mass: sawtooth profile extruded across the width, so the open side reads as one clean plaster face
{
  const sh = new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(0, 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < NS; i++) { sh.lineTo(i * GO + .03, i * RISE); sh.lineTo(i * GO + .03, (i + 1) * RISE - TT); sh.lineTo((i + 1) * GO, (i + 1) * RISE - TT); }
  sh.lineTo(-TOPZ, 0); sh.lineTo(0, 0);
  const geo = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: SW - .002, bevelEnabled: false });
  const mass = new THREE.Mesh(geo, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe9e0', roughness: .9 }));
  mass.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2; mass.position.set(.001, 0, 0); mass.receiveShadow = true; mass.castShadow = true; scene.add(mass);
  // rotating +90° about y maps the shape's x onto world -z and the extrusion onto world +x
}
// wall-mounted brass handrail on the stone wall, following the pitch
{
  const pitch = Math.atan2(TOPY, -TOPZ), L = Math.hypot(TOPY, TOPZ) + .6;
  const hr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.022, .022, L, 16), M.brass);
  hr.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2 - pitch; hr.position.set(.07, TOPY / 2 + .92, TOPZ / 2 - .05); hr.castShadow = true; scene.add(hr);
}
// art + plant on the lower hall to dress the shot
box(.9, 1.2, .03, M.black, 2.6, 1.2, TOPZ - 3.17);
const art = canvasTex(600, 800, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#e8dfd0'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = '#b08a4e'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w * .55, h * .4, 170, 0, 7); g.fill(); g.strokeStyle = '#3a2f26'; g.lineWidth = 6; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(60, h * .75); g.bezierCurveTo(w * .3, h * .5, w * .6, h * .95, w - 60, h * .6); g.stroke(); });
plane(.84, 1.14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: art, roughness: .9 }), [3.05, 1.8, TOPZ - 3.135], [0, 0, 0]);
// tall floor vase with dry branches by the stair foot
{ const pts = []; for (let i = 0; i <= 16; i++) { const t = i / 16; pts.push(new THREE.Vector2(.05 + .16 * Math.sin(Math.PI * (t * .8 + .12)), t * .9)); }
  const v = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2b2622', roughness: .35 })); v.position.set(3.4, 0, -3.6); v.castShadow = true; scene.add(v);
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const a = i / 9 * 6.28, h = 1 + R() * .6; const st = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.004, .006, h, 5), M.darkWood); st.position.set(3.4 + Math.cos(a) * .1, .9 + h / 2, -3.6 + Math.sin(a) * .1); st.rotation.set(Math.sin(a) * .3, 0, Math.cos(a) * .3); st.castShadow = true; scene.add(st); } }
// plain ceiling light (the "ordinary" state) over the stair
const bulbM = emis('#fff4dc', 0);
const bulb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.12, 24, 16), bulbM); bulb.position.set(.7, 4.6, -2.2); scene.add(bulb);
const bulbL = new THREE.PointLight('#fff1d6', 0, 14, 1.4); bulbL.position.set(.7, 4.5, -2.2); bulbL.castShadow = true; scene.add(bulbL);
const moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#9fb3d6', 0); moon.position.set(8, 4, -2); moon.target.position.set(0, 0, -2); scene.add(moon, moon.target);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffe9d2', '#5a4a3c', .2); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  m: { pos: [2.7, 1.3, 2.1], tgt: [.45, 1.2, -1.9], fov: 62 },
  c: { pos: [1.15, 1.02, .25], tgt: [.45, .62, -.95], fov: 44 },
  t: { pos: [3.3, .55, 1.6], tgt: [.5, 1.5, -2.2], fov: 64 },
  s: { pos: [3.6, 1.4, -1.9], tgt: [.4, 1.2, -2.0], fov: 60 },
};
// state: plain | dark | lit:<k>:<cct>
window.setState = (st) => {
  const [mode, ks, cct] = st.split(':');
  const k = mode === 'lit' ? +ks : 0;
  const col = cct === '2700' ? '#ffc488' : '#ffd6a2';
  strips.forEach((m, i) => { m.material = i < k ? emis(col, 3.2) : emis('#000', 0); });
  stripL.forEach((l, i) => { l.intensity = i < k ? 14 : 0; l.color.set(col); });
  const plain = mode === 'plain';
  bulbL.intensity = plain ? 26 : 0; bulbM.emissiveIntensity = plain ? 3 : 0;
  hemi.intensity = plain ? .55 : .045; hemi.color.set(plain ? '#ffe9d2' : '#8ea2c8');
  moon.intensity = plain ? 0 : .18; winM.emissiveIntensity = plain ? .6 : .35;
  scene.environmentIntensity = plain ? .25 : .03;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = plain ? .78 : .95;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/stonewall.html`

```html
// ===== Villa courtyard at night: a stacked-stone feature wall built from real 3D stones (so grazing light casts real shadows) =====
// wall face at z=0 (x -3.2..3.2, height 3.0), paving y=0 in front (z>0); planters + a bench; night sky behind
// lighting: 'top' = one flood from the front/top (flat, the texture disappears) · 'graze:<0..1>' = in-ground up-lights 15 cm from the wall, switching on left to right
const WW = 6.4, WH = 3.0;
const paveTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#9b9286'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 128) for (let x = (y / 128 % 2) * 128; x < w; x += 256) { g.fillStyle = `rgb(${140 + R() * 30},${132 + R() * 28},${120 + R() * 25})`; g.fillRect(x + 3, y + 3, 250, 122); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(12, 10, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: paveTex, roughness: .8 }), [0, 0, 4], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
// backing wall then the stones, course by course, each with its own size, colour and how far it stands proud
box(WW + .4, WH + .1, .2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a342d', roughness: 1 }), -WW / 2 - .2, 0, -.25);
const stoneMats = ['#c9b79a', '#b9a587', '#d6c6ab', '#a99479', '#c2ae8e', '#d0bfa1'].map((c) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: .96 }));
let y = 0;
while (y < WH - .02) {
  const ch = .09 + R() * .12; let x = -WW / 2;
  while (x < WW / 2 - .02) {
    const cw = Math.min(.18 + R() * .42, WW / 2 - x), depth = .06 + R() * .07;
    const s = box(cw - .012, ch - .012, depth, stoneMats[Math.floor(R() * stoneMats.length)], x + .006, y + .006, -.05 + R() * .045, { r: .012 });
    s.rotation.set((R() - .5) * .05, (R() - .5) * .05, (R() - .5) * .02);
    x += cw;
  }
  y += ch;
}
box(WW + .4, .08, .3, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9d2c6', roughness: .7 }), -WW / 2 - .2, WH, -.25); // coping
// planters with plants, a stone bench, a lantern
for (const x of [-2.4, 2.4]) { box(.9, .5, .55, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2f2b27', roughness: .8 }), x - .45, 0, .35); plant(x, .62, 1.6, .5); }
box(1.6, .45, .45, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d4cdc1', roughness: .8 }), -.8, 0, 1.2, { r: .02 });
// sky + a few distant lights
const skyTex = canvasTex(1024, 512, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#04060d'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#1d2338'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 140; i++) { g.fillStyle = `rgba(255,255,255,${R() * .6})`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h * .6, 1.5, 1.5); } }, [1, 1]);
plane(40, 20, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: skyTex, toneMapped: false }), [0, 8, -12], [0, 0, 0]);
// lights
const flood = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff0dc', 0, 14, 40 * Math.PI / 180, .4, 1.2); flood.position.set(0, 4.2, 5.2); flood.target.position.set(0, 1.3, 0); flood.castShadow = true; flood.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048); scene.add(flood, flood.target);
const ups = [-2.7, -1.62, -.54, .54, 1.62, 2.7].map((x, i) => {
  const s = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd6a0', 0, 6, 26 * Math.PI / 180, .7, 1.0); s.position.set(x, .03, .28); s.target.position.set(x, 2.4, -.05);
  s.castShadow = true; s.shadow.mapSize.set(1024, 1024); s.shadow.bias = -.0005; scene.add(s, s.target);
  const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .02, 24), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8f9399', metalness: 1, roughness: .3 })); ring.position.set(x, .01, .28); scene.add(ring);
  const lens = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.04, 20), emis('#000', 0)); lens.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; lens.position.set(x, .021, .28); scene.add(lens);
  return { s, lens, u: i / 5 };
});
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#7f93c0', '#2a2018', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.5, 6.0], tgt: [0, 1.4, 0], fov: 50 },         // the whole wall, straight on
  a: { pos: [2.6, 1.3, 3.0], tgt: [-.8, 1.5, 0], fov: 56 },      // at an angle, the texture rakes
  m: { pos: [.5, .9, 1.1], tgt: [-.2, 1.3, 0], fov: 46 },        // close on the stones above one up-light
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off | top · graze:<0..1>
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const top = T.top ?? 0, g = T.graze ?? 0;
  flood.intensity = 70 * top;
  ups.forEach(({ s, lens, u }) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (g * 1.12 - u) / .12)); s.intensity = 75 * k; lens.material = k ? emis('#ffe2b8', 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  hemi.intensity = .05 + .05 * top;
  scene.environmentIntensity = .03;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/strip_bed.html`

```html
// ===== Master bedroom with a gypsum cove (hidden LED strip) + a cut-away cove detail for the strip close-ups =====
// room: back wall z=0 (x -2.8..2.8), floor y=0, perimeter drop ceiling at 2.7 (0.6 wide), raised centre at 3.0
// detail station at x = PX: a piece of the gypsum cove with the strip glued on it, sagging, or in an aluminium profile
const RW2 = 2.8, RD = 5.6, DROP = 2.7, RAISE = 3.0, LIP = .6, IN = RW2 - LIP;
const woodFloorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { woodDraw('#8d6a48', '55,32,16')(g, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(25,14,6,.35)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [4, 4]);
plane(7, 7, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .45 }), [0, 0, 2.8], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e9e1d6', roughness: .92 });
box(5.8, RAISE + .1, .1, wallM, -2.9, 0, -.1); box(.1, RAISE + .1, RD + .2, wallM, -RW2 - .1, 0, -.1); box(.1, RAISE + .1, RD + .2, wallM, RW2, 0, -.1);
const gyp = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3efe9', roughness: .95 });
// drop ceiling band (back, left, right) with a lip on its inner edge hiding the strip, and the raised centre
box(5.6, .08, LIP, gyp, -RW2, DROP, 0, { cast: false });
box(LIP, .08, RD, gyp, -RW2, DROP, 0, { cast: false }); box(LIP, .08, RD, gyp, IN, DROP, 0, { cast: false });
box(2 * IN, .03, .04, gyp, -IN, DROP + .08, LIP - .04); box(.04, .03, RD - LIP, gyp, -IN, DROP + .08, LIP); box(.04, .03, RD - LIP, gyp, IN - .04, DROP + .08, LIP);
box(2 * IN, .06, RD - LIP + .1, gyp, -IN, RAISE, LIP - .1, { cast: false });
box(.06, RAISE - DROP, RD, gyp, -IN - .03, DROP, LIP - .03, { cast: false }); box(.06, RAISE - DROP, RD, gyp, IN - .03, DROP, LIP - .03, { cast: false });
box(2 * IN, RAISE - DROP, .06, gyp, -IN, DROP, LIP - .06, { cast: false });
// headboard wall: upholstered panel, bed, night stands with lamps, bench, rug, curtain on the right
const panelM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#c9b79c', roughness: .95 });
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) box(.48, 1.25, .08, panelM, -1.5 + i * .5, .55, .02, { r: .03 });
const bedM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#e8e1d5', roughness: .95 });
box(1.9, .35, 2.1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6b5240', roughness: .6 }), -.95, 0, .12, { r: .04 });
box(1.84, .22, 2.0, bedM, -.92, .35, .16, { r: .08 });
box(1.86, .06, 1.3, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8f7b63', roughness: .95 }), -.93, .56, .9, { r: .03 });
[[-.55, '#f2ede4'], [.05, '#f2ede4'], [-.35, '#b08a4e'], [.25, '#b08a4e']].forEach(([x, c], i) => { const m = box(.5, .32, .14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: c, roughness: .9 }), x - (i > 1 ? .05 : .2), .57, .22 + (i > 1 ? .14 : 0), { r: .07 }); m.rotation.x = -.25; });
for (const sx of [-1, 1]) {
  box(.5, .5, .42, M.darkWood, sx * 1.35 - .25, 0, .1, { r: .02 });
  const base = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07, .09, .3, 24), M.brass); base.position.set(sx * 1.35, .65, .3); scene.add(base);
  const sh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.13, .17, .22, 32, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efe6d6', roughness: .8, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); sh.position.set(sx * 1.35, .88, .3); scene.add(sh);
}
box(1.4, .42, .4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#8f7b63', roughness: .95 }), -.7, 0, 2.35, { r: .05 });
plane(3, 2.2, M.rug, [0, .006, 1.6], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const curtM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d8cfc2', roughness: 1, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, DROP - .05, 12, 1, true), curtM); c.position.set(RW2 - .12, (DROP - .05) / 2, 2.4 + i * .1); scene.add(c); }
plant(-2.35, 3.6, 1.4, .5);

// ---- the strip: segments along the cove (back, left, right); each washes the raised ceiling with an additive band ----
const washTex = canvasTex(64, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)'); gr.addColorStop(.3, 'rgba(255,255,255,.5)'); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }, [1, 1], false);
const segs = []; // { m, wm, u } u = 0..1 along the run (left front → back → right front)
const runs = [ // [x0,z0, x1,z1, inward dir]
  [-IN + .1, RD - .1, -IN + .1, LIP + .1, [1, 0]],
  [-IN + .1, LIP + .1, IN - .1, LIP + .1, [0, 1]],
  [IN - .1, LIP + .1, IN - .1, RD - .1, [-1, 0]],
];
const total = runs.reduce((s, [a, b, c, d]) => s + Math.hypot(c - a, d - b), 0); let acc = 0;
for (const [x0, z0, x1, z1, [ix, iz]] of runs) {
  const len = Math.hypot(x1 - x0, z1 - z0), n = Math.round(len / .3);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const f = (i + .5) / n, x = x0 + (x1 - x0) * f, z = z0 + (z1 - z0) * f, sl = len / n, along = Math.abs(x1 - x0) > .01;
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(along ? sl * .99 : .025, .01, along ? .025 : sl * .99), emis('#000', 0)); m.position.set(x - ix * .08, DROP + .09, z - iz * .08); scene.add(m);
    const wm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: washTex, color: '#000', transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false });
    const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(sl, 1.3), wm); w.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
    w.rotation.z = along ? 0 : Math.PI / 2; if (!along && ix < 0) w.rotation.z = -Math.PI / 2; if (along && iz < 0) w.rotation.z = Math.PI;
    w.position.set(x + ix * .6, RAISE - .005, z + iz * .6); scene.add(w);
    const v = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(sl, .3), wm); v.position.set(x - ix * .09 + ix * .0, DROP + .22, z - iz * .09); v.rotation.y = along ? 0 : (ix > 0 ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2); scene.add(v);
    segs.push({ m, wm, u: (acc + f * len) / total, i: segs.length });
  }
  acc += len;
}
const bounce = [[-IN + .5, 3], [0, 1.2], [IN - .5, 3]].map(([x, z]) => { const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff1dc', 0, x === 0 ? 3.6 : .6, x === 0 ? .6 : 4.6); l.position.set(x, RAISE - .04, z); l.lookAt(x, 0, z); scene.add(l); return l; });
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#ffeedd', '#3a2c20', .05); scene.add(hemi);
const night = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf9a', 0, 6, 2); night.position.set(0, 1.6, 3.2); scene.add(night);

// ---- detail station: a piece of the cove close up ----
const PX = 30, dark = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0d0b0a', roughness: 1 });
plane(8, 6, dark, [PX, 1, -1.2], [0, 0, 0]); plane(8, 8, dark, [PX, -.01, 1], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const gypD = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#a8a299', roughness: .95 });
box(1.6, .9, .08, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6f6a63', roughness: .95 }), PX - .8, .6, -.33); // the cove's back face
box(1.6, .1, .5, gypD, PX - .8, .5, -.25);                       // the gypsum ledge
const ALU = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c3c8ce', roughness: .28, metalness: 1 });
const pcbM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2efe8', roughness: .6 }), pcbBurnt = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#8a6a3a', roughness: .7 });
const D = { prof: new THREE.Group(), flat: new THREE.Group(), sag: new THREE.Group(), cover: null, chips: [], sagChips: [], profChips: [] };
Object.values(D).forEach(g => g && g.isGroup && scene.add(g));
const flatPcb = box(1.3, .006, .05, pcbM, PX - .65, .6, -.025, { parent: D.flat });
for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) D.chips.push(box(.035, .012, .03, emis('#000', 0), PX - .62 + i * .08, .606, -.015, { parent: D.flat }));
// sagging strip: the middle has come off the gypsum and hangs over the edge
for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) { const f = i / 25, x = PX - .65 + f * 1.3, drop = Math.sin(f * Math.PI) ** 1.5 * .28, out = Math.sin(f * Math.PI) ** 1.5 * .32;
  const seg = box(.052, .006, .05, pcbM, x - .026, .6 - drop, -.025 + out, { parent: D.sag }); seg.rotation.x = -Math.sin(f * Math.PI) * .9;
  if (i % 2 === 0) D.sagChips.push(box(.035, .012, .03, emis('#000', 0), x - .017, .606 - drop, -.015 + out, { parent: D.sag })); }
// aluminium U profile with the strip inside, and the frosted diffuser that clips on top
box(1.4, .012, .07, ALU, PX - .7, .6, -.035, { parent: D.prof }); box(1.4, .05, .008, ALU, PX - .7, .6, -.035, { parent: D.prof }); box(1.4, .05, .008, ALU, PX - .7, .6, .027, { parent: D.prof });
for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) box(1.4, .006, .004, ALU, PX - .7, .58 - k * .0, -.035 + k * .016, { parent: D.prof }); // fins under the profile
box(1.3, .006, .05, pcbM, PX - .65, .612, -.025, { parent: D.prof });
for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) D.profChips.push(box(.035, .012, .03, emis('#000', 0), PX - .62 + i * .08, .618, -.015, { parent: D.prof }));
D.cover = box(1.4, .02, .066, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#fff', emissive: '#fff4e4', emissiveIntensity: 0, roughness: .4, transparent: true, opacity: .92 }), PX - .7, .652, -.033, { parent: D.prof });
const heatGlow = new THREE.PointLight('#ff4a1a', 0, 1.2, 2); heatGlow.position.set(PX, .7, .1); scene.add(heatGlow);
const chipLight = new THREE.PointLight('#fff1dc', 0, 2.5, 2); chipLight.position.set(PX, .9, .25); scene.add(chipLight);
const keyD = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffffff', 0, 6, .7, .7, 1.2); keyD.position.set(PX + 1.2, 1.9, 1.6); keyD.target.position.set(PX, .55, 0); scene.add(keyD, keyD.target);
const rimD = new THREE.PointLight('#9fb8ff', 0, 4, 2); rimD.position.set(PX - 1.4, 1.2, -.6); scene.add(rimD);

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [1.7, 1.55, 5.3], tgt: [-.4, 1.75, .6], fov: 72 },      // the bedroom from the door corner
  c: { pos: [.9, 1.65, 3.4], tgt: [-1.6, 2.9, .7], fov: 60 },       // up at the cove corner over the bed
  b: { pos: [0, 1.2, 3.6], tgt: [0, 1.6, 0], fov: 62 },             // the bed and headboard straight on
  d: { pos: [PX + .9, 1.25, 1.55], tgt: [PX - .1, .52, 0], fov: 38 }, // the cove detail, three-quarter from above
  e: { pos: [PX + .35, .92, .6], tgt: [PX - .15, .58, 0], fov: 34 },  // macro on the chips
};
const CNEW = new THREE.Color('#fff3e2'), CAGED = new THREE.Color('#ffb24a');
const DEAD = new Set([3, 4, 9, 15, 16, 17, 24, 30, 31, 38, 44, 45]);
// room states: off | new | aged | age:<0..1> (yellowing over a year) | grow:<0..1> (the strip lighting up along the run)
// detail states: flat | burn:<0..1> | sag | prof | cover   (detail views only)
window.setState = (state) => {
  const [st, arg] = state.split(':'); const a = arg === undefined ? 1 : +arg;
  const room = ['off', 'new', 'aged', 'age', 'grow'].includes(st);
  const age = st === 'aged' ? 1 : st === 'age' ? a : 0;
  for (const s of segs) {
    let k = st === 'off' || !room ? 0 : st === 'grow' ? clampS((a * 1.15 - s.u) / .15) : 1;
    const col = CNEW.clone().lerp(CAGED, age); col.lerp(new THREE.Color('#ff9a40'), age * ((s.i * 37) % 10) / 25);
    if (age > 0) k *= DEAD.has(s.i) ? clampS(1 - (age - .35) / .3) : 1 - .45 * age * (.6 + .4 * Math.sin(s.i * 2.7) ** 2);
    s.wm.color.copy(col).multiplyScalar(1.35 * k);
    s.m.material = k > 0 ? emis('#' + col.getHexString(), 3 * k + .3) : emis('#000', 0);
  }
  const lit = room && st !== 'off' ? (st === 'grow' ? clampS(a) : 1) * (1 - .45 * age) : 0;
  bounce.forEach(l => { l.intensity = 4 * lit; l.color.copy(CNEW.clone().lerp(CAGED, age)); });
  night.intensity = room ? (st === 'off' ? .25 : .5) : 0; hemi.intensity = room ? (st === 'off' ? .12 : .07) : .02;
  // detail station
  const det = !room; D.flat.visible = det && (st === 'flat' || st === 'burn'); D.sag.visible = det && st === 'sag'; D.prof.visible = det && (st === 'prof' || st === 'cover');
  const burn = st === 'burn' ? a : 0;
  flatPcb.material = burn > .5 ? pcbBurnt : pcbM;
  D.chips.forEach((c, i) => { const dead = burn > .6 && i % 5 === 2; const col = new THREE.Color('#fff3e2').lerp(new THREE.Color('#d08a2a'), burn); c.material = dead ? emis('#1a120a', .2) : emis('#' + col.getHexString(), 4 * (1 - .55 * burn)); });
  D.sagChips.forEach(c => c.material = emis('#ffc070', 2.2));
  D.profChips.forEach(c => c.material = emis('#fff3e2', st === 'cover' ? 1 : 4));
  D.cover.visible = st === 'cover'; D.cover.material.emissiveIntensity = st === 'cover' ? 1.6 : 0;
  heatGlow.intensity = det ? 1.6 * burn : 0; chipLight.intensity = det ? (st === 'cover' ? .35 : .22) : 0;
  keyD.intensity = det ? .9 : 0; rimD.intensity = det ? .8 : 0;
  scene.environmentIntensity = room ? .04 : .1; renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
function clampS(x) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x)); }
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/stripprof.html`

```html
// ===== LED strip: bare vs in an aluminium profile with an opal cover =====
// A plaster wall with a long shadow-gap shelf at y = 1.6. Left half: a bare SMD strip (separate dots, each throwing its own
// scallop on the wall). Right half: the same strip inside an aluminium profile with an opal cover (one smooth line).
// A detail station at x = 10 shows the profile cut open (channel, strip, cover) next to a bare strip.
// state tokens joined by '+':  on · age:<0..1> bare strip turns yellow and patchy · cob (left half becomes a COB strip: smooth, no dots)
//   · cut (show the cut-open profile parts at the detail station)
const WARM = '#ffdcb0';
scene.background = new THREE.Color('#0b0a0c');
const wallM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#e9e2d7', roughness: .9 });
box(8, 3, .1, wallM, -4, 0, -.1);
box(8, .05, 2.5, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#8a6a4a', roughness: .5 }), -4, -.05, 0, { cast: false });
// a floating shelf with the strip hidden on its underside front edge
const shelfM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2622', roughness: .5 });
box(7.6, .06, .26, shelfM, -3.8, 1.6, 0);
box(.02, .02, .02, M.black, 0, 1.58, .2); // divider marker under the middle
// bare strip (left): 40 dots, each with a little point light pointing down the wall
const DOTS = [];
for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) { const x = -3.6 + i * .14; const d = box(.03, .008, .03, emis('#000', 0), x - .015, 1.592, .1, { cast: false }); const l = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 2.2, 55 * Math.PI / 180, .2, 1.6); l.position.set(x, 1.58, .08); l.target.position.set(x, .8, -.1); scene.add(l, l.target); DOTS.push({ d, l, x }); }
const pcbM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efefe6', roughness: .5 }); box(3.7, .004, .05, pcbM, -3.65, 1.596, .085, { cast: false });
// COB strip on the left (replaces the dots when "cob")
const cobBar = box(3.7, .006, .04, emis('#000', 0), -3.65, 1.592, .09, { cast: false });
const cobL = new THREE.RectAreaLight(WARM, 0, 3.7, .05); cobL.position.set(-1.8, 1.585, .1); cobL.lookAt(-1.8, .8, -.2); scene.add(cobL);
// profiled strip (right): aluminium channel + opal cover, one smooth line of light
const alu = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b8bcc0', roughness: .35, metalness: .9 });
box(3.7, .018, .03, alu, .15, 1.585, .08, { cast: false });
const opal = box(3.66, .006, .024, emis('#000', 0), .17, 1.58, .083, { cast: false });
const proL = new THREE.RectAreaLight(WARM, 0, 3.66, .04); proL.position.set(2.0, 1.575, .1); proL.lookAt(2.0, .8, -.2); scene.add(proL);
// detail station (x = 10): a short cut-open profile with its parts, next to a bare strip, on a dark plinth
box(2.4, .5, .8, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1a1818', roughness: .6 }), 8.8, 0, -.4);
const CUT = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(CUT);
{ const ch = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.5, .04, .06), alu); ch.position.set(10.35, .55, 0); CUT.add(ch);
  const st = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.46, .006, .04), pcbM); st.position.set(10.35, .58, 0); CUT.add(st);
  const cv = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.48, .01, .055), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f2ee', roughness: .3, transparent: true, opacity: .85 })); cv.position.set(10.35, .66, 0); CUT.add(cv);
  const bare = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.46, .006, .04), pcbM); bare.position.set(9.6, .53, 0); CUT.add(bare); }
const cutDots = []; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.03, .01, .03), emis('#000', 0)); d.position.set(9.4 + i * .08, .538, 0); scene.add(d); cutDots.push(d); }
const cutGlow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.46, .012, .05), emis('#000', 0)); cutGlow.position.set(10.35, .667, 0); scene.add(cutGlow);
const key = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff1e0', 6, 6, 35 * Math.PI / 180, .7, 1.5); key.position.set(9.0, 1.8, 1.4); key.target.position.set(10, .55, 0); scene.add(key, key.target);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#cdd5e6', '#2a2018', .06); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.3, 3.6], tgt: [0, 1.2, 0], fov: 66 },          // the whole wall: bare left, profiled right
  l: { pos: [-1.9, 1.3, 1.15], tgt: [-1.9, 1.25, -.1], fov: 56 },    // the bare (or COB) half close up
  r: { pos: [1.9, 1.3, 1.15], tgt: [1.9, 1.25, -.1], fov: 56 },      // the profiled half close up
  d: { pos: [9.98, 1.15, 1.5], tgt: [9.98, .58, 0], fov: 74 },         // the cut-open profile next to a bare strip
};
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const on = T.on ?? 0, age = T.age ?? 0, cob = T.cob ?? 0;
  const bareCol = new THREE.Color(WARM).lerp(new THREE.Color('#ffae3a'), age);
  DOTS.forEach(({ d, l }, i) => { const dead = age > 0 && (i * 7) % 11 < 3 * age; const k = on && !cob && !dead ? (1 - .35 * age) : 0; l.color.copy(bareCol); l.intensity = 1.0 * k; d.material = k ? emis(bareCol, 9 * k) : emis(dead && on && !cob ? '#3a2a14' : '#000', dead && on ? .5 : 0); });
  cobBar.material = on && cob ? emis(WARM, 7) : emis('#000', 0); cobL.intensity = on && cob ? 26 : 0;
  opal.material = on ? emis(WARM, 6) : emis('#000', 0); proL.intensity = on ? 26 : 0;
  CUT.visible = true; cutDots.forEach((d) => { d.material = on ? emis(WARM, 9) : emis('#000', 0); }); cutGlow.material = on ? emis(WARM, 5) : emis('#000', 0);
  hemi.intensity = .06; scene.environmentIntensity = .04; renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/versus6.html`

```html
// ===== Six product pairs, cheap vs quality, each on its own dark studio station (x = i*8): 0 spots (shallow glaring vs deep anti-glare), 1 LED strip (dotted, yellowed vs COB line), 2 drivers (cheap plastic vs finned metal, each feeding a bulb), 3 strip over a long run (12 V fading to orange vs 24 V even), 4 colour rendering (fruit under CRI 80 vs 90+), 5 outdoor floodlight on a stone wall at night (unprotected, dusty, weak vs IP65 die-cast, clean beam) =====
// views a<i> = the cheap product, b<i> = the quality one. states: on (default) · flick (the cheap driver's bulb dipped) · off
const SX = (i) => i * 8;
const studioFloor = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1d1b1a', roughness: .55 });
const studioWall = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3a3836', roughness: .9 });
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { plane(7.6, 6, studioFloor, [SX(i), 0, 1], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]); box(7.6, 4, .1, studioWall, SX(i) - 3.8, 0, -1.3); }
const cheap = [], good = [];
// --- 0: spots in a ceiling slab
{
  const X = SX(0); box(3.2, .1, 2.4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e9e6e1', roughness: .9 }), X - 1.6, 2.4, -1.2, { cast: false });
  // cheap: flat white trim, big flush cold lens, wide beam
  const trim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.085, .085, .012, 32), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f4f4f2', roughness: .4 })); trim.position.set(X - .6, 2.394, 0); scene.add(trim);
  const lensA = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.068, .068, .004, 32), emis('#eef4ff', 12)); lensA.position.set(X - .6, 2.386, 0); scene.add(lensA);
  const sA = new THREE.SpotLight('#eef4ff', 30, 6, 60 * Math.PI / 180, .2, 1.3); sA.position.set(X - .6, 2.37, 0); sA.target.position.set(X - .6, 0, 0); scene.add(sA, sA.target);
  cheap.push({ on: () => { sA.intensity = 30; lensA.material = emis('#eef4ff', 12); }, off: () => { sA.intensity = 0; lensA.material = emis('#000', 0); } });
  // quality: black deep cup, the emitter far up inside, narrow warm beam
  const cup = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07, .06, .16, 32, 1, true), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#0b0b0b', roughness: .35, metalness: .4, side: THREE.DoubleSide })); cup.position.set(X + .6, 2.33, 0); scene.add(cup);
  const rimB = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.075, .008, 8, 32), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#111', roughness: .3, metalness: .6 })); rimB.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; rimB.position.set(X + .6, 2.395, 0); scene.add(rimB);
  const lensB = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.022, .022, .004, 24), emis('#ffd9a8', 8)); lensB.position.set(X + .6, 2.41, 0); scene.add(lensB);
  const sB = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffd9a8', 34, 6, 24 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.3); sB.position.set(X + .6, 2.25, 0); sB.target.position.set(X + .6, 0, 0); scene.add(sB, sB.target);
  good.push({ on: () => { sB.intensity = 34; lensB.material = emis('#ffd9a8', 8); }, off: () => { sB.intensity = 0; lensB.material = emis('#000', 0); } });
}
// --- 1: LED strip in a shelf profile, washing the wall above
{
  const X = SX(1);
  const mk = (x0, cob) => {
    box(1.3, .04, .25, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#7a5a40', roughness: .5 }), x0, 1.1, -1.25);
    const parts = [];
    if (cob) { const m = box(1.2, .006, .012, emis('#fff1dc', 7), x0 + .05, 1.141, -1.12, { cast: false }); parts.push(m); }
    else for (let k = 0; k < 24; k++) { const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.018, .006, .012), emis('#ffc56a', 9)); d.position.set(x0 + .07 + k * .05, 1.144, -1.115); scene.add(d); parts.push(d); }
    const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight(cob ? '#fff1dc' : '#ffbf66', cob ? 30 : 16, 1.2, .05); l.position.set(x0 + .65, 1.15, -1.13); l.lookAt(x0 + .65, 3, -1.25); scene.add(l);
    return { parts, l, cob };
  };
  const A = mk(X - 1.5, false), B = mk(X + .2, true);
  cheap.push({ on: () => { A.l.intensity = 16; A.parts.forEach((p) => p.material = emis('#ffc56a', 9)); }, off: () => { A.l.intensity = 0; A.parts.forEach((p) => p.material = emis('#000', 0)); } });
  good.push({ on: () => { B.l.intensity = 30; B.parts.forEach((p) => p.material = emis('#fff1dc', 7)); }, off: () => { B.l.intensity = 0; B.parts.forEach((p) => p.material = emis('#000', 0)); } });
}
// --- 2: drivers on a bench, each feeding a globe bulb above it
{
  const X = SX(2);
  box(3.0, .06, .9, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a2725', roughness: .6 }), X - 1.5, .9, -1.1);
  // cheap: thin white plastic box with a paper label
  box(.26, .05, .12, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#efeeea', roughness: .5 }), X - .73, .96, -.7, { r: .01 });
  box(.12, .002, .07, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d6d2c4', roughness: .9 }), X - .66, 1.011, -.67, { cast: false });
  // quality: finned aluminium body with a black label plate
  const al = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#a7abb0', roughness: .35, metalness: .9 });
  box(.34, .06, .16, al, X + .43, .96, -.72); for (let k = 0; k < 7; k++) box(.34, .025, .008, al, X + .43, 1.02, -.715 + k * .024);
  box(.14, .002, .1, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#141414', roughness: .4 }), X + .53, 1.046, -.69, { cast: false });
  const cable = (x) => { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.006, .006, .9, 8), M.black); c.position.set(x, 1.45, -.95); scene.add(c); };
  cable(X - .6); cable(X + .6);
  const bulb = (x) => { const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.09, 24, 16), emis('#ffd9a8', 7)); b.position.set(x, 1.95, -.95); scene.add(b); const p = new THREE.PointLight('#ffcf94', 4, 4, 1.6); p.position.set(x, 1.9, -.85); scene.add(p); return { b, p }; };
  const A = bulb(X - .6), B = bulb(X + .6);
  cheap.push({ on: (k = 1) => { A.p.intensity = 4 * k; A.b.material = emis('#ffd9a8', 7 * k + .2); }, off: () => { A.p.intensity = 0; A.b.material = emis('#000', 0); } });
  good.push({ on: () => { B.p.intensity = 4; B.b.material = emis('#ffd9a8', 7); }, off: () => { B.p.intensity = 0; B.b.material = emis('#000', 0); } });
}
// --- 3: a long strip run along the wall (5 m): 12 V fades and turns orange toward the end; 24 V stays even
{
  const X = SX(3);
  const run = (y, v24) => {
    box(5.2, .04, .14, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d8d4cc', roughness: .8 }), X - 2.6, y, -1.25);
    const segs = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
      const f = k / 9, kk = v24 ? 1 : 1 - .82 * f; const col = v24 ? '#fff1dc' : new THREE.Color('#fff1dc').lerp(new THREE.Color('#ff8a2a'), f).getStyle();
      const m = box(.5, .006, .012, emis(col, 9 * kk), X - 2.55 + k * .51, y - .007, -1.15, { cast: false });
      const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight(col, 34 * kk, .5, .05); l.position.set(X - 2.3 + k * .51, y - .01, -1.16); l.lookAt(X - 2.3 + k * .51, y - 3, -1.25); scene.add(l);
      segs.push({ m, l, col, kk });
    }
    return segs;
  };
  const A = run(2.6, false), B = run(1.25, true);
  const sw = (S, on) => S.forEach(({ m, l, col, kk }) => { l.intensity = on ? 34 * kk : 0; m.material = on ? emis(col, 9 * kk) : emis('#000', 0); });
  cheap.push({ on: () => sw(A, 1), off: () => sw(A, 0) }); good.push({ on: () => sw(B, 1), off: () => sw(B, 0) });
}
// --- 4: colour rendering: the same fruit plate under a CRI 80 lamp (colours simulated dull) and a CRI 90+ lamp
{
  const X = SX(4);
  box(3.0, .06, 1.0, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2d2a28', roughness: .6 }), X - 1.5, .8, -1.2);
  box(.04, 1.4, 1.0, studioWall, X - .02, .86, -1.2); // divider
  const plate = (x, vivid) => {
    const p = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.24, .2, .03, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f2efe9', roughness: .3 })); p.position.set(x, .875, -.7); scene.add(p);
    const fr = vivid ? ['#d4202a', '#f2b51b', '#3f9b2f', '#e8641c', '#7a2a6b'] : ['#8f5a55', '#a99a6c', '#6d7d5f', '#a07a5e', '#6c5764'];
    [[-.09, -.05], [.08, -.06], [0, .08], [-.1, .09], [.11, .07]].forEach(([dx, dz], i) => { const s = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.06 + (i % 2) * .012, 20, 14), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: fr[i], roughness: .45 })); s.position.set(x + dx, .95, -.7 + dz); scene.add(s); });
    const lamp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 24), emis(vivid ? '#ffe3c0' : '#f3f6ff', 6)); lamp.position.set(x, 2.0, -.7); scene.add(lamp);
    const s = new THREE.SpotLight(vivid ? '#ffe3c0' : '#f3f6ff', 22, 4, 34 * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.3); s.position.set(x, 1.98, -.7); s.target.position.set(x, .8, -.7); scene.add(s, s.target);
    return { lamp, s, c: vivid ? '#ffe3c0' : '#f3f6ff' };
  };
  const A = plate(X - .75, false), B = plate(X + .75, true);
  const sw = (P, on) => { P.s.intensity = on ? 22 : 0; P.lamp.material = on ? emis(P.c, 6) : emis('#000', 0); };
  cheap.push({ on: () => sw(A, 1), off: () => sw(A, 0) }); good.push({ on: () => sw(B, 1), off: () => sw(B, 0) });
}
// --- 5: outdoor floodlights on a stone wall at night
{
  const X = SX(5);
  box(4.0, 3.0, .12, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: stoneTex, color: '#b9a78c', roughness: .9 }), X - 2.0, 0, -1.2);
  // cheap: thin grey housing, rust patches, fogged glass, weak yellow beam, dust on top
  const hA = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#7d7a74', roughness: .7, metalness: .3 });
  box(.34, .26, .07, hA, X - .9, 2.1, -1.06); for (const [dx, dy] of [[.03, .02], [.25, .18], [.1, .2]]) box(.06, .04, .004, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#7a3d1a', roughness: 1 }), X - .9 + dx, 2.1 + dy, -.988, { cast: false });
  const glassA = box(.28, .2, .004, emis('#c9a560', 1.6), X - .87, 2.13, -.99, { cast: false });
  box(.34, .02, .07, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b39a72', roughness: 1 }), X - .9, 2.36, -1.06); // dust
  const fA = new THREE.SpotLight('#ffcf80', 8, 5, 40 * Math.PI / 180, .3, 1.5); fA.position.set(X - .73, 2.2, -.95); fA.target.position.set(X - .73, 0, -1.2); scene.add(fA, fA.target);
  // quality: black die-cast with fins, clear tempered glass, strong neutral beam grazing the stone
  const hB = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#141414', roughness: .35, metalness: .7 });
  box(.36, .28, .08, hB, X + .55, 2.1, -1.07); for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) box(.36, .01, .03, hB, X + .55, 2.12 + k * .045, -1.1);
  const glassB = box(.3, .22, .004, emis('#fff4e2', 6), X + .58, 2.13, -.988, { cast: false });
  const fB = new THREE.SpotLight('#fff4e2', 40, 6, 36 * Math.PI / 180, .45, 1.3); fB.position.set(X + .73, 2.2, -.95); fB.target.position.set(X + .73, 0, -1.2); scene.add(fB, fB.target);
  cheap.push({ on: () => { fA.intensity = 8; glassA.material = emis('#c9a560', 1.6); }, off: () => { fA.intensity = 0; glassA.material = emis('#000', 0); } });
  good.push({ on: () => { fB.intensity = 40; glassB.material = emis('#fff4e2', 6); }, off: () => { fB.intensity = 0; glassB.material = emis('#000', 0); } });
}
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#d9dde8', '#2a2018', .12); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  a0: { pos: [SX(0) - .6, 1.25, 1.3], tgt: [SX(0) - .6, 2.1, -.2], fov: 52 }, b0: { pos: [SX(0) + .6, 1.25, 1.3], tgt: [SX(0) + .6, 2.1, -.2], fov: 52 },
  a1: { pos: [SX(1) - .85, 1.3, -.05], tgt: [SX(1) - .85, 1.4, -1.25], fov: 50 }, b1: { pos: [SX(1) + .85, 1.3, -.05], tgt: [SX(1) + .85, 1.4, -1.25], fov: 50 },
  a2: { pos: [SX(2) - .6, 1.4, .7], tgt: [SX(2) - .6, 1.4, -.9], fov: 40 }, b2: { pos: [SX(2) + .6, 1.4, .7], tgt: [SX(2) + .6, 1.4, -.9], fov: 40 },
  a3: { pos: [SX(3) - 2.4, 2.2, .9], tgt: [SX(3) + 1.6, 2.35, -1.25], fov: 58 }, b3: { pos: [SX(3) - 2.4, .85, .9], tgt: [SX(3) + 1.6, 1.0, -1.25], fov: 58 },
  a4: { pos: [SX(4) - .75, 1.45, .45], tgt: [SX(4) - .75, .95, -.7], fov: 50 }, b4: { pos: [SX(4) + .75, 1.45, .45], tgt: [SX(4) + .75, .95, -.7], fov: 50 },
  a5: { pos: [SX(5) - .75, 1.5, 1.8], tgt: [SX(5) - .75, 1.5, -1.2], fov: 60 }, b5: { pos: [SX(5) + .7, 1.5, 1.8], tgt: [SX(5) + .7, 1.5, -1.2], fov: 60 },
};
window.setState = (state) => {
  const st = state || 'on';
  cheap.forEach((c, i) => st === 'off' ? c.off() : (st === 'flick' && i === 2 ? c.on(.12) : c.on()));
  good.forEach((g) => st === 'off' ? g.off() : g.on());
  scene.environmentIntensity = .05; renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/villacut.html`

```html
// ===== Villa cut-away at night for contractors: one supplier for the whole villa =====
// A two-storey villa with its front wall removed (dollhouse view), x -5..5, depth z -6..0, garden in front (z 0..8).
// Ground: majlis (left), family hall (middle), kitchen (right). Upper: master (left), upper hall (middle), kids (right).
// The ground-floor gypsum ceiling hangs below the slab: drivers sit in the void above it (detail view d).
// state tokens joined by '+':
//   off                      night, nothing on
//   mix = ms+mh+mo           bought from 4 shops (ms spots, mh coves, mo outdoor): every room a different colour temperature, a cold cove, a yellow cove, greenish garden floods
//   flick:<0..1>             (with mix) the cheap drivers dip: rooms on cheap drivers go dark by this amount
//   in:<0..1>                3000 K spots, room by room   hid:<0..1>  hidden cove strips, room by room
//   out:<0..1>               outdoor: wall washers, path bollards, palm up-lights, boundary-wall lights (IP65)
//   drv                      matching drivers above the gypsum, highlighted with status LEDs   cheap  odd drivers, one red
//   all = in+hid+out         dim:<x> scales everything
const VW = 5, VD = 6, F1 = 0, F2 = 3.3, GC = 2.75, SL = 3.12, RF = 6.6;
scene.background = new THREE.Color('#070b16');
marbleTex.repeat.set(4, 2);
const wallIn = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#ece5da', roughness: .9 });
const facade = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d9cbb3', roughness: .85 });
const slabM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#9a948b', roughness: .9 });
const gypM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#f3efe8', roughness: .95 });
const woodF = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: oakTex, color: '#a07a55', roughness: .55 });
// floors / slabs / roof
box(VW * 2, .12, VD, M.floor, -VW, -.12, -VD);
box(VW * 2 + .3, SL + .3 - SL, VD + .1, slabM, -VW - .15, SL, -VD - .05);                 // first-floor slab 3.12..3.42
box(VW * 2, .06, VD, woodF, -VW, SL + .3, -VD, { cast: false });                            // upper floor finish (3.42)
box(VW * 2 + .3, .3, VD + .1, slabM, -VW - .15, RF, -VD - .05);                             // roof slab
box(VW * 2 + .3, .55, .2, facade, -VW - .15, RF + .3, -.15);                                // parapet front
box(VW * 2 + .3, .55, .2, facade, -VW - .15, RF + .3, -VD - .05);
// outer walls (back, left, right), with the front cut open
box(VW * 2, RF, .2, wallIn, -VW, 0, -VD - .2);
box(.25, RF + .3, VD + .2, facade, -VW - .25, 0, -VD - .2);
box(.25, RF + .3, VD + .2, facade, VW, 0, -VD - .2);
// front cut edges (thin facade frame so the cut reads like a section)
const cutM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#4a443d', roughness: .8 });
box(VW * 2 + .5, .3, .04, cutM, -VW - .25, SL, -.02, { cast: false });
// partitions
for (const x of [-1.7, 1.7]) { box(.14, GC, VD, wallIn, x - .07, 0, -VD); box(.14, RF - SL - .36, VD, wallIn, x - .07, SL + .36, -VD); }
// ground gypsum ceiling (hung below the slab: the void in between holds drivers and cables)
const gypsum = [];
for (const [x0, x1] of [[-VW, -1.77], [-1.63, 1.63], [1.77, VW]]) gypsum.push(box(x1 - x0, .05, VD, gypM, x0, GC, -VD, { cast: false }));
// upper ceiling under the roof slab
box(VW * 2, .05, VD, gypM, -VW, RF - .05, -VD, { cast: false });
// windows on the back wall (night outside)
const nightTex = canvasTex(256, 320, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#0a1430'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#16223c'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.7)'; for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h * .6, 2, 2); }, [1, 1]);
const winM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: nightTex, emissive: '#ffffff', emissiveMap: nightTex, emissiveIntensity: .25, roughness: .2 });
for (const [x, y] of [[-3.4, 1.0], [3.4, 1.0], [-3.4, 4.3], [0, 4.3], [3.4, 4.3]]) { plane(1.3, 1.3, winM, [x, y + .65, -VD + .01], [0, 0, 0]); box(1.4, .06, .06, M.black, x - .7, y - .03, -VD + .01); box(1.4, .06, .06, M.black, x - .7, y + 1.3, -VD + .01); }
// ---------- furniture ----------
const cushM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fabricTex, color: '#b9a27f', roughness: .9 });
const redM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#7a2b26', roughness: .85 });
// majlis: low floor seating along the walls + a carpet + coffee pots tray
box(3.0, .32, .7, cushM, -4.85, 0, -5.9, { r: .05 }); box(3.0, .45, .18, cushM, -4.85, .32, -5.9, { r: .05 });
box(.7, .32, 3.2, cushM, -4.85, 0, -5.2, { r: .05 });
for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) box(.4, .32, .14, redM, -4.6 + i * .7, .34, -5.72, { r: .05 });
box(2.4, .012, 2.6, M.rug, -4.0, .001, -4.6, { cast: false });
box(.8, .06, .5, M.brass, -3.2, .2, -3.5); box(.05, .2, .05, M.brass, -2.85, 0, -3.3);
// family hall: TV wall in fluted panel, sofa, coffee table
box(3.1, 2.3, .06, M.fluted, -1.55, .2, -VD + .01, { cast: false });
box(1.6, .9, .04, M.screen, -.8, 1.0, -VD + .08);
box(2.4, .42, .9, M.fabric, -1.2, 0, -2.4, { r: .06 }); box(2.4, .5, .22, M.fabric, -1.2, .42, -1.72, { r: .06 });
box(1.0, .34, .6, M.tableTop, -.5, 0, -4.0);
plant(1.25, -5.3, 1.3, .45);
// kitchen: back counter + wall cabinets + island + pendants
const doorM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6f7d73', roughness: .55 });
box(3.0, .88, .62, doorM, 1.9, 0, -VD); box(3.0, .04, .66, M.tableTop, 1.9, .88, -VD);
box(3.0, .7, .36, doorM, 1.9, 1.55, -VD);

const pendKit = [3.0, 3.85].map((x) => { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.005, .005, .9, 6), M.black); c.position.set(x, GC - .45, -3.15); scene.add(c); const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.16, 24, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1a19', roughness: .4, metalness: .5 })); d.position.set(x, GC - .9, -3.15); scene.add(d); return d; });
// master bedroom: bed with a padded headboard, side tables
const U = SL + .36;
box(2.0, .5, 2.2, M.fabric, -4.1, U, -5.8, { r: .06 }); box(2.2, 1.1, .12, M.cushion, -4.2, U, -5.95, { r: .04 });
box(.45, .5, .4, M.darkWood, -4.85, U, -5.8); box(.45, .5, .4, M.darkWood, -1.95, U, -5.8);
box(2.0, .05, 1.0, M.goldCush, -4.1, U + .5, -4.6, { cast: false });
// upper hall: sofa + console
box(2.2, .42, .85, M.fabric, -1.1, U, -5.4, { r: .06 }); box(2.2, .5, .2, M.fabric, -1.1, U + .42, -5.6, { r: .06 });
box(1.2, .8, .35, woodF, -.6, U, -1.0);
// kids room: two small beds, a toy box
const kidM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#7fa8c9', roughness: .8 }), kidM2 = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#e7b85c', roughness: .8 });
box(.95, .4, 1.8, kidM, 2.0, U, -5.8, { r: .05 }); box(.95, .4, 1.8, kidM2, 3.8, U, -5.8, { r: .05 });
box(.6, .4, .4, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c75b4a', roughness: .7 }), 2.9, U, -2.2, { r: .04 });
// ---------- interior lights: rooms ----------
// room = { x0, x1, yC (ceiling), spots:[[x,z],..], cove (strip along the back edge of the ceiling) }
const ROOMS = [
  { n: 'majlis', x0: -VW, x1: -1.77, yc: GC, sp: [[-4.0, -4.5], [-2.6, -2.4]], mixC: '#8fb4ff', mixK: 1.3 },   // 6500 K blue-white
  { n: 'hall', x0: -1.63, x1: 1.63, yc: GC, sp: [[-.9, -4.6], [.2, -2.2]], mixC: '#ff8a2a', mixK: 1.0 },        // 2700 K orange
  { n: 'kitchen', x0: 1.77, x1: VW, yc: GC, sp: [[2.6, -5.0], [4.2, -2.4]], mixC: '#c4ffa8', mixK: 1.2 },       // 5000 K greenish
  { n: 'master', x0: -VW, x1: -1.77, yc: RF - .05, sp: [[-3.1, -4.4], [-3.1, -2.0]], mixC: '#ffa030', mixK: .9 },
  { n: 'upper', x0: -1.63, x1: 1.63, yc: RF - .05, sp: [[-.5, -4.4], [.6, -2.0]], mixC: '#9ec0ff', mixK: 1.3 },
  { n: 'kids', x0: 1.77, x1: VW, yc: RF - .05, sp: [[3.4, -4.4], [3.4, -2.0]], mixC: '#ffffff', mixK: 1.3 },
];
const WARM = '#ffdcb0';
ROOMS.forEach((r) => {
  r.rings = []; r.lights = [];
  for (const [x, z] of r.sp) {
    const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.06, .06, .02, 20), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, r.yc - .005, z); scene.add(ring);
    const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 7, 46 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.3); s.position.set(x, r.yc - .04, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target);
    r.rings.push(ring); r.lights.push(s);
  }
  // hidden cove: a strip along the back of the ceiling (a gypsum lip in front of it)
  const w = r.x1 - r.x0 - .2;
  box(w, .14, .06, gypM, r.x0 + .1, r.yc - .14, -VD + .32, { cast: false });
  r.strip = box(w, .02, .03, emis('#000', 0), r.x0 + .1, r.yc - .04, -VD + .2, { cast: false });
  r.cove = new THREE.RectAreaLight(WARM, 0, w, .25); r.cove.position.set((r.x0 + r.x1) / 2, r.yc - .06, -VD + .18); r.cove.lookAt((r.x0 + r.x1) / 2, r.yc - 2, -VD + 2.2); scene.add(r.cove);
  r.fill = new THREE.PointLight(WARM, 0, 6, 2); r.fill.position.set((r.x0 + r.x1) / 2, r.yc - .7, -2.4); scene.add(r.fill);
});
// ---------- drivers in the void above the ground gypsum ----------
const drivers = [];
[-3.9, -2.6, -.9, .5, 2.6, 4.0].forEach((x, i) => {
  const bx = box(.42, .1, .2, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d8d8d8', roughness: .4, metalness: .6 }), x - .21, GC + .05, -.9 - (i % 2) * .5);
  const led = box(.04, .025, .02, emis('#000', 0), x + .12, GC + .1, -.71 - (i % 2) * .5, { cast: false });
  const lab = box(.2, .055, .005, emis('#000', 0), x - .14, GC + .075, -.7 - (i % 2) * .5 + .001, { cast: false });
  const cab = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.008, .008, 1.2, 6), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#222', roughness: .6 })); cab.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; cab.position.set(x - .8, GC + .09, -.8 - (i % 2) * .5); scene.add(cab);
  drivers.push({ bx, led, lab });
});
const voidL = new THREE.PointLight('#ffd9a8', 0, 3, 2); voidL.position.set(-1, GC + .3, -1.6); scene.add(voidL);
// ---------- outside: lawn, path, palms, boundary wall, entrance ----------
const lawn = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#2a3a22', roughness: 1 });
plane(40, 30, lawn, [0, -.13, 8], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
const pave = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b9ad99', roughness: .9 });
box(2.0, .03, 8.5, pave, -1.0, -.12, 0, { cast: false });
box(VW * 2 + 2, .03, 1.2, pave, -VW - 1, -.12, 0, { cast: false });
const bwall = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cbb89b', roughness: .95 });
box(6.0, 1.3, .25, bwall, -9.5, -.12, 8.4); box(6.0, 1.3, .25, bwall, 3.5, -.12, 8.4);
box(.25, 1.3, 8.6, bwall, -9.5, -.12, 0); box(.25, 1.3, 8.6, bwall, 9.25, -.12, 0);
const trunkM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#6b5034', roughness: .9 }), frondM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#3d5a2c', roughness: .8, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
function palm(x, z, h) {
  const t = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.13, .2, h, 10), trunkM); t.position.set(x, h / 2 - .12, z); t.castShadow = true; scene.add(t);
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const a = i / 9 * Math.PI * 2; const f = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(.35, 1.9), frondM); f.position.set(x + Math.cos(a) * .8, h - .2, z + Math.sin(a) * .8); f.rotation.set(-Math.PI / 2 + .55, 0, 0); f.rotation.y = -a + Math.PI / 2; f.rotateX(.0); f.lookAt(x + Math.cos(a) * 2, h - 1.0, z + Math.sin(a) * 2); scene.add(f); }
}
palm(-6.8, 3.2, 5.0); palm(6.9, 3.6, 4.6);
for (const [x, z] of [[-4, 6.6], [4.5, 6.4], [-7.5, 6.8]]) { const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.55, 12, 10), M.leaf); b.position.set(x, .3, z); b.scale.y = .7; scene.add(b); }
// outdoor fixtures
const OUT = [];
function outSpot(pos, tgt, ang, k, fix) { const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 12, ang * Math.PI / 180, .5, 1.2); s.position.set(...pos); s.target.position.set(...tgt); scene.add(s, s.target); OUT.push({ s, k, fix }); return s; }
const fixM = () => emis('#000', 0);
// wall washers on the facade side walls (front face of the end walls) + up-lights on the palms + boundary-wall lights + path bollards
for (const x of [-VW - .13, VW + .13]) { const f = box(.16, .1, .1, fixM(), x - .08, 0, .05, { cast: false }); outSpot([x, .05, .35], [x, RF, .1], 14, 60, f); }
for (const [x, z, h] of [[-6.8, 3.2, 5.0], [6.9, 3.6, 4.6]]) { const f = box(.14, .06, .14, fixM(), x - .07 + .35, -.1, z - .07, { cast: false }); outSpot([x + .35, 0, z], [x, h, z], 22, 70, f); }
for (const x of [-8.2, -6.0, -4.2, 4.4, 6.2, 8.0]) { const f = box(.14, .08, .06, fixM(), x - .07, 1.05, 8.37, { cast: false }); outSpot([x, 1.05, 8.3], [x, -.1, 7.2], 50, 10, f); }
const bol = [];
for (const z of [1.6, 3.4, 5.2, 7.0]) for (const x of [-1.25, 1.25]) { box(.12, .55, .12, M.black, x - .06, -.1, z - .06); const head = box(.12, .06, .12, fixM(), x - .06, .37, z - .06, { cast: false }); const l = new THREE.PointLight(WARM, 0, 2.2, 2); l.position.set(x, .35, z); scene.add(l); bol.push({ head, l }); }
// entrance canopy light
const moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#8ea6d8', .35); moon.position.set(-8, 14, 10); scene.add(moon);
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#3a4a74', '#141008', .25); scene.add(hemi);
const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 3.9, 21.5], tgt: [0, 2.7, -2], fov: 46 },      // the whole villa + garden, front elevation
  a: { pos: [10.5, 3.2, 15], tgt: [0, 3.2, -2.5], fov: 50 },     // three-quarter from the right: side wall washer + palms
  g: { pos: [0, 1.6, 8.2], tgt: [0, 1.45, -3], fov: 58 },      // ground floor close (majlis | hall | kitchen)
  u: { pos: [0, 4.95, 8.2], tgt: [0, 4.8, -3], fov: 58 },      // upper floor close
  e: { pos: [-2.6, .9, 11.5], tgt: [0, 2.6, 0], fov: 62 },      // garden, low: path bollards + palms + facade
  d: { pos: [-1.25, 3.0, .75], tgt: [-.75, 2.84, -1.2], fov: 44 },  // the void above the gypsum: drivers
};
function stripMat(col, k) { return k > 0 ? emis(col, 6 * k) : emis('#000', 0); }
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.replace('all', 'in+hid+out').replace('mix', 'ms+mh+mo').split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const ms = T.ms ?? 0, mh = T.mh ?? 0, mo = T.mo ?? 0, mix = ms, inn = T.in ?? 0, hid = T.hid ?? 0, out = T.out ?? 0, fl = T.flick ?? 0, dm = T.dim ?? 1;
  const order = [1, 0, 2, 4, 3, 5]; // which room comes on first
  ROOMS.forEach((r, i) => {
    const rank = order.indexOf(i);
    const kIn = mix ? 1 : clamp01((inn * 1.15 - rank / 6) / .25);
    const kHid = mh ? (i === 1 || i === 3 ? 1 : 0) : clamp01((hid * 1.15 - rank / 6) / .25);
    const col = mix ? r.mixC : WARM;
    const cheap = mix && (i === 0 || i === 4) ? 1 - fl * .85 : 1;
    const kk = (mix ? r.mixK : 1) * cheap * dm;
    r.lights.forEach((s) => { s.color.set(col); s.intensity = 38 * kIn * kk; });
    r.rings.forEach((ring) => { ring.material = kIn * cheap > .02 ? emis(col, 3 * kIn * cheap) : emis('#000', 0); });
    r.fill.color.set(col); r.fill.intensity = 1.4 * kIn * kk;
    const cc = mh ? (i === 1 ? '#7fb0ff' : '#ff9a20') : WARM;
    r.cove.color.set(cc); r.cove.intensity = 16 * kHid * cheap * dm; r.strip.material = stripMat(cc, kHid * cheap);
  });
  pendKit.forEach((d) => { d.material.emissive = new THREE.Color(inn > .5 || mix ? '#5a3a1a' : '#000'); });
  // outdoor
  const oc = mo ? '#b8ffb0' : WARM;
  OUT.forEach(({ s, k, fix }, i) => { const on = mo ? (i % 3 === 1 ? 0 : 1) : clamp01((out * 1.2 - i / OUT.length) / .2); s.color.set(oc); s.intensity = k * on * dm * (mo ? 1.3 : 1); fix.material = on ? emis(oc, 4 * on) : emis('#000', 0); });
  bol.forEach(({ head, l }, i) => { const on = mo ? 0 : clamp01((out * 1.2 - i / bol.length) / .2); l.intensity = 1.6 * on * dm; head.material = on ? emis(WARM, 5 * on) : emis('#000', 0); });
  // drivers
  const dv = T.drv ?? 0, ch = T.cheap ?? 0;
  drivers.forEach(({ bx, led, lab }, i) => {
    if (ch) { bx.material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: ['#e8e2d0', '#555', '#b9b2a0', '#2c2c2c', '#d0d0d0', '#8a8070'][i], roughness: .6 }); led.material = emis(i === 2 || i === 4 ? '#ff2a1a' : '#000', i === 2 || i === 4 ? 8 : 0); lab.material = emis('#000', 0); bx.scale.set([1, .7, 1.2, .8, 1, .9][i], [1, 1.3, .9, 1.4, 1, 1.1][i], 1); }
    else { bx.material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#d8d8d8', roughness: .35, metalness: .6, emissive: dv ? '#3a2208' : '#000' }); led.material = emis(dv ? '#2bff6a' : '#000', dv ? 8 : 0); lab.material = emis(dv ? '#ff8a1e' : '#000', dv ? 3 : 0); bx.scale.set(1, 1, 1); }
  });
  voidL.intensity = dv || ch ? .06 : 0;
  const lit = Math.max(ms, (inn + hid) / 2);
  hemi.intensity = .25 + .1 * lit; scene.environmentIntensity = .05 + .1 * lit; moon.intensity = .35;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.05;
};
function clamp01(v) { return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v)); }
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/3d/bodies/villaproj.html`

```html
// ===== Project of the week: one villa ground floor from open site to lit, room by room =====
// Plan 12 x 9 m: open kitchen (back-left), entrance foyer (front-left), big family hall + dining (right). Walls 3 m.
// state tokens joined by '+':
//   site           raw concrete floor + block walls, no furniture, no ceiling boards
//   plan           (with site) a blueprint on the floor: room outlines, spot circles, cove lines, sconce marks
//   wire           (with site) metal ceiling studs, orange cables from the board to every point, drivers next to the coves
//   done           finished: marble floor, plaster walls, gypsum ceiling, furniture
//   lit:<0..1>     rooms switch on one by one: hall (cove + spots + linear pendant), kitchen (spots + under-cabinet + pendants), entrance (sconces + console spot)
//   top            hide ceilings/studs boards for the bird's-eye view t (cables and drivers stay visible in wire)
//   dim:<x>        scales the lights
const X0 = -6, X1 = 6, Z0 = -4.5, Z1 = 4.5, HW = 3.0, CY = 2.8;
scene.background = new THREE.Color('#0b0a0c');
marbleTex.repeat.set(5, 4);
const concrete = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: canvasTex(512, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#8f8a83'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let i = 0; i < 9000; i++) { const c = 110 + R() * 60; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${c},${c - 4},${c - 8},.25)`; g.fillRect(R() * w, R() * h, 2, 2); } }, [6, 5]), roughness: .95 });
const block = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: canvasTex(512, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#a7a199'; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(70,66,60,.55)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 32) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); for (let x = (y / 32) % 2 ? 0 : 64; x < w; x += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, y); g.lineTo(x, y + 32); g.stroke(); } } }, [4, 3]), roughness: .95 });
const plaster = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: plasterTex, color: '#ece5da', roughness: .9 });
const floorFin = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: marbleTex, roughness: .2 });
const FLOOR = plane(X1 - X0, Z1 - Z0, concrete, [0, 0, 0], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
// walls: [x0, z0, x1, z1] runs; gaps are made by splitting runs
const WALLS = [];
function wall(x0, z0, x1, z1, h = HW) { const t = .16, len = Math.hypot(x1 - x0, z1 - z0); const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(x1 === x0 ? t : len + t, h, z1 === z0 ? t : len + t), block); m.position.set((x0 + x1) / 2, h / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2); m.castShadow = m.receiveShadow = true; scene.add(m); WALLS.push(m); return m; }

const P = { parent: FURN };

box(3.6, 2.4, .06, M.fluted, 1.0, .1, Z0 + .09, { ...P, cast: false }); box(1.8, 1.0, .04, M.screen, 1.9, 1.0, Z0 + .16, P);
box(3.2, .42, .95, fab, 1.2, 0, -1.7, { ...P, r: .06 }); box(3.2, .5, .22, fab, 1.2, .42, -.97, { ...P, r: .06 }); box(.95, .42, 1.8, fab, 4.4, 0, -1.7, { ...P, r: .06 });
box(1.2, .36, .7, M.tableTop, 2.2, 0, -3.0, P);

// entrance: console + round mirror + plant

const mir = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.45, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#cfd6da', roughness: .05, metalness: 1 })); mir.position.set(X0 + .1, 1.6, 2.6); mir.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2; FURN.add(mir);
// ---------- light fixtures ----------
const WARM = '#ffdcb0';
function spotAt(x, z, ang = 40, room) { const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.05, .05, .02, 18), emis('#000', 0)); ring.position.set(x, CY - .005, z); scene.add(ring); const s = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 7, ang * Math.PI / 180, .55, 1.3); s.position.set(x, CY - .04, z); s.target.position.set(x, 0, z); scene.add(s, s.target); return { ring, s, room }; }
const SPOTS = [...P_HALL.map(([x, z]) => spotAt(x, z, 40, 0)), ...P_KIT.map(([x, z]) => spotAt(x, z, 38, 1)), ...P_ENT.map(([x, z]) => spotAt(x, z, 34, 2))];

const linPend = box(1.8, .05, .08, M.black, 1.2, 1.55, 2.06, { cast: false }); const linGlow = box(1.7, .01, .05, emis('#000', 0), 1.25, 1.545, 2.075, { cast: false });
for (const x of [1.4, 2.8]) { const w = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.004, .004, CY - 1.58, 6), M.black); w.position.set(x, (CY + 1.58) / 2, 2.1); FURN.add(w); }

const under = box(4.4, .012, .03, emis('#000', 0), X0 + .3, 1.585, Z0 + .3, { cast: false }); const underL = new THREE.RectAreaLight(WARM, 0, 4.4, .1); underL.position.set(X0 + 2.5, 1.57, Z0 + .3); underL.lookAt(X0 + 2.5, 0, Z0 + .45); scene.add(underL);
const KP = [-4.1, -3.0].map((x) => { const c = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.005, .005, 1.0, 6), M.black); c.position.set(x, CY - .5, -1.2); FURN.add(c); const d = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.16, 24, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#1b1a19', roughness: .4, metalness: .5, emissive: '#000' })); d.position.set(x, CY - 1.0, -1.2); FURN.add(d); const pt = new THREE.PointLight('#ffc98a', 0, 2.5, 2); pt.position.set(x, CY - 1.1, -1.2); scene.add(pt); return { d, pt }; });
const SC = SCONCE.map(([x, z]) => { const b = box(.1, .3, .12, M.brass, x, 1.75, z - .06, { cast: false }); const g = box(.02, .3, .08, emis('#000', 0), x + .1, 1.75, z - .04, { cast: false }); const up = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 3, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.5); up.position.set(x + .15, 2.0, z); up.target.position.set(x + .15, 3, z); const dn = new THREE.SpotLight(WARM, 0, 3, 30 * Math.PI / 180, .6, 1.5); dn.position.set(x + .15, 1.7, z); dn.target.position.set(x + .15, 0, z); scene.add(up, up.target, dn, dn.target); return { g, up, dn }; });

scene.children.filter((o) => o.isMesh && o.material === M.brass).forEach((o) => FIX.push(o));
const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight('#cdd5e6', '#2a2018', .5); scene.add(hemi);
const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight('#dfe6f5', .6); sun.position.set(6, 14, 10); scene.add(sun);
const VIEWS = {
  t: { pos: [13.5, 17, 3.2], tgt: [-.4, 0, .1], fov: 50 },      // bird's eye over the whole floor
  c: { pos: [2.8, 1.25, 3.8], tgt: [2.4, 3.2, -1.6], fov: 68 },     // hall ceiling looking up
  h: { pos: [3.0, 1.65, 4.25], tgt: [2.4, 1.3, -3.2], fov: 64 },    // the hall
  k: { pos: [1.4, 1.65, -1.2], tgt: [-4.2, 1.1, -2.9], fov: 62 },   // the open kitchen from the hall
  e: { pos: [1.0, 1.6, 2.4], tgt: [-5.5, 1.45, 2.6], fov: 58 },     // the entrance through the arch
};
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const site = !!T.site, done = !!T.done, top = !!T.top, lit = T.lit ?? 0, dm = T.dim ?? 1;

  PLAN.visible = !!T.plan;
  STUDS.visible = !!T.wire && !top;
  WIRE.visible = !!T.wire;

  const k = (room) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (lit * 1.2 - room / 3) / .35)) * (done ? 1 : 0);

  COVES.forEach(({ strip, l }) => { const v = k(0); l.intensity = 14 * v * dm; strip.material = v > .02 ? emis(WARM, 6 * v) : emis('#000', 0); });
  linL.intensity = 18 * k(0) * dm; linGlow.material = k(0) > .02 ? emis(WARM, 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  underL.intensity = 20 * k(1) * dm; under.material = k(1) > .02 ? emis(WARM, 6) : emis('#000', 0);
  KP.forEach(({ d, pt }) => { pt.intensity = 1.2 * k(1) * dm; d.material.emissive.set(k(1) > .1 ? '#5a3a1a' : '#000'); });
  SC.forEach(({ g, up, dn }) => { const v = k(2); up.intensity = dn.intensity = 9 * v * dm; g.material = v > .02 ? emis(WARM, 5 * v) : emis('#000', 0); });
  FIX.forEach((m) => { m.visible = done; });
  const L = done ? Math.min(1, lit) : 0;
  hemi.intensity = done ? .06 + .14 * L : .55; sun.intensity = done ? .05 : .6; scene.environmentIntensity = done ? .04 + .1 * L : .2;
  scene.background.set(done ? '#0b0a0c' : '#1a1c22');
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = done ? 1.05 : .95;
};
```

```html
// ===== Built-in wardrobe with hidden LED (sensor doors, under-shelf strips, lit rail, lit drawer) =====
// x: 0..2.4 width, y up, z: back panel at 0, front at DEP
const WW = 2.4, WH = 2.6, DEP = .6, T2 = .025;
const SEC = [[0, .9], [.9, 1.6], [1.6, 2.4]]; // hanging | shelves+drawers | long hanging
marbleTex.repeat.set(4, 4);

const carcM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: walnutTex, roughness: .45 });

const goldM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c9a063', roughness: .25, metalness: 1 });

const woodFloorTex = canvasTex(1024, 1024, (g, w, h) => { for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { woodDraw(i % 2 ? '#8a6440' : '#7e5a38', '50,30,15')(g, w, h); } g.strokeStyle = 'rgba(30,18,8,.45)'; g.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y <= h; y += 128) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(0, y); g.lineTo(w, y); g.stroke(); } }, [3, 3]);
const floorW = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: woodFloorTex, roughness: .4 });
plane(10, 10, floorW, [1.2, 0, 2], [-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]);
box(8, 3.2, .1, roomWallM, -2.8, 0, -.1);                    // back wall
box(.1, 3.2, 6, roomWallM, -1.4, 0, -.1);                    // left wall
box(.1, 3.2, 6, roomWallM, 4.0, 0, -.1);                     // right wall
box(8, .1, 6, M.ceil, -2.8, 3.0, -.1, { cast: false });     // ceiling

// carcass
box(WW, WH, .015, backM, 0, 0, .0, { cast: false });                 // fabric back
box(WW + .04, T2, DEP, carcM, -.02, WH, 0); box(WW + .04, .08, DEP, carcM, -.02, 0, 0); // top + plinth
for (const x of [0, .9, 1.6, 2.4]) box(T2, WH, DEP, carcM, x - T2 / 2, 0, 0);       // side panels
// cornice fascia above
box(WW + .04, .4, .02, doorM, -.02, WH, DEP - .02);

const LEDs = { side: [], shelf: [], rail: [], drawer: [] }, LEDm = { side: [], shelf: [], rail: [], drawer: [] };
function ledLine(group, x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1, nx, ny, nz, inten) { // emissive line + area light facing (nx,ny,nz)
  const w = Math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0, z1 - z0);
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(Math.max(.008, Math.abs(x1 - x0)), Math.max(.008, Math.abs(y1 - y0)), Math.max(.008, Math.abs(z1 - z0))), emis('#000', 0));
  m.position.set((x0 + x1) / 2, (y0 + y1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2); scene.add(m); LEDm[group].push(m);
  const horiz = Math.abs(y1 - y0) < .01;
  const l = new THREE.RectAreaLight('#fff1dc', 0, w, .025); l.position.copy(m.position);
  if (!horiz) { l.width = .025; l.height = w; }
  l.lookAt(m.position.x + nx, m.position.y + ny, m.position.z + nz); scene.add(l); LEDs[group].push([l, inten]);
}
// vertical profiles on the inside of every side panel (front edge), facing into each section
for (const [a, b] of SEC) { ledLine('side', a + .02, .12, DEP - .05, a + .02, WH - .03, DEP - .05, 1, 0, -.6, 8); ledLine('side', b - .02, .12, DEP - .05, b - .02, WH - .03, DEP - .05, -1, 0, -.6, 8); }

// ---- section 1: hanging (rail at 1.95) + top shelf with boxes

for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) box(.24, .2, .38, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: ['#efe6d6', '#2b2622', '#c9a063'][i], roughness: .6 }), .05 + i * .28, 2.165, .1, { r: .01 });
const rail = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.016, .016, .86, 16), goldM); rail.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; rail.position.set(.45, 1.95, .3); scene.add(rail);

const garment = (x, z, col, len, w = .46) => {
  const g = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(g);
  const hk = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.03, .004, 6, 16, Math.PI * 1.4), goldM); hk.position.set(0, 1.97, 0); hk.rotation.z = Math.PI * .8; g.add(hk);
  const hb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.006, .006, w * .9, 8), M.darkWood); hb.rotation.z = Math.PI / 2; hb.position.set(0, 1.9, 0); g.add(hb);

  const o = Object.fromEntries(st.split('|').map(p => p.split(':')));
  const open = +(o.door ?? 0), lights = (o.lights ?? '').split('+').filter(Boolean), dOpen = +(o.drawer ?? 0), room = +(o.room ?? 0);

  sensM.emissiveIntensity = lights.length ? 2 : 0;

};
```

```html
  hbs.push({ lens, s, u: (z - 1.8) / 20 });
}
// dust in the air (for IP65): small specks, visible in the light

const dust = new THREE.InstancedMesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.006, 6, 4), dustM, 700); const dd = new THREE.Object3D();
for (let i = 0; i < 700; i++) { dd.position.set((R() - .5) * 2.4, CH - 1.0 - R() * 3.5, 1.8 + 4 * 1 + (R() - .5) * 2.4); dd.updateMatrix(); dust.setMatrixAt(i, dd.matrix); } scene.add(dust);

const VIEWS = {
  w: { pos: [0, 1.8, -.5], tgt: [0, 2.2, 12], fov: 62 },         // down the main aisle
  u: { pos: [.3, 1.5, 2.8], tgt: [-.2, CH - 1.2, 7.5], fov: 66 },  // looking up at the fixtures
  r: { pos: [.0, 1.4, 4.0], tgt: [1.0, 1.0, 6.0], fov: 55 },      // close on a rack: can you read the labels?
  d: { pos: [1.3, CH - 1.6, 4.3], tgt: [0, CH - 1.1, 5.8], fov: 46 }, // one high-bay close, dust in the air
};
// state tokens joined by '+':  off · old (old yellow lamps) · hb:<0..1> (LED high-bays row by row) · dust
window.setState = (state) => {
  const T = Object.fromEntries(state.split('+').map((p) => { const [k, v] = p.split(':'); return [k, v === undefined ? 1 : +v]; }));
  const old = T.old ?? 0, hb = T.hb ?? 0, du = T.dust ?? 0;

  hbs.forEach(({ lens, s, u }) => { const k = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (hb * 1.15 - u) / .15)); s.intensity = 38 * k; lens.material = k ? emis('#f8fbff', 5 * k) : emis('#000', 0); });
  dustM.opacity = .4 * du;
  hemi.intensity = .04 + .08 * Math.min(1, hb); scene.environmentIntensity = .03 + .06 * Math.min(1, hb);
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;
};
```
