# 5. Studio / bot client — toolkit

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/studio.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Client for the content-studio Supabase function (the content queue the owner approves from Telegram).

Env: STUDIO_TOKEN (required), STUDIO_URL (default: the QUALITY LIGHT project's content-studio function)
  python3 studio/studio.py settings
  python3 studio/studio.py list [status ...]            e.g. list idea_approved revise
  python3 studio/studio.py topics                        everything ever proposed (avoid repeats)
  python3 studio/studio.py create items.json             JSON list of items (kind, topic, hook, angle, status, ...)
  python3 studio/studio.py update <id> patch.json|'{json}'
  python3 studio/studio.py upload <local file> <remote path>   prints the public URL
  python3 studio/studio.py notify "text"                 message to the owner on Telegram
  python3 studio/studio.py tts script.txt voice.txt [title] [item_id]   Gemini voice-over (Achird), sent to the owner's bot to approve
  python3 studio/studio.py voice <id>                   status of a voice-over (ready / approved / failed) and its WAV URL
"""
import json, os, sys, urllib.request

URL = os.environ.get('STUDIO_URL', 'https://yubebbfsmlopmnluajgf.supabase.co/functions/v1/content-studio')
TOKEN = os.environ.get('STUDIO_TOKEN') or sys.exit('STUDIO_TOKEN is not set')


def api(body):
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL, data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-studio-token': TOKEN})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=240) as r:
            return json.load(r)
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        sys.exit(f'HTTP {e.code}: {e.read().decode()[:500]}')


def load(arg):
    return json.load(open(arg, encoding='utf-8')) if os.path.exists(arg) else json.loads(arg)


def main():
    cmd, args = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2:]
    if cmd == 'upload':
        local, remote = args
        r = api({'action': 'upload_url', 'path': remote})
        types = {'.mp4': 'video/mp4', '.jpg': 'image/jpeg', '.jpeg': 'image/jpeg', '.png': 'image/png'}
        data = open(local, 'rb').read()
        req = urllib.request.Request(r['signed_url'], data=data, method='PUT',
                                     headers={'Content-Type': types.get(os.path.splitext(local)[1].lower(), 'application/octet-stream'), 'x-upsert': 'true'})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=900) as res:
            res.read()
        print(r['public_url'])
        return
    body = {'settings': lambda: {'action': 'settings'},
            'list': lambda: {'action': 'list', **({'status': args} if args else {})},
            'topics': lambda: {'action': 'topics'},
            'create': lambda: {'action': 'create', 'items': load(args[0])},
            'update': lambda: {'action': 'update', 'id': args[0], 'patch': load(args[1])},
            'notify': lambda: {'action': 'notify', 'text': ' '.join(args)},
            'tts': lambda: {'action': 'tts', 'script': open(args[0], encoding='utf-8').read().strip(),
                            'voice_description': open(args[1], encoding='utf-8').read().strip(), 'title': args[2] if len(args) > 2 else None, 'item_id': args[3] if len(args) > 3 else None,
                            'voice': os.environ.get('GEMINI_TTS_VOICE', 'Achird')},
            'voice': lambda: {'action': 'voice', 'id': args[0]}}[cmd]()
    print(json.dumps(api(body), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/tts.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Voice-over with Gemini TTS (the same voices as Google AI Studio).

Usage: python3 studio/tts.py <script.txt> <voice_description.txt> <out.wav>
Env:   GOOGLE_AI_API_KEY (required), GEMINI_TTS_VOICE (default Achird, the owner's AI Studio voice), GEMINI_TTS_MODEL (default: newest *tts* model the key can use)
Writes a 48 kHz mono 16-bit WAV ready for align.py / synth.py (vo48.wav) plus the raw 24 kHz file next to it.
"""
import base64, json, os, subprocess, sys, urllib.request

API = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta'
KEY = os.environ.get('GOOGLE_AI_API_KEY') or sys.exit('GOOGLE_AI_API_KEY is not set')


def call(path, body=None):
    req = urllib.request.Request(f'{API}/{path}', data=json.dumps(body).encode() if body else None,
                                 headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-goog-api-key': KEY})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=600) as r:
        return json.load(r)


def pick_model():
    if os.environ.get('GEMINI_TTS_MODEL'):
        return os.environ['GEMINI_TTS_MODEL']
    names = [m['name'].split('/')[-1] for m in call('models?pageSize=1000').get('models', []) if 'tts' in m['name']]
    if not names:
        sys.exit('no TTS model available for this key')
    # prefer pro over flash, newest version first
    names.sort(key=lambda n: ('pro' in n, n), reverse=True)
    return names[0]


def main():
    script, voice, out = sys.argv[1:4]
    text = open(script, encoding='utf-8').read().strip()
    style = open(voice, encoding='utf-8').read().strip()
    model = pick_model()
    body = {
        'contents': [{'parts': [{'text': f'{style}\n\n{text}'}]}],
        'generationConfig': {
            'responseModalities': ['AUDIO'],
            'speechConfig': {'voiceConfig': {'prebuiltVoiceConfig': {'voiceName': os.environ.get('GEMINI_TTS_VOICE', 'Achird')}}},
        },
    }
    res = call(f'models/{model}:generateContent', body)
    part = res['candidates'][0]['content']['parts'][0]['inlineData']
    raw = out.replace('.wav', '_24k.pcm')
    open(raw, 'wb').write(base64.b64decode(part['data']))
    rate = 24000
    if 'rate=' in part.get('mimeType', ''):
        rate = int(part['mimeType'].split('rate=')[1].split(';')[0])
    subprocess.run(['ffmpeg', '-nostdin', '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-f', 's16le', '-ar', str(rate), '-ac', '1', '-i', raw,
                    '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '1', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', out], check=True)
    print(f'{model} -> {out}')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/watch.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Wait for the owner's next move in the QL Studio bot and print it (exit 0), so the Claude session wakes up and continues.

Usage: python3 studio/watch.py [state_file]   (default /tmp/ql_watch_since)
Polls the content-events function every 20 s; remembers the last seen time in the state file. Gives up after
~110 minutes with exit code 3 (just start it again). Env: STUDIO_TOKEN.
"""
import json, os, sys, time, urllib.request
from datetime import datetime, timezone

URL = os.environ.get('STUDIO_EVENTS_URL', 'https://yubebbfsmlopmnluajgf.supabase.co/functions/v1/content-events')
state = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '/tmp/ql_watch_since'
since = open(state).read().strip() if os.path.exists(state) else datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
deadline = time.time() + 110 * 60
while time.time() < deadline:
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(URL, data=json.dumps({'since': since}).encode(),
                                     headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-studio-token': os.environ['STUDIO_TOKEN']})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as r:
            ev = json.load(r)
        if ev['questions'] or ev['voices'] or ev['items']:
            open(state, 'w').write(ev['now'])
            print(json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1))
            sys.exit(0)
    except Exception as e:  # network hiccup: keep watching
        print('watch: retry after error:', e, file=sys.stderr)
    time.sleep(20)
sys.exit(3)
```


# 6. Static post template — toolkit/post

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/post/shot.js`

```javascript
// Screenshot a 1080x1350 post page.  Usage: node studio/post/shot.js <page.html> <out.jpg>
const { chromium } = (() => { try { return require('playwright'); } catch { return require('/opt/node22/lib/node_modules/playwright'); } })();
const path = require('path');
(async () => {
  const [,, html, out] = process.argv;
  const b = await chromium.launch({ args: ['--allow-file-access-from-files'] });
  const p = await b.newPage({ viewport: { width: 1080, height: 1350 } });
  await p.goto('file://' + path.resolve(html));
  await p.evaluate(() => document.fonts.ready);
  await p.waitForTimeout(300);
  await p.screenshot({ path: out, type: 'jpeg', quality: 92 });
  await b.close();
})();
```

```html
<!doctype html><html lang="ar" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<!-- Image post template (1080x1350). Copy to studio/post/<name>.html with a background render as bg.jpg next to it, edit the texts, then: node studio/post/shot.js studio/post/<name>.html out.jpg
     Wrap Latin/number runs (CRI 90+, @handles, phone) in <bdi dir="ltr"> so they keep their order inside Arabic text. -->
<style>
@font-face{font-family:Cairo;font-weight:600;src:url(../reel/template/fonts/cairo2.ttf)}
@font-face{font-family:Cairo;font-weight:800;src:url(../reel/template/fonts/cairo3.ttf)}
@font-face{font-family:Cairo;font-weight:900;src:url(../reel/template/fonts/cairo4.ttf)}
:root{--o:#EF5924;--ink:#120e0c}
html,body{margin:0;width:1080px;height:1350px;overflow:hidden;font-family:Cairo;color:#fff;background:var(--ink)}
.bg{position:absolute;inset:0;background:url(bg.jpg) center/cover}
.shade{position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(12,9,8,.85) 0%,rgba(12,9,8,.15) 38%,rgba(12,9,8,.2) 55%,rgba(12,9,8,.92) 100%)}
.top{position:absolute;top:70px;left:70px;right:70px;text-align:center}
.kicker{display:inline-block;background:var(--o);padding:6px 28px;border-radius:40px;font-weight:800;font-size:36px}
h1{font-weight:900;font-size:76px;line-height:1.25;margin:26px 0 0;text-shadow:0 4px 30px rgba(0,0,0,.6)}
.list{position:absolute;bottom:190px;left:70px;right:70px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:18px}
.row{background:rgba(18,14,12,.86);border:3px solid var(--o);border-radius:30px;padding:16px 30px;font-weight:800;font-size:40px;display:flex;gap:18px;align-items:center}
.row b{color:#ffb15a;font-weight:900;direction:ltr;unicode-bidi:isolate}
bdi,[dir=ltr]{unicode-bidi:isolate}
.foot{position:absolute;bottom:50px;left:70px;right:70px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;font-weight:800;font-size:32px}
.foot img{height:90px}
</style></head><body>
<div class="bg"></div><div class="shade"></div>
<div class="top"><span class="kicker">نصيحة إضاءة 💡</span><h1>العنوان الرئيسي هنا</h1></div>
<div class="list">
  <div class="row"><b>3000K</b> النقطة الأولى</div>
  <div class="row"><b>CRI 90+</b> النقطة الثانية</div>
</div>
<div class="foot"><img src="../reel/template/logo3d.png"><span>حولي – شارع تونس · <bdi dir="ltr">5111 1725</bdi> · <bdi dir="ltr">@quality_light1</bdi></span></div>
</body></html>
```

```javascript
// ---------- accent: one focused spot transforms a corner ----------
const CROP = { sx: 150, sy: 230, sw: 780, sh: 1100 }; // painting area in the close renders
function versus(t, t0, a, b, la, lb, ca = C.red, cb = GOOD, y = 640) { // two cards side by side, bad (left) vs good (right)
  [[a, la, ca, 300, '✗'], [b, lb, cb, 780, '✓']].forEach(([im, lab, col, x, mk], i) => {
    const p = E.back(prog(t, t0 + i * .35, t0 + .45 + i * .35)); if (p <= 0) return;
    const w = 450, h = 635; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y + h / 2); ctx.scale(p, p);
    imgCard(IM[im], -w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, { r: 28, ...CROP }); ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 8; rrect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 28); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(w / 2 - 50, -h / 2 + 50, 40, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt(mk, w / 2 - 50, -h / 2 + 54, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' });
    ctx.restore(); label(lab, x, y + h + 70, t, t0 + .2 + i * .35, 0, { size: 44, bg: col, color: i ? '#06210f' : '#fff' });
  });
}
function beamGauge(x, y, t, t0, deg0, deg1) { // a wedge that narrows from deg0 to deg1
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; const d = lerp(deg0, deg1, E.inOut(prog(t, t0 + .4, t0 + 1.6)));
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)'; rrect(-300, -60, 600, 520, 34); ctx.fill();
  const r = 400, h = d / 2 * Math.PI / 180; const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, r); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,214,160,.95)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,214,160,.05)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.arc(0, 0, r, Math.PI / 2 - h, Math.PI / 2 + h); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1a1a1a'; rrect(-50, -40, 100, 50, 12); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff3dd'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd6a0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 8, 20, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 5; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 150, Math.PI / 2 - h, Math.PI / 2 + h); ctx.stroke();
  txt(Math.round(d) + '°', 0, 230, { size: 70, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function angleDiagram(x, y, t, t0) { // ceiling + wall + spot aimed 30° off vertical
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; const s = E.inOut(prog(t, t0 + .3, t0 + 1.2));
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(-320, -230, 640, 470, 34); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#d8cbb8'; ctx.lineWidth = 8; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-260, -170); ctx.lineTo(220, -170); ctx.lineTo(220, 200); ctx.stroke(); // ceiling + wall (right)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1f3a4f'; ctx.fillRect(206, -40, 14, 150); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.fillRect(206, -10, 14, 60); // painting on the wall
  const sx = -40, sy = -170, ang = lerp(0, 30, s) * Math.PI / 180, L = 300; const ex = sx + Math.sin(ang) * L * 1.15, ey = sy + Math.cos(ang) * L;
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.35)'; ctx.setLineDash([12, 12]); ctx.lineWidth = 4; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); ctx.lineTo(sx, sy + 320); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(sx, sy, ex, ey); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,214,160,.9)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,214,160,.1)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); const sp = .2; ctx.lineTo(sx + Math.sin(ang - sp) * L * 1.2, sy + Math.cos(ang - sp) * L * 1.1); ctx.lineTo(sx + Math.sin(ang + sp) * L * 1.2, sy + Math.cos(ang + sp) * L * 1.1); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#111'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx, sy + 8, 22, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff3dd'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx, sy + 14, 10, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx, sy, 120, Math.PI / 2 - ang, Math.PI / 2); ctx.stroke();
  txt(Math.round(lerp(0, 30, s)) + '°', sx + 70, sy + 175, { size: 58, weight: 900, color: C.o, dir: 'ltr' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function cctBar(x, y, t, t0) { // 2700K .. 6500K bar with a marker on 3000K
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(-380, 0, 380, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#ffb35c'); g.addColorStop(.35, '#ffe2b8'); g.addColorStop(.65, '#fff8ee'); g.addColorStop(1, '#bcd4ff');
  ctx.fillStyle = g; rrect(-380, -30, 760, 60, 30); ctx.fill();
  txt('2700K', -330, 80, { size: 34, weight: 800, color: '#ffcf96', dir: 'ltr' }); txt('6500K', 330, 80, { size: 34, weight: 800, color: '#bcd4ff', dir: 'ltr' });
  const mx = lerp(300, -300, E.inOut(prog(t, t0 + .3, t0 + 1.3))); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 25; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(mx, 0, 42, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 8; ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
}
function dimmer(x, y, t, v, a) { // horizontal slider
  if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(-380, -80, 760, 160, 80); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#3a332d'; rrect(-300, -12, 600, 24, 12); ctx.fill();
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(-300, 0, 300, 0); g.addColorStop(0, '#6b4a2a'); g.addColorStop(1, '#ffd6a0'); ctx.fillStyle = g; rrect(-300, -12, 600 * v, 24, 12); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd6a0'; ctx.shadowBlur = 20 + 30 * v; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-300 + 600 * v, 0, 38, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('💡', -345, 4, { size: 40, dir: 'ltr', alpha: .5 }); txt('💡', 345, 4, { size: 48, dir: 'ltr' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function ring(x, y, rx, ry, t, t0, t1, col = C.amber) { if (t < t0 || (t1 && t > t1)) return; const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a * (.65 + .35 * Math.sin(t * 5)); ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(x, y, rx, ry, 0, 0, 7); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); }

function S1(t) { // 0–4.15 a corner with a nice painting / decor piece
  if (t < 2.6) bgImg(IM.w_dark, 1, 1.02 + .04 * prog(t, 0, 2.6), 540, 900);
  else bgImg(IM.v_dark, 1, 1.0 + .04 * prog(t, 2.6, 4.15), 540, 900);
  shade2();
  pop('عندك ركن بالبيت…', 540, 360, t, .05, 4.15, { size: 88, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  sub2('فيه لوحة حلوة 🖼️', t, 1.5, 2.6, '#3a2f3f');
  if (t < 2.6) ring(572, 790, 235, 320, t, 1.6, 2.6);
  if (t >= 2.6) sub2('أو قطعة ديكور غالية 🏺', t, 2.6, 4.15, '#3a2f3f');
}
function S2(t) { // 4.15–7.45 nobody notices: lost in general light
  bgImg(IM.w_flat, 1, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, 4.15, 7.45), 540, 900); shade2();
  title2('بس محد ينتبه لها… 😐', t, 4.32, 7.45, '#fff', 66);
  sub2('ضايعة بالضو العام', t, 5.58, 7.45, '#3a2f3f');
  if (t > 5.58) { const a = E.out(prog(t, 5.58, 6.0)); for (const [x, y] of [[300, 120], [790, 120]]) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; glow(x, y, 160, '#fff4e2', .5); ctx.restore(); } }
}
function S3(t) { // 7.45–12.75 the secret: no redecorating, one spot
  bgImg(IM.w_dark, 1, 1.1, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  pop('تبي تعرف السر؟ 🤫', 540, 520, t, 7.73, 12.75, { size: 92, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  if (t > 8.92 && t < 10.45) { pop('ما تحتاج تغيّر الديكور', 540, 800, t, 8.92, 10.45, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#f3dcc0' }); const s = prog(t, 9.6, 10.0); if (s > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.red; ctx.lineWidth = 10; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(230, 815); ctx.lineTo(230 + 620 * s, 790); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); } }
  if (t >= 10.45) {
    pop('تحتاج سبوت واحد بس', 540, 790, t, 10.45, 12.75, { size: 72, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 25 });
    const p = E.back(prog(t, 10.9, 11.4)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 1150); ctx.scale(p, p); glow(0, 0, 260, C.o, .45); txt('1', 0, 20, { size: 300, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', glow: 40 }); ctx.restore(); }
    if (t > 11.5) sparkles(t, 11.5, 10, 300, 950, 480, 400);
  }
}
function S4(t) { // 12.75–17.8 spot on: light exactly on the piece, the rest calms down
  keyed(t, [[12.75, 'w_dark'], [13.15, 'w_spot']], 1.03 + .05 * prog(t, 12.75, 17.8), 540, 900, .35); flash(t, 13.15, .25);
  shade2();
  title2('السبوت المركّز 🎯', t, 13.01, 17.8, '#fff', 66);
  sub2('يحط الضو على القطعة بالضبط', t, 13.9, 15.95, C.o);
  sub2('والباقي يهدى حواليها 🌙', t, 15.97, 17.8, '#3a2f3f');
}
function S5(t) { // 17.8–20.85 suddenly the painting is the star
  const z = lerp(1.0, 1.08, E.inOut(prog(t, 17.8, 20.85)));
  bgImg(IM.c_spot, 1, z, 540, 900); flash(t, 18.04, .4); shade2();
  pop('وفجأة…', 540, 330, t, 18.04, 18.75, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  if (t >= 18.75) pop('اللوحة صارت نجمة المكان ⭐', 540, 330, t, 18.75, 20.85, { size: 68, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  sub2('والركن كله يتغيّر ✨', t, 19.7, 20.85, C.o);
  if (t > 18.8) sparkles(t, 18.8, 16, 160, 400, 760, 900);
}
function S6(t) { // 20.85–30.8 narrow beam 15°–24°
  if (t < 23.1) { bgImg(IM.c_spot, 1, 1.08, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.55)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); pop('بس خلّك ذكي بالاختيار 🧠', 540, 900, t, 21.0, 23.1, { size: 72, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 }); return; }
  bgImg(IM.c_spot, 1, 1.1, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  title2('زاوية الشعاع ضيّقة', t, 23.12, 30.8, '#fff', 66, 300);
  if (t < 28.6) { beamGauge(540, 700, t, 23.4, 60, 24); pop('24° – 15°', 540, 1430, t, 26.24, 28.6, { size: 110, weight: 900, color: C.o, glow: 30 }); }
  else { versus(t, 28.64, 'c_wide', 'c_spot', 'ضو مشتت', 'اللوحة تبرز', C.red, GOOD, 520); }
}
function S7(t) { // 30.8–37.1 aim ~30° so no reflection or glare
  bgImg(IM.c_spot, 1, 1.1, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  title2('وجّه السبوت بزاوية ثلاثين درجة', t, 30.98, 37.1, '#fff', 58, 300);
  if (t < 33.6) angleDiagram(540, 820, t, 31.3);
  else versus(t, 33.6, 'c_bad', 'c_spot', 'انعكاس وزغللة', 'بدون انعكاس ✓', C.red, GOOD, 500);
}
function S8(t) { // 37.1–41.15 3000K warm
  keyed(t, [[37.1, 'c_cold'], [38.6, 'c_spot']], 1.06, 540, 900, .7); shade2();
  title2('ولون الإضاءة؟ 🎨', t, 37.39, 41.15, '#fff', 66);
  cctBar(540, 1500, t, 37.6);
  pop('3000K', 540, 1320, t, 38.51, 41.15, { size: 110, weight: 900, color: C.amber, glow: 30 });
  sub2('دافي ومريح ☕', t, 39.96, 41.15, C.o, '#fff', 1700);
}
function S9(t) { // 41.15–45.7 CRI 90+
  bgImg(IM.c_spot, 1, 1.1, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  title2('مؤشر ألوان 90 وفوق 🌈', t, 41.29, 45.7, '#fff', 62, 300);
  versus(t, 41.7, 'c_lowcri', 'c_spot', 'ألوان باهتة', 'ألوان حقيقية', C.red, GOOD, 500);
  if (t > 43.3) sparkles(t, 43.3, 10, 560, 520, 440, 600);
}
function S10(t) { // 45.7–49.75 dimmer: calm or bold
  const v = t < 47.13 ? lerp(1, .15, E.inOut(prog(t, 46.0, 46.9))) : .15 + .85 * (.5 + .5 * Math.sin((t - 47.13) * 2.6 - Math.PI / 2));
  bgImg(IM.c_dim, 1, 1.06, 540, 900); bgImg(IM.c_spot, clamp((v - .15) / .85), 1.06, 540, 900); shade2();
  title2('ولو حطيت ديمر 🎚️', t, 45.83, 49.75, '#fff', 66);
  dimmer(540, 1420, t, v, E.out(prog(t, 45.9, 46.3)));
  label(v < .5 ? 'هادي 🌙' : 'بارز ✨', 540, 1640, t, 47.13, 0, { size: 56, bg: v < .5 ? '#3a2f3f' : C.o });
}
function S11(t) { // 49.75–57.3 QUALITY LIGHT picks the spot + placement; fee deducted
  bgImg(IM.w_spot, 1, 1.12, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const lp = prog(t, 49.83, 50.3); glow(540, 300, 230, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 300, 240 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 460, 270, { alpha: prog(t, 50.1, 50.7) });
  if (t < 54.0) {
    ['السبوت المناسب 🔦', 'مكانه 📍', 'وزاويته 📐'].forEach((s, i) => { const t0 = [51.49, 52.6, 53.2][i]; const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 760 + i * 190); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.92)'; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 5; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(260, 0, 44, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt('✓', 260, 6, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); txt(s, -40, 4, { size: 54, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); });
    label('نختار لك…', 540, 600, t, 51.49, 54.0, { size: 50, bg: C.o });
  } else {
    const q = E.back(prog(t, 54.1, 54.55)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.rotate(-.05); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#f3e6c4'; rrect(-330, -110, 660, 220, 30); ctx.fill(); txt('سعر التصميم', 0, -6, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#1a1416' }); const s = prog(t, 55.0, 55.5); if (s > 0) { ctx.strokeStyle = C.red; ctx.lineWidth = 12; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-250, 20); ctx.lineTo(-250 + 500 * s, -30); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.restore(); }
    label('ينخصم عند الشراء', 540, 1120, t, 56.34, 57.3, { size: 62, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  }
}
function S12(t) { // 57.3–end address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM.c_spot, 1, 1.05 + .03 * prog(t, 57.3, 63.4), 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.76)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, 57.45, 57.8)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, 57.45, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, 58.2, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, 59.56, 59.95)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, 60.5, 60.9); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, 60.6, 61.1) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, 60.91, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, 61.5, 62.0) });
  if (t > 61.0) sparkles(t, 61.0, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
const SCENES = [[0, 4.15, S1, .7], [4.15, 7.45, S2, .4], [7.45, 12.75, S3, .5], [12.75, 17.8, S4, .9], [17.8, 20.85, S5, .9], [20.85, 30.8, S6, .8], [30.8, 37.1, S7, .8], [37.1, 41.15, S8, .9], [41.15, 45.7, S9, .9], [45.7, 49.75, S10, .9], [49.75, 57.3, S11, .9], [57.3, 63.4, S12, 1]];
function draw(t) {
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0b0a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < 49.6) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  finish(t);
  if (t < .15) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${1 - t / .15})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
  if (t > 62.8) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - 62.8) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of ['w_dark', 'w_flat', 'w_spot', 'w_wide', 'c_dark', 'c_flat', 'c_spot', 'c_bad', 'c_cold', 'c_lowcri', 'c_dim', 'c_wide', 'v_dark', 'v_flat', 'u_spot', 'u_dark']) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/examples/furniture_colors.js`

```javascript
// ---------- colours: why furniture changes colour under different light ----------
const CROPM = { sx: 40, sy: 520, sw: 1000, sh: 1410 }; // sofa area in the main view
function card2(t, t0, im, x, y, lab, col, mk, i = 0, w = 450, h = 635) {
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .45)); if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y + h / 2); ctx.scale(p, p);
  imgCard(IM[im], -w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, { r: 28, ...CROPM }); ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 8; rrect(-w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, 28); ctx.stroke();
  if (mk) { ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(w / 2 - 50, -h / 2 + 50, 40, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt(mk, w / 2 - 50, -h / 2 + 54, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); }
  ctx.restore(); if (lab) label(lab, x, y + h + 70, t, t0 + .2, 0, { size: 44, bg: col, color: col === GOOD ? '#06210f' : '#fff' });
}
function badge(t, n, t0, s) { // numbered reason badge
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 250); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 62, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  txt(String(n), 0, 6, { size: 76, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore();
  label(s, 540, 395, t, t0 + .15, 0, { size: 58, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)' });
}
const SPEC = ['#7b2cff', '#2c5bff', '#13b5c9', '#2ec44a', '#d6e02a', '#ffb21f', '#ff6a1f', '#e3172b'];
function spectrum(x, y, w, h, t, t0, gap) { // rainbow bar; gap>0 knocks out the red end
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + w, 0); SPEC.forEach((c, i) => g.addColorStop(i / (SPEC.length - 1), c));
  ctx.fillStyle = g; rrect(x, y, w, h, h / 2); ctx.fill();
  if (gap > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,16,14,.92)'; ctx.fillRect(x + w * (1 - .3 * gap), y - 4, w * .3 * gap + 2, h + 8); ctx.strokeStyle = C.red; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.setLineDash([14, 10]); ctx.strokeRect(x + w * .7, y - 8, w * .3, h + 16); ctx.setLineDash([]); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function rayDiagram(t, t0) { // lamp -> sofa -> eye
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(90, 560, 900, 760, 40); ctx.fill();
  // lamp
  ctx.fillStyle = '#2a2420'; rrect(170, 620, 140, 60, 14); ctx.fill(); glow(240, 700, 90, '#fff3dd', .7); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff6e6'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(240, 690, 22, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  // sofa
  ctx.fillStyle = '#e3cfae'; rrect(380, 1080, 380, 110, 26); ctx.fill(); rrect(380, 1010, 380, 90, 26); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#b3262b'; rrect(410, 990, 90, 80, 16); ctx.fill();
  // eye
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff'; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(860, 760, 70, 40, 0, 0, 7); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fillStyle = '#5aa9ff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(860, 760, 24, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  // rays: incoming rainbow, outgoing filtered beige
  const p1 = E.inOut(prog(t, t0 + .3, t0 + 1.2)), p2 = E.inOut(prog(t, t0 + 1.4, t0 + 2.3));
  SPEC.forEach((c, i) => { const sx = 240, sy = 715, ex = 470 + i * 26, ey = 1000; ctx.strokeStyle = c; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); ctx.lineTo(lerp(sx, ex, p1), lerp(sy, ey, p1)); ctx.stroke(); });
  if (p2 > 0) { ['#e3cfae', '#d8b98e', '#f0dcc0'].forEach((c, i) => { const sx = 620 + i * 20, sy = 1000, ex = 820 + i * 15, ey = 790; ctx.strokeStyle = c; ctx.lineWidth = 8; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(sx, sy); ctx.lineTo(lerp(sx, ex, p2), lerp(sy, ey, p2)); ctx.stroke(); }); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function gauge(x, y, t, t0, v0, v1, col) { // CRI dial 0..100
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; const v = lerp(v0, v1, E.inOut(prog(t, t0 + .3, t0 + 1.3)));
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(-230, -200, 460, 330, 34); ctx.fill();
  ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.strokeStyle = '#3a332d'; ctx.lineWidth = 30; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 40, 160, Math.PI, 0); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 40, 160, Math.PI, Math.PI + Math.PI * v / 100); ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  txt(String(Math.round(v)), 0, 10, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); txt('CRI', 0, 95, { size: 40, weight: 800, color: '#cbbfb0', dir: 'ltr' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function tip(t, t0, y, big, small, col) {
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, y); ctx.scale(p, p);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.92)'; rrect(-420, -95, 840, 190, 40); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 6; rrect(-420, -95, 840, 190, 40); ctx.stroke();
  txt(big, 250, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: col, dir: 'ltr' }); txt(small, -120, 4, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore();
}

function S1(t) { // 0–3.9 bought a beige sofa: in the showroom it was gorgeous
  bgImg(IM.m_show, 1, 1.03 + .04 * prog(t, 0, 3.9), 540, 1000); shade2();
  pop('شريت كنبة لونها بيج 🛋️', 540, 360, t, .22, 3.9, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  sub2('بالمعرض كانت تجنن 😍', t, 2.11, 3.9, C.o);
  if (t > 2.3) sparkles(t, 2.3, 10, 140, 900, 800, 400);
}
function S2(t) { // 3.9–9.5 at home: grey? yellow?
  if (t < 6.43) bgImg(IM.m_warm, 1, 1.03, 540, 1000);
  else keyed(t, [[6.43, 'm_cold'], [8.13, 'm_yellow']], 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, 6.43, 9.5), 540, 1000, .35);
  shade2();
  title2('بس أول ما حطيتها بالبيت… 🏠', t, 4.16, 9.5, '#fff', 62);
  if (t >= 6.43 && t < 8.13) pop('صارت رمادية؟! 😳', 540, 1600, t, 6.43, 8.13, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: '#cfe0ff', glow: 25, glowColor: '#5a7cff' });
  if (t >= 8.13) pop('أو صفرا؟! 😬', 540, 1600, t, 8.13, 9.5, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: '#ffc46b', glow: 25 });
}
function S3(t) { // 9.5–13.7 the sofa didn't change, the light did
  bgImg(IM.m_show, 1, 1.1, 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  pop('لا… الكنبة ما تغيّرت ✋', 540, 420, t, 9.76, 13.7, { size: 76, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  const w = 300, h = 420; [['m_cold', 210], ['m_show', 540], ['m_yellow', 870]].forEach(([k, x], i) => { const p = E.back(prog(t, 10.0 + i * .2, 10.4 + i * .2)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, 900); ctx.scale(p, p); imgCard(IM[k], -w / 2, -h / 2, w, h, { r: 22, sx: 140, sy: 760, sw: 820, sh: 1150 }); ctx.restore(); });
  if (t >= 11.69) pop('اللي تغيّر هو الضو 💡', 540, 1360, t, 11.69, 13.7, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
}
function S4(t) { // 13.7–21.2 colour = the light that bounces back
  bgImg(IM.m_show, 1, 1.12, 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  title2('اللون اللي تشوفه… 👁️', t, 14.05, 21.2, '#fff', 66, 330);
  if (t < 17.9) { rayDiagram(t, 14.3); label('هو الضو اللي يرتد من القطعة لعيونك', 540, 1430, t, 15.52, 17.9, { size: 48, bg: C.o }); }
  else {
    spectrum(140, 820, 800, 90, t, 17.92, 0);
    pop('فلو الضو ناقص لون…', 540, 680, t, 17.92, 21.2, { size: 64, weight: 900, color: '#fff' });
    spectrum(140, 1020, 800, 90, t, 18.4, E.out(prog(t, 18.6, 19.2)));
    label('القطعة بعد تخسره ❌', 540, 1300, t, 19.44, 21.2, { size: 56, bg: C.red });
  }
}
function S5(t) { // 21.2–31.2 reason 1: colour temperature, cold 6500K
  if (t < 24.79) { bgImg(IM.m_show, 1, 1.1, 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); badge(t, 1, 21.74, 'درجة حرارة اللون 🌡️'); return; }
  keyed(t, [[24.79, 'm_show'], [25.0, 'm_cold']], 1.04, 540, 1000, .5);
  if (t > 27.9) { const z = lerp(1, 1.0, 0); bgImg(IM.c_cold, E.out(prog(t, 27.9, 28.4)), 1.03 + .03 * prog(t, 27.9, 31.2), 540, 960); }
  shade2();
  title2('الأبيض البارد ❄️', t, 24.79, 31.2, '#cfe0ff', 62);
  if (t < 27.9) pop('6500K', 540, 1550, t, 25.4, 27.9, { size: 120, weight: 900, color: '#a9c2ff', glow: 30, glowColor: '#5a7cff' });
  sub2('الخشب يطلع باهت 🪵', t, 27.99, 29.5, '#2b2f45', '#fff', 1560);
  sub2('والبيج يميل للرمادي 🩶', t, 29.53, 31.2, '#2b2f45', '#fff', 1680);
}
function S6(t) { // 31.2–36.6 warm 3000K brings out wood, leather, fabrics
  keyed(t, [[31.2, 'c_cold'], [31.6, 'c_warm']], 1.03 + .04 * prog(t, 31.2, 36.6), 540, 960, .6); flash(t, 31.6, .18); shade2();
  title2('والضو الدافي 🔥', t, 31.5, 36.6, C.amber, 66);

}
function S7(t) { // 36.6–45.6 reason 2: CRI; cheap lamp 80 or less

  if (t >= 44.14) label('والخشب يطلع ميت 🪵', 540, 1690, t, 44.14, 0, { size: 52, bg: '#3a2f3f' });
}
function S8(t) { // 45.6–51.7 CRI 90+: true colours like daylight
  const k = E.inOut(prog(t, 46.0, 47.3)); bgImg(IM.c_warm, 1, 1.04, 540, 960);
  if (k < 1) { const pos = 1080 * (1 - k); ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, pos, H); ctx.clip(); bgImg(IM.c_lowcri, 1, 1.04, 540, 960); ctx.restore(); if (k > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 25; ctx.fillRect(pos - 4, 0, 8, H); ctx.restore(); } }
  shade2();
  title2('بس لو المؤشر 90 وفوق ✅', t, 45.94, 51.7, '#fff', 62);
  if (t < 47.9) gauge(540, 1500, t, 46.1, 80, 95, GOOD);
  if (t >= 47.98) label('الألوان تطلع على حقيقتها 🎨', 540, 1560, t, 47.98, 0, { size: 52, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  if (t >= 49.97) label('مثل ضو النهار ☀️', 540, 1690, t, 49.97, 0, { size: 52, bg: C.o });
  if (t > 48.1) sparkles(t, 48.1, 12, 140, 700, 800, 700);
}
function S9(t) { // 51.7–57.7 mistake 3: mixing colour temperatures
  bgImg(IM.m_mixed, 1, 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, 51.7, 57.7), 540, 1000); shade2();
  badge(t, 3, 52.02, 'الغلطة الثالثة ⚠️');

  if (t >= 55.62) pop('نفس الكنبة… بلونين! 😵', 540, 1600, t, 55.62, 57.7, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
}
function S10(t) { // 57.7–65.8 tips
  bgImg(IM.m_warm, 1, 1.1, 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

function S11(t) { // 65.8–74.5 QUALITY LIGHT: light that shows true colour; fee deducted
  bgImg(IM.m_warm, 1, 1.12, 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const lp = prog(t, 66.1, 66.6); glow(540, 300, 230, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 300, 240 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 460, 270, { alpha: prog(t, 66.4, 67.0) });
  if (t < 70.8) {
    pop('نختار لك الإضاءة اللي تبيّن', 540, 620, t, 67.0, 70.8, { size: 56, weight: 900, color: '#fff' });
    pop('أثاثك بلونه الحقيقي 🛋️', 540, 710, t, 68.3, 70.8, { size: 60, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 20 });
    const c = E.out(prog(t, 68.6, 69.1)); if (c > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= c; imgCard(IM.c_warm, 190, 820, 700, 760, { r: 30, sx: 60, sy: 500, sw: 960, sh: 1040 }); ctx.restore(); }
  } else {
    const q = E.back(prog(t, 70.88, 71.3)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.rotate(-.05); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#f3e6c4'; rrect(-330, -110, 660, 220, 30); ctx.fill(); txt('سعر التصميم', 0, -6, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#1a1416' }); const s = prog(t, 71.8, 72.3); if (s > 0) { ctx.strokeStyle = C.red; ctx.lineWidth = 12; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-250, 20); ctx.lineTo(-250 + 500 * s, -30); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.restore(); }
    label('ينخصم عند الشراء', 540, 1120, t, 72.6, 74.5, { size: 62, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  }
}
function S12(t) { // 74.5–end address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM.c_warm, 1, 1.05 + .03 * prog(t, 74.5, 80.6), 540, 960); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.76)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, 74.63, 75.0)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, 74.8, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, 75.4, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, 77.05, 77.45)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, 77.9, 78.3); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, 78.0, 78.5) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, 78.44, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, 79.0, 79.5) });
  if (t > 78.6) sparkles(t, 78.6, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
const SCENES = [[0, 3.9, S1, .7], [3.9, 9.5, S2, .4], [9.5, 13.7, S3, .5], [13.7, 21.2, S4, .7], [21.2, 31.2, S5, .8], [31.2, 36.6, S6, .9], [36.6, 45.6, S7, .8], [45.6, 51.7, S8, .9], [51.7, 57.7, S9, .8], [57.7, 65.8, S10, .9], [65.8, 74.5, S11, .9], [74.5, 80.6, S12, 1]];
function draw(t) {
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0b0a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < 65.6) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  finish(t);
  if (t < .15) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${1 - t / .15})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
  if (t > 80.0) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - 80.0) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of ['m_show', 'm_cold', 'm_warm', 'm_yellow', 'm_mixed', 'm_lowcri', 'c_show', 'c_cold', 'c_warm', 'c_yellow', 'c_lowcri']) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/examples/garden.js`

```javascript
// ---------- garden: dark after sunset -> layered evening garden ----------
function layerBadge(t, n, t0, label0) { // numbered layer badge at the top-left
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(130, 200); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 58, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
  txt(String(n), 0, 5, { size: 70, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore();
  label(label0, 370, 200, t, t0 + .1, 0, { size: 50, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)' });
}
function twinkle(t, a) { if (a <= 0) return; for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) { const x = rand(i) * 1080, y = rand(i + 50) * 560, k = .5 + .5 * Math.sin(t * (2 + rand(i + 9) * 3) + i); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a * k; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#cfe0ff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 12; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 2 + rand(i + 3) * 2, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); } }
function arrowUp(x, y0, y1, a) { if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.strokeStyle = C.amber; ctx.fillStyle = C.amber; ctx.lineWidth = 8; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.shadowColor = C.amber; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y0); ctx.lineTo(x, y1 + 30); ctx.stroke(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x - 26, y1 + 36); ctx.lineTo(x, y1); ctx.lineTo(x + 26, y1 + 36); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
function spec(x, y, t, t0, big, small, col = C.o) { // a spec tile
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(p, p);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)'; rrect(-210, -130, 420, 260, 34); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = 5; rrect(-210, -130, 420, 260, 34); ctx.stroke();
  txt(big, 0, -20, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: col, dir: 'ltr' }); txt(small, 0, 75, { size: 38, weight: 800, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore();
}
function sunsetSwitch(x, y, t, t0) { // a small sun sinking below a horizon and lights turning on
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return; const s = clamp((t - t0 - .3) / 1.4);
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)'; rrect(-300, -150, 600, 300, 34); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); rrect(-300, -150, 600, 300, 34); ctx.clip();
  const sk = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -150, 0, 40); sk.addColorStop(0, s < 1 ? '#f08a4b' : '#0b1430'); sk.addColorStop(1, s < 1 ? '#ffd08a' : '#172447'); ctx.globalAlpha *= 1; ctx.fillStyle = sk; ctx.fillRect(-300, -150, 600, 190);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffcc55'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, -40 + 110 * s, 46, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#20301a'; ctx.fillRect(-300, 40, 600, 110);
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const on = s >= 1; ctx.fillStyle = on ? '#ffd6a2' : '#444'; ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd6a2'; ctx.shadowBlur = on ? 25 : 0; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-200 + i * 100, 90, 12, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function gardenPlan(x, y, w, h, t, t0) { // top view drawing with light symbols
  ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f3ed'; rrect(x, y, w, h, 26); ctx.fill(); ctx.clip();
  const X = v => x + w / 2 + v * (w - 120) / 9.4, Z = v => y + 60 + (-v / 14.6) * (h - 120);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#d6e3c3'; ctx.fillRect(X(-4.7), Z(0), X(4.4) - X(-4.7), Z(-14.3) - Z(0));
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2a221c'; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.strokeRect(X(-4.7), Z(0), X(4.4) - X(-4.7), Z(-14.3) - Z(0));
  ctx.fillStyle = '#c9a47a'; ctx.fillRect(X(-2.7), Z(-10.8), X(2.7) - X(-2.7), Z(-13.9) - Z(-10.8));
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efe9e0'; for (let i = 0; i < 13; i++) { const z = -.4 - i * .78, xx = Math.sin(i * .45) * .55; ctx.fillRect(X(xx - .45), Z(z + .275), X(.9) - X(0), Z(-.55) - Z(0)); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#7da35c'; for (const [px, pz] of [[-2.9, -6.2], [-3.3, -10.2], [3.2, -9]]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(X(px), Z(pz), 34, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }
  const sym = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < 13; i += 2) { const z = -.4 - i * .78, xx = Math.sin(i * .45) * .55; sym.push([xx - .85, z], [xx + .85, z]); }
  sym.push([-2.45, -5.75], [-2.85, -9.75], [3.65, -8.55]);
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) sym.push([-3.9 + i * 1.95, -14.05]); for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) sym.push([-4.55, -1.5 - i * 2.1]);
  sym.forEach(([sx, sz], i) => { const q = prog(t, t0 + i * .035, t0 + .3 + i * .035); if (q <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= q; ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 14; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(X(sx), Z(sz), 11, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); });
  ctx.restore();
}

function S1(t) { // 0–6.1 a garden nobody uses after sunset
  const k = E.inOut(prog(t, 3.8, 5.6)); const z = 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, 0, 6.1);
  bgImg(IM.g_day, 1, z, 540, 1000); bgImg(IM.g_dusk, k, z, 540, 1000); shade2();
  pop('عندك حوش وحديقة…', 540, 480, t, .05, 6.1, { size: 88, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  sub2('بس قولّي بصراحة…', t, 2.3, 3.75, '#3a2f3f');
  sub2('متى آخر مرة قعدت فيها بعد المغرب؟ 🌇', t, 3.84, 6.1, C.o);
}
function S2(t) { // 6.1–13.45 darkness, nobody stays out
  if (t < 9.05 || t >= 10.3) { const z = 1.06 + .05 * prog(t, 6.1, 13.45); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, 6.15, 7.0)); bgImg(IM.g_dusk, 1 - k, z, 540, 1000); bgImg(IM.g_night, k, z, 540, 1000); }
  else bgImg(IM.p_dark, 1, 1.03, 540, 960);
  shade2();
  pop('أول ما تغيب الشمس…', 540, 480, t, 6.41, 9.0, { size: 82, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  sub2('تصير ظلمة 🌑', t, 7.76, 9.0, '#2b2f45');
  if (t >= 9.05 && t < 10.3) title2('ما تشوف الممر…', t, 9.1, 10.3, '#cfd8ff', 70);
  title2('والشجر يختفي…', t, 10.33, 13.45, '#cfd8ff', 70);
  sub2('والكل يدخل داخل 🚪', t, 11.4, 13.45, '#3a2f3f');
  if (t > 11.4) { const a = E.out(prog(t, 11.4, 11.9)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a * (.6 + .4 * Math.sin(t * 5)); ctx.strokeStyle = C.amber; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(1000, 1040, 90, 210, 0, 0, 7); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); }
}
function S3(t) { // 13.45–16.45 night is the best weather
  bgImg(IM.g_night, 1, 1.12 - .04 * prog(t, 13.45, 16.45), 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,10,25,.25)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); twinkle(t, E.out(prog(t, 13.6, 14.4)));
  pop('مع إن أحلى جو…', 540, 820, t, 13.66, 16.45, { size: 92, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('هو جو الليل 🌙', 540, 960, t, 14.99, 16.45, { size: 100, weight: 900, color: '#cfd8ff', glow: 30, glowColor: '#5a6cff' });
}
function S4(t) { // 16.45–18.75 layer by layer
  bgImg(IM.g_night, 1, 1.08, 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.55)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  pop('خلّنا نشغّلها…', 540, 640, t, 16.64, 0, { size: 90, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  const names = ['الممرات', 'الشجر', 'الجدار', 'الجلسة'];
  names.forEach((n, i) => { const p = E.back(prog(t, 17.1 + i * .22, 17.45 + i * .22)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 820 + i * 150); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(-300, -55, 600, 110, 55); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 4; rrect(-300, -55, 600, 110, 55); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(230, 0, 38, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt(String(i + 1), 230, 4, { size: 44, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); txt(n, -30, 4, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); });
  sub2('طبقة طبقة ✨', t, 17.3, 0, C.o);
}
function S5(t) { // 18.75–25.25 1: paths
  if (t < 20.55) { keyed(t, [[18.75, 'g_night'], [18.95, 'g_path']], 1.05, 540, 1000, .5); flash(t, 18.95, .2); }
  else keyed(t, [[20.55, 'p_path']], 1.02 + .05 * prog(t, 20.55, 25.25), 540, 960);
  shade2(); layerBadge(t, 1, 18.88, 'الممرات');
  title2('إضاءة منخفضة على الجوانب', t, 20.61, 25.25, '#fff', 58, 360);
  sub2('توريك الطريق… بدون زغللة ✓', t, 22.26, 25.25, GOOD, '#06210f');
}
function S6(t) { // 25.25–33.0 2: trees
  if (t < 26.65) { keyed(t, [[25.25, 'g_path'], [25.5, 'g_tree']], 1.05, 540, 1000, .5); flash(t, 25.5, .2); }
  else { keyed(t, [[26.65, 't_dark'], [27.3, 't_tree']], 1.02 + .05 * prog(t, 26.65, 33), 540, 960, .8); }
  shade2(); layerBadge(t, 2, 25.47, 'الشجر والنخل');
  title2('سبوت أرضي… من تحت لفوق', t, 26.71, 29.65, '#fff', 60, 360);
  arrowUp(700, 1700, 1050, E.out(prog(t, 27.3, 27.8)) * (1 - prog(t, 29.4, 29.7)));
  label('والنخلة تصير لوحة بالليل 🌴', 540, 1650, t, 29.7, 33.0, { size: 52, bg: C.o });
  if (t > 30.0) sparkles(t, 30.0, 12, 200, 300, 700, 700);
}
function S7(t) { // 33.0–37.95 3: walls
  keyed(t, [[33.0, 'w_dark'], [33.4, 'w_wall']], 1.03 + .05 * prog(t, 33, 37.95), 540, 960, .7); flash(t, 33.4, .15);
  shade2(); layerBadge(t, 3, 33.23, 'الجدار');
  title2('إضاءة تغسل الطوفة بنعومة', t, 35.13, 37.95, '#fff', 58, 360);
  sub2('وتعطي الحوش عمق', t, 37.1, 37.95, C.o);
}
function S8(t) { // 37.95–44.3 4: seating
  keyed(t, [[37.95, 's_dark'], [38.3, 's_all']], 1.03 + .06 * prog(t, 37.95, 44.3), 540, 960, .7); flash(t, 38.3, .25);
  shade2(); layerBadge(t, 4, 38.17, 'الجلسة');
  title2('إضاءة مخفية تحت الكنب', t, 38.4, 39.9, '#fff', 60, 360);
  title2('وحبال إضاءة دافية فوق 💡', t, 39.93, 44.3, C.o2, 58, 360);
  label('والقعدة صارت تنادي عليك 🛋️', 540, 1650, t, 41.84, 44.3, { size: 52, bg: C.o });
  if (t > 42.0) sparkles(t, 42.0, 14, 120, 300, 840, 600);
}
function S9(t) { // 44.3–54.5 IP65, 3000K, sunset sensor
  bgImg(IM.g_all, 1, 1.12, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.74)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  title2('مقاومة للمية والغبار 💧', t, 44.55, 54.5, '#fff', 60, 300);
  spec(300, 600, t, 46.79, 'IP65', 'مية + غبار', '#5aa9ff');
  spec(780, 600, t, 48.42, '3000K', 'لون دافي', C.amber);
  if (t > 50.6) sunsetSwitch(540, 1060, t, 50.7);
  label('حساس غروب أو تايمر ⏱️', 540, 1300, t, 50.75, 0, { size: 50, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)' });
  label('تشتغل بروحها مع المغرب 🌇', 540, 1650, t, 52.71, 0, { size: 52, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
}
function S10(t) { // 54.5–59.95 same garden: before/after
  const z = 1.04; bgImg(IM.g_all, 1, z, 540, 1000);
  const pos = lerp(1080, 0, E.inOut(prog(t, 54.8, 56.1)));
  if (pos > 1) { ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, pos, H); ctx.clip(); bgImg(IM.g_night, 1, z, 540, 1000); ctx.restore(); ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 25; ctx.fillRect(pos - 4, 0, 8, H); ctx.restore(); }
  shade2();
  title2('نفس الحديقة…', t, 54.75, 0, '#fff', 84, 330);
  label('بس صارت أحلى مكان بالبيت ✨', 540, 1530, t, 56.2, 0, { size: 52, bg: C.o });

}

  const p = E.back(prog(t, 67.73, 68.1)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }

  const lp = prog(t, 71.72, 72.1); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, 71.85, 72.35) });

const SCENES = [[0, 6.1, S1, .7], [6.1, 13.45, S2, .4], [13.45, 16.45, S3, .4], [16.45, 18.75, S4, .6], [18.75, 25.25, S5, .9], [25.25, 33.0, S6, .9], [33.0, 37.95, S7, .9], [37.95, 44.3, S8, 1], [44.3, 54.5, S9, .9], [54.5, 59.95, S10, 1], [59.95, 67.6, S11, .9], [67.6, 75.2, S12, 1]];
function draw(t) {
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0b0a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < 59.8) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  finish(t);
  if (t < .15) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${1 - t / .15})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
  if (t > 74.6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - 74.6) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of ['g_day', 'g_dusk', 'g_night', 'g_path', 'g_tree', 'g_wall', 'g_all', 'p_dark', 'p_path', 't_dark', 't_tree', 'w_dark', 'w_wall', 's_dark', 's_all']) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/examples/stairs.js`

```javascript
// ---------- staircase: ordinary -> hidden LED under every tread ----------
function bgImg(img, a = 1, z = 1, cx = 540, cy = 960) { if (!img || a <= 0) return; const w = 1080 * z, h = 1920 * z; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.drawImage(img, cx - w * (cx / 1080), cy - h * (cy / 1920), w, h); ctx.restore(); }
function shade2() { const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 560); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(8,6,5,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, 560); const g2 = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 1380, 0, 1920); g2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(8,6,5,0)'); g2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'); ctx.fillStyle = g2; ctx.fillRect(0, 1380, W, 540); }
function title2(s, t, t0, t1, col = '#fff', size = 68, y = 330) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, t1, { size, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)', color: col }); }
function sub2(s, t, t0, t1, bg = C.o, color = '#fff', y = 1650) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, t1, { size: 50, bg, color }); }
// sequence of renders: step k lit (0 = all dark)
function seqImg(view, k) { return k <= 0 ? IM[view + '_dark'] : IM[`${view}_${Math.min(14, k)}`]; }
function seq(view, t, t0, t1, z, cx, cy) { // lights climb from the first step to the top between t0 and t1
  const f = clamp((t - t0) / (t1 - t0)) * 14, k = Math.floor(f), fr = f - k;
  bgImg(seqImg(view, k), 1, z, cx, cy); if (k < 14 && fr > 0) bgImg(seqImg(view, k + 1), E.out(fr), z, cx, cy);
}
// footprint icon
function foot(x, y, s, a, rot = 0) {
  if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(rot); ctx.scale(s, s); ctx.fillStyle = C.o2; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 16, 26, 0, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 36, 12, 13, 0, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-13 + i * 6.5, -32 - (i === 0 ? 4 : i === 1 ? 6 : 4 - i), 4.2 - i * .4, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.restore();
}
// motion sensor icon with pulsing waves
function sensor(x, y, s, t, a) {
  if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 34 * s, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 14 * s, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const p = ((t * 1.2 + i / 3) % 1); ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,122,53,${1 - p})`; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, (40 + 110 * p) * s, Math.PI * .15, Math.PI * .85); ctx.stroke(); }
  ctx.restore();
}
// cross-section of a tread with the LED profile under the nosing
function section(x, y, sc, t, t0) {
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .5)); if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(sc, sc);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(14,11,9,.88)'; rrect(-420, -300, 840, 640, 34); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o2; ctx.lineWidth = 4; rrect(-420, -300, 840, 640, 34); ctx.stroke();
  // light cone from under the nosing down onto the riser and the tread below
  const lp = prog(t, t0 + .9, t0 + 1.6);
  if (lp > 0) { const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, -40, 0, 250); g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,214,162,${.85 * lp})`); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,214,162,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(78, -52); ctx.lineTo(130, -52); ctx.lineTo(330, 250); ctx.lineTo(70, 250); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#efe9e0'; ctx.fillRect(40, -64, 30, 314);           // riser below this tread (recessed behind the nosing)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#a06e3e'; rrect(-380, -120, 540, 56, 8); ctx.fill(); // tread with nosing overhang (ends at x=160)
  ctx.fillStyle = '#a06e3e'; rrect(40, 250, 380, 40, 6); ctx.fill();    // tread of the step below
  // aluminium channel + LED + diffuser fixed under the nosing
  ctx.fillStyle = '#9aa0a6'; ctx.fillRect(74, -64, 70, 26); ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0b0a'; ctx.fillRect(80, -64, 58, 16);
  ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd6a2'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30 * lp; ctx.fillStyle = lp > 0 ? '#ffe6c4' : '#777'; ctx.fillRect(86, -52, 46, 8); ctx.restore();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.85)'; ctx.fillRect(76, -42, 66, 6);
  // labels
  const lab = (s, lx, ly, px, py, tt0) => { const q = E.out(prog(t, tt0, tt0 + .35)); if (q <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= q; ctx.strokeStyle = C.o2; ctx.lineWidth = 3; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(px, py); ctx.lineTo(lx, ly); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fillStyle = C.o2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, 7, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt(s, lx + (lx > 0 ? 10 : -10), ly, { size: 36, weight: 800, color: '#fff', align: lx > 0 ? 'left' : 'right' }); ctx.restore(); };
  lab('بروفايل ألمنيوم', -30, -210, 110, -60, t0 + .3);
  lab('غطا ناعم', 220, 40, 120, -39, t0 + .8);
  lab('شريط LED', -120, 120, 90, -48, t0 + 1.2);
  txt('درجة', -200, -92, { size: 34, weight: 900, color: '#fff' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function dotsVsLine(t, t0) {
  const a = E.out(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (a <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a;
  card(90, 1300, 420, 230, 26, 1, '#ff5a4a'); card(570, 1300, 420, 230, 26, 1, GOOD);
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd6a2'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff3dd'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(140 + i * 40, 1390, 9, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
  ctx.save(); ctx.shadowColor = '#ffd6a2'; ctx.shadowBlur = 30; ctx.fillStyle = '#fff3dd'; rrect(620, 1380, 320, 20, 10); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
  txt('نقاط وزغللة', 300, 1475, { size: 40, weight: 900, color: '#ff8a7a' }); txt('خط ناعم ومتساوي', 780, 1475, { size: 40, weight: 900, color: GOOD });
  xMark(470, 1320, 40, prog(t, t0 + .3, t0 + .6)); checkMark(950, 1320, 40, prog(t, t0 + .5, t0 + .8), GOOD);

}

// t-view step positions (bottom -> top) for footprints
const TSTEP = [[40, 1560], [110, 1430], [170, 1330], [255, 1244], [340, 1176], [415, 1112], [480, 1060], [545, 1012], [605, 968], [660, 928], [712, 892], [760, 858], [818, 826], [900, 798]];

function stairPlan(x, y, w, h, t, t0) { // simple plan drawing of a straight flight with LED lines
  ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f3ed'; rrect(x, y, w, h, 26); ctx.fill(); ctx.clip();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2a221c'; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.strokeRect(x + 120, y + 110, w - 240, h - 220);
  for (let i = 1; i < 14; i++) { const yy = y + 110 + (h - 220) * i / 14; ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + 120, yy); ctx.lineTo(x + w - 120, yy); ctx.stroke(); const q = prog(t, t0 + i * .06, t0 + .3 + i * .06); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.setLineDash([16, 10]); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + 135, yy - 6); ctx.lineTo(x + 135 + (w - 270) * q, yy - 6); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); } }
  ctx.fillStyle = '#2a221c'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + w / 2, y + 130); ctx.lineTo(x + w / 2 - 22, y + 175); ctx.lineTo(x + w / 2 + 22, y + 175); ctx.fill();
  txt('UP', x + w / 2, y + 205, { size: 30, weight: 900, color: '#2a221c', dir: 'ltr' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function S9(t) { // 48.6–55.5 QUALITY LIGHT designs it, fee deducted
  bgImg(IM.m_3000, 1, 1.12, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const lp = prog(t, 48.96, 49.4); glow(540, 290, 230, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 290, 240 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 450, 270, { alpha: prog(t, 49.2, 49.8) });
  if (t < 53.0) {
    pop('نصمم إضاءة الدرج كاملة', 540, 590, t, 50.06, 53.0, { size: 64, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 20 });
    const c = E.out(prog(t, 50.2, 50.7)); if (c > 0) {
      const x = 140, y = 690, w = 800, h = 900; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= c; stairPlan(x, y, w, h, t, 50.8); ctx.restore();
      const wp = E.inOut(prog(t, 51.8, 52.7)); if (wp > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(x, y, w * wp, h); ctx.clip(); imgCard(IM.m_3000, x, y, w, h, { r: 26, shadow: false, sx: 90, sy: 420, sw: 900, sh: 1012 }); ctx.restore(); if (wp < 1) { ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 25; ctx.fillRect(x + w * wp - 4, y, 8, h); ctx.restore(); } }
      label('من المخطط… لين الواقع', 540, 1660, t, 50.9, 53.0, { size: 46, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
    }
  } else {
    const q = E.back(prog(t, 53.06, 53.5)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.rotate(-.05); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#f3e6c4'; rrect(-330, -110, 660, 220, 30); ctx.fill(); txt('سعر التصميم', 0, -6, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#1a1416' }); const s = prog(t, 53.7, 54.2); if (s > 0) { ctx.strokeStyle = C.red; ctx.lineWidth = 12; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-250, 20); ctx.lineTo(-250 + 500 * s, -30); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.restore(); }
    label('ينخصم عند الشراء', 540, 1120, t, 54.0, 55.5, { size: 62, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  }
}
function S10(t) { // 55.5–end address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM.m_3000, 1, 1.05 + .03 * prog(t, 55.5, 64), 540, 1000); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, 55.75, 56.15)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, 55.75, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, 56.41, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, 58.89, 59.3)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, 60.46, 60.9); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, 60.6, 61.1) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, 61.5, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, 61.9, 62.4) });
  if (t > 61.7) sparkles(t, 61.7, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
const SCENES = [[0, 5.9, S1, .7], [5.9, 10.7, S2, .4], [10.7, 13.95, S3, .5], [13.95, 22.85, S4, .9], [22.85, 28.6, S5, .9], [28.6, 36.1, S6, 1], [36.1, 44.1, S7, .8], [44.1, 48.6, S8, 1], [48.6, 55.5, S9, .9], [55.5, 64.2, S10, 1]];
function draw(t) {
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0b0a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < 48.5) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  finish(t);
  if (t < .15) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${1 - t / .15})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
  if (t > 63.6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - 63.6) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  IM.m_plain = await load('r/ST_m_plain.jpg'); IM.m_dark = await load('r/ST_m_dark.jpg'); IM.t_dark = await load('r/ST_t_dark.jpg');
  for (let k = 1; k <= 14; k++) { IM['m_' + k] = await load(`r/ST_m_lit-${k}-3000.jpg`); IM['t_' + k] = await load(`r/ST_t_lit-${k}-3000.jpg`); }
  IM.m_3000 = IM.m_14; IM.m_2700 = await load('r/ST_m_lit-14-2700.jpg');
  IM.c_lit = await load('r/ST_c_lit-14-3000.jpg'); IM.c_dark = await load('r/ST_c_dark.jpg'); IM.s_lit = await load('r/ST_s_lit-14-3000.jpg');
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `skills/quality-light-video-generator/toolkit/reel/examples/wardrobe.js`

```javascript
// ---------- wardrobe: dark closet -> sensor doors, hidden LED, lit rail, lit drawers ----------
function bgImg(img, a = 1, z = 1, cx = 540, cy = 960) { if (!img || a <= 0) return; const w = 1080 * z, h = 1920 * z; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.drawImage(img, cx - w * (cx / 1080), cy - h * (cy / 1920), w, h); ctx.restore(); }
function shade2() { const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 560); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(8,6,5,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, 560); const g2 = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 1380, 0, 1920); g2.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(8,6,5,0)'); g2.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(8,6,5,.8)'); ctx.fillStyle = g2; ctx.fillRect(0, 1380, W, 540); }
function title2(s, t, t0, t1, col = '#fff', size = 68, y = 330) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, t1, { size, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)', color: col }); }
function sub2(s, t, t0, t1, bg = C.o, color = '#fff', y = 1650) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, t1, { size: 50, bg, color }); }
// crossfade between keyed images: list of [time, imageKey]
function keyed(t, keys, z = 1, cx = 540, cy = 960, dur = .45) {
  let a = keys[0][1], b = a, k = 1;
  for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) if (t >= keys[i][0]) { a = i ? keys[i - 1][1] : keys[0][1]; b = keys[i][1]; k = E.out(prog(t, keys[i][0], keys[i][0] + dur)); }
  bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); if (a !== b) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy);
}
function flash(t, t0, a = .35) { if (t > t0 && t < t0 + .6) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(255,236,210,${a * (1 - prog(t, t0, t0 + .6))})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); ctx.restore(); } }
function sensor(x, y, s, t, a, on = true) {
  if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.translate(x, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 40 * s, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = on ? C.o : '#555'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 16 * s, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  if (on) for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const p = ((t * 1.2 + i / 3) % 1); ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(255,122,53,${1 - p})`; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, (46 + 110 * p) * s, Math.PI * .15, Math.PI * .85); ctx.stroke(); }
  ctx.restore();
}
function onOff(on, x, y, t, t0, t1) { label(on ? 'ON 💡' : 'OFF', x, y, t, t0, t1, { size: 54, bg: on ? GOOD : '#3a2f3f', color: on ? '#06210f' : '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); }
function swatch(x, y, col, name, a, low) { // a garment colour chip
  if (a <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.fillStyle = col; rrect(x - 110, y - 70, 220, 140, 22); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.6)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; rrect(x - 110, y - 70, 220, 140, 22); ctx.stroke();
  txt(name, x, y + 110, { size: 40, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore();
}
function wardrobePlan(x, y, w, h, t, t0) { // elevation drawing with LED lines
  ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#f7f3ed'; rrect(x, y, w, h, 26); ctx.fill(); ctx.clip();
  const X = v => x + 90 + (w - 180) * v / 2.4, Y = v => y + h - 90 - (h - 180) * v / 2.6;
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2a221c'; ctx.lineWidth = 6; ctx.strokeRect(X(0), Y(2.6), X(2.4) - X(0), Y(0) - Y(2.6));
  ctx.lineWidth = 3; for (const v of [.9, 1.6]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(X(v), Y(0)); ctx.lineTo(X(v), Y(2.6)); ctx.stroke(); }
  const shelves = [[0, .9, 2.15], [0, .9, .3], [0, .9, .62], [.9, 1.6, 1.15], [.9, 1.6, 1.45], [.9, 1.6, 1.75], [.9, 1.6, 2.05], [.9, 1.6, 2.35], [1.6, 2.4, 2.15]];
  for (const [a, b, v] of shelves) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(X(a), Y(v)); ctx.lineTo(X(b), Y(v)); ctx.stroke(); }
  for (let d = 0; d < 3; d++) ctx.strokeRect(X(.92), Y(.1 + d * .33 + .3), X(1.58) - X(.92), Y(.1) - Y(.4));
  ctx.setLineDash([10, 8]); for (const [a, b] of [[0, .9], [1.6, 2.4]]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(X(a + .03), Y(1.95)); ctx.lineTo(X(b - .03), Y(1.95)); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.setLineDash([]);
  // LED lines appear one after another
  const leds = [...shelves.map(([a, b, v]) => [X(a + .03), Y(v) + 6, X(b - .03), Y(v) + 6]), [X(.03), Y(1.93), X(.87), Y(1.93)], [X(1.63), Y(1.93), X(2.37), Y(1.93)], ...[0, .9, 1.6, 2.4].map(v => [X(v) + (v ? -8 : 8), Y(.1), X(v) + (v ? -8 : 8), Y(2.55)])];
  leds.forEach(([x0, y0, x1, y1], i) => { const q = prog(t, t0 + i * .05, t0 + .35 + i * .05); if (q <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.strokeStyle = C.o; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 12; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y0); ctx.lineTo(x0 + (x1 - x0) * q, y0 + (y1 - y0) * q); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore(); });
  txt('2.40 m', x + w / 2, y + h - 40, { size: 30, weight: 800, color: '#2a221c', dir: 'ltr' });
  ctx.restore();
}

function S1(t) { // 0–4.9 morning, searching in a dim closet
  bgImg(IM.f_closed, 1, 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, 0, 5), 540, 1000); shade2();
  pop('تفتح الدولاب الصبح… ☀️', 540, 520, t, .22, 4.9, { size: 82, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  swatch(330, 1250, '#f7f5f0', 'الثوب الأبيض', E.out(prog(t, 1.8, 2.2)));
  swatch(750, 1250, '#1f2a44', 'القميص الكحلي', E.out(prog(t, 3.4, 3.8)));
  if (t > 1.9) { const r = .5 + .5 * Math.sin(t * 5); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= .9; txt('🔍', 540 + 140 * Math.sin(t * 2.2), 1000 + 30 * r, { size: 90, dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
}
function S2(t) { // 4.9–9.6 everything looks the same in the dark
  bgImg(IM.f_dark, 1, 1.1 + .05 * prog(t, 4.9, 9.6), 540, 1050); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5,6,12,.25)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); shade2();
  pop('كله معتم…', 540, 480, t, 5.21, 9.6, { size: 100, weight: 900, color: '#cfd8ff', glow: 25, glowColor: '#5a6cff' });
  const a = E.out(prog(t, 6.42, 6.8)); if (a > 0) { swatch(330, 1250, '#20232c', '؟', a); swatch(750, 1250, '#1d2029', '؟', a); txt('=', 540, 1250, { size: 110, weight: 900, color: '#fff', alpha: a, dir: 'ltr' }); }
  sub2('الألوان تتشابه… وتطلع غلط 😬', t, 6.42, 9.6, '#7a1f1f');
}
function S3(t) { // 9.6–12.35 imagine
  const z = 1.12 - .06 * E.inOut(prog(t, 9.6, 12.3)); bgImg(IM.f_dark, 1, z, 540, 1000); bgImg(IM.f_all, E.inOut(prog(t, 10.9, 12.0)) * .55, z, 540, 1000); shade2();
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.3)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  pop('بس تخيّل دولابك…', 540, 830, t, 10.0, 12.35, { size: 90, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('بالشكل هذا ✨', 540, 970, t, 10.9, 12.35, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  if (t > 11.0) sparkles(t, 11.0, 14, 160, 700, 760, 700);
}
function S4(t) { // 12.35–20.3 door sensor
  keyed(t, [[12.35, 'd_0'], [12.9, 'd_5on'], [13.9, 'd_1on'], [17.07, 'd_5on'], [18.71, 'd_5off'], [19.41, 'd_0']], 1.04, 540, 960, .35);
  flash(t, 12.9); flash(t, 17.9, .25); shade2();
  title2('تفتح الباب…', t, 12.66, 13.8, '#fff', 74);
  title2('والإضاءة تشتغل بروحها!', t, 13.84, 16.0, C.o2, 66);
  title2('حساس على الباب', t, 16.01, 20.3, '#fff', 70);
  sensor(540, 640, 1.1, t, E.out(prog(t, 16.05, 16.4)), t < 18.6);
  onOff(true, 540, 1650, t, 17.07, 18.6); if (t >= 18.71) onOff(false, 540, 1650, t, 18.71, 20.3);
  label('تفتح', 270, 1500, t, 17.07, 18.6, { size: 46, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)' }); label('تسكّر', 270, 1500, t, 18.71, 20.3, { size: 46, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)' });
}
function S5(t) { // 20.3–28.85 hidden profiles: sides, under every shelf, soft light
  if (t < 25.9) {
    keyed(t, [[20.3, 'f_dark'], [20.63, 'f_side'], [23.47, 'f_shelf']], 1.04, 540, 980, .5);
    flash(t, 20.63, .2); flash(t, 23.47, .2); shade2();
    title2('شريط LED داخل بروفايل ألمنيوم', t, 20.63, 23.4, '#fff', 54);
    title2('تحت كل رف… وعلى الجوانب', t, 23.47, 25.9, C.o2, 60);
  } else {

    if (t > 26.4) sparkles(t, 26.4, 10, 160, 600, 760, 700);
  }
}
function S6(t) { // 28.85–36.4 the hanging rail itself is lit
  if (t < 31.4) {
    keyed(t, [[28.85, 'f_shelf'], [29.4, 'f_rail']], 1.08 + .1 * E.inOut(prog(t, 29.2, 31.4)), 380, 900, .5); flash(t, 29.4, .2); shade2();
    title2('وعمود التعليق نفسه منوّر 💡', t, 29.18, 31.4, '#fff', 60);
  } else {

    title2('ينوّر الملابس من فوق', t, 31.46, 35.25, C.o2, 66);

  }
}
function S7(t) { // 36.4–43.5 drawers light up inside
  keyed(t, [[36.4, 'dr_closed'], [37.6, 'dr_open'], [39.13, 'dr_lit']], 1.03 + .06 * prog(t, 36.4, 43.5), 540, 1000, .5); flash(t, 39.13, .3);
  const gl = E.out(prog(t, 39.13, 39.7)); if (gl > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter'; const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(540, 1250, 50, 540, 1250, 620); g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,214,162,${.28 * gl})`); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,214,162,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 600, W, 1300); ctx.restore(); }
  shade2();
  title2('والأدراج بعد…', t, 36.54, 39.1, '#fff', 76);
  title2('تفتح الدرج والنور يشتغل داخله', t, 39.13, 43.5, C.o2, 56);
  label('⌚ الساعة', 300, 1500, t, 40.6, 43.5, { size: 46, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)' }); label('العطر 🧴', 780, 1500, t, 41.2, 43.5, { size: 46, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.84)' });
  sub2('تلقاها بثانية ⚡', t, 42.38, 43.5, GOOD, '#06210f');
}
function S8(t) { // 43.5–49.25 colour rendering: CRI 90+
  const z = 1.06; bgImg(IM.r_all, 1, z, 540, 1000);
  const pos = lerp(1080, 540, E.inOut(prog(t, 43.9, 44.6)));
  ctx.save(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, pos, H); ctx.clip(); ctx.filter = 'saturate(.12) sepia(.55) hue-rotate(40deg) brightness(.82) contrast(.85)'; bgImg(IM.r_all, 1, z, 540, 1000); ctx.filter = 'none'; ctx.restore();
  if (pos < 1079) { ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.shadowColor = '#fff'; ctx.shadowBlur = 25; ctx.fillRect(pos - 4, 0, 8, H); ctx.restore(); }
  shade2();
  title2('والأهم: تجسيد الألوان', t, 43.86, 49.25, '#fff', 64);
  label('CRI ضعيف', 270, 520, t, 44.2, 49.25, { size: 50, bg: '#7a1f1f' }); label('CRI 90+', 810, 520, t, 44.5, 49.25, { size: 56, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f', dir: 'ltr' });
  const s1 = E.out(prog(t, 46.15, 46.5)), s2 = E.out(prog(t, 47.84, 48.2));
  if (s1 > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= s1; ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(14,11,9,.85)'; rrect(150, 1170, 780, 330, 30); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
  if (s1 > 0 && s2 < 1) { swatch(330, 1290, '#3b3a35', 'كحلي؟', s1 * (1 - s2)); swatch(750, 1290, '#1f2a44', 'كحلي ✓', s1 * (1 - s2)); }
  if (s2 > 0) { swatch(330, 1290, '#2e2a26', 'أسود؟', s2); swatch(750, 1290, '#0a0a0a', 'أسود ✓', s2); }
}
function S9(t) { // 49.25–53.95 low voltage, safe, no heat
  bgImg(IM.s_all, 1, 1.15, 540, 900); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.72)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, 49.45, 49.85)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 640); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = GOOD; ctx.shadowColor = GOOD; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 150, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.shield, 540, 640, 190 * p, 1, { color: '#06210f', lw: 10 }); icon(IC.shieldCheck, 540, 640, 190 * p, prog(t, 49.8, 50.3), { color: '#06210f', lw: 12 }); }

function S10(t) { // 53.95–59.4 a boutique at home

      label('حسب مقاساتك 📐', 540, 1660, t, 61.6, 63.95, { size: 50, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
    }
  } else {
    const q = E.back(prog(t, 63.98, 64.4)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.rotate(-.05); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#f3e6c4'; rrect(-330, -110, 660, 220, 30); ctx.fill(); txt('سعر التصميم', 0, -6, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#1a1416' }); const s = prog(t, 64.6, 65.1); if (s > 0) { ctx.strokeStyle = C.red; ctx.lineWidth = 12; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-250, 20); ctx.lineTo(-250 + 500 * s, -30); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.restore(); }
    label('ينخصم عند الشراء', 540, 1120, t, 64.9, 66.3, { size: 62, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });

}
function S12(t) { // 66.3–end address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM.room, 1, 1.05 + .03 * prog(t, 66.3, 75), 540, 960); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(8,6,5,.74)'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, 66.56, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });

  const lp = prog(t, 71.69, 72.1); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, 71.8, 72.3) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, 72.91, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });

const SCENES = [[0, 4.9, S1, .6], [4.9, 9.6, S2, .4], [9.6, 12.35, S3, .5], [12.35, 20.3, S4, .9], [20.3, 28.85, S5, .9], [28.85, 36.4, S6, .9], [36.4, 43.5, S7, .9], [43.5, 49.25, S8, 1], [49.25, 53.95, S9, .8], [53.95, 59.4, S10, 1], [59.4, 66.3, S11, .9], [66.3, 75.0, S12, 1]];
function draw(t) {
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); ctx.globalAlpha = 1; ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0b0a'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < 59.3) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  finish(t);
  if (t < .15) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${1 - t / .15})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
  if (t > 74.4) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - 74.4) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of ['f_closed', 'f_dark', 'f_side', 'f_shelf', 'f_rail', 'f_all', 'd_0', 'd_5on', 'd_1on', 'd_5off', 's_dark', 's_all', 'r_dark', 'r_all', 'dr_closed', 'dr_open', 'dr_lit', 'room']) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```
