## `docs/video-handoff/samples/contractors_villacut/part.js`

```javascript
// ---------- Contractors: one supplier for the whole villa (villacut 3D body): dark villa → bought from 4 shops (mismatched colours, cheap flickering drivers) → QUALITY LIGHT: interior, hidden, outdoor IP65, steady drivers → bring the BOQ / drawing → CTA ----------
const L = {
  hook: .17, why: 1.01, five: 2.0,
  s1: 4.05, s2: 5.42, s3: 6.67, s4: 8.16,
  end1: 10.05, cols: 10.96, flick: 12.8,
  ql: 14.8, one: 16.05, inn: 17.88, hid: 18.69, out: 19.61, ip: 20.0, drv: 21.36,
  boq: 23.2, quote: 25.18,
  cta: 27.64, tun: 28.82, opp: 29.78, wa: 31.5, end: 33.1,
};
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function seq(keys, times, t, d, z, cx, cy) { let i = 0; while (i + 1 < keys.length && t >= times[i + 1]) i++; if (i === 0) { bgImg(IM[keys[0]], 1, z, cx, cy); return; } xfade(keys[i - 1], keys[i], t, times[i], d, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const _bgImg = bgImg; bgImg = function (...a) { ctx.filter = 'brightness(1.1) contrast(1.04) saturate(1.15)'; _bgImg(...a); ctx.filter = 'none'; };
const KEYS = ['w_off', 'w_ms', 'w_ms-mh', 'w_ms-mh-mo', 'd_cheap', 'g_mix', 'g_mix-flick-1', 'w_in-0.35', 'w_in-0.7', 'w_in', 'g_in-hid', 'u_in-hid', 'a_all', 'e_all', 'd_in-drv', 'w_all', 'u_all'];
const BAD = 'rgba(160,30,25,.92)', DARK = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)';
function chipAt(s, y, t, t0, bg = DARK, color = '#fff', size = 54) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size, bg, color }); }
function shopCard(n, s, y, t, t0, col) { // a small numbered "shop receipt" card sliding in from the right
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .35)); if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540 + (1 - p) * 600, y);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(250,246,238,.96)'; rrect(-400, -62, 800, 124, 26); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(320, 0, 42, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  txt(n, 320, 4, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' });
  txt(s, -30, 4, { size: 52, weight: 900, color: '#1a1416' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function kChip(s, x, y, t, t0, col) { const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .3)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,8,8,.82)'; rrect(-105, -36, 210, 72, 36); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-62, 0, 18, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt(s, 22, 3, { size: 40, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
function check(s, y, t, t0) { const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .3)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, y); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(-380, -58, 760, 116, 58); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = GOOD; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(300, 0, 38, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt('✓', 300, 4, { size: 52, weight: 900, color: '#06210f', dir: 'ltr' }); txt(s, -40, 4, { size: 52, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); }
function S1(t) { // contractor? why go round 5 shops for one villa?
  bgImg(IM.w_off, 1, 1.18 - .05 * prog(t, 0, L.s1), 540, 1040); shade2();
  pop('مقاول؟ 👷', 540, 330, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('ليش تلف على 5 محلات…', 540, 470, t, L.why, 0, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 20 });
  sub2('…عشان فيلا وحدة؟ 🧾', t, L.five, 0, DARK);
}
function S2(t) { // a shop for spots, a shop for strips, a shop for outdoor, drivers from a fourth
  if (t < L.s4) seq(['w_off', 'w_ms', 'w_ms-mh', 'w_ms-mh-mo'], [L.s1 - 1, L.s1 + .1, L.s2 + .1, L.s3 + .1], t, .45, 1.16 - .04 * prog(t, L.s1, L.s4), 540, 1040);
  else xfade('w_ms-mh-mo', 'd_cheap', t, L.s4, .45, 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, L.s4, L.end1), 540, 900);
  shade2();
  title2('4 محلات… لفيلا وحدة 🤯', t, L.s1, 0, C.o2, 62);
  const ys = t < L.s4 ? [1330, 1480, 1630] : [1180, 1330, 1480];
  shopCard('1', 'محل للسبوتات 💡', ys[0], t, L.s1, '#5b8cff');
  shopCard('2', 'محل للشرايط 〰️', ys[1], t, L.s2, '#ff8a2a');
  shopCard('3', 'محل للخارجي 🌴', ys[2], t, L.s3, '#4caf50');
  shopCard('4', 'درايفرات من محل رابع 🔌', 1630, t, L.s4, '#9c27b0');
}
function S3(t) { // and in the end? mismatched light colours + drivers that flicker with the dimmer
  const fl = t > L.flick + .3 && t < L.ql ? (Math.floor((t - L.flick) * 7) % 3 === 0) : false;
  bgImg(IM[fl ? 'g_mix-flick-1' : 'g_mix'], 1, 1.06 + .05 * prog(t, L.end1, L.ql), 540, 960); shade2();
  pop('وبالأخير؟ 😬', 540, 300, t, L.end1, 0, { size: 86, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  kChip('6500K', 170, 760, t, L.cols + .1, '#8fb4ff'); kChip('2700K', 540, 640, t, L.cols + .3, '#ff8a2a'); kChip('5000K', 910, 760, t, L.cols + .5, '#c4ffa8');
  chipAt('❌ ألوان ضو مو متطابقة', 1470, t, L.cols, BAD);
  chipAt('❌ درايفرات ترمّش مع الديمر', 1610, t, L.flick, BAD);
}
function S4(t) { // at QUALITY LIGHT the whole villa from one place
  if (t < L.one) { bgImg(IM.w_off, 1, 1.1, 540, 1040); dim(.45); }
  else seq(['w_off', 'w_in-0.35', 'w_in-0.7', 'w_in'], [L.one - 1, L.one, L.one + .6, L.one + 1.2], t, .5, 1.1 + .04 * prog(t, L.one, L.inn), 540, 1040);
  if (t >= L.one) shade2();
  const lp = prog(t, L.ql, L.ql + .45), fade = 1 - prog(t, L.one + .1, L.one + .5);
  if (fade > 0) { glow(540, 820, 260, C.o, .45 * lp * fade); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 800, 280 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) * fade }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1000, 330, { alpha: prog(t, L.ql + .3, L.ql + .8) * fade }); }
  if (t >= L.one) title2('الفيلا كلها من مكان واحد ✅', t, L.one, 0, GOOD, 60);
}
function S5(t) { // interior, hidden, outdoor against dust and water, drivers that don't flicker
  let k, z = 1.06 + .03 * prog(t, L.inn, L.boq), cy = 960;
  if (t < L.hid) k = ['w_in', 'g_in-hid'];
  else if (t < L.out) k = ['g_in-hid', 'u_in-hid'];
  else if (t < L.drv) k = ['u_in-hid', 'e_all'];
  else k = ['e_all', 'd_in-drv'];
  const t0 = t < L.hid ? L.inn : t < L.out ? L.hid : t < L.drv ? L.out : L.drv;
  xfade(k[0], k[1], t, t0 - .15, .4, z, 540, cy); shade2();
  title2('من مكان واحد 👇', t, L.inn, 0, C.o2, 58);
  check('داخلي 💡', 1240, t, L.inn); check('مخفي ✨', 1380, t, L.hid); check('خارجي IP65 🌧️', 1520, t, L.out); check('درايفرات ما ترمّش 🔌', 1660, t, L.drv);
}
function boqCard(t, t0) { // a BOQ sheet with rows ticking off
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .45)); if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.rotate(-.04); ctx.scale(p, p);

}
function S6(t) { // bring the BOQ or the drawing, and we prepare one quote for the whole villa

function S7(t) { // address, WhatsApp, sign-off

  const lp = prog(t, L.opp - .2, L.opp + .2); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, L.opp - .1, L.opp + .4) });
  pop('مورد واحد… لمشروعك كله ✨', 540, 1500, t, L.opp + .3, 0, { size: 70, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });

const SCENES = [[0, L.s1, S1], [L.s1, L.end1, S2], [L.end1, L.ql, S3], [L.ql, L.inn, S4], [L.inn, L.boq, S5], [L.boq, L.cta, S6], [L.cta, L.end, S7]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < L.ql - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .6)) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/diwaniya_diwan/part.js`

```javascript
// ---------- Kuwaiti diwaniya, layer by layer (diwan 3D body): satisfying switch-on, no voice; one click per layer ----------
const L = { cove: 2.0, spots: 4.5, chand: 7.0, ent: 9.5, close: 11.3, ql: 12.6, end: 15.2 };
function seq(keys, t, t0, t1, z, cx, cy) { const n = keys.length - 1, f = clamp((t - t0) / (t1 - t0)) * n, k = Math.min(n, Math.floor(f)), fr = f - k; bgImg(IM[keys[k]], 1, z, cx, cy); if (k < n && fr > 0) bgImg(IM[keys[k + 1]], E.inOut(fr), z, cx, cy); }
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const _bgImg = bgImg; bgImg = function (...a) { ctx.filter = 'brightness(1.06) contrast(1.04)'; _bgImg(...a); ctx.filter = 'none'; };
const KEYS = ['w_off', 'w_cove-0.5', 'w_cove', 'w_cove-spots-0.5', 'w_cove-spots', 'w_cove-spots-chand-0.5', 'w_cove-spots-chand', 'w_all', 'c_all', 's_all'];
function word(s, t, t0, col = '#fff') { pop(s, 540, 300, t, t0 + .05, 0, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: col, glow: 30 }); }
function S1(t) { // the dark diwaniya, then each layer on a click
  const z = 1.02 + .06 * prog(t, 0, L.close);
  if (t < L.cove) bgImg(IM.w_off, 1, z, 540, 960);
  else if (t < L.spots) seq(['w_off', 'w_cove-0.5', 'w_cove'], t, L.cove, L.cove + .7, z, 540, 960);
  else if (t < L.chand) seq(['w_cove', 'w_cove-spots-0.5', 'w_cove-spots'], t, L.spots, L.spots + .6, z, 540, 960);
  else if (t < L.ent) seq(['w_cove-spots', 'w_cove-spots-chand-0.5', 'w_cove-spots-chand'], t, L.chand, L.chand + .6, z, 540, 960);
  else xfade('w_cove-spots-chand', 'w_all', t, L.ent, .15, z, 540, 960);
  shade2();
  if (t < L.cove) word('الديوانية 🌙', t, 0);
  else if (t < L.spots) word('مخفي ✨', t, L.cove);
  else if (t < L.chand) word('سبوتات', t, L.spots);
  else if (t < L.ent) word('ثريا ✨', t, L.chand);
  else word('المدخل', t, L.ent);
}
function S2(t) { // the chandelier and the seating, then the sign-off
  if (t < L.close + .8) bgImg(IM.c_all, 1, 1.03 + .05 * prog(t, L.close, L.close + .8), 540, 900);
  else xfade('c_all', 's_all', t, L.close + .8, .5, 1.03 + .05 * prog(t, L.close + .8, L.end), 540, 900);
  shade2();
  if (t >= L.ql) dim(.5 * prog(t, L.ql, L.ql + .5));
  const lp = prog(t, L.ql, L.ql + .4); if (lp > 0) { glow(540, 820, 230, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 820, 240 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 980, 270, { alpha: prog(t, L.ql + .3, L.ql + .8) }); }
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.ql + .6, L.ql + 1.0)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 1180); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t >= L.ql + 1.0) txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1330, { size: 46, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.ql + 1.0, L.ql + 1.4) });
  if (t < L.ql) word('ديوانيتك تستاهل ✨', t, L.close, '#fff');
}
const SCENES = [[0, L.close, S1], [L.close, L.end, S2]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .6)) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

```javascript
// ---------- 11 things to ask the electrician before finishing (2D motion graphics, Kuwaiti voice 81 s) ----------
const L = {
  hook: .23, eleven: 2.4, regret: 4.77,
  P: [[6.5, 7.44], [12.72, 13.72], [18.03, 19.45], [24.38, 25.17], [28.71, 29.55], [33.31, 34.09], [37.93, 38.78], [44.61, 45.49], [51.28, 52.07], [57.18, 58.0], [62.65, 64.7]],
  safety: 66.96, kids: 68.22,

};
const ITEMS = [
  ['مخطط العفش', 'ولو رسمة بإيدك… والأفياش بمكانها'],
  ['منسوب الأرضية النهائي', 'عشان الارتفاعات تطلع مضبوطة'],
  ['4 خطوط احتياط', 'بالطبلون… على الأقل'],
  ['خط تكييف لكل غرفة', 'حتى لو ما بتركّب الحين'],
  ['إضاءة طوارئ', 'بالدرج والممرات'],
  ['نقاط كاميرات', 'الباب والحوش والبلكونة'],
  ['لوحة تيار خفيف', 'ومواسير بروحها بعيد عن الكهربا'],
  ['أفياش المطبخ من الحين', 'ورا الكبتات وفوق الكاونتر'],
  ['سويتشات تحويلة', 'الممرات وغرفة النوم'],
  ['مخرج للشفاط', 'المطبخ والحمامات'],
  ['قاطع التسريب الأرضي + الأرضي', '🚫 مو اختياري'],
];
const NAVY = '#0b1428', LINE = 'rgba(120,170,255,.10)', GREENC = '#3ddc84';
function bg(t, warm = 0) {
  const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); g.addColorStop(0, mixHex(NAVY, '#1a120c', warm)); g.addColorStop(1, mixHex('#060a14', '#0b0806', warm)); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.strokeStyle = LINE; ctx.lineWidth = 2; const off = (t * 18) % 60;
  for (let x = -60 + off; x < W; x += 60) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, H); ctx.stroke(); }
  for (let y = -60 + off; y < H; y += 60) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); ctx.stroke(); }
  const sx = ((t * 260) % (W + 800)) - 400; const sg = ctx.createLinearGradient(sx - 200, 0, sx + 200, 0); sg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); sg.addColorStop(.5, 'rgba(255,190,120,.05)'); sg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = sg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
}
function stroke(col, lw) { ctx.strokeStyle = col; ctx.lineWidth = lw; ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round'; }
function bolt(x, y, s, a = 1, col = C.o2) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.scale(s, s); ctx.globalAlpha *= a; ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.shadowColor = col; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(10, -60); ctx.lineTo(-30, 8); ctx.lineTo(-2, 8); ctx.lineTo(-12, 60); ctx.lineTo(30, -10); ctx.lineTo(2, -10); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
// ---- per-point diagrams, drawn around (540, 880), p = 0..1 build progress, t = time ----
const DIA = [
  (p, t) => { // furniture plan: room outline, furniture blocks drop in, socket dots appear
    stroke('#cfe3ff', 6); ctx.strokeRect(300, 640, 480, 480);
    const fur = [[330, 670, 220, 90], [330, 790, 90, 160], [600, 960, 150, 130], [560, 680, 190, 60]];
    fur.forEach(([x, y, w, h], i) => { const k = E.back(prog(p, i * .12, i * .12 + .3)); if (k <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha *= Math.min(1, k); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(207,227,255,.18)'; stroke('#cfe3ff', 3); ctx.fillRect(x, y - (1 - k) * 40, w, h); ctx.strokeRect(x, y - (1 - k) * 40, w, h); ctx.restore(); });
    [[560, 650], [312, 860], [768, 900], [470, 1105]].forEach(([x, y], i) => { const k = E.back(prog(p, .55 + i * .1, .75 + i * .1)); if (k > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = C.o2; ctx.shadowColor = C.o2; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 16 * k, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; } });
  },

    const lx = lerp(300, 780, prog(p, .3, .8)); stroke('#ff3b3b', 5); ctx.shadowColor = '#ff3b3b'; ctx.shadowBlur = 20; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(300, 1080); ctx.lineTo(lx, 1080); ctx.stroke(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    const k = prog(p, .7, 1); if (k > 0) { stroke('#cfe3ff', 4); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(700, 1080); ctx.lineTo(700, 940); ctx.stroke(); icon(IC.socket, 700, 900, 80, k, { color: C.o2, lw: 6 }); txt('📏', 620, 1010, { size: 50, alpha: k }); }
  },
  (p, t) => { // distribution board: rows of breakers, last four glow green as spares
    ctx.fillStyle = '#20252e'; rrect(330, 620, 420, 520, 24); ctx.fill(); stroke('#8c96a6', 4); rrect(330, 620, 420, 520, 24); ctx.stroke();
    for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 6; c++) { const i = r * 6 + c, x = 360 + c * 62, y = 670 + r * 150; const k = prog(p, i * .03, i * .03 + .15); if (k <= 0) continue; const spare = i >= 14; ctx.fillStyle = spare ? (prog(p, .7, .85) > 0 ? GREENC : '#3a414c') : '#e9edf2'; if (spare && prog(p, .7, .85) > 0) { ctx.shadowColor = GREENC; ctx.shadowBlur = 25; } ctx.fillRect(x, y, 46, 110 * k); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; ctx.fillStyle = '#20252e'; ctx.fillRect(x + 14, y + 40, 18, 30 * k); }
    if (p > .8) txt('احتياط ✓', 540, 1080, { size: 46, weight: 900, color: GREENC, alpha: prog(p, .8, 1) });
  },

    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const x = 300 + (i % 2) * 250, y = 640 + Math.floor(i / 2) * 250; stroke('#cfe3ff', 4); ctx.strokeRect(x, y, 230, 230); const k = E.back(prog(p, .1 + i * .15, .35 + i * .15)); if (k > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = '#eef3f8'; rrect(x + 40, y + 20, 150 * Math.min(1, k), 40, 10); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(120,200,255,.6)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) { const o = ((t * 60 + j * 25) % 70); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x + 60 + j * 40, y + 70 + o); ctx.lineTo(x + 50 + j * 40, y + 90 + o); ctx.stroke(); } } }

    const on = prog(p, .35, .5); ctx.fillStyle = '#f2f2f2'; rrect(470, 640, 140, 50, 10); ctx.fill(); if (on > 0) { const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(540, 690, 10, 540, 690, 380); g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(255,236,190,${.55 * on})`); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,236,190,0)'); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(470, 690); ctx.lineTo(260, 1160); ctx.lineTo(820, 1160); ctx.lineTo(610, 690); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe9bd'; ctx.fillRect(480, 668, 120, 16); }

GREENC : '#2a4'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(475 + i * 40, 690, 8, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); }
    const k = prog(p, .3, .8); [[480, BLUE], [600, C.o2]].forEach(([x, col], i) => { stroke(col, 18); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 790); ctx.lineTo(x, 790 + 360 * k); ctx.stroke(); stroke('rgba(255,255,255,.5)', 3); for (let y = 800; y < 790 + 360 * k; y += 40) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y + ((t * 80) % 40), 4, 0, 7); ctx.stroke(); } });

function dots(i, t) { for (let k = 0; k < 11; k++) { const x = 540 + (k - 5) * 64, on = k <= i; ctx.fillStyle = on ? function SP(i) { // one checklist point
  return (t) => {
    const [t0, td] = L.P[i], end = i < 10 ? L.P[i + 1][0] : L.ql;
    bg(t, i === 10 ? .4 : 0);
    txt('تطلبها من الكهربائي ⚡', 540, 210, { size: 44, weight: 800, color: 'rgba(255,255,255,.7)' }); dots(i, t);
    const b = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .4)); if (b > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 450); ctx.scale(b, b); ctx.fillStyle = i === 10 ? '#d32f2f' : C.o; ctx.shadowColor = ctx.fillStyle; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 90, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt(String(i + 1), 0, 8, { size: 110, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
    DIA[i](prog(t, t0 + .3, Math.min(end - .3, td + 2.5)), t);
    const tp = E.out(prog(t, td - .2, td + .3)); if (tp > 0) txt(ITEMS[i][0], 540, 1330 + (1 - tp) * 30, { size: ITEMS[i][0].length > 18 ? 66 : 80, weight: 900, color: '#fff', alpha: tp, glow: 18 });
    const sp = E.out(prog(t, td + .6, td + 1.1)); if (sp > 0) label(ITEMS[i][1], 540, 1470, t, td + .6, 0, { size: 52, bg: i === 10 ? 'rgba(211,47,47,.92)' : 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)', color: '#fff' });
    if (i === 10 && t > L.kids) pop('🛡️ سلامة عيالك', 540, 1640, t, L.kids, 0, { size: 72, weight: 900, color: '#ffd27a', glow: 25 });
  };
}
function S0(t) { // hook: before the electrician starts… 11 things… or you'll regret it
  bg(t, 0);
  const k = E.back(prog(t, .1, .6)); bolt(540, 560, 2.2 * k, 1);
  pop('قبل لا يبدي الكهربائي', 540, 850, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 78, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 20 });
  pop('شغله ببيتك… 🏠', 540, 960, t, L.hook + .5, 0, { size: 70, weight: 900, color: '#fff' });
  const n = Math.min(11, Math.floor(prog(t, L.eleven, L.eleven + 1.1) * 11) + (t > L.eleven ? 1 : 0)); if (t > L.eleven) { const s = E.back(prog(t, L.eleven, L.eleven + .3)); txt(String(Math.max(1, n)), 540, 1230, { size: 300, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 50, scale: s, dir: 'ltr' }); txt('شغلة لازم تطلبها منه', 540, 1430, { size: 64, weight: 900, color: '#fff', alpha: prog(t, L.eleven + .4, L.eleven + .8) }); }
  if (t > L.regret) { const p = E.back(prog(t, L.regret, L.regret + .35)); ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 1620); ctx.rotate(-.05); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = '#d32f2f'; rrect(-250, -62, 500, 124, 30); ctx.fill(); txt('ولا بتندم ❌', 0, 4, { size: 64, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); }
}
function SQ(t) { // QUALITY LIGHT helps you choose the right light for every point
  bg(t, .6); dim(.1);
  const lp = prog(t, L.ql, L.ql + .45); glow(540, 700, 260, C.o, .45 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 680, 280 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 880, 330, { alpha: prog(t, L.ql + .3, L.ql + .8) });
  label('💡 نساعدك تختار الإضاءة الصح', 540, 1180, t, L.help, 0, { size: 58, bg: 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)', color: '#fff' });
  label('لكل نقطة ✓', 540, 1320, t, L.help + .9, 0, { size: 58, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  label('💾 احفظ الفيديو وارسله لكهربائيك', 540, 1560, t, L.help + 1.4, 0, { size: 46, bg: C.o, color: '#fff' });
}
function SC(t) { // address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bg(t, .6);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, L.cta + .1, L.cta + .5)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, L.cta + .3, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, L.opp, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa - .2, L.wa + .2)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, L.cta + .4, L.cta + .8); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, L.cta + .5, L.cta + 1) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, L.sign, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.sign + .5, L.sign + 1.0) });
  if (t > L.sign) sparkles(t, L.sign, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const SCENES = [[0, L.P[0][0], S0], ...L.P.map(([a], i) => [a, i < 10 ? L.P[i + 1][0] : L.ql, SP(i)]), [L.ql, L.cta, SQ], [L.cta, L.end, SC]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .25), clamp((b - t) / .25)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < L.ql - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .6)) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/electrician11_motion/script.txt`

```
[direct, serious] قبل لا يبدي الكهربائي شغله ببيتك… هذي إحدعش شغلة لازم تطلبها منه، ولا بتندم.
[clear] واحد: عطه مخطط العفش… ولو رسمة بإيدك… عشان الأفياش والسويتشات تجي بمكانها الصح.
[clear] اثنين: خله ياخذ منسوب الأرضية النهائي… نفس المبلّط، عشان الارتفاعات تطلع مضبوطة.
[confident] ثلاثة: بالطبلون… خله يترك لك أربع خطوط احتياط على الأقل.
[confident] أربعة: خط تكييف لكل غرفة… حتى لو ما بتركّب الحين.
[clear] خمسة: إضاءة طوارئ بالدرج والممرات… لين ينقطع الكهربا.
[clear] ستة: نقاط كاميرات… عند الباب، وبالحوش ولا البلكونة.
[explaining] سبعة: لوحة تيار خفيف للراوتر والكاميرات… وخطوطها بمواسير بروحها، بعيد عن الكهربا.
[explaining] ثمانية: بالمطبخ… حدد الأفياش ورا الكبتات وفوق الكاونتر من الحين… عشان ما تكسّر السيراميك.
[friendly] تسعة: سويتشات تحويلة بالممرات الطويلة… وبغرفة النوم، عند الباب وعند السرير.
[clear] عشرة: مخرج للشفاط بالمطبخ والحمامات… حتى لو مو الحين.
[warning, serious] وأهم شي… إحدعش: قاطع التسريب الأرضي والأرضي… هذي مو اختيارية، هذي سلامة عيالك.
[warm, confident, closing] وبكواليتي لايت… نساعدك تختار الإضاءة الصح لكل نقطة. زورنا بحولي، شارع تونس، مقابل مجمع الرحاب… أو كلمنا واتساب. خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/electrician11_motion/voice.txt`

```
Male Kuwaiti voice, late 20s to mid 30s, warm and confident with a smooth, slightly low tone. Speaks in natural Kuwaiti Gulf dialect like a friendly lighting and electrical expert giving a friend a practical checklist before finishing his house. Direct and serious at the start, then clear, brisk and confident through each numbered point with short pauses between points, serious on the safety point at the end, then warm and inviting. Clear articulation, medium-brisk pace. Studio-quality, close-mic, no background noise.
```

```javascript
// ---------- Villa facade at night (facade 3D body): dark villa → one cold floodlight on the gate → layers: wall washers, palm up-lights, path lights → IP65 in the rain, 3000 K → seen from the end of the street → CTA ----------
const L = {
  hook: .35, night: 1.91, where: 3.08, bad: 4.46, dark: 6.38,
  layers: 8.08, wash: 10.28, palm: 13.13, path: 14.53,
  ip: 17.48, ip65: 18.27, dust: 19.82, k3: 21.48,
  sud: 23.64, street: 24.55,
  cta: 27.25, tun: 28.41, opp: 29.34, wa: 30.87, sign: 32.24, end: 34.2,
};
function seq(keys, times, t, d, z, cx, cy) { let i = 0; while (i + 1 < keys.length && t >= times[i + 1]) i++; if (i === 0) { bgImg(IM[keys[0]], 1, z, cx, cy); return; } xfade(keys[i - 1], keys[i], t, times[i], d, z, cx, cy); }
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const _bgImg = bgImg; bgImg = function (...a) { ctx.filter = 'brightness(1.12) contrast(1.04)'; _bgImg(...a); ctx.filter = 'none'; };
const KEYS = ['w_off', 'w_bad', 'w_wash-0.5', 'w_wash', 'w_wash-palm', 'w_wash-palm-path-0.5', 'w_wash-palm-path', 'w_all', 'g_all', 's_all', 's_off'];
const BAD = 'rgba(160,30,25,.92)', DARK = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)';
function chipAt(s, y, t, t0, bg = DARK, color = '#fff', size = 54) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size, bg, color }); }
function step(n, s, y, t, t0) { const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .35)); if (p <= 0) return; ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, y); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.9)'; rrect(-420, -58, 840, 116, 58); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(362, 0, 42, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); txt(n, 362, 5, { size: 52, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); txt(s, -30, 4, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); }
function rain(t, a) { ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = a; ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(200,215,255,.55)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3; for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) { const x = (i * 97.3 + t * 260) % 1180 - 50, y = ((i * 211.7) + t * 2300) % 2000 - 60; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.lineTo(x - 10, y + 70); ctx.stroke(); } ctx.restore(); }
function S1(t) { // your house looks nice by day… but at night? where did it go?
  bgImg(IM.w_off, 1, 1.08 - .04 * prog(t, 0, L.bad), 540, 860); shade2();
  pop('بيتك بالنهار حلو… ☀️', 540, 330, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('بس بالليل؟ 🌙', 540, 460, t, L.night, 0, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  pop('وين راح؟ 🤔', 540, 1600, t, L.where, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 30 });
}
function S2(t) { // one white floodlight on the gate, and the rest is dark
  bgImg(IM.w_bad, 1, 1.06 + .03 * prog(t, L.bad, L.layers), 540, 900); shade2();
  chipAt('❌ كشاف واحد أبيض على البوابة', 330, t, L.bad + .1, BAD);
  chipAt('…والباقي ظلام 🌑', 1620, t, L.dark, DARK);
}
function S3(t) { // the facade needs layers: wall washers, palm up-lights, path lights to the door
  const z = 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, L.layers, L.ip);
  seq(['w_off', 'w_wash-0.5', 'w_wash', 'w_wash-palm', 'w_wash-palm-path-0.5', 'w_wash-palm-path'], [L.layers - 1, L.wash, L.wash + .6, L.palm, L.path, L.path + .6], t, .5, z, 540, 880);
  shade2();
  title2('الواجهة تبيلها طبقات ✨', t, L.layers, 0, C.o2, 62);
  step('1', 'وول واشر يغسل الجدار ⬆️', 1360, t, L.wash);
  step('2', 'أب لايت على النخل 🌴', 1500, t, L.palm);
  step('3', 'إضاءة ممر توصلك للباب 🚶', 1640, t, L.path);
}
function S4(t) { // all IP65, against dust and water, at a warm 3000 K
  bgImg(IM.g_all, 1, 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, L.ip, L.sud), 540, 960); shade2(); rain(t, prog(t, L.ip, L.ip + .5) * .9);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, L.ip65, L.ip65 + .4)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 420); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = '#1565c0'; ctx.shadowColor = '#42a5f5'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-230, -90, 460, 180, 40); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('IP65', 0, 8, { size: 120, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  chipAt('🌧️ ضد الغبار والمية', 1480, t, L.dust, DARK);
  chipAt('🔥 ثلاث آلاف كلفن… دافي ✓', 1620, t, L.k3, GOOD, '#06210f');
}
function S5(t) { // and suddenly your villa stands out from the end of the street
  xfade('s_off', 's_all', t, L.sud + .2, .6, 1.12 + .08 * prog(t, L.sud, L.cta), 560, 900); shade2();
  pop('وفجأة… ✨', 540, 330, t, L.sud, 0, { size: 90, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 30 });
  chipAt('فيلتك تبين من أول الشارع 🏡', 1600, t, L.street, GOOD, '#06210f');
}
function S6(t) { // address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM.w_all, 1, 1.1 + .03 * prog(t, L.cta, L.end), 540, 900); dim(.55);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, L.cta + .1, L.cta + .5)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, L.cta + .3, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, L.tun, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa - .2, L.wa + .2)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, L.opp - .2, L.opp + .2); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, L.opp - .1, L.opp + .4) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, L.sign, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.sign + .5, L.sign + 1.0) });
  if (t > L.sign) sparkles(t, L.sign, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
const SCENES = [[0, L.bad, S1], [L.bad, L.layers, S2], [L.layers, L.ip, S3], [L.ip, L.sud, S4], [L.sud, L.cta, S5], [L.cta, L.end, S6]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < L.cta - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .6)) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/magtrack_maglounge/part.js`

```javascript
// ---------- Magnetic track in a lounge (maglounge 3D body): product hero, 17 s ----------
const L = { hook: .35, noel: 2.19, track: 3.94, click: 5.56, slide: 6.86, spot: 8.98, lin: 9.97, pend: 10.6, same: 11.9, cta: 13.81, wa: 15.75, end: 17.4 };
function seq(keys, times, t, d, z, cx, cy) { let i = 0; while (i + 1 < keys.length && t >= times[i + 1]) i++; if (i === 0) { bgImg(IM[keys[0]], 1, z, cx, cy); return; } xfade(keys[i - 1], keys[i], t, times[i], d, z, cx, cy); }
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const KEYS = ['w_n-0-room', 'c_n-1-on-room', 'c_n-2-on-room', 'c_n-3-on-room', 'c_n-3-on-slide-0-room', 'c_n-3-on-slide-0.5-room', 'w_n-3-on-room', 'w_n-3-on-linear-room', 'w_n-3-on-linear-pend-room'];
const DARK = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)';
function chipAt(s, y, t, t0, bg = DARK, color = '#fff', size = 54) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size, bg, color }); }
function S1(t) { // want to move the light… without an electrician?
  bgImg(IM['w_n-0-room'], 1, 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, 0, L.track), 540, 960); shade2();
  pop('تبي تغيّر مكان الضو؟ 💡', 540, 330, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 76, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('…بدون كهربائي؟ 🤔', 540, 1600, t, L.noel, 0, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
}
function S2(t) { // the magnetic track: click the head on… and slide it wherever you want
  const z = 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, L.track, L.spot);
  if (t < L.slide) seq(['c_n-1-on-room', 'c_n-2-on-room', 'c_n-3-on-room'], [L.track - 1, L.click, L.click + .5], t, .25, z, 540, 900);
  else seq(['c_n-3-on-slide-0-room', 'c_n-3-on-slide-0.5-room', 'c_n-3-on-room'], [L.slide - 1, L.slide + .5, L.slide + 1.0], t, .45, z, 540, 900);
  shade2();
  title2('التراك المغناطيسي 🧲', t, L.track, 0, C.o2, 66);
  chipAt('🧲 تلزق الكشاف… كليك', 1520, t, L.click);
  chipAt('↔️ وتسحبه وين ما تبي', 1650, t, L.slide, GOOD, '#06210f');
}
function S3(t) { // spot, linear, pendant… all on the same track
  seq(['w_n-3-on-room', 'w_n-3-on-linear-room', 'w_n-3-on-linear-pend-room'], [L.spot - 1, L.lin, L.pend], t, .35, 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, L.spot, L.cta), 540, 900);
  shade2();
  [['🔦 سبوت', L.spot], ['➖ خطي', L.lin], ['💡 معلّق', L.pend]].forEach(([s, t0], i) => label(s, 540, 1340 + i * 120, t, t0, 0, { size: 56, bg: DARK, color: '#fff' }));
  label('كلهم على نفس المجرى ✓', 540, 330, t, L.same, 0, { size: 60, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
}
function S4(t) { // come see it at the showroom
  bgImg(IM['w_n-3-on-linear-pend-room'], 1, 1.1, 540, 900); dim(.55);
  pop('تعال جرّبه بيدك 👋', 540, 360, t, L.cta, 0, { size: 76, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('بالمعرض – حولي، شارع تونس', 540, 480, t, L.cta + .4, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const lp = prog(t, L.cta + .2, L.cta + .6); glow(540, 800, 210, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 790, 220 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 940, 260, { alpha: prog(t, L.cta + .4, L.cta + .9) });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa - .1, L.wa + .25)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 1180); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1330, { size: 46, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.wa + .3, L.wa + .7) });
}
const SCENES = [[0, L.track, S1], [L.track, L.spot, S2], [L.spot, L.cta, S3], [L.cta, L.end, S4]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 2.5 && t < L.cta - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .5) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .5)) / .5)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/projweek_villaproj/part.js`

```javascript
// ---------- Project of the week (villaproj 3D body): open site → the plan on the floor → cables + drivers before the gypsum → gypsum closed → the villa lights up room by room → hall / kitchen / entrance → designers: from the lighting plan to supply → CTA ----------
const L = {
  hook: .21, open: 1.51, lit: 2.96,
  plan: 4.86, map: 5.7, spot: 7.13, hid: 7.6, sconce: 8.6,
  wire: 10.7, drv: 12.0, before: 13.83,
  now: 15.61, closed: 16.58, rooms: 17.72,
  hall: 20.51, kit: 21.7, ent: 23.42,
  des: 25.6, work: 27.85, supply: 30.19,
  cta: 31.58, tun: 32.78, opp: 33.78, wa: 35.43, sign: 36.78, end: 39.7,
};
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function seq(keys, times, t, d, z, cx, cy) { let i = 0; while (i + 1 < keys.length && t >= times[i + 1]) i++; if (i === 0) { bgImg(IM[keys[0]], 1, z, cx, cy); return; } xfade(keys[i - 1], keys[i], t, times[i], d, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const _bgImg = bgImg; bgImg = function (...a) { ctx.filter = a[0] === IM.c_done ? 'brightness(2.3) contrast(1.05)' : 'brightness(1.08) contrast(1.04)'; _bgImg(...a); ctx.filter = 'none'; };
const KEYS = ['t_site-top', 't_site-plan-top', 'c_site-wire', 't_site-wire-top', 'c_done', 'c_done-lit-1', 't_done-top-dim-0.6', 't_done-top-lit-0.35-dim-0.6', 't_done-top-lit-0.7-dim-0.6', 't_done-top-lit-1-dim-0.6', 'h_done-lit-1', 'k_done-lit-1', 'e_done-lit-1', 'h_done'];
const DARK = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)';
function chipAt(s, y, t, t0, bg = DARK, color = '#fff', size = 54) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size, bg, color }); }
function step(n, s, t, t0, col = C.o) { // numbered step badge + title at the top
  const p = E.back(prog(t, t0, t0 + .35)); if (p <= 0) return;
  ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 300); ctx.scale(p, p);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)'; rrect(-380, -62, 760, 124, 62); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(318, 0, 46, 0, 7); ctx.fill();
  txt(n, 318, 5, { size: 58, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' });
  txt(s, -40, 4, { size: 54, weight: 900, color: '#fff' });
  ctx.restore();
}
function S1(t) { // a villa: from an open ceiling until it lit up
  if (t < L.lit) bgImg(IM['t_site-top'], 1, 1.06 - .03 * prog(t, 0, L.lit), 540, 960);
  else xfade('t_site-top', 't_done-top-lit-1-dim-0.6', t, L.lit, .5, 1.03 + .03 * prog(t, L.lit, L.plan), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  label('مشروع الأسبوع 🏗️', 540, 220, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 56, bg: C.o, color: '#fff' });
  pop('من سقف مفتوح…', 540, 380, t, L.open, 0, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('لين ما ولّعت ✨', 540, 1620, t, L.lit, 0, { size: 84, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
}
function S2(t) { // first we draw the plan: where the spots go, the hidden light, the wall lights
  xfade('t_site-top', 't_site-plan-top', t, L.map - .3, .6, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.plan, L.wire), 540, 960); shade2();
  step('1', 'نرسم المخطط 📐', t, L.plan);
  chipAt('🎯 وين السبوت', 1400, t, L.spot, 'rgba(255,122,26,.92)');
  chipAt('✨ وين المخفي', 1530, t, L.hid, 'rgba(255,179,71,.95)', '#1a1416');
  chipAt('🔆 وين الأباليك', 1660, t, L.sconce, 'rgba(255,211,77,.95)', '#1a1416');
}
function S3(t) { // then cables and drivers, before the gypsum is closed
  if (t < L.drv) bgImg(IM['c_site-wire'], 1, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.wire, L.drv), 540, 900);
  else xfade('c_site-wire', 't_site-wire-top', t, L.drv, .5, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.drv, L.now), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  step('2', 'الأسلاك والدرايفرات 🔌', t, L.wire);
  chipAt('⏳ قبل لا يتسكّر الجبس', 1600, t, L.before, 'rgba(160,30,25,.92)');
}
function S4(t) { // now the gypsum is closed, and the villa lights up room by room
  if (t < L.rooms) xfade('c_site-wire', 'c_done', t, L.closed - .2, .5, 1.04 + .03 * prog(t, L.now, L.rooms), 540, 900);
  else seq(['c_done', 't_done-top-dim-0.6', 't_done-top-lit-0.35-dim-0.6', 't_done-top-lit-0.7-dim-0.6', 't_done-top-lit-1-dim-0.6'], [L.rooms - 1, L.rooms, L.rooms + .7, L.rooms + 1.4, L.rooms + 2.1], t, .45, 1.03 + .04 * prog(t, L.rooms, L.hall), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  step('3', 'الجبس تسكّر ✅', t, L.now, GOOD);
  if (t > L.rooms) pop('غرفة… غرفة 💡', 540, 1620, t, L.rooms + .2, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
}
function S5(t) { // a warm hall, a clear kitchen, an entrance that welcomes you
  if (t < L.kit) bgImg(IM['h_done-lit-1'], 1, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.hall, L.kit), 540, 960);
  else if (t < L.ent) xfade('h_done-lit-1', 'k_done-lit-1', t, L.kit - .1, .4, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.kit, L.ent), 540, 960);
  else xfade('k_done-lit-1', 'e_done-lit-1', t, L.ent - .1, .4, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.ent, L.des), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  const s = t < L.kit ? ['صالة دافية 🛋️', L.hall] : t < L.ent ? ['مطبخ واضح 🍳', L.kit] : ['مدخل يرحّب فيك 🚪', L.ent];
  label(s[0], 540, 1600, t, s[1], 0, { size: 66, bg: DARK, color: C.o2 });
}
function S6(t) { // designer or decor office? we work with you from the lighting plan to supply
  bgImg(IM['t_done-top-lit-1-dim-0.6'], 1, 1.08 + .04 * prog(t, L.des, L.cta), 540, 960); dim(.45);
  const lp = prog(t, L.des + .1, L.des + .5); glow(540, 760, 230, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 740, 240 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 910, 290, { alpha: prog(t, L.des + .3, L.des + .8) });
  pop('مصمم أو مكتب ديكور؟ 🎨', 540, 360, t, L.des, 0, { size: 74, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  label('📐 من مخطط الإضاءة', 540, 1260, t, L.work, 0, { size: 60, bg: DARK, color: '#fff' });
  label('📦 لين التوريد', 540, 1400, t, L.supply, 0, { size: 60, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
  txt('(تصوير 3D توضيحي)', 540, 1820, { size: 34, weight: 700, color: 'rgba(255,255,255,.7)', alpha: prog(t, L.des, L.des + .5) });
}
function S7(t) { // address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM['h_done-lit-1'], 1, 1.08 + .03 * prog(t, L.cta, L.end), 540, 1000); dim(.55);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, L.cta + .1, L.cta + .5)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, L.cta + .3, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, L.tun, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa - .2, L.wa + .2)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, L.opp - .2, L.opp + .2); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, L.opp - .1, L.opp + .4) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, L.sign, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.sign + .6, L.sign + 1.1) });
  if (t > L.sign) sparkles(t, L.sign, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
const SCENES = [[0, L.plan, S1], [L.plan, L.wire, S2], [L.wire, L.now, S3], [L.now, L.hall, S4], [L.hall, L.des, S5], [L.des, L.cta, S6], [L.cta, L.end, S7]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < L.des - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .6)) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/salonspots/part.js`

```javascript
// ---------- Living room: where do the spots go? (salonspots 3D body) ----------
const L = { hook: .36, mid: 1.46, wall: 2.26, most: 4.05, res: 6.1, face: 7.12, dark: 8.18, right: 10.18, seat: 11.06, cm: 12.95, art: 15.72, table: 17.6, tv: 19.33, notv: 20.41, refl: 21.95, photo: 23.48, wa1: 24.52, plan: 25.81, cta: 27.32, tun: 28.52, opp: 29.48, wa: 31.07, sign: 32.44, end: 34.2 };
function seq(keys, times, t, d, z, cx, cy) { let i = 0; while (i + 1 < keys.length && t >= times[i + 1]) i++; if (i === 0) { bgImg(IM[keys[0]], 1, z, cx, cy); return; } xfade(keys[i - 1], keys[i], t, times[i], d, z, cx, cy); }
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const _bgImg = bgImg; bgImg = function (...a) { ctx.filter = a[0] === IM['p_good-1'] ? 'brightness(.78) contrast(1.08)' : 'none'; _bgImg(...a); ctx.filter = 'none'; };
const KEYS = ['w_mid', 'w_good-0.5', 'w_good-1', 'p_mid', 'p_good-1', 't_mid-tv', 't_good-1', 'c_marks'];
const BAD = 'rgba(160,30,25,.92)', DARK = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)';
function chipAt(s, y, t, t0, bg = DARK, color = '#fff', size = 54) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size, bg, color }); }
function S1(t) { // living-room spots: in the middle, or by the wall?
  bgImg(IM.c_marks, 1, 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, 0, L.most), 540, 960); dim(.15); shade2();
  pop('سبوتات الصالة… 🤔', 540, 330, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 78, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  label('بالنص؟', 540, 1440, t, L.mid, 0, { size: 62, bg: DARK, color: '#fff' });
  label('ولا عند الجدار؟', 540, 1590, t, L.wall, 0, { size: 62, bg: DARK, color: '#fff' });
}
function S2(t) { // most people put them in the middle: shadow on faces, dark walls
  if (t < L.dark) bgImg(IM.w_mid, 1, 1.05 + .04 * prog(t, L.most, L.dark), 540, 960); else xfade('w_mid', 'p_mid', t, L.dark, .5, 1.05 + .04 * prog(t, L.dark, L.right), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  title2('أغلب الناس يحطونها بالنص ❌', t, L.most, 0, '#ff8a80', 58);
  chipAt('😕 ظل على الوجه', 1480, t, L.face, BAD);
  chipAt('🌑 والجدار مظلم', 1620, t, L.dark, BAD);
}
function S3(t) { // the right way: along the seating ~60 cm off the wall, one on the painting, one over the table
  const z = 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, L.right, L.tv);
  if (t < L.art) seq(['w_mid', 'w_good-0.5', 'w_good-1'], [L.right - 1, L.seat, L.seat + .8], t, .5, z, 540, 960);
  else xfade('w_good-1', 'p_good-1', t, L.art, .5, z, 540, 960);
  shade2();
  title2('الصح ✅', t, L.right, 0, '#7dff9c', 70);
  if (t < L.art) { chipAt('🛋️ تمشي مع الجلسة', 1420, t, L.seat, GOOD, '#06210f'); chipAt('📏 تقريبًا 60 سم من الجدار', 1560, t, L.cm); }
  else { chipAt('🖼️ سبوت موجّه على اللوحة', 1480, t, L.art, GOOD, '#06210f'); chipAt('☕ وواحد فوق الطاولة', 1620, t, L.table); }
}
function S4(t) { // the TV? no spot above it, because of the reflection
  if (t < L.refl + .6) bgImg(IM['t_mid-tv'], 1, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.tv, L.photo), 540, 960); else xfade('t_mid-tv', 't_good-1', t, L.refl + .6, .5, 1.04 + .04 * prog(t, L.tv, L.photo), 540, 960);
  if (t < L.refl + .9) { const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(470, 760, 10, 470, 760, 260); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,245,225,.85)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,245,225,0)'); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = prog(t, L.notv, L.notv + .4); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(150, 450, 640, 640); ctx.restore(); }
  shade2();
  title2('والتلفزيون؟ 📺', t, L.tv, 0, C.o2, 66);
  chipAt('❌ لا تحط سبوت فوقه', 1480, t, L.notv, BAD);
  chipAt('…عشان الانعكاس ✨', 1620, t, L.refl);
}
function S5(t) { // photograph your living room, send it on WhatsApp, we place them with you
  bgImg(IM['w_good-1'], 1, 1.08, 540, 960); dim(.45);
  pop('📸 صوّر صالتك', 540, 420, t, L.photo, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa1, L.wa1 + .35)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-300, -70, 600, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('طرّشها واتساب 📲', 0, 4, { size: 58, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); }
  chipAt('📐 ونوزعها وياك', 1400, t, L.plan, GOOD, '#06210f', 60);
}
function S6(t) { // address, WhatsApp, sign-off
  bgImg(IM['p_good-1'], 1, 1.08 + .03 * prog(t, L.cta, L.end), 540, 1000); dim(.55);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, L.cta + .1, L.cta + .5)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 380); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 110, 0, 7); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); icon(IC.pin, 540, 380, 120 * p, 1, { color: '#fff', lw: 10 }); }
  pop('حولي', 540, 590, t, L.cta + .3, 0, { size: 96, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 700, t, L.tun, 0, { size: 50, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa - .2, L.wa + .2)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 900); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  const lp = prog(t, L.opp - .2, L.opp + .2); glow(540, 1180, 200, C.o, .35 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 1170, 200 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 40, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 1310, 250, { alpha: prog(t, L.opp - .1, L.opp + .4) });
  pop('خلّ بيتك ينوّر صح ✨', 540, 1500, t, L.sign, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: C.o2, glow: 30 });
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1640, { size: 50, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.sign + .5, L.sign + 1.0) });
  if (t > L.sign) sparkles(t, L.sign, 12, 160, 1400, 760, 300);
}
const SCENES = [[0, L.most, S1], [L.most, L.right, S2], [L.right, L.tv, S3], [L.tv, L.photo, S4], [L.photo, L.cta, S5], [L.cta, L.end, S6]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 3.0 && t < L.cta - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .6) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .6)) / .6)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```

## `docs/video-handoff/samples/stripprof/part.js`

```javascript
// ---------- LED strip: bare vs aluminium profile + opal cover, and COB (stripprof 3D body), 23 s ----------
const L = { hook: .23, why: 1.33, prof: 4.32, parts: 6.16, cool: 8.34, bare: 10.8, dots: 12.15, heat: 13.35, smooth: 15.84, cob: 18.31, cta: 20.18, wa: 21.31, end: 23.1 };
function xfade(a, b, t, t0, d, z, cx, cy) { bgImg(IM[a], 1, z, cx, cy); const k = E.inOut(prog(t, t0, t0 + d)); if (k > 0) bgImg(IM[b], k, z, cx, cy); }
function dim(a) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8,6,5,${a})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
const KEYS = ['w_off', 'w_on', 'l_on', 'l_on-age-1', 'r_on', 'l_on-cob', 'd_on'];
const BAD = 'rgba(160,30,25,.92)', DARK = 'rgba(18,14,12,.88)';
function chipAt(s, y, t, t0, bg = DARK, color = '#fff', size = 54) { label(s, 540, y, t, t0, 0, { size, bg, color }); }
function S1(t) { // same strip… why one shows dots and the other is smooth?
  if (t < .5) bgImg(IM.w_off, 1, 1.05, 540, 960); else xfade('w_off', 'w_on', t, .5, .4, 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, .5, L.prof), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  pop('نفس الشريط… 💡', 540, 330, t, L.hook, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  label('نقاط 😕', 300, 1300, t, L.why + .6, 0, { size: 54, bg: BAD, color: '#fff' });
  label('ناعم ✨', 790, 1300, t, L.why + 1.3, 0, { size: 54, bg: GOOD, color: '#06210f' });
}
function S2(t) { // the difference is the profile: aluminium channel + white cover, spreads the light and cools the strip
  if (t < L.parts) bgImg(IM.r_on, 1, 1.05 + .04 * prog(t, L.prof, L.parts), 540, 960); else xfade('r_on', 'd_on', t, L.parts, .45, 1.04 + .05 * prog(t, L.parts, L.bare), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  title2('الفرق بالبروفايل ✓', t, L.prof, 0, C.o2, 66);
  chipAt('🔩 قناة ألمنيوم + غطاء أبيض', 1480, t, L.parts);
  chipAt('✨ يوزّع الضو… ❄️ ويبرّد الشريط', 1620, t, L.cool, GOOD, '#06210f');
}
function S3(t) { // without a profile: dots on the wall, and heat yellows it fast
  if (t < L.heat) bgImg(IM.l_on, 1, 1.05 + .04 * prog(t, L.bare, L.heat), 540, 960); else xfade('l_on', 'l_on-age-1', t, L.heat, .6, 1.05 + .05 * prog(t, L.heat, L.smooth), 540, 960);
  shade2();
  title2('بدون بروفايل؟ ❌', t, L.bare, 0, '#ff8a80', 66);
  chipAt('نقاط بالجدار 😕', 1480, t, L.dots, BAD);
  chipAt('🔥 والحرارة تصفّره بسرعة', 1620, t, L.heat, BAD);
}
function S4(t) { // want a smooth line with no dots at all? COB strip
  xfade('l_on', 'l_on-cob', t, L.cob - .1, .5, 1.05 + .04 * prog(t, L.smooth, L.cta), 540, 960); shade2();
  title2('تبي خط ناعم بدون نقاط أبد؟', t, L.smooth, 0, C.o2, 58);
  const p = E.back(prog(t, L.cob, L.cob + .4)); if (p > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 1500); ctx.scale(p, p); ctx.fillStyle = C.o; ctx.shadowColor = C.o; ctx.shadowBlur = 40; rrect(-260, -80, 520, 160, 40); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('شريط COB', 0, 6, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: '#fff' }); ctx.restore(); }
}
function S5(t) { // visit / WhatsApp
  bgImg(IM.w_on, 1, 1.1, 540, 960); dim(.55);
  pop('زورنا بحولي 📍', 540, 380, t, L.cta, 0, { size: 80, weight: 900, color: '#fff', glow: 25 });
  pop('شارع تونس – مقابل مجمع الرحاب', 540, 500, t, L.cta + .3, 0, { size: 48, weight: 800, color: '#f3dcc0' });
  const lp = prog(t, L.cta + .2, L.cta + .6); glow(540, 800, 210, C.o, .4 * lp); drawImg(LOGO3D, 540, 790, 220 * lerp(.3, 1, E.back(lp)), { glow: 50, alpha: Math.min(1, lp * 3) }); drawImg(WM, 540, 940, 260, { alpha: prog(t, L.cta + .4, L.cta + .9) });
  const q = E.back(prog(t, L.wa - .1, L.wa + .25)); if (q > 0) { ctx.save(); ctx.translate(540, 1180); ctx.scale(q, q); ctx.fillStyle = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowColor = '#25D366'; ctx.shadowBlur = 50; rrect(-330, -70, 660, 140, 70); ctx.fill(); ctx.shadowBlur = 0; txt('+965 5111 1725', 0, 4, { size: 66, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr' }); ctx.restore(); }
  txt('@quality_light1', 540, 1330, { size: 46, weight: 900, color: '#fff', dir: 'ltr', alpha: prog(t, L.wa + .3, L.wa + .7) });
}
const SCENES = [[0, L.prof, S1], [L.prof, L.bare, S2], [L.bare, L.smooth, S3], [L.smooth, L.cta, S4], [L.cta, L.end, S5]];
function draw(t) {
  beginFrame();
  for (const [a, b, fn] of SCENES) { if (t < a || t >= b) continue; const al = Math.min(a === 0 ? 1 : clamp((t - a) / .3), clamp((b - t) / .3)); ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = al; fn(t); ctx.restore(); }
  if (t > 2.5 && t < L.cta - .2) drawImg(LOGO3D, 100, 1850, 90, { alpha: .85 });
  for (const [a] of SCENES.slice(1)) sweep(t, a);
  endFrame(t);
  if (t > L.end - .5) { ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(0,0,0,${clamp((t - (L.end - .5)) / .5)})`; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H); }
}
async function init() {
  const load = src => new Promise(r => { const i = new Image(); i.onload = () => r(i); i.src = src; });
  [LOGO3D, WM] = await Promise.all([load('logo3d.png'), load('wordmark.png')]);
  for (const k of KEYS) IM[k] = await load(`r/${k}.jpg`);
  for (const w of [600, 700, 800, 900]) await document.fonts.load(`${w} 60px Cairo`);
  await document.fonts.ready; makeGrain(); window.READY = true;
}
init();
```
